    + '.w128t .nm i{width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--c);flex:none;}'
    + '.w128t .nm b{font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-size:20px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.3px;color:' + INK + ';line-height:1.05;}'
    + '.w128t .ld{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);}'
    + '.w128t .st{display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;}'
    + '.w128t .st span{font-size:12px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.06);border-radius:8px;padding:4px 8px;color:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '.w128t .st span.od{background:rgba(179,64,47,.1);color:' + FLAG + ';}'
    + '.w128t .st b{font-weight:700;}'
    + '.w128t .ft{display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;font-size:12px;color:#5c5a56;border-top:1px dashed rgba(16,63,57,.14);padding-top:8px;}'
    + '.w128t .ft span{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;}'
    + '.w128t .ft .w128i{width:14px;height:14px;color:rgba(16,63,57,.45);}'
    + '.w128t .ft .stale{color:' + FLAG + ';font-weight:600;}'
    + '.w128t .ft .stale .w128i{color:' + FLAG + ';}'
    + 'li.w128flash{animation:w128flash 1.8s ease 1;border-radius:12px;}'
    + '@keyframes w128flash{0%,40%{background:rgba(220,165,39,.28);}100%{background:transparent;}}'
    + '@media (max-width:1100px){.w128teams{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));}}'
    + '@media (max-width:900px){.w128brief,.w128row{grid-template-columns:1fr;}.w128brief .nx{border-left:none;border-top:1px solid rgba(255,250,242,.1);}}'
    + '@media (max-width:640px){.w128teams{grid-template-columns:1fr;}.w128kpis{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));}.w128k b{font-size:28px;}.w128brief p{font-size:15.5px;}.w128d{flex-wrap:wrap;}.w128d .ac{flex-direction:row;width:100%;padding-left:74px;}.w128brief .tx{padding:18px;}}'
    + '@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.w128k,.w128t{transition:none;}li.w128flash{animation:none;background:rgba(220,165,39,.2);}}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- data: what the core already loaded, plus a few reads of our own ---------- */
  var X = { loaded: 0, loading: null, fund: [], goals: [], shout: [], wins: [], vol: [], fundGoals: null, donations: null, board: null };
  async function q(p) { try { var r = await p; return (r && !r.error && r.data) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function fyStart(d) { d = d || today(); return mk(d.getMonth() >= 9 ? d.getFullYear() : d.getFullYear() - 1, 9, 1, 12); }
  function fyEnd(d) { var s = fyStart(d); return mk(s.getFullYear() + 1, 8, 30, 12); }
  async function load(force) {
    if (X.loading) return X.loading;
    if (X.loaded && !force && Date.now() - X.loaded < 60000) return;
    X.loading = (async function () {
      var t = today(), from = ymd(addDays(t, -45)), to = ymd(addDays(t, 60));
      var jobs = [
        q(db.from('fund_events').select('id,title,start_date,end_date,status,raised,target_min,target_max,lead,season,precision').order('start_date')),
        q(db.from('goals').select('id,department,title,status,quarter,due_date,updated_at,parent_goal_id')),
        q(db.from('hub_shoutouts').select('id,created_at,kind,person,member_id,team,text,active').eq('active', true).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(20)),
        q(db.from('goal_wins').select('id,department,win,meeting_date,created_at').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(20)),
        staffOk() ? q(db.from('volunteer_events').select('id,title,code,event_date,status,location,kind').gte('event_date', from).lte('event_date', to).order('event_date')) : Promise.resolve([]),
        staffOk() ? q(db.from('hub_settings').select('value').eq('key', 'fund_goals')) : Promise.resolve([])
      ];
      if (tabOk('finances')) jobs.push(q(db.from('finance_transactions').select('amount,category,txn_date,duplicate_of').eq('category', '4100 Donation').gte('txn_date', ymd(fyStart())).limit(2000)));
      var r = await Promise.all(jobs);
      X.fund = r[0]; X.goals = r[1]; X.shout = r[2]; X.wins = r[3]; X.vol = r[4];
      X.fundGoals = (r[5][0] && r[5][0].value) || X.fundGoals || null;
      X.donations = r[6] ? r[6].filter(function (x) { return !x.duplicate_of && Number(x.amount || 0) > 0 && x.category === '4100 Donation'; }).reduce(function (s, x) { return s + Number(x.amount || 0); }, 0) : null;
      /* the board: only for people who can see it (wave126 answers from board_home) */
      X.board = null;
      try {
        if (window.w126 && (tabOk('governance') || tabOk('committees'))) {
          await window.w126.me(); await window.w126.load();
          var st = window.w126.state();
          if (st && st.data) X.board = { data: st.data, cycle: window.w126.cycle ? window.w126.cycle() : null };
        }
      } catch (e) { X.board = null; }
      X.loaded = Date.now(); X.loading = null;
    })();
    return X.loading;
  }

  /* ---------- the facts ---------- */
  function decisions() {
    var all = []; try { all = (typeof DB_DECISIONS !== 'undefined' ? DB_DECISIONS : []) || []; } catch (e) { all = []; }
    var t = today();
    return all.filter(function (d) { return !d.done; }).map(function (d) {
      var o = dayOfIso(d.opened_at) || dayOfIso(d.updated_at) || t;
      return { d: d, ref: d.id, title: d.title, area: d.area, cat: d.reason_category, move: d.suggested_move, why: d.reason_note, rec: d.recommendation, owner: decider(d), age: Math.max(0, daysBetween(o, t)), conf: !!d.confidential };
    }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.age - a.age; });
  }
  function decider(d) {
    var o = String((d && d.owner) || '').trim();
    if (o === 'Both' || !o) { try { if (typeof window.w116ExecOf === 'function') o = String(window.w116ExecOf(d) || ''); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } if (!o || o === 'Both') o = 'Steel and Suhaib'; }
    return o.replace(/\s*&\s*/g, ' and ');
  }
  function doneGoal(g) { return /\b(closed|done|completed?|achieved|met)\b/i.test(String(g.status || '')) && !/\bincomplete\b/i.test(String(g.status || '')); }
  function onTrackGoal(g) { return doneGoal(g) || /on track/i.test(String(g.status || '')); }
  function sentence(s) { s = String(s || '').trim(); return /[.!?\u2026]$/.test(s) ? s : s + '.'; }
  function tasks() { try { return (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function updates() { try { return (typeof DB_UPDATES !== 'undefined' ? DB_UPDATES : []) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function members() { try { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function changes() { try { return (typeof DB_CHANGES !== 'undefined' ? DB_CHANGES : []) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function openTask(t) { return !t.done; }
  function overdue(t) { var d = parseDay(t.due_date); return !t.done && d && d < today(); }
  function closedWithin(t, days) { if (!t.done) return false; var d = dayOfIso(t.closed_at); return !!d && daysBetween(d, today()) <= days; }
  function teamStats(dept) {
    var ts = tasks().filter(function (t) { return t.department === dept; });
    var ups = updates().filter(function (u) { return u.department === dept; }).map(function (u) { return dayOfIso(u.created_at); }).filter(Boolean).sort(function (a, b) { return b - a; });
    var gs = (X.goals || []).filter(function (g) { return g.department === dept; });
    var leads = members().filter(function (m) { return m.department === dept && /\b(lead|co-lead|director|co-director|head|manager)\b/i.test(m.role || ''); }).map(function (m) { return String(m.name || '').split(' ')[0]; });
    return { dept: dept, open: ts.filter(openTask).length, overdue: ts.filter(overdue).length, closed30: ts.filter(function (t) { return closedWithin(t, 30); }).length, last: ups[0] || null, goals: gs, leads: leads, people: members().filter(function (m) { return m.department === dept; }).length };
  }
  /* the fundraising year: the events' results inside the fiscal year that contains today */
  function fundYear() {
    var s = fyStart(), e = fyEnd(), t = today();
    var inYear = function (f) { var d = parseDay(f.start_date); return d && d >= s && d <= e; };
    var raised = (X.fund || []).filter(inYear).reduce(function (a, f) { return a + Number(f.raised || 0); }, 0);
    /* the plan year comes from the fundraising settings (staff); the board cannot read them, so it falls back to the seeded plan */
    var g = X.fundGoals || {}, planLabel = String(g.plan || '2026-27');
    var fyLabel = s.getFullYear() + '-' + String(s.getFullYear() + 1).slice(2);
    var target = Number(g.target || 20000);
    var planYear = !planLabel || planLabel === fyLabel;
    var missing = (X.fund || []).filter(function (f) { var d = parseDay(f.end_date) || parseDay(f.start_date); return d && d < t && f.precision !== 'season' && ['planned', 'confirmed', 'tentative', 'in progress', 'to confirm'].indexOf(String(f.status || '').toLowerCase()) >= 0 && (f.raised == null || f.raised === ''); });
    return { start: s, end: e, raised: raised, target: target, planYear: planYear, fyLabel: fyLabel, planLabel: planLabel, missing: missing, daysLeft: daysBetween(t, e) };
  }
  function orgGoals() { return (X.goals || []).filter(function (g) { return g.department === 'org'; }); }
  function atRiskGoal(g) { return /delayed|incomplete|at risk|off track|blocked|stuck/i.test(String(g.status || '')); }
  function boardMeetings() { var b = X.board && X.board.data; return b ? (b.meetings || []).filter(function (m) { return m.kind === 'board' && m.meets_at && m.status !== 'held' && m.status !== 'cancelled'; }).map(function (m) { return { m: m, d: dayOfIso(m.meets_at) }; }).filter(function (x) { return x.d && x.d >= today(); }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.d - b.d; }) : []; }
  function boardAtRisk() { var b = X.board && X.board.data; return b ? (b.items || []).filter(function (i) { return i.status === 'at_risk'; }) : []; }

  /* ---------- the brief ---------- */
  function briefText() {
    var ds = decisions(), fy = fundYear(), t = today(), s = [];
    var old = ds.filter(function (d) { return d.age > 60; });
    if (ds.length) s.push('<b>' + words(ds.length) + (ds.length === 1 ? ' decision is' : ' decisions are') + ' waiting on the executives</b>' + (old.length ? (old.length === ds.length ? (ds.length === 1 ? ', for ' + old[0].age + ' days.' : ', every one of them for more than 60 days.') : ', ' + words(old.length).toLowerCase() + ' of them for more than 60 days.') : '.'));
    else s.push('<b>No decisions are waiting on the executives.</b>');
    if (fy.daysLeft >= 0 && fy.daysLeft <= 14) s.push('The fiscal year closes on ' + DAYS[fy.end.getDay()] + ' (' + fmtDay(fy.end) + ')' + (fy.raised ? ', with <b>' + money(fy.raised) + '</b> raised by the events this year.' : '.'));
    else if (fy.planYear) s.push('The events have raised <b>' + money(fy.raised) + '</b> of the ' + money(fy.target) + ' target this fiscal year.');
    else if (fy.raised) s.push('The events have raised <b>' + money(fy.raised) + '</b> this fiscal year.');
    if (fy.missing.length) s.push(words(fy.missing.length) + ' past ' + (fy.missing.length === 1 ? 'event still needs its result' : 'events still need their results') + ' (' + listJoin(fy.missing.slice(0, 3).map(function (f) { return f.title; })) + ').');
    var quiet = TEAMS.filter(function (d) { var st = teamStats(d); return !st.last || daysBetween(st.last, t) > 21; });
    if (quiet.length >= 2) s.push(words(quiet.length) + ' teams have had no meeting update in three weeks.');
    var bm = boardMeetings()[0];
    if (bm) s.push('The ' + (bm.m.title || 'board meeting').replace(/^Q(\d) board meeting$/i, 'Q$1 board meeting') + ' is ' + DAYS[bm.d.getDay()] + ', ' + fmtDay(bm.d) + '.');
    return s.join(' ');
  }
  function nextItems() {
    var t = today(), out = [];
    var bm = boardMeetings()[0];
    if (bm) out.push({ k: 'Board', d: bm.d, t: bm.m.title || 'Board meeting', go: '#governance' });
    var fe = (X.fund || []).filter(function (f) { var d = parseDay(f.start_date); return d && d >= t && f.status !== 'idea' && f.status !== 'cancelled' && f.status !== 'done'; })[0];
    if (fe) out.push({ k: 'Fundraising', d: parseDay(fe.start_date), t: fe.title, go: '#fundevents', month: fe.precision === 'month' });
    var ve = (X.vol || []).filter(function (v) { var d = parseDay(v.event_date); return d && d >= t && v.status !== 'cancelled'; })[0];
    if (ve) out.push({ k: 'Serving', d: parseDay(ve.event_date), t: servName(ve), go: '#volunteering' });
    return out;
  }
  function briefHtml() {
    var t = today(), fy = fundYear();
    var kick = DAYS[t.getDay()] + ', ' + fmtDay(t) + (fy.daysLeft >= 0 && fy.daysLeft <= 30 ? ' \u00b7 fiscal year ends in ' + plural(fy.daysLeft, 'day') : '');
    var nx = nextItems();
    return '<div class="w128brief"><div class="tx"><div class="kk">' + E(kick) + '</div><p>' + briefText() + '</p>'
      + (staffOk() ? '<a class="lk" data-w128go="#mypage">' + I('list') + 'Your own tasks and requests are on Home</a>' : '') + '</div>'
      + (nx.length ? '<div class="nx"><div class="kk">Next up</div>' + nx.map(function (x) { return '<a class="it" data-w128go="' + E(x.go) + '"><span class="dt"><b>' + (x.month ? E(MONTHS[x.d.getMonth()].slice(0, 3)) : E(String(x.d.getDate()))) + '</b><small>' + (x.month ? 'month' : E(MONTHS[x.d.getMonth()].slice(0, 3))) + '</small></span><span class="w"><small>' + E(x.k) + '</small>' + E(clip(x.t, 48)) + '</span>' + I('chev') + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '')
      + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the numbers: each one opens the page that explains it ---------- */
  function tilesHtml() {
    var ds = decisions(), fy = fundYear(), og = orgGoals(), t = today();
    var ts = tasks(), open = ts.filter(openTask).length, od = ts.filter(overdue).length, c30 = ts.filter(function (x) { return closedWithin(x, 30); }).length;
    var tiles = [];
    tiles.push({ acc: true, n: ds.length, l: ds.length === 1 ? 'decision waiting' : 'decisions waiting', s: ds.length ? 'oldest ' + ds[0].age + ' days' : 'nothing waiting', go: '#overview/decisions', ic: 'flag' });
    var pct = fy.planYear && fy.target ? Math.min(100, fy.raised / fy.target * 100) : null;
    tiles.push({ n: money(fy.raised), l: 'raised by the events', s: fy.planYear ? 'of the ' + money(fy.target) + ' target, fiscal ' + fy.fyLabel : 'fiscal ' + fy.fyLabel + (fy.planLabel ? '; the ' + money(fy.target) + ' target starts with ' + fy.planLabel : ''), bar: pct, go: '#fundevents', ic: 'target' });
    if (og.length) { var on = og.filter(onTrackGoal).length, rk = og.filter(atRiskGoal).length; tiles.push({ n: og.length, l: og.length === 1 ? 'organizational goal' : 'organizational goals', s: [on ? on + ' on track or done' : '', og.length - on - rk ? (og.length - on - rk) + ' in progress' : '', rk ? rk + ' at risk' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(', '), go: '#orggoals', ic: 'spark' }); }
    tiles.push({ n: open, l: 'open tasks', s: (od ? od + ' overdue' : 'none overdue') + (c30 ? ', ' + c30 + ' closed in 30 days' : ''), fold: 'overdue', ic: 'list', warn: od > 0 });
    if (staffOk()) { var next30 = (X.vol || []).filter(function (v) { var d = parseDay(v.event_date); return d && d >= t && daysBetween(t, d) <= 30 && v.status !== 'cancelled'; }); var nv = next30[0]; tiles.push({ n: next30.length, l: next30.length === 1 ? 'serving in the next 30 days' : 'servings in the next 30 days', s: nv ? 'next ' + fmtShort(parseDay(nv.event_date)) : 'none planned yet', go: '#volunteering', ic: 'heart' }); }
    if (X.donations != null) tiles.splice(2, 0, { n: money(X.donations), l: 'donations this fiscal year', s: 'from the ledger, since ' + fmtDay(fyStart()), go: '#finances', ic: 'money' });
    var ppl = members().length, joined = changes().filter(function (c) { return c.entity_type === 'member' && c.field === 'invited' && /sent|admin|leader|member/.test(String(c.new_value || '')) && dayOfIso(c.changed_at) && daysBetween(dayOfIso(c.changed_at), t) <= 30; }).length;
    if (ppl && tiles.length < 6) tiles.push({ n: ppl, l: 'people on the team', s: joined ? joined + ' joined the Hub in 30 days' : 'across ' + TEAMS.length + ' teams and the board', go: '#orgchart', ic: 'people' });
    return '<div class="w128kpis">' + tiles.map(function (x) {
      return '<a class="w128k' + (x.acc ? ' acc' : '') + (x.warn ? ' warn' : '') + '" role="button" tabindex="0" ' + (x.fold ? 'data-w128fold="' + x.fold + '"' : 'data-w128go="' + E(x.go) + '"') + '><span class="ic">' + I(x.ic) + '</span><b>' + E(x.n) + '</b><span class="l">' + E(x.l) + '</span><span class="s">' + E(x.s) + '</span>' + (x.bar != null ? '<span class="bar"><i style="width:' + x.bar.toFixed(1) + '%"></i></span>' : '') + '</a>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- needs a decision ---------- */
  function decHtml() {
    var ds = decisions(), show = ds.slice(0, 4);
    var h = '<section class="w128box w128dec" id="w128dec"><div class="hd"><h3>' + I('flag') + 'Needs a decision</h3><span class="sp"></span>' + (ds.length ? '<a class="w128lnk" data-w128all="1">All ' + ds.length + ' in the list ' + I('chev') + '</a>' : '') + '</div>';
    if (!ds.length) return h + '<p class="w128empty">Nothing is waiting on the executives. New calls from meetings land here.</p></section>';
    h += show.map(function (x) {
      var hot = x.age > 60 ? ' hot' : x.age > 30 ? ' warm' : '';
      return '<div class="w128d"><span class="age' + hot + '">' + (x.age === 0 ? 'today' : x.age + (x.age === 1 ? ' day' : ' days')) + '</span><div class="tx"><b>' + E(x.title) + '</b>'
        + (x.move ? '<span>Suggested: ' + E(clip(x.move, 170)) + '</span>' : '')
        + (x.rec ? '<span class="rec">' + I('spark') + 'Recommendation: ' + E(clip(x.rec, 170)) + '</span>' : '')
        + '<em>Decides: <b>' + E(x.owner) + '</b>' + (x.area ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(x.area) : '') + (x.cat ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(x.cat) : '') + '</em></div>'
        + '<div class="ac">' + (staffOk() ? '<a class="w128btn sm" data-w128decide="' + E(x.ref) + '">' + I('check') + 'Record the decision</a>' : '') + '<a class="w128btn ghost sm" data-w128see="' + E(x.ref) + '">In the list</a></div></div>';
    }).join('');
    if (ds.length > show.length) h += '<p class="w128more">' + plural(ds.length - show.length, 'more decision', 'more decisions') + ' in the list below.</p>';
    return h + '</section>';
  }

  /* ---------- to watch ---------- */
  function watchRows() {
    var t = today(), rows = [], ds = decisions(), fy = fundYear();
    var old = ds.filter(function (d) { return d.age > 60; });
    if (old.length) rows.push({ sev: 'hot', t: '<b>' + plural(old.length, 'decision has', 'decisions have') + ' waited more than 60 days</b>, the oldest ' + old[0].age + ' days: ' + E(sentence(clip(old[0].title, 70))), go: '#overview/decisions' });
    if (fy.missing.length) rows.push({ sev: 'warm', t: '<b>No result recorded</b> for ' + E(listJoin(fy.missing.map(function (f) { return f.title + ' (' + fmtDay(parseDay(f.end_date) || parseDay(f.start_date)) + ')'; }))) + '.', go: '#fundevents' });
    var over = TEAMS.map(teamStats).filter(function (s) { return s.overdue >= 5; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.overdue - a.overdue; });
    if (over.length) rows.push({ sev: over[0].overdue >= 20 ? 'hot' : 'warm', t: '<b>Overdue work:</b> ' + E(listJoin(over.map(function (s) { return deptLabel(s.dept) + ' ' + s.overdue; }))) + ' tasks past their due date.', go: '#team/' + over[0].dept + '/tasks' });
    var quiet = TEAMS.map(teamStats).filter(function (s) { return !s.last || daysBetween(s.last, t) > 21; });
    if (quiet.length) { var lastAny = quiet.map(function (s) { return s.last; }).filter(Boolean).sort(function (a, b) { return b - a; })[0]; rows.push({ sev: 'warm', t: '<b>No meeting update in three weeks</b> from ' + E(listJoin(quiet.map(function (s) { return deptLabel(s.dept); }))) + (lastAny ? ' (latest ' + fmtDay(lastAny) + ')' : '') + '. Upload their meeting transcripts to keep their pages current.', go: '#transcripts' }); }
    var rg = (X.goals || []).filter(atRiskGoal);
    if (rg.length) rows.push({ sev: 'warm', t: '<b>' + plural(rg.length, 'goal needs', 'goals need') + ' attention:</b> ' + E(listJoin(rg.slice(0, 3).map(function (g) { return clip(g.title, 60) + ' (' + [g.department && g.department !== 'org' ? deptLabel(g.department) : 'organization', String(g.status || '').toLowerCase()].filter(Boolean).join(', ') + ')'; }))) + '.', go: rg.some(function (g) { return g.department === 'org'; }) ? '#orggoals' : '#goals' });
    var br = boardAtRisk();
    if (br.length) rows.push({ sev: 'hot', t: '<b>' + plural(br.length, 'board item is', 'board items are') + ' at risk:</b> ' + E(sentence(listJoin(br.slice(0, 3).map(function (i) { return clip(i.title, 70); })))), go: '#governance' });
    var soon = (X.fund || []).filter(function (f) { var d = parseDay(f.start_date); return d && d >= t && daysBetween(t, d) <= 21 && !f.lead && f.status !== 'idea' && f.status !== 'cancelled'; });
    if (soon.length) rows.push({ sev: 'warm', t: '<b>No lead yet</b> for ' + E(listJoin(soon.map(function (f) { return f.title + ' (' + fmtDay(parseDay(f.start_date)) + ')'; }))) + '.', go: '#fundevents' });
    if (fy.daysLeft >= 0 && fy.daysLeft <= 14) rows.push({ sev: 'info', t: '<b>Year-end ' + fmtDay(fy.end) + ':</b> close the books, record the last event results and set up the ' + (fy.end.getFullYear()) + '-' + String(fy.end.getFullYear() + 1).slice(2) + ' goals.', go: tabOk('finances') ? '#finances' : '#fundevents' });
    return rows;
  }
  function watchHtml() {
    var rows = watchRows();
    return '<section class="w128box w128watch"><div class="hd"><h3>' + I('alert') + 'To watch</h3></div>'
      + (rows.length ? rows.map(function (r) { return '<a class="w128w ' + r.sev + '" data-w128go="' + E(r.go) + '"><i></i><span>' + r.t + '</span>' + I('chev') + '</a>'; }).join('') : '<p class="w128empty">Nothing needs watching right now.</p>')
      + '</section>';
  }

  /* ---------- highlights ---------- */
  function winsRows() {
    var t = today(), rows = [];
    (X.fund || []).filter(function (f) { var d = parseDay(f.end_date) || parseDay(f.start_date); return f.status === 'done' && Number(f.raised || 0) > 0 && d && daysBetween(d, t) <= 75 && d <= t; }).forEach(function (f) {
      var tg = Number(f.target_min || 0); rows.push({ d: parseDay(f.end_date) || parseDay(f.start_date), ic: 'target', t: '<b>' + E(f.title) + ' raised ' + money(f.raised) + '</b>' + (tg ? ' against a ' + money(tg) + ' target' : '') + '.', go: '#fundevents' });
    });
    (X.shout || []).filter(function (s) { var d = dayOfIso(s.created_at); return d && daysBetween(d, t) <= 30; }).slice(0, 3).forEach(function (s) {
      rows.push({ d: dayOfIso(s.created_at), ic: 'hand', t: E(sentence(clip(s.text, 150))), go: s.member_id ? '#person/' + s.member_id : '#orgchart' });
    });
    (X.goals || []).filter(function (g) { var d = dayOfIso(g.updated_at); return doneGoal(g) && d && daysBetween(d, t) <= 45; }).slice(0, 3).forEach(function (g) {
      rows.push({ d: dayOfIso(g.updated_at), ic: 'spark', t: '<b>Goal closed</b>' + (g.department && g.department !== 'org' ? ' by ' + E(deptLabel(g.department)) : '') + ': ' + E(sentence(clip(g.title, 90))), go: g.department === 'org' ? '#orggoals' : '#goals' });
    });
    (X.wins || []).filter(function (w) { var d = parseDay(w.meeting_date) || dayOfIso(w.created_at); return d && daysBetween(d, t) <= 45; }).slice(0, 3).forEach(function (w) {
      rows.push({ d: parseDay(w.meeting_date) || dayOfIso(w.created_at), ic: 'spark', t: '<b>A win' + (w.department ? ' for ' + E(deptLabel(w.department)) : '') + ':</b> ' + E(sentence(clip(w.win, 120))), go: '#goals' });
    });
    var c30 = TEAMS.map(teamStats).filter(function (s) { return s.closed30 > 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.closed30 - a.closed30; });
    var tot = c30.reduce(function (a, s) { return a + s.closed30; }, 0);
    if (tot) rows.push({ d: t, ic: 'check', t: '<b>' + plural(tot, 'task', 'tasks') + ' closed in the last 30 days</b>, most by ' + E(listJoin(c30.slice(0, 2).map(function (s) { return deptLabel(s.dept) + ' (' + s.closed30 + ')'; }))) + '.', go: '#dashboard', fold: 'numbers' });
    var held = (X.vol || []).filter(function (v) { var d = parseDay(v.event_date); return d && d < t && daysBetween(d, t) <= 30 && v.status !== 'cancelled' && v.status !== 'planned'; });
    if (held.length) rows.push({ d: parseDay(held[held.length - 1].event_date), ic: 'heart', t: '<b>' + plural(held.length, 'serving', 'servings') + ' held in the last 30 days.</b>', go: '#volunteering' });
    var joined = changes().filter(function (c) { return c.entity_type === 'member' && c.field === 'invited' && /sent|admin|leader|member/.test(String(c.new_value || '')) && dayOfIso(c.changed_at) && daysBetween(dayOfIso(c.changed_at), t) <= 30; });
    if (joined.length) rows.push({ d: dayOfIso(joined[0].changed_at), ic: 'people', t: '<b>' + plural(joined.length, 'person', 'people') + ' joined the Hub</b> in the last 30 days.', go: '#orgchart' });
    return rows.sort(function (a, b) { return (b.d ? b.d.getTime() : 0) - (a.d ? a.d.getTime() : 0); }).slice(0, 7);
  }
  function winsHtml() {
    var rows = winsRows();
    return '<section class="w128box w128wins"><div class="hd"><h3>' + I('spark') + 'Highlights</h3><span class="sub">the last 30 days or so</span></div>'
      + (rows.length ? rows.map(function (r) { return '<a class="w128h" ' + (r.fold ? 'data-w128fold="' + r.fold + '"' : 'data-w128go="' + E(r.go) + '"') + '><span class="ic">' + I(r.ic) + '</span><span class="tx">' + r.t + (r.d ? '<small>' + E(fmtDay(r.d)) + '</small>' : '') + '</span></a>'; }).join('') : '<p class="w128empty">Wins from meetings, event results and shout-outs show up here.</p>')
      + '</section>';
  }

  /* ---------- coming up ---------- */
  function nextRows() {
    var t = today(), end = addDays(t, 45), rows = [], fy = fundYear();
    var inWin = function (d) { return d && d >= t && d <= end; };
    if (inWin(fy.end)) rows.push({ d: fy.end, k: 'Year-end', t: 'The fiscal year ends', go: tabOk('finances') ? '#finances' : '#fundevents' });
    boardMeetings().filter(function (x) { return inWin(x.d); }).forEach(function (x) { rows.push({ d: x.d, k: 'Board', t: x.m.title || 'Board meeting', go: '#governance' }); });
    try { var cy = X.board && X.board.cycle; if (cy && cy.next) { var due = addDays(dayOfIso(cy.next.meets_at) || t, -7); if (inWin(due)) rows.push({ d: due, k: 'Committees', t: 'Committees meet by this date', go: '#committees' }); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    (X.fund || []).filter(function (f) { return inWin(parseDay(f.start_date)) && f.status !== 'idea' && f.status !== 'cancelled'; }).forEach(function (f) { rows.push({ d: parseDay(f.start_date), k: 'Fundraising', t: f.title + (f.precision === 'month' ? ' (all month)' : ''), go: '#fundevents' }); });
    (X.vol || []).filter(function (v) { return inWin(parseDay(v.event_date)) && v.status !== 'cancelled'; }).slice(0, 4).forEach(function (v) { rows.push({ d: parseDay(v.event_date), k: 'Serving', t: servName(v), go: '#volunteering' }); });
    /* audit 26/09/26: skip what the brief's Next up already shows (the next board meeting, event and serving) */
    var shown = nextItems().map(function (x) { return x.k + '|' + ymdOf(x.d) + '|' + String(x.t || '').toLowerCase(); });
    rows = rows.filter(function (r) { return shown.indexOf(r.k + '|' + ymdOf(r.d) + '|' + String(r.t || '').replace(/ \(all month\)$/, '').toLowerCase()) < 0; });
    return rows.sort(function (a, b) { return a.d - b.d; }).slice(0, 8);
  }
  function ymdOf(d) { try { return d.getFullYear() + '-' + (d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + d.getDate(); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function nextHtml() {
    var rows = nextRows();
    return '<section class="w128box w128next"><div class="hd"><h3>' + I('cal') + 'Coming up</h3><span class="sp"></span><a class="w128lnk" data-w128go="#calendar">Calendar ' + I('chev') + '</a></div>'
      + (rows.length ? rows.map(function (r) { return '<a class="w128n" data-w128go="' + E(r.go) + '"><span class="dt"><b>' + r.d.getDate() + '</b><small>' + E(MONTHS[r.d.getMonth()].slice(0, 3)) + '</small></span><span class="tx"><small>' + E(r.k) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(DAYS[r.d.getDay()]) + '</small>' + E(clip(r.t, 70)) + '</span>' + I('chev') + '</a>'; }).join('') : '<p class="w128empty">Nothing dated in the next six weeks yet.</p>')
      + '</section>';
  }

  /* ---------- teams at a glance ---------- */
  function teamsHtml() {
    var t = today();
    var cards = TEAMS.filter(function (d) { return tabOk(d); }).map(function (d) {
      var s = teamStats(d), last = s.last ? daysBetween(s.last, t) : null;
      var gOn = s.goals.filter(onTrackGoal).length, gRisk = s.goals.filter(atRiskGoal).length, gAll = s.goals.length;
      return '<a class="w128t" data-w128go="#team/' + E(d) + '" style="--c:' + E(deptColor(d)) + '"><div class="nm"><i></i><b>' + E(deptLabel(d)) + '</b></div>'
        + '<div class="ld">' + (s.leads.length ? 'Led by ' + E(listJoin(s.leads.slice(0, 2))) : plural(s.people, 'person', 'people') + ' on the roster') + '</div>'
        + '<div class="st"><span><b>' + s.open + '</b> open</span><span class="' + (s.overdue ? 'od' : '') + '"><b>' + s.overdue + '</b> overdue</span><span><b>' + s.closed30 + '</b> closed in 30 days</span></div>'
        + '<div class="ft"><span class="' + (last == null || last > 21 ? 'stale' : '') + '">' + I('mic') + (last == null ? 'No meeting update yet' : last === 0 ? 'Updated today' : 'Updated ' + (last === 1 ? 'yesterday' : last + ' days ago')) + '</span>'
        + (gAll ? '<span>' + I('target') + plural(gAll, 'goal') + (gRisk ? ', ' + gRisk + ' at risk' : gOn ? ', ' + gOn + ' on track' : '') + '</span>' : '') + '</div></a>';
    }).join('');
    if (!cards) return '';
    return '<div class="w128sec"><h2>Teams at a glance</h2><span>Open and overdue work, what closed lately, and when each team last had a meeting on the Hub.</span></div><div class="w128teams">' + cards + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- paint ---------- */
  function hostEl() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('overview'); if (!panel) return null;
    var h = document.getElementById('w128');
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = 'w128'; }
    var mast = panel.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
    var want = mast ? mast.nextSibling : panel.firstChild;
    if (h.parentNode !== panel || (mast && mast.nextElementSibling !== h)) panel.insertBefore(h, want);
    panel.classList.add('w128on');
    tidy(panel);
    return h;
  }
  /* the help button leaves the old "Your plate" card for the masthead */
  function tidy(panel) {
    try {
      var help = panel.querySelector('.card.w28my .w43help, .card.w28my [data-w43help]');
      var mast = panel.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
      if (help && mast && !mast.querySelector('.w43help')) mast.appendChild(help);
      var all = document.getElementById('decisionList'); var card = all && all.closest ? all.closest('.card') : null;
      if (card && !card.id) card.id = 'w128all';
    } catch (e) { /* cosmetic */ }
  }
  function paint() {
    var h = hostEl(); if (!h) return;
    var html = briefHtml() + tilesHtml()
      + '<div class="w128row">' + decHtml() + watchHtml() + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w128row">' + winsHtml() + nextHtml() + '</div>'
      + teamsHtml();
    if (h.__h !== html) { h.innerHTML = html; h.__h = html; }
  }
  async function show() {
    paint();
    await load();
    if (onOverview()) paint();
  }

  /* ---------- the doors ---------- */
  function scrollTo(el) { if (el && el.scrollIntoView) { try { el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); } catch (e) { el.scrollIntoView(); } } }
  function openFold(k) {
    var b = document.querySelector('#w111folds [data-w111f="' + k + '"]');
    if (!b) { location.hash = k === 'numbers' ? '#dashboard' : '#overview'; return; }
    if (!b.classList.contains('on')) b.click();
    setTimeout(function () { scrollTo(document.getElementById('w111folds')); }, 60);
  }
  function seeDecision(ref) {
    var li = document.querySelector('#decisionList li[data-decision="' + ref + '"]');
    if (!li) { scrollTo(document.getElementById('w128all') || document.getElementById('decisionList')); return; }
    scrollTo(li); li.classList.remove('w128flash'); void li.offsetWidth; li.classList.add('w128flash');
  }
  function decide(ref) {
    var dec = null; try { dec = (typeof getDecisions === 'function' ? getDecisions() : []).filter(function (d) { return String(d.ref) === String(ref); })[0]; } catch (e) { dec = null; }
    if (!dec || typeof decideModal !== 'function') { seeDecision(ref); return; }
    decideModal(dec, { checked: false });
  }
  function go(h) {
    if (!h) return;
    if (h.indexOf('#team/') === 0) { var d = h.split('/')[1]; if (!tabOk(d)) return; }
    else { var root = h.replace(/^#/, '').split('/')[0]; if (root && root !== 'person' && root !== 'project' && !tabOk(root) && ['volunteering', 'importantinfo'].indexOf(root) < 0) { try { toast('That page is not in your access profile'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return; } }
    location.hash = h;
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('#w128')) return;
    var el;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w128decide]'))) { e.preventDefault(); decide(el.getAttribute('data-w128decide')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w128see]'))) { e.preventDefault(); seeDecision(el.getAttribute('data-w128see')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w128all]'))) { e.preventDefault(); scrollTo(document.getElementById('w128all') || document.getElementById('decisionList')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w128fold]'))) { e.preventDefault(); openFold(el.getAttribute('data-w128fold')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w128go]'))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      var h = el.getAttribute('data-w128go');
      if (h === '#overview/decisions') { scrollTo(document.getElementById('w128all') || document.getElementById('decisionList')); return; }
      go(h); return;
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if ((e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') && e.target && e.target.matches && e.target.matches('#w128 [role=button]')) { e.preventDefault(); e.target.click(); }
  });

  /* ---------- the tour follows the new page ---------- */
  function tour() {
    try {
      var W = window.w122; if (!W || !W.tours || !W.tours.overview || W.tours.overview.__w128) return;
      var OPT = { opt: 1 };
      var S = function (sel, tt, p, o) { var x = { s: sel, t: tt, p: p }; if (o) Object.keys(o).forEach(function (k) { x[k] = o[k]; }); return x; };
      W.tours.overview.steps = [
        S('#overview > .w118mast', 'The executive view', 'Where Welfare Avenue stands: the calls waiting on the executives, what is going well, what to watch and what is coming.'),
        S('#w128 .w128brief', 'The brief', 'Two or three sentences built from the Hub\u2019s data, and what is next: the board meeting, the next fundraising event and the next serving.', OPT),
        S('#w128 .w128kpis', 'The numbers', 'Each number opens the page that explains it. Open tasks opens the overdue folder right here. Our fiscal year runs October 1 to September 30.', OPT),
        S('#w128dec', 'Needs a decision', 'The oldest calls first, with who decides, the suggested move and the recommendation. Record the decision here or find it in the full list.', OPT),
        S('#w128 .w128watch', 'To watch', 'What could slip: old decisions, events without a result, overdue work, quiet teams, delayed goals and board items at risk.', OPT),
        S('#w128 .w128wins', 'Highlights', 'Event results, shout-outs, goals closed and work finished lately.', OPT),
        S('#w128 .w128next', 'Coming up', 'The next six weeks: the year-end, board meetings, the committee deadline, events and servings.', OPT),
        S('#w128 .w128teams', 'Teams at a glance', 'Each team\u2019s open and overdue work, what closed lately and when it last had a meeting on the Hub. Open a card for the team page.', OPT),
        S('#w116news', 'What\u2019s new', 'Major announcements and changes since you last looked.', OPT),
        S('#w111folds', 'Folders', 'Overdue tasks and the delivery numbers, folded away until you open them.', OPT),
        S('#decisionList', 'Every executive decision', 'The full list with filters by category and by who decides. Tick one to record what was decided.', OPT),
        S('#overview .add-row', 'Add a decision', 'Anyone can raise a call that needs an executive.', OPT),
        S('#decDoneWrap', 'Decided', 'Every decision made, with its note, stays here for the record.', OPT)
      ];
      W.tours.overview.__w128 = true;
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  tour();

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp128 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      var out = __sp128.apply(this, arguments);
      try { if (id === 'overview') setTimeout(show, 20); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return out;
    };
  }
  function wrapDecisions() {
    try {
      if (typeof renderDecisions !== 'function' || renderDecisions.__w128) return;
      var inner = renderDecisions;
      renderDecisions = function () { var out = inner.apply(this, arguments); try { if (onOverview()) { X.loaded = 0; setTimeout(show, 30); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
      Object.keys(inner).forEach(function (k) { try { renderDecisions[k] = inner[k]; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
      renderDecisions.__w128 = true;
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  window.w128 = { state: function () { return X; }, paint: paint, load: load, show: show, brief: briefText, watch: watchRows, wins: winsRows, next: nextRows, team: teamStats };
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa128 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var out = await __sa128.apply(this, arguments);
      try { wrapDecisions(); tour(); if (onOverview()) show(); } catch (e) { console.warn('wave128 boot', e); }
      return out;
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v129 analytics depth (25/09/26): every number the platforms keep, as far back as they keep it
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "more granular data from Instagram and Facebook", "even do WhatsApp maybe", and "look as far
   back as possible in time for analytics and have all analytics, as many as possible, visible to me."
   - Ranges (wave120 edited): 7, 30 and 90 days, 6 months, 1 year, 2 years, all time, or two dates;
     longer ranges chart by week or by month (social_range() in wa129).
   - History: social-pull v1.6 walks Facebook and Instagram back two years (the most Meta keeps),
     every Facebook post and every Instagram post and reel with its numbers, and the website since
     the property started. The coverage card shows how far back each platform goes, with Load more
     history for staff.
   - Every post: a table of every post in the range with reach, views, likes, comments, shares,
     saves and the engagement rate; filter by platform, type and words, sort by any column, and
     download it as a CSV. The daily numbers download as a CSV too.
   - Audience: Instagram followers by age, gender, city and country (weekly, from the API); LinkedIn
     followers and visitors by industry, seniority, function, location and company size (the export).
     Meta no longer gives Facebook Page audiences.
   - LinkedIn: set the follower total once and the Hub rebuilds the year of totals from the daily
     new followers in the export.
   - WhatsApp: the check says whether a WhatsApp Business account is shared with the Hub and what to
     do if not.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa129) return; window.__wa129 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', GOLD = '#DCA527', FLAG = '#B3402F', TEAL = '#2A8C7A', PURPLE = '#6F64AB', INK = '#1F201E';
  var HEAD = "'Barlow Condensed','Arial Narrow','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var PL = {
    instagram: ['Instagram', '#C13584', 'IG'], facebook: ['Facebook', '#1877F2', 'f'], linkedin: ['LinkedIn', '#0A66C2', 'in'],
    tiktok: ['TikTok', '#111111', 'TT'], ga4: ['Website', TEAL, 'W'], youtube: ['YouTube', '#FF0000', 'YT'], whatsapp: ['WhatsApp', '#25D366', 'WA']
  };
  var COLS = [['published_at', 'Date'], ['reach', 'Reach'], ['views', 'Views'], ['likes', 'Likes'], ['comments', 'Comments'], ['shares', 'Shares'], ['saves', 'Saves'], ['clicks', 'Clicks'], ['engagement', 'Engaged'], ['rate', 'Rate']];
  var X = { q: '', plat: '', kind: '', sort: 'published_at', dir: -1, show: 25, busy: {}, wa: null, liTotal: '', aud: 'followers', open: { coverage: true, explorer: true, audience: true }, run: null };
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;'); }
  function num(v) { v = Number(v); if (!isFinite(v)) return '\u2013'; var a = Math.abs(v); if (a >= 1e6) return (v / 1e6).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'M'; if (a >= 1e4) return Math.round(v / 1e3) + 'k'; if (a >= 1e3) return (v / 1e3).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'k'; return String(Math.round(v)); }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function toastS(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function data() { try { return (window.w120 && window.w120.data()) || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function mk(p) { var c = PL[p] || [p, GREEN, '?']; return '<span class="w129mk" style="background:' + c[1] + '">' + E(c[2]) + '</span>'; }
  function label(p) { return (PL[p] || [p])[0]; }
  var MON = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
  function day(iso) { if (!iso) return ''; var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/.exec(String(iso)); return m ? MON[Number(m[2]) - 1] + ' ' + Number(m[3]) + ', ' + m[1] : String(iso); }
  function monthY(iso) { var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})/.exec(String(iso || '')); return m ? MON[Number(m[2]) - 1] + ' ' + m[1] : ''; }
  function engOf(p) { var e = Number(p.engagement); if (p.engagement != null && isFinite(e) && e > 0) return e; var s = 0, any = false; ['likes', 'comments', 'shares', 'saves'].forEach(function (k) { if (p[k] != null && isFinite(Number(p[k]))) { s += Number(p[k]); any = true; } }); return any ? s : null; }
  function rateOf(p) { var e = engOf(p), r = Number(p.reach) || Number(p.views) || 0; return e != null && r > 0 ? e / r : null; }
  function csvCell(v) { v = v == null ? '' : String(v); return /[",\n]/.test(v) ? '"' + v.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"' : v; }
  function download(name, rows) {
    var text = rows.map(function (r) { return r.map(csvCell).join(','); }).join('\n');
    try { var blob = new Blob(['\ufeff' + text], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' }); var a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob); a.download = name; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); setTimeout(function () { URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href); a.remove(); }, 400); toastS('Downloaded ' + name); }
    catch (e) { toastS('Could not download it'); }
  }

  /* ---------- styles ---------- */
  var css = document.createElement('style'); css.id = 'wa129css';
  css.textContent = ''
    + '#w129{font-family:' + BODY + ';}'
    + '#w129 .cardy h4 .sub{font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:12px;font-weight:500;color:var(--wa-muted);}'
    + '#w129 .cardy h4 .sp{flex:1;}'
    + '.w129mk{width:24px;height:24px;border-radius:8px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;color:#FFFAF2;flex:none;text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;}'
    + '.w129btn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;background:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ' !important;border:1px solid ' + GREEN + ';border-radius:9px;padding:6px 11px;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:none !important;white-space:nowrap;}'
    + '.w129btn.ghost{background:transparent;color:' + GREEN + ' !important;border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.22);}'
    + '.w129btn[disabled]{opacity:.55;cursor:default;}'
    /* coverage */

  /* ---------- how far back each platform goes ---------- */
  function histDone(a) { var h = (a.meta && a.meta.history) || {}; if (a.platform === 'instagram') return !!(h.daily_done && h.totals_done && h.posts_done); return !!(h.daily_done && h.posts_done); }
  function canBack(a) { return a && (a.platform === 'facebook' || a.platform === 'instagram' || a.platform === 'ga4') && a.status !== 'csv'; }
  function coverageHtml(d) {
    var accs = d.accounts || [], cov = d.coverage || {};
    var rows = ['instagram', 'facebook', 'linkedin', 'tiktok', 'ga4'].map(function (p) {
      var a = accs.filter(function (x) { return x.platform === p; })[0];
      if (!a) return p === 'tiktok' ? '<div class="r">' + mk(p) + '<b>' + E(label(p)) + '</b><span class="m">Not connected. TikTok\u2019s analytics API needs an approval first; its CSV export works in Set up.</span><span class="act"><span class="st off">Not connected</span></span></div>' : '';
      var c = cov[p] || {}, h = (a.meta && a.meta.history) || {};
      var running = X.run && X.run.id === a.id;
      var st = a.status === 'csv' ? '<span class="st csv">From the export</span>' : running ? '<span class="st run">Loading\u2026</span>' : histDone(a) ? '<span class="st">All the history</span>' : h.started_at ? '<span class="st run">Partly loaded</span>' : '<span class="st off">Recent only</span>';
      var m = c.from ? '<em>Daily numbers</em> since ' + E(day(c.from)) + ' (' + num(c.days) + ' days)' : 'No daily numbers yet';
      m += c.posts ? ' \u00b7 <em>' + num(c.posts) + (p === 'ga4' ? ' pages' : ' posts') + '</em>' + (c.posts_from && p !== 'ga4' ? ' since ' + E(monthY(c.posts_from)) : '') : '';
      if (p === 'instagram' || p === 'facebook') m += '<br>Meta keeps two years of daily numbers' + (p === 'facebook' ? '; posts go back to the start of the Page.' : '; posts and reels go back to the first one.');
      if (p === 'linkedin') m += '<br>LinkedIn\u2019s export covers the last year. A new export extends it.';
      if (p === 'ga4') m += '<br>The website keeps what Google Analytics collected since the property started.';
      if (running && X.run.text) m += '<div class="w129bar"><i style="width:' + Math.max(4, Math.min(100, X.run.pct || 0)).toFixed(0) + '%"></i></div><span style="font-size:11.5px">' + E(X.run.text) + '</span>';
      var act = st;
      if (staff() && canBack(a) && !histDone(a)) act += '<button class="w129btn ghost" data-w129back="' + a.id + '"' + (X.run ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Load more history</button>';
      if (staff() && p === 'linkedin') act += '<input type="number" min="0" id="w129li" placeholder="Followers now" value="' + E(X.liTotal) + '"><button class="w129btn ghost" data-w129li="' + a.id + '"' + (X.busy.li ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (X.busy.li ? 'Saving\u2026' : 'Set the total') + '</button>';
      return '<div class="r">' + mk(p) + '<b>' + E(label(p)) + (a.handle ? ' <span style="font-weight:500;color:var(--wa-muted)">@' + E(a.handle) + '</span>' : '') + '</b><span class="m">' + m + '</span><span class="act">' + act + '</span></div>';
    }).join('');
    var wa = X.wa;
    var waMsg = wa ? (wa.ok ? 'Readable: ' + num(wa.analytics && wa.analytics.sent) + ' messages sent in the last 30 days.' : (wa.wabas && wa.wabas.length ? 'A WhatsApp Business account is shared (' + E(wa.wabas[0].name || wa.wabas[0].id) + ') but its numbers are not readable yet.' : (wa.has_permission ? 'The token can read WhatsApp, but no WhatsApp Business account is shared with the Hub\u2019s system user. In Meta Business settings: Accounts > WhatsApp accounts > your account > Assign people > the system user. If Welfare Avenue uses the WhatsApp Business app on a phone, it has no analytics API; the WhatsApp Business Platform does.' : E(wa.next || wa.error || '')))) : 'Message numbers can join once a WhatsApp Business account is shared with the Hub\u2019s system user in Meta Business settings.';
    rows += '<div class="r">' + mk('whatsapp') + '<b>WhatsApp</b><span class="m">' + waMsg + '</span><span class="act"><span class="st off">' + (wa && wa.ok ? 'Readable' : 'Not shared') + '</span>' + (staff() ? '<button class="w129btn ghost" data-w129wa="1"' + (X.busy.wa ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (X.busy.wa ? 'Checking\u2026' : 'Check') + '</button>' : '') + '</span></div>';
    var todo = (d.accounts || []).filter(function (a) { return canBack(a) && !histDone(a); });
    return '<div class="cardy"><h4>How far back <span class="sub">each platform, and what it keeps</span></h4><div class="w129cov">' + rows + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w129foot"><span>Pick <b>All time</b> or <b>2 years</b> above to see all of it.</span><span class="sp"></span>'
      + (staff() && todo.length ? '<button class="w129btn" data-w129all="1"' + (X.run ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Load all the history</button>' : '')
      + '<button class="w129btn ghost" data-w129csvd="1"' + (X.busy.csvd ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (X.busy.csvd ? 'Preparing\u2026' : 'Download the daily numbers (CSV)') + '</button></div></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- every post ---------- */
  function kindOf(p) { var k = String(p.kind || 'post').toLowerCase(); if (/reel/.test(k)) return 'reel'; if (/video/.test(k)) return 'video'; if (/carousel|album/.test(k)) return 'carousel'; if (/story/.test(k)) return 'story'; if (/image|photo/.test(k)) return 'image'; return k === 'feed' ? 'post' : k; }
  function explorerRows(d) {
    var q = X.q.toLowerCase();
    var rows = (d.posts_all || []).filter(function (p) { return (!X.plat || p.platform === X.plat) && (!X.kind || kindOf(p) === X.kind) && (!q || String(p.caption || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(q) >= 0); });
    var key = X.sort, dir = X.dir;
    rows.sort(function (a, b) {
      var va = key === 'rate' ? rateOf(a) : key === 'engagement' ? engOf(a) : key === 'published_at' ? String(a.published_at || '') : a[key];
      var vb = key === 'rate' ? rateOf(b) : key === 'engagement' ? engOf(b) : key === 'published_at' ? String(b.published_at || '') : b[key];
      if (va == null && vb == null) return 0; if (va == null) return 1; if (vb == null) return -1;
      return (va < vb ? -1 : va > vb ? 1 : 0) * dir;
    });
    return rows;
  }
  function explorerHtml(d) {
    var all = d.posts_all || [];
    var plats = []; all.forEach(function (p) { if (plats.indexOf(p.platform) < 0) plats.push(p.platform); });
    var kinds = []; all.forEach(function (p) { var k = kindOf(p); if (kinds.indexOf(k) < 0) kinds.push(k); });
    var rows = explorerRows(d);
    var tot = { reach: 0, views: 0, eng: 0 }; rows.forEach(function (p) { tot.reach += Number(p.reach) || 0; tot.views += Number(p.views) || 0; tot.eng += engOf(p) || 0; });
    var h = '<div class="cardy"><h4>Every post <span class="sub">' + E(window.w120 ? window.w120.label() : '') + '</span><span class="sp"></span><button class="w129btn ghost" data-w129csvp="1"' + (rows.length ? '' : ' disabled') + '>Download these posts (CSV)</button></h4>'
      + '<div class="w129tools"><span class="chips"><a data-w129plat=""' + (X.plat ? '' : ' class="on"') + '>All</a>' + plats.map(function (p) { return '<a data-w129plat="' + E(p) + '"' + (X.plat === p ? ' class="on"' : '') + '>' + E(label(p)) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</span>'
      + '<select data-w129kind><option value="">Every type</option>' + kinds.sort().map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + E(k) + '"' + (X.kind === k ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(k.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + k.slice(1) + 's') + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
      + '<select class="w129ss" data-w129ss aria-label="Sort">' + '<option value="published_at|-1"' + (X.sort + '|' + X.dir === 'published_at|-1' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Newest first</option>' + '<option value="published_at|1"' + (X.sort + '|' + X.dir === 'published_at|1' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Oldest first</option>' + '<option value="reach|-1"' + (X.sort + '|' + X.dir === 'reach|-1' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Most reach</option>' + '<option value="views|-1"' + (X.sort + '|' + X.dir === 'views|-1' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Most views</option>' + '<option value="engagement|-1"' + (X.sort + '|' + X.dir === 'engagement|-1' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Most engaged</option>' + '<option value="rate|-1"' + (X.sort + '|' + X.dir === 'rate|-1' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Best engagement rate</option>' + '</select>'
      + '<input type="search" data-w129q placeholder="Search the captions" value="' + E(X.q) + '"></div>';
    if (!rows.length) return h + '<p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--wa-muted);margin:0">' + (all.length ? 'No post matches.' : 'No posts in this range yet.') + '</p></div>';
    h += '<div class="w129sum"><span><b>' + num(rows.length) + '</b>posts</span><span><b>' + num(tot.reach) + '</b>reach</span><span><b>' + num(tot.views) + '</b>views</span><span><b>' + num(tot.eng) + '</b>engagements</span>' + (tot.reach ? '<span><b>' + (tot.eng / tot.reach * 100).toFixed(1) + '%</b>engaged per reach</span>' : '') + '</div>';
    h += '<div class="w129wrap"><table class="w129tbl"><thead><tr><th class="l' + (X.sort === 'published_at' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w129sort="published_at">Post' + (X.sort === 'published_at' ? (X.dir < 0 ? ' \u2193' : ' \u2191') : '') + '</th>'
      + COLS.slice(1).map(function (c) { return '<th data-w129sort="' + c[0] + '"' + (X.sort === c[0] ? ' class="on"' : '') + ' title="' + (c[0] === 'rate' ? 'Engagements divided by reach (or views)' : c[0] === 'engagement' ? 'Likes, comments, shares and saves' : '') + '">' + E(c[1]) + (X.sort === c[0] ? (X.dir < 0 ? ' \u2193' : ' \u2191') : '') + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>';
    h += rows.slice(0, X.show).map(function (p) {
      var r = rateOf(p);
      var cell = function (v, l, k) { return '<td data-l="' + l + '"' + (k ? ' class="k"' : '') + '>' + (v == null || v === '' ? '<span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">\u2013</span>' : num(v)) + '</td>'; };
      return '<tr><td class="p"><a ' + (p.url ? 'href="' + E(p.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener"' : '') + '><div class="in">' + (p.thumb_url ? '<img src="' + E(p.thumb_url) + '" alt="" loading="lazy" referrerpolicy="no-referrer">' : '<span class="ph">' + mk(p.platform) + '</span>') + '<div><div class="cap">' + (p.caption ? E(p.caption) : '<span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">No caption</span>') + '</div><div class="mt">' + mk(p.platform) + E(day(p.published_at)) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(kindOf(p)) + '</div></div></div></a></td>'
        + cell(p.reach, 'Reach', 1) + cell(p.views, 'Views', 1) + cell(p.likes, 'Likes') + cell(p.comments, 'Comments') + cell(p.shares, 'Shares') + cell(p.saves, 'Saves') + cell(p.clicks, 'Clicks') + cell(engOf(p), 'Engaged', 1) + '<td class="rt k" data-l="Rate">' + (r == null ? '<span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">\u2013</span>' : '<b>' + (r * 100).toFixed(1) + '%</b>') + '</td></tr>';
    }).join('') + '</tbody></table></div>';
    if (rows.length > X.show) h += '<div class="w129foot"><button class="w129btn ghost" data-w129more="1">Show ' + Math.min(50, rows.length - X.show) + ' more</button><span>' + num(rows.length - X.show) + ' more posts</span></div>';
    return h + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the audience ---------- */
  var RN = null;
  function country(c) { try { RN = RN || new Intl.DisplayNames(['en'], { type: 'region' }); return RN.of(String(c).toUpperCase()) || c; } catch (e) { return c; } }
  function lead(x) { var m = /\d+/.exec(String(x)); return m ? Number(m[0]) : 1e9; }
  /* one breakdown: the top rows as bars, each as a share of everyone counted (or the follower total when it is larger) */
  function bars(title, pairs, color, note, max, o) {
    o = o || {};
    var all = (pairs || []).filter(function (x) { return x && x[0] != null && Number(x[1]) > 0; });
    if (!all.length) return '';
    var sum = all.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + Number(x[1]); }, 0), den = Math.max(sum, Number(o.total) || 0);
    var list = o.order ? all.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return lead(a[0]) - lead(b[0]); }) : all.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return Number(b[1]) - Number(a[1]); });
    list = list.slice(0, max || 8);
    var top = Math.max.apply(null, list.map(function (x) { return Number(x[1]) || 0; })) || 1;
    return '<div class="b" style="--c:' + color + '"><h5>' + title + '</h5>' + list.map(function (x) {
      var nm = o.name ? o.name(x[0]) : x[0], pct = den ? Number(x[1]) / den * 100 : 0;
      /* wave149: hovering a bar shows the count behind the percentage */
      var pc = (pct > 0 && pct < 1 ? pct.toFixed(1) : Math.round(pct)) + '%';
      return '<div class="row" data-w149tip="' + E(nm + ': ' + Math.round(Number(x[1])).toLocaleString('en-CA') + ' (' + pc + ')') + '"><div class="t"><span>' + E(nm) + '</span><i style="width:' + Math.max(3, Number(x[1]) / top * 100).toFixed(1) + '%"></i></div><b>' + pc + '</b></div>';
    }).join('') + (note ? '<p class="w129note">' + note + '</p>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function audienceHtml(d) {
    var accs = d.accounts || [];
    var ig = accs.filter(function (a) { return a.platform === 'instagram'; })[0], li = accs.filter(function (a) { return a.platform === 'linkedin'; })[0];
    var igd = ig && ig.meta && ig.meta.demographics && ig.meta.demographics.followers, lid = li && li.meta && li.meta.demographics;
    var parts = '';
    if (igd) {
      var c = PL.instagram[1], asOf = ig.meta.demographics.as_of, igTot = d.followers && d.followers.instagram && Number(d.followers.instagram.now);
      parts += bars(mk('instagram') + 'Followers by age', igd.age, c, null, 8, { order: 1 })
        + bars(mk('instagram') + 'Followers by gender', igd.gender, c, null, 4)
        + bars(mk('instagram') + 'Top cities', igd.city, c, null, 8, { total: igTot })
        + bars(mk('instagram') + 'Top countries', igd.country, c, asOf ? 'Instagram API, ' + E(day(asOf)) + '. Refreshed every week.' : null, 6, { total: igTot, name: country });
    }
    if (lid) {
      var g = lid[X.aud] || lid.followers || {}, lc = PL.linkedin[1], liTot = X.aud === 'visitors' ? 0 : (d.followers && d.followers.linkedin && Number(d.followers.linkedin.now));
      var names = { industry: 'by industry', seniority: 'by seniority', job_function: 'by job function', location: 'by location', company_size: 'by company size' };
      parts += Object.keys(names).map(function (k, i) { return bars(mk('linkedin') + (X.aud === 'visitors' ? 'Page visitors ' : 'Followers ') + names[k], g[k], lc, i === 4 ? 'LinkedIn export' + (lid.period ? ', ' + E(day(lid.period[0])) + ' to ' + E(day(lid.period[1])) : '') + '.' : null, 7, { order: k === 'company_size', total: liTot }); }).join('');
    }
    if (!parts) return '';
    return '<div class="cardy"><h4>Who follows us <span class="sub">the audience each platform shares</span><span class="sp"></span>' + (lid ? '<span class="w129seg"><a data-w129aud="followers"' + (X.aud === 'followers' ? ' class="on"' : '') + '>LinkedIn followers</a><a data-w129aud="visitors"' + (X.aud === 'visitors' ? ' class="on"' : '') + '>LinkedIn visitors</a></span>' : '') + '</h4>'
      + '<div class="w129aud">' + parts + '</div><p class="w129note" style="margin:10px 0 0">Facebook: Meta no longer shares Page audience breakdowns (age, gender, city) through its API; Meta Business Suite still shows them.</p></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- paint into the Analytics page, after wave120's own content ---------- */
  var HOST = null;
  function host() {
    var wrap = document.querySelector('#socialstats .w120wrap'); if (!wrap) return null;
    if (!HOST) { HOST = document.createElement('div'); HOST.id = 'w129'; }
    if (HOST.parentNode !== wrap || wrap.lastElementChild !== HOST) wrap.appendChild(HOST);
    return HOST;
  }
  function paint() {
    watch();
    var d = data(); if (!d || !(d.accounts || []).length) { if (HOST) { HOST.innerHTML = ''; HOST.__h = ''; } return; }
    var h = host(); if (!h) return;
    var html = explorerHtml(d) + audienceHtml(d) + coverageHtml(d);
    if (h.__h !== html) {
      var focus = document.activeElement && document.activeElement.matches && document.activeElement.matches('#w129 [data-w129q]') ? document.activeElement.selectionStart : null;
      h.innerHTML = html; h.__h = html;
      if (focus != null) { var q = h.querySelector('[data-w129q]'); if (q) { q.focus(); try { q.setSelectionRange(focus, focus); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
    }
  }
  var paintT = null;
  function soon() { clearTimeout(paintT); paintT = setTimeout(paint, 30); }
  /* wave120 rewrites its section on every paint: put the same block straight back (a microtask, before the browser draws), so nothing flickers */
  function watch() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('socialstats'); if (!sec || sec.__w129) return;
    sec.__w129 = true;
    try { new MutationObserver(function () { var d = data(); if (d && (d.accounts || []).length && HOST && HOST.__h) host(); soon(); }).observe(sec, { childList: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  document.addEventListener('w120:data', function () { X.show = 25; soon(); });

  /* ---------- history, one step at a time ---------- */
  async function runBackfill(ids) {
    if (X.run || !window.w120) return;
    for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
      var id = ids[i];
      X.run = { id: id, text: 'Starting\u2026', pct: 2 }; paint();
      for (var step = 0; step < 40; step++) {
        var r = null;
        try { r = await window.w120.fn({ action: 'backfill', account_id: id, budget: 40 }); } catch (e) { r = { ok: false, error: e.message || String(e) }; }
        if (!r || r.ok === false) { toastS('History stopped: ' + String((r && r.error) || 'no answer').slice(0, 80)); break; }
        var back = r.daily_from || r.totals_from;
        var covered = back ? Math.round((Date.now() - new Date(back + 'T12:00:00Z').getTime()) / 86400000) : 0;
        X.run.pct = Math.min(99, covered / 730 * 100);
        X.run.text = (back ? 'Back to ' + day(back) : 'Working') + (r.posts ? ', ' + num(r.posts) + ' more posts this step' : '') + (r.done ? '. Done.' : '\u2026');
        paint();
        if (r.done) break;
      }
    }
    X.run = null;
    try { await window.w120.reload(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    toastS('The history is loaded');
  }

  /* LinkedIn: the follower total today, and the year rebuilt from the daily new followers */
  async function setLinkedIn(id) {
    var inp = document.getElementById('w129li'); var n = Math.round(Number(inp && inp.value));
    if (!(n > 0)) { toastS('Type the follower total shown on the LinkedIn page'); return; }
    X.liTotal = String(n); X.busy.li = true; paint();
    try {
      var r = await db.from('social_daily').select('day,value').eq('platform', 'linkedin').eq('metric', 'new_followers').order('day', { ascending: false }).limit(1000);
      var rows = [], cum = 0, today = (window.w120 && window.w120.dates ? window.w120.dates()[1] : new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10));
      rows.push({ day: today, metric: 'followers', value: n });
      ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.day) < today) rows.push({ day: String(x.day), metric: 'followers', value: n - cum }); cum += Number(x.value) || 0; });
      await window.w120.fn({ action: 'import_csv', platform: 'linkedin', rows: rows });
      toastS('LinkedIn: ' + num(n) + ' followers, and the year rebuilt');
      await window.w120.reload();
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not save it: ' + (e.message || e)); }
    X.busy.li = false; paint();
  }
  async function checkWhatsApp() {
    X.busy.wa = true; paint();
    try { var r = await window.w120.fn({ action: 'probe' }); X.wa = (r && r.whatsapp) || { ok: false, error: 'No answer' }; }
    catch (e) { X.wa = { ok: false, error: e.message || String(e) }; }
    X.busy.wa = false; paint();
  }
  async function csvDaily() {
    var d = data(); if (!d) return;
    X.busy.csvd = true; paint();
    try {
      var out = [['platform', 'day', 'metric', 'value']], from = d.from, to = d.to;
      for (var i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
        var q = db.from('social_daily').select('platform,day,metric,value').lte('day', to).order('day', { ascending: true }).order('platform').range(i * 1000, i * 1000 + 999);
        if (from) q = q.gte('day', from);
        var r = await q; var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
        rows.forEach(function (x) { out.push([x.platform, x.day, x.metric, x.value]); });
        if (rows.length < 1000) break;
      }
      download('welfare-avenue-analytics-daily-' + (from || 'start') + '-to-' + to + '.csv', out);
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not build the file'); }
    X.busy.csvd = false; paint();
  }
  function csvPosts() {
    var d = data(); if (!d) return;
    var rows = explorerRows(d);
    var out = [['platform', 'published', 'type', 'caption', 'link', 'reach', 'views', 'likes', 'comments', 'shares', 'saves', 'clicks', 'engagements', 'engagement rate']];
    rows.forEach(function (p) { var r = rateOf(p); out.push([p.platform, String(p.published_at || '').slice(0, 10), kindOf(p), String(p.caption || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' '), p.url || '', p.reach, p.views, p.likes, p.comments, p.shares, p.saves, p.clicks, engOf(p), r == null ? '' : (r * 100).toFixed(2) + '%']); });
    download('welfare-avenue-posts-' + (d.from || 'start') + '-to-' + d.to + '.csv', out);
  }

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('#w129')) return;
    var el;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w129plat]'))) { X.plat = el.getAttribute('data-w129plat'); X.show = 25; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w129sort]'))) { var k = el.getAttribute('data-w129sort'); if (X.sort === k) X.dir = -X.dir; else { X.sort = k; X.dir = -1; } paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w129more]'))) { X.show += 50; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w129csvp]'))) { csvPosts(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w129csvd]'))) { csvDaily(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w129aud]'))) { X.aud = el.getAttribute('data-w129aud'); paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w129back]'))) { runBackfill([Number(el.getAttribute('data-w129back'))]); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w129all]'))) { var d = data(); runBackfill(((d && d.accounts) || []).filter(function (a) { return canBack(a) && !histDone(a); }).map(function (a) { return a.id; })); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w129li]'))) { setLinkedIn(Number(el.getAttribute('data-w129li'))); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w129wa]'))) { checkWhatsApp(); return; }
  });
  var qT = null;
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.matches) return;
    if (t.matches('#w129 [data-w129q]')) { clearTimeout(qT); var v = t.value; qT = setTimeout(function () { X.q = v; X.show = 25; paint(); }, 200); return; }
    if (t.matches('#w129li')) { X.liTotal = t.value; return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.matches) return;
    if (t.matches('#w129 [data-w129kind]')) { X.kind = t.value; X.show = 25; paint(); }
    if (t.matches('#w129 [data-w129ss]')) { var sv = String(t.value).split('|'); X.sort = sv[0]; X.dir = Number(sv[1]) || -1; paint(); }
  });

  /* ---------- the page tour ---------- */
  function tour() {
    try {
      var W = window.w122; if (!W || !W.tours || !W.tours.socialstats || W.tours.socialstats.__w129) return;
      var st = W.tours.socialstats.steps, OPT = { opt: 1 };
      var S2 = function (sel, tt, p) { return { s: sel, t: tt, p: p, opt: 1 }; };
      st.forEach(function (x) { if (x.s === '#socialstats .w120ctl') x.p = 'Look at the last week, month or quarter, six months, one or two years, all time, or pick two dates. Long ranges chart by week or by month.'; if (x.s === '#socialstats .cardy.chart') { x.t = 'Over time'; x.p = 'The numbers per platform by day, week or month. Switch the measure at the top of the chart.'; } if (x.s === '#socialstats .posts') x.p = 'The posts in the range, ranked by what worked.'; });
      st.push(S2('#w129 .w129tbl', 'Every post', 'Every post in the range with its reach, views, likes, comments, shares, saves and engagement rate. Filter, search, sort any column, and download it.'));
      st.push(S2('#w129 .w129aud', 'Who follows us', 'Instagram followers by age, gender, city and country, and LinkedIn followers and visitors by industry, seniority, function, location and company size.'));
      st.push(S2('#w129 .w129cov', 'How far back', 'How far back each platform goes. Meta keeps two years of daily numbers; posts go back to the first one. Download the daily numbers here.'));
      W.tours.socialstats.__w129 = true;
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  tour();
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp129 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) { var out = __sp129.apply(this, arguments); try { if (id === 'socialstats') { watch(); setTimeout(paint, 60); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa129 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () { var out = await __sa129.apply(this, arguments); try { tour(); watch(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }
  window.w129 = { paint: paint, state: function () { return X; }, rows: function () { var d = data(); return d ? explorerRows(d) : []; } };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v130 the browser test fixes (26/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel, on the browser test (#43): "For the bugs worth fixing first,
   feel free to fix them all." What this wave changes, by the numbers in
   the findings doc (docs/26-09-26 - hub browser test):
   1.  Phones: menus never wider than the screen (Activity, The Brain,
       the audit trail) and the onboarding row on a person page wraps.
   5.  Hub inbox: one header (the masthead), with the fuller purpose line.
   6.  Board view: a page the board cannot open says so, on the Executive
       dashboard where the viewer lands, with the pages the board view
       does open; links to staff pages stop being links for directors.
       Staff sent back from a page outside their access profile get the
       same notice, pointing to Request more access.
   7.  Board view: no tick boxes on the executive decisions, and the
       intro no longer says "Tick an item".
   8.  Calendar: a Board layer (board meetings, the committees' week
       before, the fiscal year-end) lives in wave124's parts.
   9.  Servings without a title read "Sunday serving, Montreal" (waves
       124 and 128, and the cancelled-serving notes in 116g and 106).
   10. Ask the Hub: a round button that opens its label on hover or
       focus, and steps aside while you type (on phones the bottom bar
       does too).
   11. First visit: one coach mark at a time. The check-in note is two
       short lines (edited in 118b) so it stays clear of the tiles; Meet
       Ask the Hub waits
       until the check-in note is gone and never shows beside the Get
       started card (which already has "Ask the Hub a question").
   12. Request more access: never for admins, and no flash before the
       role is known.
   13. Policies: Open and read and the sign-off sit apart (stacked on a
       phone), the signed pill stays on one line, and the sign-off only
       works once the document has been opened.
   14. Refresh the recommendation (waves 3 and 116, edited in place).
   15. Goal page footnotes sit inside their card.
   Bugs 2 to 4 (People and access, access requests on a phone, the
   Access profiles matrix) are fixed by the People and access redesign
   that follows this wave.
   ===================================================================== */

  function denyCheck() {

  var GO_ATTRS = ['data-w116go', 'data-w123go', 'data-w124go', 'data-w128go', 'data-w126go', 'data-w116tgo', 'data-w121go', 'data-w116kgo', 'data-w116pgo', 'data-w114go', 'data-jgo', 'data-w93go', 'data-w87go', 'data-w80go', 'data-w73go', 'data-w56go', 'data-w38go', 'data-w127go', 'data-w118cgo', 'data-w112go', 'data-mpgo'];

  function navHash(el) {
    for (var n = el, i = 0; n && n.nodeType === 1 && n !== document.body && i < 8; n = n.parentElement, i++) {
      if (n.tagName === 'A') { var hr = n.getAttribute('href'); if (hr && hr.charAt(0) === '#') return { el: n, h: hr }; }
      for (var j = 0; j < GO_ATTRS.length; j++) { var v = n.getAttribute(GO_ATTRS[j]); if (v && v.charAt(0) === '#') return { el: n, h: v }; }
    }
    return null;
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    if (!boardMode()) return;
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || t.closest('#w130deny')) return;

  function fab() {

    if (b.getAttribute('aria-label') !== 'Ask the Hub') b.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Ask the Hub');

    }
  }
  function isField(el) {
    if (!el || !el.tagName) return false;
    if (el.closest && el.closest('#waChatPanel')) return false;
    var t = el.tagName;
    if (t === 'TEXTAREA') return true;
    if (t === 'INPUT') return ['text', 'search', 'email', 'url', 'tel', 'number', 'password', ''].indexOf(String(el.getAttribute('type') || '').toLowerCase()) >= 0;
    return !!el.isContentEditable;
  }
  document.addEventListener('focusin', function (e) { if (isField(e.target)) document.body.classList.add('w130typing'); });

  /* ---------- 13. policies: open it before signing it ---------- */
  function polKey() { var em = ''; try { em = String((USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase(); } catch (e) { em = ''; } return 'w130pol:' + em; }
  function polOpened() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(polKey()) || '{}') || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } }
  function polMark(k) { if (!k) return; var o = polOpened(); o[k] = Date.now(); try { localStorage.setItem(polKey(), JSON.stringify(o)); } catch (e) { POL_MEM[k] = 1; } }
  var POL_MEM = {};
  function polKeyOf(b) { return b.getAttribute('data-w105sk') || b.getAttribute('data-w122sign') || ''; }
  function polRow(el) { return (el.closest && (el.closest('.w105sign') || el.closest('.w122trk .it'))) || null; }
  function polLink(el) { var row = polRow(el); var a = row ? row.querySelector('a[href]') : null; return a && !/^#/.test(a.getAttribute('href') || '') ? a : null; }
  function polScan() {
    var btns = document.querySelectorAll('[data-w105sk],[data-w122sign]'); if (!btns.length) return;
    var o = polOpened();
    for (var i = 0; i < btns.length; i++) {
      /* wave279: when the database keeps the opens (wa279Pol, parts279/b_hooks.js), its Sign button rule stands and this lock steps aside */
      var b = btns[i], k = polKeyOf(b), W = window.wa279Pol, known = !!(k && W && W.left(k) !== 'unknown'), lock = !!(k && !known && !o[k] && !POL_MEM[k] && polLink(b));
      if (lock && !b.hasAttribute('data-w130lock')) { b.setAttribute('data-w130lock', '1'); b.setAttribute('title', 'Open and read it first'); b.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true'); }
      else if (!lock && b.hasAttribute('data-w130lock')) { b.removeAttribute('data-w130lock'); b.removeAttribute('title'); b.removeAttribute('aria-disabled'); }
    }
  }
  function polOpen(e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return false;
    var a = t.closest('.w105sign a[href], .w122trk .it a[href]');
    if (!a || /^#/.test(a.getAttribute('href') || '')) return false;
    var row = polRow(a), b = row && row.querySelector('[data-w105sk],[data-w122sign]');
    if (b) { polMark(polKeyOf(b)); setTimeout(polScan, 30); }
    return true;
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    if (polOpen(e)) return;
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var b = t.closest('[data-w105sk],[data-w122sign]');
    if (!b || !b.hasAttribute('data-w130lock')) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); if (e.stopImmediatePropagation) e.stopImmediatePropagation();

  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('auxclick', function (e) { polOpen(e); }, true);

  /* ---------- the paint: cheap, idempotent, run after navigation and DOM changes ---------- */
  function paint() {
    try { fab(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { hubmailMast(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

    try { nudgeWait(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { polScan(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  var pT = null;
  function soon(ms) { clearTimeout(pT); pT = setTimeout(paint, ms == null ? 200 : ms); }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;

/* =====================================================================
   WA v131 People and access, one place (26/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "In the admin panel 'People and access' and 'access profiles'
   need to make more sense. Please review, I need setting up and managing
   profiles to be more seamless."

   What was confusing: two sections for one job; a wide table with a
   classic role (admin, leader, member, viewer) and an access profile side
   by side; invites that set a classic role, with the profile picked
   somewhere else; hand-picked pages behind a link; the requests in a
   third card; a comparison table in a box with its own scroll.

   Now one section, Admin > People and access, with three tabs:
   - People: what needs doing first (access requests, people without a
     profile, people not invited, missing emails, inactive people who
     can still sign in), then everyone by team: their sign-in status and
     their profile. Click anyone for their sheet: pick the profile (the
     team's own comes first, with a Lead switch), see the pages it opens
     and what changes, invite, resend, copy a sign-in link, add an email,
     remove. Invite someone (or a whole list) with the profile chosen up
     front, from the team.
   - Profiles: the nine profiles as cards; open one to set its pages
     (Everyone, Leads, Nobody), its words and see who has it; new pages
     to decide; every profile compared, full height; the automatic
     profile for new people.
   - Requests: grant a page (on top of the profile), the lead option or
     Admin in one click, or decline.
   The role is never picked by hand any more: it follows the profile
   (team profile = member, with Lead = leader; Executive = leader; Board
   member = viewer; Admin = admin). The classic view (the old table, the
   roles table and the hand-picked pages) stays one link away.
   Data: hub_roles, team_members, wa_hub_logins(), hub_access_profiles,
   hub_access_settings, wa_access_requests; actions through hub-invite
   (invite, bulk_invite, link, set_email, remove). No schema change.
   ===================================================================== */
