  function nameFromEmail(em) { return String(em || '').split('@')[0].split(/[._-]+/).filter(Boolean).map(function (w) { return w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1); }).join(' '); }
  function ago(ts) {
    if (!ts) return '';
    var d = new Date(ts).getTime(); if (isNaN(d)) return '';
    var s = Math.max(0, (Date.now() - d) / 1000);
    if (s < 3600) return 'just now';
    if (s < 86400) return Math.round(s / 3600) + ' h ago';
    var days = Math.round(s / 86400);
    if (days === 1) return 'yesterday';
    if (days < 45) return days + ' days ago';
    return new Date(d).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' });
  }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower((USER && USER.email) || ''); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function actor() { try { return (typeof who === 'function' && who()) || myEmail(); } catch (e) { return myEmail(); } }
  function amAdmin() { try { return FOUNDERS.indexOf(myEmail()) >= 0 || !!(typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'admin'); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function teamLabel(d) { try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && window.WA_TEAMS.labels && window.WA_TEAMS.labels[d]) || (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; } catch (e) { return d || ''; } }
  function teamColor(d) { try { return (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[d]) || GREEN; } catch (e) { return GREEN; } }
  function labelOf(t) { return LABEL[t] || (function () { try { var b = document.querySelector('#tabBar .tab[data-tab="' + t + '"]'); return b ? String(b.textContent || '').trim() : t; } catch (e) { return t; } })(); }
  async function logChange(type, id, field, oldV, newV) {
    try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: type, entity_id: String(id), field: field, old_value: oldV == null ? null : String(oldV).slice(0, 500), new_value: newV == null ? null : String(newV).slice(0, 500), changed_by: actor() }); } catch (e) { /* the audit trail is best effort */ }
  }
  async function invoke(body) {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke('hub-invite', { body: body });
    if (r && r.error) {
      var msg = '';
      try { if (r.error.context && r.error.context.json) msg = (await r.error.context.json()).error; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      throw new Error(msg || r.error.message || 'failed');
    }
    if (!r || !r.data) throw new Error('no reply from the Hub');
    if (r.data.error) throw new Error(r.data.error);
    return r.data;
  }
  function copyText(text) {
    try {
      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) return navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(function () { return true; }, function () { return fallbackCopy(text); });
    } catch (e) { /* fall through */ }
    return Promise.resolve(fallbackCopy(text));
  }
  function fallbackCopy(text) {
    try { var ta = document.createElement('textarea'); ta.value = text; ta.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;left:-9999px;top:0'; document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); var ok = document.execCommand('copy'); ta.remove(); return !!ok; } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  /* the same words as wave113's message, so both views send one message */
  function inviteMessage(name) {
    return 'Hi ' + (first(name) || 'there') + ', you have been added to the Welfare Avenue Hub, the team\'s shared workspace: meetings turn into tasks, goals and decisions, and everyone can see how the work moves.\n\nSign in with your Welfare Avenue Google account at https://wa-exec-dashboard.netlify.app/\n\nThe 60 second tour starts on your first visit, and a short getting-started checklist on your home page walks you through the rest.';
  }

    /* the page editor */

    /* phones */
    + '@media (max-width:900px){.w131pgrid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));}}'
    + '@media (max-width:700px){'

    + '.w131p .w131av{grid-area:av;align-self:start;}.w131p .nm{grid-area:nm;}.w131p .w131st{grid-area:st;}.w131p .acc{grid-area:ac;min-width:0;}.w131p .chv{grid-area:ch;align-self:center;}'
    + '.w131do{flex-wrap:wrap;padding:12px 14px;}.w131do .ac{width:100%;justify-content:flex-start;padding-left:44px;}'

    + '.w131pick{grid-template-columns:1fr;}'
    + '#w131sheet .two{grid-template-columns:1fr;}'
    + '#w131sheet .bd{padding:14px 14px 22px;}#w131sheet .ph{padding:14px 14px 12px;}#w131sheet .ft{padding:10px 14px calc(12px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));}'
    + '.w131mx{display:none;}.w131phone{display:block;}'
    + '.w131rq{grid-template-columns:40px minmax(0,1fr);padding:14px;}.w131rq .ac{grid-column:1/-1;justify-content:flex-start;}'

    + '}';
  (function () {
    var st = document.getElementById('w131css');
    if (!st) { st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'w131css'; (document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(st); }
    st.textContent = css
      /* the section: this card shows for People and access (and the old Access profiles address); the old cards wait behind the classic view */
      + '.w118adm:not([data-sec="people"]):not([data-sec="access"]) ~ #w131card{display:none !important;}'
      + 'body:not(.w131classic) #w113card,body:not(.w131classic) #w113card + .card,body:not(.w131classic) #w127card{display:none !important;}'
      + 'body.w131classic #w131card{display:none !important;}'
      + 'body:not(.w131classic) .w118adm a[data-w118sec="access"],body:not(.w131classic) nav.wa-nav .w118sub a[data-w118sec="access"]{display:none !important;}'
      + 'body:not(.w131classic) .w118adm[data-sec="access"] a[data-w118sec="people"]{background:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ';}'
      + 'nav.wa-nav .w118sub.w131acc a[data-w118sec="people"]{color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:700;}';
  })();

  /* ---------- state ---------- */
  var S = {
    loaded: false, loading: null, members: [], roles: {}, logins: {}, profiles: [], byKey: {}, settings: {}, requests: [], hr: [],
    tab: 'people', q: '', team: '', status: '', sheet: null, busy: false, cache: null, pedits: {}, ptab: 'pages', res: null
  };
  async function load(force) {
    if (S.loading) return S.loading;
    if (S.loaded && !force) return;
    S.loading = (async function () {
      try {
        var r = await Promise.all([
          db.from('team_members').select('id,name,email,department,role,active,avatar_url').order('name'),
          db.from('hub_roles').select('*'),
          db.rpc('wa_hub_logins'),
          db.from('hub_access_profiles').select('*').order('sort'),
          db.from('hub_access_settings').select('*').eq('id', 1),
          db.from('wa_access_requests').select('*').order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(80)
        ]);
        S.members = (r[0] && r[0].data) || [];
        S.roles = {}; ((r[1] && r[1].data) || []).forEach(function (x) { if (x && x.email) S.roles[lower(x.email)] = x; });
        S.logins = {}; ((r[2] && r[2].data) || []).forEach(function (l) { if (l && l.email) S.logins[lower(l.email)] = l; });
        S.profiles = ((r[3] && r[3].data) || []).slice().sort(function (a, b) { return (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0); });
        S.byKey = {}; S.profiles.forEach(function (p) { if (!p.pages || typeof p.pages !== 'object') p.pages = {}; if (!Array.isArray(p.can)) p.can = []; S.byKey[p.key] = p; });
        S.settings = (r[4] && r[4].data && r[4].data[0]) || S.settings || {};
        S.requests = (r[5] && r[5].data) || [];
        S.loaded = true;
      } catch (e) { /* keep what we had */ }
      S.cache = null;
      S.loading = null;
    })();
    return S.loading;
  }

  /* ---------- the access rules (the same as wave127 and wa_page_ok) ---------- */
  function isTeamP(p) { return !!(p && p.tier === 'member'); }
  function handPicked(row) { return !!(row && Array.isArray(row.tabs) && row.tabs.length); }
  function valueOf(p, tab) { var v = p && p.pages ? p.pages[tab] : undefined; return v === 'all' || v === 'lead' || v === 'none' ? v : undefined; }
  function knownTabs() {
    var t = [];
    try { t = (typeof WA40_TABS !== 'undefined' ? WA40_TABS : []).slice(); } catch (e) { t = []; }
    if (t.indexOf('adminpanel') < 0) t.push('adminpanel');
    GROUPS.forEach(function (g) { g[1].forEach(function (x) { if (t.indexOf(x) < 0) t.push(x); }); });
    return t.filter(function (x) { return x !== 'team' && x !== 'needsme' && x !== 'personpage' && x !== 'projectpage'; });
  }
  function groupsWithNew() {
    var seen = {}, gs = GROUPS.map(function (g) { g[1].forEach(function (t) { seen[t] = 1; }); return [g[0], g[1].slice()]; });
    var extra = knownTabs().filter(function (t) { return !seen[t]; });
    if (extra.length) gs.unshift(['New pages', extra]);
    return gs;
  }
  function undecided() { return knownTabs().filter(function (t) { return S.profiles.some(function (p) { return p.tier !== 'admin' && valueOf(p, t) === undefined; }); }); }
  function defaultsFor(role) { try { return ((typeof WA40_DEFAULTS !== 'undefined' && WA40_DEFAULTS[role]) || (typeof WA40_DEFAULTS !== 'undefined' && WA40_DEFAULTS.member) || []).slice(); } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function leadRole(role) { return role === 'leader' || role === 'admin'; }
  function classicPages(row, email) {
    var role = row ? row.role : (/@welfareavenue\.com$/.test(lower(email)) ? 'member' : 'viewer');
    if (role === 'blocked') return [];
    var a = handPicked(row) ? row.tabs.slice() : defaultsFor(role);
    function add(t) { if (a.indexOf(t) < 0) a.push(t); }
    if (role !== 'viewer') { add('calendar'); add('photos'); }
    if (a.indexOf('marketing') >= 0) { add('socialstats'); add('contentcal'); }
    if (a.indexOf('fundraising') >= 0 || a.indexOf('finances') >= 0) add('fundevents');
    if (leadRole(role)) { add('governance'); add('committees'); }
    if (role !== 'admin') a = a.filter(function (t) { return t !== 'adminpanel'; });
    if (role === 'admin') a = knownTabs();
    return a.filter(function (t) { return t !== 'team'; });
  }
  function pagesOfProfile(key, lead, row, email) {
    var p = S.byKey[key]; if (!p) return classicPages(row, email);
    if (p.tier === 'admin') return knownTabs();
    var ld = !!lead || p.tier === 'leader', out = [];
    knownTabs().forEach(function (t) { var v = valueOf(p, t); if (v === 'all' || (v === 'lead' && ld)) out.push(t); });
    /* a page this profile has not decided follows its built-in gate */
    classicPages(row, email).forEach(function (t) { if (valueOf(p, t) === undefined && out.indexOf(t) < 0) out.push(t); });
    return out;
  }
  function roleFor(key, lead) { var p = S.byKey[key]; if (!p) return 'member'; if (p.tier === 'admin') return 'admin'; if (p.tier === 'leader') return 'leader'; if (p.tier === 'viewer') return 'viewer'; return lead ? 'leader' : 'member'; }
  function profileName(key, lead) { var p = S.byKey[key]; if (!p) return key || ''; return p.label + (isTeamP(p) && lead ? ' (lead)' : ''); }
  function teamProfile(dept) { return S.profiles.filter(function (p) { return isTeamP(p) && p.team === dept; })[0] || null; }
  /* the profile someone is on, as the menu and the database read it */
  function assignedOf(row) {
    if (!row || row.role === 'blocked' || handPicked(row)) return null;
    if (row.role === 'admin') return S.byKey.admin || null;
    var p = row.profile ? S.byKey[row.profile] : null; if (!p) return null;
    if ((RANK[row.role] == null ? -1 : RANK[row.role]) < RANK[p.tier]) return null;
    return p;
  }

  /* ---------- the people ---------- */
  function statusOf(m, row, login, email) {
    if (row && row.role === 'blocked') return 'removed';
    if (m && m.active === false) return 'removed';
    if (!email) return 'noemail';
    if (login && login.last_sign_in_at) return 'active';
    if (login || row) return 'invited';
    return 'notinvited';
  }
  function suggest(x) {
    if (!x.email || x.status === 'removed') return null;
    var role = x.row ? x.row.role : null;
    if (x.founder || role === 'admin') return { key: 'admin', lead: false, why: x.founder ? 'Founder' : 'Admin today' };
    if (x.dept === 'board') return S.byKey.board ? { key: 'board', lead: false, why: 'On the board, not on the staff team' } : null;
    var tp = teamProfile(x.dept); if (!tp) return null;
    return { key: tp.key, lead: role === 'leader', why: 'On the ' + teamLabel(x.dept) + ' roster' + (role === 'leader' ? ', leads today' : '') };
  }
  function people() {
    if (S.cache) return S.cache;
    var out = [], seen = {};
    function mk(m, em) {
      var row = em ? S.roles[em] || null : null, login = em ? S.logins[em] || null : null;
      var x = {
        id: m ? m.id : null, name: m ? (m.name || nameFromEmail(em)) : ((row && row.note) || nameFromEmail(em)),
        title: m ? (m.role || '') : '', dept: m ? (m.department || '') : '', email: em, avatar: m ? m.avatar_url : null,
        roster: !!m, active: m ? m.active !== false : true, row: row, login: login, founder: FOUNDERS.indexOf(em) >= 0
      };
      x.status = statusOf(m, row, login, em);
      x.role = row ? row.role : '';
      x.hand = handPicked(row);
      x.assigned = assignedOf(row);
      x.lead = !!(x.assigned && isTeamP(x.assigned) && x.role === 'leader');
      x.pages = row ? (x.assigned ? pagesOfProfile(x.assigned.key, x.lead, row, em) : classicPages(row, em)) : [];
      x.sug = suggest(x);
      x.same = !!(x.sug && x.assigned && x.assigned.key === x.sug.key && (!isTeamP(x.assigned) || x.lead === !!x.sug.lead));
      return x;
    }
    S.members.forEach(function (m) { var em = lower(m.email); if (em) seen[em] = 1; out.push(mk(m, em)); });
    Object.keys(S.roles).forEach(function (em) { if (!seen[em]) out.push(mk(null, em)); });
    S.cache = out;
    return out;
  }
  function personBy(key) {
    key = String(key || '');
    return people().filter(function (x) { return (x.email && x.email === lower(key)) || (x.id != null && 'id:' + x.id === key); })[0] || null;
  }
  function pkey(x) { return x.email || ('id:' + x.id); }
  function openRequests() { return (S.requests || []).filter(function (q) { return q.status === 'new'; }); }
  function accessChip(x) {
    if (x.status === 'removed') return '<span class="w131chip gone"><span>Removed</span></span>';
    if (x.hand) return '<span class="w131chip hand" title="Hand-picked pages replace the profile"><i style="--c:' + GOLD + '"></i><span>Hand-picked, ' + x.row.tabs.length + ' pages</span></span>';
    if (x.assigned) return '<span class="w131chip" style="--c:' + E(x.assigned.color || GREEN) + '"><i></i><span>' + E(x.assigned.label + (x.lead ? ', lead' : '')) + '</span></span>';
    if (x.row) return '<span class="w131chip none"><span>No profile (classic ' + E(x.role || 'member') + ')</span></span>';
    if (!x.email) return '<span class="w131chip none"><span>Needs an email</span></span>';
    return x.sug && S.byKey[x.sug.key] ? '<span class="w131chip none" title="The profile they get when invited"><span>Will get ' + E(profileName(x.sug.key, x.sug.lead)) + '</span></span>' : '<span class="w131chip none"><span>No access</span></span>';
  }
  function statusHtml(x) {
    var t = { active: 'Signed in', invited: 'Invited', notinvited: 'Not invited', noemail: 'No email', removed: 'Removed' }[x.status] || x.status;
    var sub = '';
    if (x.status === 'active' && x.login) sub = 'last ' + ago(x.login.last_sign_in_at);
    else if (x.status === 'invited') sub = x.login && x.login.account_created ? 'not signed in yet' : 'has access, not signed in';
    else if (x.status === 'notinvited') sub = 'on the roster';
    else if (x.status === 'noemail') sub = 'add one to invite';
    return '<span class="w131st ' + E(x.status) + '"><i></i><span>' + E(t) + (sub ? '<small>' + E(sub) + '</small>' : '') + '</span></span>';
  }
  function avatar(x) {
    var c = x.dept ? teamColor(x.dept) : '#6b7b77';
    return '<span class="w131av" style="--c:' + E(c) + '">' + (x.avatar ? '<img src="' + E(x.avatar) + '" alt="" loading="lazy" onerror="this.remove()">' : '') + E(initials(x.name)) + '</span>';
  }
  function pagesHtml(list, cmp) {
    return groupsWithNew().map(function (g) {
      var ts = g[1].filter(function (t) { return list.indexOf(t) >= 0 || (cmp && cmp.indexOf(t) >= 0); });
      if (!ts.length) return '';
      return '<div class="w131grp"><small>' + E(g[0]) + '</small><div class="w131tags">' + ts.map(function (t) {
        var inNew = list.indexOf(t) >= 0, inOld = cmp ? cmp.indexOf(t) >= 0 : inNew;
        var cls = cmp ? (inNew && !inOld ? 'new' : (!inNew && inOld ? 'gone' : '')) : '';
        return '<span' + (cls ? ' class="' + cls + '"' : '') + (LOCK[t] ? ' title="' + E(LOCK[t]) + '"' : '') + '>' + (LOCK[t] ? I('lock') : '') + E(labelOf(t)) + '</span>';
      }).join('') + '</div></div>';
    }).join('');
  }
  function diffText(next, today) {
    var g = next.filter(function (t) { return today.indexOf(t) < 0; }).map(labelOf), l = today.filter(function (t) { return next.indexOf(t) < 0 && t !== 'adminpanel'; }).map(labelOf);
    if (!g.length && !l.length) return '<span class="w131mut">The same pages as today.</span>';
    return (g.length ? '<span class="g">Gains ' + E(listJoin(g)) + '.</span> ' : '') + (l.length ? '<span class="l">Loses ' + E(listJoin(l)) + '.</span>' : '');
  }

  /* ---------- what needs doing ---------- */
  function todos() {
    var xs = people(), out = [];
    var rq = openRequests();
    if (rq.length) out.push({ k: 'req', cls: 'warn', ic: 'inbox', t: '<b>' + plural(rq.length, 'access request is', 'access requests are') + ' waiting</b>', s: listJoin(rq.slice(0, 4).map(function (q) { return (q.name || q.email) + ' (' + reqWhat(q) + ')'; })), a: '<a class="w131btn sm gold" data-w131tab="requests">Review</a>' });
    var nop = xs.filter(function (x) { return x.row && x.status !== 'removed' && !x.assigned && !x.hand && x.sug && S.byKey[x.sug.key]; });
    if (nop.length) out.push({ k: 'noprof', cls: 'warn', ic: 'key', t: '<b>' + plural(nop.length, 'person has', 'people have') + ' no access profile</b>', s: listJoin(nop.slice(0, 5).map(function (x) { return first(x.name) + ' \u2192 ' + profileName(x.sug.key, x.sug.lead); })) + (nop.length > 5 ? ' and ' + (nop.length - 5) + ' more' : ''), a: '<a class="w131btn sm" data-w131apply="noprof">' + I('check') + 'Give ' + (nop.length === 1 ? 'it' : 'them their team\u2019s profile') + '</a>' });
    var mis = xs.filter(function (x) { return x.assigned && isTeamP(x.assigned) && x.dept && TEAMS.indexOf(x.dept) >= 0 && x.dept !== 'board' && x.assigned.team !== x.dept && x.status !== 'removed'; });
    if (mis.length) out.push({ k: 'mis', cls: 'warn', ic: 'alert', t: '<b>' + plural(mis.length, 'profile names', 'profiles name') + ' a different team from the roster</b>', s: listJoin(mis.map(function (x) { return first(x.name) + ': ' + x.assigned.label + ', roster says ' + teamLabel(x.dept); })), a: '<a class="w131btn sm ghost" data-w131filter="mis">Show them</a>' });
    var ni = xs.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'notinvited' && x.roster; });
    if (ni.length) out.push({ k: 'ni', cls: '', ic: 'mail', t: '<b>' + plural(ni.length, 'person on the roster is', 'people on the roster are') + ' not invited yet</b>', s: listJoin(ni.slice(0, 5).map(function (x) { return x.name; })) + (ni.length > 5 ? ' and ' + (ni.length - 5) + ' more' : ''), a: '<a class="w131btn sm ghost" data-w131filter="notinvited">Show them</a><a class="w131btn sm" data-w131invall="1">' + I('mail') + 'Invite ' + (ni.length === 1 ? 'them' : 'all ' + ni.length) + '</a>' });
    var ne = xs.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'noemail'; });
    if (ne.length) out.push({ k: 'ne', cls: '', ic: 'user', t: '<b>' + plural(ne.length, 'person has', 'people have') + ' no email yet</b>', s: 'Add an email to invite them: ' + listJoin(ne.slice(0, 5).map(function (x) { return x.name; })) + (ne.length > 5 ? ' and ' + (ne.length - 5) + ' more' : ''), a: '<a class="w131btn sm ghost" data-w131filter="noemail">Show them</a>' });
    var gone = xs.filter(function (x) { return x.roster && x.active === false && x.row && x.row.role !== 'blocked'; });
    if (gone.length) out.push({ k: 'gone', cls: 'risk', ic: 'out', t: '<b>' + plural(gone.length, 'person is', 'people are') + ' marked inactive but can still sign in</b>', s: listJoin(gone.map(function (x) { return x.name; })), a: gone.length === 1 ? '<a class="w131btn sm danger" data-w131open="' + E(pkey(gone[0])) + '">Review</a>' : '<a class="w131btn sm ghost" data-w131filter="gone">Show them</a>' });
    var adm = xs.filter(function (x) { return x.role === 'admin' && x.status !== 'removed' && x.roster; });
    if (adm.length > 5) out.push({ k: 'adm', cls: '', ic: 'shield', t: '<b>' + adm.length + ' people are admins</b>', s: 'Admin is for the people who run the Hub itself. Executive gives every page but Admin: ' + listJoin(adm.filter(function (x) { return !x.founder; }).map(function (x) { return first(x.name); })) + '.', a: '<a class="w131btn sm ghost" data-w131filter="admin">Show them</a>' });
    return out;
  }
  function reqWhat(q) {
    var w = String(q.wanted || '');
    if (w.indexOf('tab:') === 0) return labelOf(w.slice(4));
    if (w === 'role:leader') return 'lead access';
    if (w === 'role:admin') return 'Admin';
    return 'something else';
  }

  /* ---------- People: what needs doing, then everyone by team ---------- */
  var FILTERS = [['', 'Everyone'], ['active', 'Signed in'], ['invited', 'Invited'], ['notinvited', 'Not invited'], ['noemail', 'No email'], ['noprof', 'No profile'], ['removed', 'Removed']];
  var SPECIAL = { mis: 'Profile and roster disagree', gone: 'Inactive but can sign in', admin: 'Admins' };
  function matchStatus(x, f) {
    if (f === 'removed') return x.status === 'removed';
    if (f === 'gone') return x.roster && x.active === false && x.row && x.row.role !== 'blocked';
    if (x.status === 'removed') return false;
    if (!f) return true;
    if (f === 'noprof') return !!x.row && !x.assigned && !x.hand;
    if (f === 'mis') return !!(x.assigned && isTeamP(x.assigned) && x.dept && x.dept !== 'board' && x.assigned.team !== x.dept);
    if (f === 'admin') return x.role === 'admin';
    return x.status === f;
  }
  function matchQuery(x) {
    if (S.team && (S.team === 'none' ? (x.dept && TEAMS.indexOf(x.dept) >= 0) : x.dept !== S.team)) return false;
    if (!S.q) return true;
    var q = lower(S.q);
    return lower([x.name, x.email, x.title, teamLabel(x.dept), x.assigned ? x.assigned.label : ''].join(' ')).indexOf(q) >= 0;
  }
  function rowHtml(x) {
    return '<button type="button" class="w131p" data-w131open="' + E(pkey(x)) + '">' + avatar(x)
      + '<span class="nm"><b>' + E(x.name) + '</b><span>' + E(x.title || (x.roster ? '' : 'Not on the roster')) + (x.email ? (x.title || !x.roster ? ' \u00b7 ' : '') + E(x.email) : '') + '</span></span>'
      + statusHtml(x) + '<span class="acc">' + accessChip(x) + '</span><span class="chv">' + I('chev') + '</span></button>';
  }
  function peopleHtml() {
    var xs = people(), td = todos(), h = '';
    if (td.length) {
      h += '<div class="w131box w131todo"><div class="hd">' + I('spark') + 'What needs doing</div>' + td.map(function (t) {
        return '<div class="w131do ' + t.cls + '" data-w131do="' + t.k + '"><span class="ic">' + I(t.ic) + '</span><div class="tx">' + t.t + (t.s ? '<small>' + E(t.s) + '</small>' : '') + '</div><div class="ac">' + t.a + '</div></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
    } else h += '<div class="w131box w131alldone">' + I('check') + '<span>Everyone is set up: each person with access has a profile, and no request is waiting.</span></div>';
    var base = xs.filter(matchQuery);
    var counts = {}; FILTERS.forEach(function (f) { counts[f[0]] = base.filter(function (x) { return matchStatus(x, f[0]); }).length; });
    h += '<div class="w131bar"><label class="w131q">' + I('search') + '<input type="search" id="w131q" placeholder="Search a name, email, title or profile" value="' + E(S.q) + '" aria-label="Search people"></label>'
      + '<select id="w131team" aria-label="Team"><option value="">Every team</option>' + TEAMS.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '"' + (S.team === d ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '<option value="none"' + (S.team === 'none' ? ' selected' : '') + '>No team</option></select></div>'
      + '<div class="w131chips" role="group" aria-label="Show">' + FILTERS.map(function (f) { return '<a data-w131st="' + f[0] + '"' + (S.status === f[0] ? ' class="on"' : '') + '>' + E(f[1]) + ' <b>' + counts[f[0]] + '</b></a>'; }).join('')
      + (SPECIAL[S.status] ? '<a class="on" data-w131st="">' + E(SPECIAL[S.status]) + ' <b>' + base.filter(function (x) { return matchStatus(x, S.status); }).length + '</b> \u00d7</a>' : '') + '</div>';
    var list = base.filter(function (x) { return matchStatus(x, S.status); });
    if (!list.length) return h + '<div class="w131box"><p class="w131empty">Nobody matches. Clear the search or pick another filter.</p></div>' + footHtml();
    var groups = TEAMS.map(function (d) { return [d, teamLabel(d), teamColor(d)]; }).concat([['', 'No team', '#8b8d88']]);
    groups.forEach(function (g) {
      var inG = list.filter(function (x) { return g[0] ? x.dept === g[0] : !(x.dept && TEAMS.indexOf(x.dept) >= 0); });
      if (!inG.length) return;
      inG.sort(function (a, b) { var la = a.lead || a.role === 'admin' ? 0 : 1, lb = b.lead || b.role === 'admin' ? 0 : 1; return (la - lb) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
      h += '<section class="w131box w131team" style="padding:4px 0 2px;--c:' + E(g[2]) + '"><div class="th"><i></i><h3>' + E(g[1]) + '</h3><span class="n">' + inG.length + '</span><span class="sp"></span>'
        + (g[0] ? '<a class="w131lnk" data-w131newin="' + E(g[0]) + '" style="font-size:12.5px;white-space:nowrap" aria-label="Invite someone to ' + E(g[1]) + '">' + I('plus') + ' Invite<span class="t"> to this team</span></a>' : '') + '</div>'
        + inG.map(rowHtml).join('') + '</section>';
    });
    return h + footHtml();
  }
  function footHtml() {
    return '<p class="w131foot">A person\u2019s access profile decides the pages they see and, for the finances and the board, what the database lets them read. Their role follows the profile. '
      + '<a class="w131lnk" data-w131classic="1">Open the classic view</a> for the old table, the roles and hand-picked pages.</p>';
  }

  /* ---------- the person sheet ---------- */
  function openPerson(key) { S.sheet = { kind: 'person', key: key, pick: null, lead: null, rm: false, email: '', res: null, link: null }; renderSheet(); }
  function sheetPick(x) {
    var sh = S.sheet;
    var key = sh.pick || (x.assigned ? x.assigned.key : (x.sug ? x.sug.key : null));
    var lead = sh.lead != null ? sh.lead : (sh.pick ? false : (x.assigned ? x.lead : !!(x.sug && x.sug.lead)));
    if (key && !isTeamP(S.byKey[key])) lead = false;
    return { key: key, lead: lead };
  }
  function tierText(p) { return p.tier === 'admin' ? 'Everything, and Admin' : p.tier === 'leader' ? 'Every page but Admin' : p.tier === 'viewer' ? 'Directors, not on staff' : 'Team, lead option'; }
  function pickerHtml(sel, lead, dept, lockedAdmin) {
    var teamPs = S.profiles.filter(isTeamP).slice().sort(function (a, b) { return (a.team === dept ? -1 : 0) - (b.team === dept ? -1 : 0) || (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0); });
    var others = S.profiles.filter(function (p) { return !isTeamP(p); });
    function opt(p) {
      var dis = lockedAdmin && p.key !== 'admin';
      return '<button type="button" class="w131opt' + (sel === p.key ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w131pick="' + E(p.key) + '"' + (dis ? ' disabled' : '') + ' style="--c:' + E(p.color || GREEN) + '" aria-pressed="' + (sel === p.key) + '"><i></i><span>' + E(p.label) + '<small>' + E(tierText(p)) + '</small></span>' + (p.team && p.team === dept ? '<em>their team</em>' : '') + '</button>';
    }
    var h = '<div class="w131pick"><div class="gl">Team profiles</div>' + teamPs.map(opt).join('') + '<div class="gl">Everything else</div>' + others.map(opt).join('') + '</div>';
    var sp = S.byKey[sel];
    if (sp && isTeamP(sp)) h += '<div class="w131lead"><button type="button" class="w131sw' + (lead ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w131lead="1" role="switch" aria-checked="' + !!lead + '" aria-label="Lead of the team"></button><div><b>Lead of ' + E(teamLabel(sp.team) || sp.label) + '</b><span>' + E(sp.lead_note || 'A lead also gets Agendas, Activity, the HR centre for their team and the board pages.') + '</span></div></div>';
    return h;
  }
  function personSheet(x) {
    var sh = S.sheet, pk = sheetPick(x), p = pk.key ? S.byKey[pk.key] : null;
    var next = p ? pagesOfProfile(p.key, pk.lead, x.row, x.email) : [];
    var changed = !!p && (!x.assigned || x.assigned.key !== p.key || (isTeamP(p) && x.lead !== pk.lead) || x.hand);
    var body = '';
    /* sign-in */
    var sign = '';
    if (x.status === 'active') sign = '<p>Signed in ' + E(ago(x.login && x.login.last_sign_in_at)) + '.</p><div class="row"><a class="w131btn sm ghost" data-w131link="1">' + I('link') + 'Copy a sign-in link</a></div>';
    else if (x.status === 'invited') sign = '<p>Has access but has not signed in yet' + (x.login && x.login.account_created ? ' (account made ' + E(ago(x.login.account_created)) + ')' : '') + '.</p><div class="row"><a class="w131btn sm" data-w131resend="1">' + I('mail') + 'Send the invitation again</a><a class="w131btn sm ghost" data-w131link="1">' + I('link') + 'Copy a sign-in link</a></div>';
    else if (x.status === 'notinvited') sign = '<p>On the roster, not invited yet. Pick the profile below, then invite them: the Hub emails a sign-in invitation from hub@ and sends a Slack welcome when it can find them.</p>';
    else if (x.status === 'noemail') sign = '<p>No email yet, so they cannot be invited.</p><label class="f">Their Welfare Avenue email<input type="email" id="w131em" placeholder="name@welfareavenue.com" value="' + E(sh.email || '') + '"></label><div class="row"><a class="w131btn sm" data-w131setem="1">' + I('check') + 'Save the email</a></div>';
    else if (x.status === 'removed') sign = '<p>' + (x.row && x.row.role === 'blocked' ? 'Removed from the Hub: they cannot sign in.' : 'Marked as no longer active on the roster.') + ' Their work and history stay.</p>';
    if (sh.link) sign += '<div class="note" style="margin-top:8px">' + sh.link + '</div>';
    if (sh.res) sign += '<div class="res" style="margin-top:10px">' + sh.res + '</div>';
    body += '<div class="sec"><h4>Sign-in</h4>' + sign + '</div>';
    /* access */
    var acc = '';
    if (x.status === 'noemail') acc = '<p class="w131mut" style="font-size:13px">Add their email first; then pick a profile and invite them.</p>';
    else {
      acc += '<p style="font-size:13px">' + (x.founder ? 'Founders always keep Admin.' : x.hand ? 'Today: <b>hand-picked pages</b> (' + x.row.tabs.length + '), which replace any profile.' : x.assigned ? 'Today: <b>' + E(profileName(x.assigned.key, x.lead)) + '</b>.' : x.row ? 'Today: <b>no profile</b>; the classic ' + E(x.role || 'member') + ' pages apply.' : 'They get this profile when they are invited.') + (x.sug && S.byKey[x.sug.key] && !x.same && !x.founder && x.status !== 'removed' ? ' Suggested: <b>' + E(profileName(x.sug.key, x.sug.lead)) + '</b> (' + E(x.sug.why) + ').' : '') + '</p>';
      acc += pickerHtml(pk.key, pk.lead, x.dept, x.founder);
      if (p) {
        acc += '<div style="margin-top:14px"><h4>' + (x.row && x.status !== 'removed' ? 'What they will see' : 'What they get') + ' \u00b7 ' + next.length + ' pages</h4>'
          + (x.row && x.status !== 'removed' && changed ? '<p class="w131diff">' + diffText(next, x.pages) + '</p>' : '')
          + pagesHtml(next, x.row && x.status !== 'removed' && changed ? x.pages : null) + '</div>';
      }
      if (x.hand && !x.founder) acc += '<div class="note" style="margin-top:12px">Saving a profile replaces the ' + x.row.tabs.length + ' hand-picked pages. <a class="w131lnk" data-w131hand="1">Edit the hand-picked pages instead</a></div>';
    }
    body += '<div class="sec"><h4>Access profile</h4>' + acc + '<div class="err" id="w131err"></div></div>';
    /* remove */
    if (x.email && x.status !== 'removed' && !x.founder && x.email !== myEmail()) {
      body += '<div class="sec"><h4>Remove</h4>' + (sh.rm
        ? '<p>Remove <b>' + E(x.name) + '</b> from the Hub? Their access is blocked, their sign-in is revoked and they are marked inactive on the roster. Their tasks, updates and history stay, and you can invite them again later.</p><div class="row"><a class="w131btn sm danger solid" data-w131rmgo="1">Remove them</a><a class="w131btn sm ghost" data-w131rm="0">Cancel</a></div>'
        : '<p class="w131mut" style="font-size:13px">Takes away their access and signs them out everywhere.</p><div class="row"><a class="w131btn sm danger" data-w131rm="1">' + I('out') + 'Remove from the Hub</a></div>') + '</div>';
    }
    /* the footer: the one thing to do */
    var foot = '';
    if (x.status === 'notinvited' || x.status === 'removed') {
      foot = '<label class="ck" style="font-size:13px"><input type="checkbox" id="w131send" checked> Email the invitation</label><span class="sp"></span><a class="w131btn' + (p ? '' : ' off') + '" data-w131invite="1">' + I('mail') + (x.status === 'removed' ? 'Invite again' : 'Invite') + (p ? ' as ' + E(profileName(p.key, pk.lead)) : '') + '</a>';
    } else if (x.status !== 'noemail') {
      foot = (x.id ? '<a class="w131btn sm ghost" data-w131go="#person/' + E(x.id) + '">' + I('user') + 'Their page</a>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span><a class="w131btn' + (changed && !x.founder ? '' : ' off') + '" data-w131save="1">' + I('check') + (changed ? 'Save: ' + E(p ? profileName(p.key, pk.lead) : '') : 'Saved') + '</a>';
    } else foot = '<span class="sp"></span><a class="w131btn ghost" data-w131close="1">Close</a>';
    return { kicker: x.roster ? teamLabel(x.dept) || 'No team' : 'Not on the roster', title: x.name, sub: [x.title, x.email].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 '), body: body, foot: foot };
  }

  /* ---------- person actions ---------- */
  function sheetErr(m) { var e = document.getElementById('w131err'); if (e) e.textContent = m || ''; }
  async function afterChange(email) {
    await load(true);
    try { if (typeof wa40Load === 'function') await wa40Load(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (lower(email) === myEmail() && typeof wa40Enforce === 'function') wa40Enforce(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (window.w127 && window.w127.load) window.w127.load(true); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    paint(); if (S.sheet) renderSheet();
  }
  async function saveProfile(x, key, lead, why) {
    var p = S.byKey[key]; if (!p || !x.email) return false;
    if (x.founder && key !== 'admin') { sheetErr('Founders always keep Admin.'); return false; }
    if (x.email === myEmail() && x.role === 'admin' && key !== 'admin') { sheetErr('You cannot take Admin away from yourself here. Ask another admin.'); return false; }
    var role = roleFor(key, lead);
    var r = await db.from('hub_roles').upsert({ email: x.email, role: role, profile: key, tabs: null, updated_by: actor(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }, { onConflict: 'email' });
    if (r && r.error) { sheetErr('Could not save it: ' + (r.error.message || 'try again')); return false; }
    logChange('hub_role', x.email, 'profile', x.hand ? 'hand-picked pages' : x.assigned ? profileName(x.assigned.key, x.lead) : (x.row ? x.row.role + ' (classic)' : null), profileName(key, lead) + (why ? ' (' + why + ')' : ''));
    return true;
  }
  async function doSave() {
    var x = personBy(S.sheet.key); if (!x || S.busy) return;
    var pk = sheetPick(x); if (!pk.key) return;
    S.busy = true;
    var ok = await saveProfile(x, pk.key, pk.lead);
    S.busy = false;
    if (ok) { toastS(first(x.name) + ': ' + profileName(pk.key, pk.lead)); S.sheet.pick = null; S.sheet.lead = null; await afterChange(x.email); }
  }
  function resultHtml(res, name) {
    return (res.email_sent ? '<b class="ok">Invitation emailed</b>' + (res.note && /Supabase/.test(res.note) ? ' <span class="w131mut">(by Supabase; the hub@ send failed)</span>' : ' <span class="w131mut">from hub@welfareavenue.com</span>') : '<b class="no">No email sent</b>' + (res.note && res.note !== 'not requested' ? ' <span class="w131mut">(' + E(String(res.note).slice(0, 110)) + ')</span>' : ' <span class="w131mut">(you chose not to)</span>'))
      + '<br>Slack welcome: ' + (res.slack === 'sent' || res.slack === 'sent_channel' ? '<b class="ok">sent</b>' : '<b class="no">not sent</b> <span class="w131mut">(' + E(res.slack === 'no_slack' ? 'not in the WA Slack yet' : (res.slack || 'skipped')) + ')</span>')
      + '<div style="margin-top:10px"><textarea class="msg" readonly id="w131msg">' + E(inviteMessage(name)) + '</textarea></div><div class="row" style="margin-top:8px"><a class="w131btn sm ghost" data-w131copymsg="1">Copy the message</a><a class="w131btn sm ghost" data-w131link="1">' + I('link') + 'Copy a sign-in link</a></div>';
  }
  async function doInvite(x, key, lead, send) {
    var body = { action: 'invite', name: x.name, email: x.email, role_title: x.title || '', hub_role: roleFor(key, lead), send_email: !!send };
    if (x.dept && TEAMS.indexOf(x.dept) >= 0) body.department = x.dept;
    var res = await invoke(body);
    var r = await db.from('hub_roles').upsert({ email: x.email, role: roleFor(key, lead), profile: key, tabs: null, updated_by: actor(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }, { onConflict: 'email' });
    if (r && r.error) throw new Error('invited, but the profile did not save: ' + (r.error.message || ''));
    logChange('hub_role', x.email, 'profile', null, profileName(key, lead) + ' (with the invitation)');
    return res;
  }
  async function inviteFromSheet() {
    var x = personBy(S.sheet.key); if (!x || S.busy) return;
    var pk = sheetPick(x); if (!pk.key) { sheetErr('Pick a profile first.'); return; }
    var send = !!(document.getElementById('w131send') || {}).checked;
    S.busy = true; var btn = document.querySelector('#w131sheet [data-w131invite]'); if (btn) { btn.classList.add('off'); btn.textContent = 'Inviting\u2026'; }
    try {
      var res = await doInvite(x, pk.key, pk.lead, send);
      S.sheet.res = resultHtml(res, x.name); S.sheet.pick = null; S.sheet.lead = null;
      toastS(res.email_sent ? 'Invitation emailed to ' + first(x.name) : first(x.name) + ' can sign in now; share the message or a link');
      S.busy = false; await afterChange(x.email);
    } catch (e) { S.busy = false; sheetErr('Could not invite: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 140)); renderSheetKeepErr(); }
  }
  async function resend() {
    var x = personBy(S.sheet.key); if (!x || S.busy) return;
    S.busy = true;
    try {
      var body = { action: 'invite', name: x.name, email: x.email, role_title: x.title || '', hub_role: x.role && x.role !== 'blocked' ? x.role : 'member', send_email: true };
      if (x.dept && TEAMS.indexOf(x.dept) >= 0) body.department = x.dept;
      var res = await invoke(body);
      S.sheet.res = resultHtml(res, x.name);
      toastS(res.email_sent ? 'Invitation sent again' : 'The email did not go; copy a sign-in link instead');
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not send it: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 90)); }
    S.busy = false; renderSheet();
  }
  async function signLink() {
    var x = personBy(S.sheet.key); if (!x || !x.email) return;
    S.sheet.link = 'Making a one-time sign-in link\u2026'; renderSheet();
    try {
      var res = await invoke({ action: 'link', email: x.email });
      var ok = await copyText(res.link);
      S.sheet.link = (ok ? '<b>Copied.</b> ' : 'Here it is: ') + 'A one-time link that signs ' + E(first(x.name)) + ' straight in' + (res.existing_account ? '' : ' and sets up their account') + '. Send it only to them.<input type="text" readonly value="' + E(res.link) + '" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;margin-top:6px;font-size:11.5px;padding:6px 8px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.2);border-radius:8px" onclick="this.select()">';
      toastS(ok ? 'Sign-in link copied' : 'Link ready to copy');
    } catch (e) { S.sheet.link = 'Could not make a link: ' + E(String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 110)); }
    renderSheet();
  }
  async function setEmail() {
    var x = personBy(S.sheet.key); if (!x || !x.id || S.busy) return;
    var inp = document.getElementById('w131em'); var em = lower(inp && inp.value);
    S.sheet.email = em;
    if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(em)) { sheetErr('Type a valid email.'); return; }
    S.busy = true;
    try {
      await invoke({ action: 'set_email', member_id: Number(x.id), email: em });
      toastS('Email saved; ' + first(x.name) + ' can be invited now');
      S.sheet.key = em; S.sheet.email = ''; S.busy = false;
      await afterChange(em);
    } catch (e) { S.busy = false; sheetErr('Could not save it: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
  }
  async function removeGo() {
    var x = personBy(S.sheet.key); if (!x || S.busy) return;
    S.busy = true;
    try {
      var res = await invoke({ action: 'remove', email: x.email });
      toastS('Removed from the Hub' + (res.auth_deleted ? ' and signed out everywhere' : ''));
      S.sheet.rm = false; S.busy = false;
      await afterChange(x.email);
    } catch (e) { S.busy = false; sheetErr('Could not remove them: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
  }
  function renderSheetKeepErr() { var e = document.getElementById('w131err'); var m = e ? e.textContent : ''; renderSheet(); if (m) sheetErr(m); }

  /* ---------- Profiles: the nine profiles, what each opens, who has it ---------- */
  function tierChip(p) {
    if (p.tier === 'admin') return '<span class="w131tier admin">Everything</span>';
    if (p.tier === 'leader') return '<span class="w131tier exec">All but Admin</span>';
    if (p.tier === 'viewer') return '<span class="w131tier board">Board</span>';
    return '<span class="w131tier">Team, lead option</span>';
  }
  function holders(key) { return people().filter(function (x) { return x.assigned && x.assigned.key === key && x.status !== 'removed'; }); }
  function pagesCount(p) {
    var mem = pagesOfProfile(p.key, false, null, 'x@welfareavenue.com'), lead = isTeamP(p) ? pagesOfProfile(p.key, true, null, 'x@welfareavenue.com').filter(function (t) { return mem.indexOf(t) < 0; }) : [];
    return { mem: mem.length, lead: lead.length };
  }
  function profilesHtml() {
    var u = undecided(), s = S.settings || {};
    var h = '<div class="w131intro"><p>A profile is a set of pages. Everyone on the Hub has one: their team\u2019s (with a <b>Lead</b> switch for team leads), <b>Board member</b> for directors, <b>Executive</b> for the leadership team or <b>Admin</b> for the people who run the Hub. Open a profile to change its pages; everyone on it sees the change the next time they open the Hub.</p>'
      + '<label><button type="button" class="w131sw' + (s.auto_profile ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w131auto="1" role="switch" aria-checked="' + !!s.auto_profile + '" aria-label="New people get their team\u2019s profile"></button>New people get their team\u2019s profile automatically</label></div>';
    if (u.length) h += '<div class="w131box w131todo" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div class="w131do warn"><span class="ic">' + I('alert') + '</span><div class="tx"><b>' + plural(u.length, 'page has', 'pages have') + ' no decision yet</b><small>' + E(listJoin(u.map(labelOf))) + '. Until you decide, ' + (u.length === 1 ? 'it follows its' : 'they follow their') + ' built-in access.</small></div><div class="ac"><a class="w131btn sm gold" data-w131decide="1">Decide</a></div></div></div>';
    h += '<div class="w131pgrid">' + S.profiles.map(function (p) {
      var hs = holders(p.key), n = pagesCount(p);
      return '<button type="button" class="w131box w131pc" data-w131prof="' + E(p.key) + '" style="--c:' + E(p.color || GREEN) + '"><span class="bar"></span><span class="in"><span class="hd"><h3>' + E(p.label) + '</h3>' + tierChip(p) + '</span>'
        + '<p>' + E(p.summary || '') + '</p>'
        + '<span class="nums"><span>' + (p.tier === 'admin' ? 'Every page' : n.mem + ' pages') + '</span>' + (n.lead ? '<span>+' + n.lead + ' for leads</span>' : '') + '<span>' + plural(hs.length, 'person', 'people') + '</span></span></span></button>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
    h += '<div class="w131sec"><h2>Compare the profiles</h2><span>Every page, profile by profile. A lock means the database enforces it too. Open a profile above to change it.</span></div>' + matrixHtml()
      + '<div class="w131box w131phone"><p class="w131empty">The comparison needs a wider screen. Open a profile above to see and change its pages.</p></div>';
    return h;
  }
  function matrixHtml() {
    var ps = S.profiles, SHORT = { marketing: 'Marketing', fundraising: 'Fundraising', progdev: 'Program Dev.', opsmtl: 'Montreal ops', opsto: 'Toronto ops', hr: 'HR & People', board: 'Board', executive: 'Executive', admin: 'Admin' };
    var h = '<div class="w131mx"><table><thead><tr><th class="pg">Page</th>' + ps.map(function (p) { return '<th style="--c:' + E(p.color || GREEN) + '"><i></i>' + E(SHORT[p.key] || p.label) + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>';
    groupsWithNew().forEach(function (g) {
      h += '<tr class="gp"><td class="pg" colspan="' + (ps.length + 1) + '">' + E(g[0]) + '</td></tr>';
      g[1].forEach(function (t) {
        h += '<tr><td class="pg">' + E(labelOf(t)) + (LOCK[t] ? '<span class="lk" title="' + E(LOCK[t]) + '">' + I('lock') + '</span>' : '') + '</td>' + ps.map(function (p) {
          var v = p.tier === 'admin' ? 'all' : valueOf(p, t);
          return '<td>' + (v === 'all' ? '<span class="all" title="Everyone on it"></span>' : v === 'lead' ? '<span class="lead">Leads</span>' : v === 'none' ? '<span class="none" title="Nobody"></span>' : '<span class="und" title="Not decided yet">?</span>') + '</td>';
        }).join('') + '</tr>';
      });
    });
    return h + '</tbody></table></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the profile sheet: its pages, its people, its words ---------- */
  function openProfile(key) { S.sheet = { kind: 'profile', key: key }; S.ptab = 'pages'; S.pedits = {}; renderSheet(); }
  function editVal(p, t) { var e = S.pedits[t]; return e !== undefined ? e : (p.tier === 'admin' ? 'all' : valueOf(p, t)); }
  function profileSheet() {
    var p = S.byKey[S.sheet.key]; if (!p) return null;
    var team = isTeamP(p), hs = holders(p.key), n = Object.keys(S.pedits).length, body = '';
    body += '<div class="w131stabs" role="tablist">' + [['pages', 'Pages'], ['people', 'People (' + hs.length + ')'], ['words', 'What it says']].map(function (t) { return '<a data-w131ptab="' + t[0] + '"' + (S.ptab === t[0] ? ' class="on"' : '') + '>' + E(t[1]) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    if (S.ptab === 'pages') {
      if (p.tier === 'admin') body += '<div class="sec"><p>Admin opens every page, including Admin itself, and only admins can change access. There is nothing to set here.</p></div>';
      else {
        body += '<div class="sec"><p style="font-size:13px">For each page: <b>Everyone</b> on the profile' + (team ? ', only its <b>Leads</b>' : '') + ', or <b>Nobody</b>. A lock means the database enforces it too.</p><div class="w131ed">' + groupsWithNew().map(function (g) {
          var rows = g[1].filter(function (t) { return t !== 'adminpanel'; }).map(function (t) {
            var v = editVal(p, t), ch = S.pedits[t] !== undefined;
            var opts = team ? [['all', 'Everyone'], ['lead', 'Leads'], ['none', 'Nobody']] : [['all', 'Everyone'], ['none', 'Nobody']];
            return '<div class="w131er' + (ch ? ' ch' : '') + '"><span class="lb">' + E(labelOf(t)) + (LOCK[t] ? ' ' + I('lock') : '') + (v === undefined ? '<small>Not decided yet: follows its built-in access</small>' : (LOCK[t] ? '<small>' + E(LOCK[t]) + '</small>' : '')) + '</span><span class="w131seg3" role="group" aria-label="' + E(labelOf(t)) + '">'
              + opts.map(function (o) { return '<button type="button" data-w131cell="' + E(t) + '|' + o[0] + '" class="' + (v === o[0] ? 'on ' + o[0] : '') + '" aria-pressed="' + (v === o[0]) + '">' + o[1] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</span></div>';
          }).join('');
          return rows ? '<div class="gl">' + E(g[0]) + '</div>' + rows : '';
        }).join('') + '</div></div>';
      }
    } else if (S.ptab === 'people') {
      body += '<div class="sec">' + (hs.length ? hs.map(function (x) { return '<button type="button" class="w131p" data-w131open="' + E(pkey(x)) + '" style="padding:8px 0;grid-template-columns:40px minmax(0,1fr) auto 20px">' + avatar(x) + '<span class="nm"><b>' + E(x.name) + '</b><span>' + E([x.title, teamLabel(x.dept)].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</span></span><span>' + (x.lead ? '<span class="w131tier exec">Lead</span>' : '') + '</span><span class="chv">' + I('chev') + '</span></button>'; }).join('') : '<p class="w131mut">Nobody has it yet.</p>') + '</div>';
      var sug = people().filter(function (x) { return x.sug && x.sug.key === p.key && !x.same && x.row && x.status !== 'removed'; });
      if (sug.length) body += '<div class="sec"><h4>Suggested for</h4><p>' + E(listJoin(sug.map(function (x) { return x.name; }))) + '</p><div class="row"><a class="w131btn sm" data-w131apply="prof:' + E(p.key) + '">' + I('check') + 'Give it to ' + (sug.length === 1 ? first(sug[0].name) : 'all ' + sug.length) + '</a></div></div>';
    } else {
      body += '<div class="sec"><form data-w131words="1" onsubmit="return false"><label class="f">Name<input type="text" name="label" maxlength="60" value="' + E(p.label) + '"></label>'
        + '<label class="f">Who it is for<input type="text" name="summary" maxlength="220" value="' + E(p.summary || '') + '"></label>'
        + '<label class="f">What it lets you do (one line each)<textarea name="can">' + E((p.can || []).join('\n')) + '</textarea></label>'
        + (team ? '<label class="f">What a lead adds<textarea name="lead_note" style="min-height:80px">' + E(p.lead_note || '') + '</textarea></label>' : '') + '</form></div>';
    }
    body += '<div class="err" id="w131err"></div>';
    var foot = S.ptab === 'pages' && p.tier !== 'admin'
      ? (n ? '<a class="w131btn sm ghost" data-w131pundo="1">' + I('undo') + 'Undo</a>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span><a class="w131btn' + (n ? '' : ' off') + '" data-w131psave="1">' + I('check') + (n ? 'Save ' + plural(n, 'change', 'changes') : 'No changes') + '</a>'
      : S.ptab === 'words' ? '<span class="sp"></span><a class="w131btn" data-w131wsave="1">' + I('check') + 'Save the words</a>' : '<span class="sp"></span><a class="w131btn ghost" data-w131close="1">Close</a>';
    return { kicker: 'Access profile', title: p.label, sub: p.summary || '', body: body, foot: foot };
  }
  function cell(t, v) {
    var p = S.byKey[S.sheet && S.sheet.key]; if (!p || p.tier === 'admin') return;
    if (v === valueOf(p, t)) delete S.pedits[t]; else S.pedits[t] = v;
    renderSheet();
  }
  async function savePages() {
    var p = S.byKey[S.sheet && S.sheet.key]; if (!p || S.busy) return;
    var ed = S.pedits, keys = Object.keys(ed); if (!keys.length) return;
    S.busy = true;
    try {
      var next = Object.assign({}, p.pages, ed);
      var r = await db.from('hub_access_profiles').update({ pages: next, updated_by: myEmail(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('key', p.key);
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      logChange('access_profile', p.key, 'pages', null, keys.map(function (t) { return labelOf(t) + ': ' + (ed[t] === 'all' ? 'everyone' : ed[t] === 'lead' ? 'leads' : 'nobody'); }).join('; '));
      try { await db.from('hub_access_settings').update({ reviewed_at: new Date().toISOString(), reviewed_by: myEmail(), updated_by: myEmail(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', 1); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      S.pedits = {}; S.busy = false;
      toastS('Saved. Everyone on ' + p.label + ' sees it the next time they open the Hub.');
      await afterChange(myEmail());
    } catch (e) { S.busy = false; sheetErr('Could not save it: ' + ((e && e.message) || 'try again')); }
  }
  async function saveWords() {
    var p = S.byKey[S.sheet && S.sheet.key], f = document.querySelector('#w131sheet form[data-w131words]'); if (!p || !f || S.busy) return;
    var label = String(f.label.value || '').trim(); if (!label) { sheetErr('Give the profile a name.'); return; }
    var patch = { label: label, summary: String(f.summary.value || '').trim(), can: String(f.can.value || '').split('\n').map(function (s) { return s.trim(); }).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 12), updated_by: myEmail(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    if (f.lead_note) patch.lead_note = String(f.lead_note.value || '').trim();
    S.busy = true;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('hub_access_profiles').update(patch).eq('key', p.key);
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      logChange('access_profile', p.key, 'words', p.label, label);
      S.busy = false; toastS('Saved'); await afterChange(myEmail());
    } catch (e) { S.busy = false; sheetErr('Could not save it: ' + ((e && e.message) || 'try again')); }
  }
  async function setAuto(on) {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('hub_access_settings').update({ auto_profile: !!on, updated_by: myEmail(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', 1);
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      S.settings.auto_profile = !!on; logChange('access_profile', 'settings', 'auto_profile', String(!on), String(!!on));
      toastS(on ? 'New people now get their team\u2019s profile' : 'New people wait for you to pick a profile');
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not change it'); }
    paint();
  }

  /* ---------- new pages to decide: one sheet, every profile ---------- */
  function openDecide() { S.sheet = { kind: 'decide' }; S.dedits = {}; renderSheet(); }
  function decideSheet() {
    var u = undecided(); if (!u.length && !Object.keys(S.dedits || {}).length) return { kicker: 'New pages', title: 'Nothing to decide', sub: '', body: '<div class="sec"><p>Every page has a decision in every profile.</p></div>', foot: '<span class="sp"></span><a class="w131btn ghost" data-w131close="1">Close</a>' };
    var body = '<div class="sec"><p style="font-size:13px">A release added ' + (u.length === 1 ? 'this page' : 'these pages') + '. Decide who sees ' + (u.length === 1 ? 'it' : 'them') + ' in each profile; until then ' + (u.length === 1 ? 'it follows its' : 'they follow their') + ' built-in access.</p></div>';
    u.forEach(function (t) {
      body += '<div class="sec"><h4>' + E(labelOf(t)) + '</h4><div class="w131ed">' + S.profiles.filter(function (p) { return p.tier !== 'admin'; }).map(function (p) {
        var k = p.key + '|' + t, v = (S.dedits || {})[k] !== undefined ? S.dedits[k] : valueOf(p, t), team = isTeamP(p);
        var opts = team ? [['all', 'Everyone'], ['lead', 'Leads'], ['none', 'Nobody']] : [['all', 'Everyone'], ['none', 'Nobody']];
        return '<div class="w131er"><span class="lb">' + E(p.label) + '</span><span class="w131seg3">' + opts.map(function (o) { return '<button type="button" data-w131dcell="' + E(k) + '|' + o[0] + '" class="' + (v === o[0] ? 'on ' + o[0] : '') + '">' + o[1] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</span></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div></div>';
    });
    var n = Object.keys(S.dedits || {}).length;
    return { kicker: 'New pages', title: plural(u.length, 'page to decide', 'pages to decide'), sub: '', body: body + '<div class="err" id="w131err"></div>', foot: '<span class="sp"></span><a class="w131btn' + (n ? '' : ' off') + '" data-w131dsave="1">' + I('check') + (n ? 'Save ' + plural(n, 'decision', 'decisions') : 'Pick above') + '</a>' };
  }
  async function saveDecide() {
    var ed = S.dedits || {}, keys = Object.keys(ed); if (!keys.length || S.busy) return;
    var byP = {}; keys.forEach(function (k) { var a = k.split('|'); (byP[a[0]] = byP[a[0]] || {})[a[1]] = ed[k]; });
    S.busy = true;
    try {
      for (var pk in byP) {
        var p = S.byKey[pk]; if (!p) continue;
        var r = await db.from('hub_access_profiles').update({ pages: Object.assign({}, p.pages, byP[pk]), updated_by: myEmail(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('key', pk);
        if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
        logChange('access_profile', pk, 'pages', null, Object.keys(byP[pk]).map(function (t) { return labelOf(t) + ': ' + byP[pk][t]; }).join('; '));
      }
      S.dedits = {}; S.busy = false; toastS('Saved'); closeSheet(); await afterChange(myEmail());
    } catch (e) { S.busy = false; sheetErr('Could not save it: ' + ((e && e.message) || 'try again')); }
  }

  /* ---------- give many people their suggested profile ---------- */
  function applyList(which) {
    var xs = people().filter(function (x) { return x.row && x.status !== 'removed' && x.sug && S.byKey[x.sug.key] && !x.same && !x.founder; });
    if (which === 'noprof') xs = xs.filter(function (x) { return !x.assigned && !x.hand; });
    else if (String(which).indexOf('prof:') === 0) { var k = which.slice(5); xs = xs.filter(function (x) { return x.sug.key === k; }); }
    return xs;
  }
  function openApply(which) {
    var xs = applyList(which); if (!xs.length) { toastS('Nothing to change'); return; }
    S.sheet = { kind: 'apply', which: which }; renderSheet();
  }
  function applySheet() {
    var xs = applyList(S.sheet.which);
    var body = '<div class="sec"><p>' + (xs.length === 1 ? E(xs[0].name) + ' gets' : plural(xs.length, 'person gets', 'people get')) + ' the profile suggested from the roster. They see the change the next time they open the Hub, and you can change anyone afterwards.</p></div>'
      + '<div class="sec">' + xs.map(function (x) { var nx = pagesOfProfile(x.sug.key, x.sug.lead, x.row, x.email); return '<p><b>' + E(x.name) + '</b> \u2192 ' + E(profileName(x.sug.key, x.sug.lead)) + (x.hand ? ' <span class="w131mut">(replaces hand-picked pages)</span>' : '') + '<br><span class="w131diff">' + diffText(nx, x.pages) + '</span></p>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + (!(S.settings || {}).auto_profile ? '<div class="sec"><label class="ck"><input type="checkbox" id="w131autoalso" checked> Also give new people their team\u2019s profile automatically</label></div>' : '') + '<div class="err" id="w131err"></div>';
    return { kicker: 'Give the suggested profile', title: xs.length === 1 ? xs[0].name : plural(xs.length, 'person', 'people'), sub: '', body: body, foot: '<a class="w131btn ghost" data-w131close="1">Cancel</a><span class="sp"></span><a class="w131btn gold" data-w131applygo="1">' + I('check') + 'Apply to ' + plural(xs.length, 'person', 'people') + '</a>' };
  }
  async function applyGo() {
    var xs = applyList(S.sheet.which); if (!xs.length || S.busy) return;
    var also = document.getElementById('w131autoalso'), btn = document.querySelector('#w131sheet [data-w131applygo]');
    S.busy = true; var done = 0, failed = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < xs.length; i++) {
      if (btn) btn.textContent = 'Applying ' + (i + 1) + ' of ' + xs.length + '\u2026';
      try { if (await saveProfile(xs[i], xs[i].sug.key, xs[i].sug.lead, 'suggested from the roster')) done++; else failed.push(xs[i].name); } catch (e) { failed.push(xs[i].name); }
    }
    try {
      var patch = { applied_at: new Date().toISOString(), applied_by: myEmail(), updated_by: myEmail(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
      if (also && also.checked) patch.auto_profile = true;
      await db.from('hub_access_settings').update(patch).eq('id', 1);
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    S.busy = false; closeSheet();
    toastS(failed.length ? 'Done for ' + done + '; could not change ' + listJoin(failed) : 'Done for ' + plural(done, 'person', 'people'));
    await afterChange(myEmail());
  }

  /* ---------- Requests: grant or decline, in one click ---------- */
  function reqPerson(q) { return personBy(q.email) || { name: q.name || nameFromEmail(q.email), email: lower(q.email), dept: '', row: S.roles[lower(q.email)] || null, pages: [], status: 'invited' }; }
  /* what Grant does for this request, in words, and the change it makes */
  function grantPlan(q) {
    var x = reqPerson(q), w = String(q.wanted || '');
    if (x.status === 'removed') return { ok: false, why: 'They were removed from the Hub; invite them again first.' };
    if (w.indexOf('tab:') === 0) {
      var t = w.slice(4);
      if (x.founder || x.role === 'admin') return { ok: false, why: 'Admins already see every page.' };
      if ((x.pages || []).indexOf(t) >= 0) return { ok: true, noop: true, say: 'They can already open ' + labelOf(t) + '; granting closes the request.' };
      var base = (x.pages || []).slice(); base.push(t);
      return { ok: true, say: 'Adds ' + labelOf(t) + ' to their pages' + (x.assigned ? ' on top of ' + profileName(x.assigned.key, x.lead) + ' (their pages become hand-picked; pick a profile later to go back)' : '') + '.', patch: { tabs: base, role: x.row ? (x.row.role === 'blocked' ? 'member' : x.row.role) : 'member', profile: x.row ? x.row.profile || null : null } };
    }
    if (w === 'role:leader') {
      var tp = x.assigned && isTeamP(x.assigned) ? x.assigned : teamProfile(x.dept);
      if (x.role === 'leader' || x.role === 'admin') return { ok: true, noop: true, say: 'They already lead; granting closes the request.' };
      if (tp) return { ok: true, say: 'Makes them a lead on ' + tp.label + ': ' + (tp.lead_note || 'Agendas, Activity, the HR centre for their team and the board pages') + '.', profile: tp.key, lead: true };
      return { ok: true, say: 'Gives them the classic leader role (every page but Admin). No team profile fits their roster team.', patch: { role: 'leader', tabs: null, profile: null } };
    }
    if (w === 'role:admin') {
      if (x.role === 'admin') return { ok: true, noop: true, say: 'They are already an admin; granting closes the request.' };
      return { ok: true, say: 'Moves them to Admin: every page, and they can change anyone\u2019s access.', profile: 'admin', lead: false };
    }
    return { ok: false, why: 'A free-text request: open their access, make the change, then mark it done.' };
  }
  function requestsHtml() {
    var open = openRequests(), done = (S.requests || []).filter(function (q) { return q.status !== 'new'; });
    var h = '<div class="w131box" style="padding:0">';
    if (!open.length) h += '<p class="w131empty">No request is waiting. People ask from My page (Request more access) or from Your access in their profile menu.</p>';
    open.forEach(function (q) {
      var x = reqPerson(q), plan = grantPlan(q);
      h += '<div class="w131rq" data-w131rq="' + q.id + '">' + avatar(x) + '<div class="tx"><b>' + E(x.name) + '</b> <span class="w131mut" style="font-size:12.5px">' + E(q.email) + '</span>'
        + '<div class="what">' + I('key') + E(reqWhat(q)) + '</div>'
        + (q.reason ? '<p>\u201c' + E(q.reason) + '\u201d</p>' : '')
        + '<small>Asked ' + E(ago(q.created_at)) + '. Today: ' + (x.assigned ? E(profileName(x.assigned.key, x.lead)) : x.hand ? 'hand-picked pages' : E((x.row && x.row.role) || q.role_now || 'member')) + '. ' + E(plan.ok ? plan.say : plan.why) + '</small></div>'
        + '<div class="ac">' + (plan.ok ? '<a class="w131btn sm" data-w131grant="' + q.id + '">' + I('check') + 'Grant</a>' : '<a class="w131btn sm" data-w131open="' + E(pkey(x)) + '">Open their access</a><a class="w131btn sm ghost" data-w131done="' + q.id + '">Mark done</a>')
        + '<a class="w131btn sm ghost" data-w131decline="' + q.id + '">Decline</a></div></div>';
    });
    h += '</div>';
    if (done.length) {
      h += '<div class="w131sec"><h2>Decided</h2><span>The last ' + Math.min(done.length, 20) + '.</span></div><div class="w131box" style="padding:0">' + done.slice(0, 20).map(function (q) {
        var x = reqPerson(q);
        return '<div class="w131rq">' + avatar(x) + '<div class="tx"><b>' + E(x.name) + '</b> <span class="w131mut" style="font-size:12.5px">' + E(reqWhat(q)) + '</span><small>' + E(q.status === 'granted' ? 'Granted' : q.status === 'declined' ? 'Declined' : q.status) + (q.decided_by ? ' by ' + E(String(q.decided_by).split('@')[0].replace(/[._]/g, ' ')) : '') + (q.decided_at ? ', ' + E(ago(q.decided_at)) : '') + '</small></div><div class="ac"><span class="w131rqst ' + E(q.status) + '">' + E(q.status) + '</span></div></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
    }
    return h;
  }
  async function decide(id, grant, manual) {
    var q = (S.requests || []).filter(function (r) { return String(r.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!q || S.busy) return;
    S.busy = true;
    try {
      if (grant && !manual) {
        var plan = grantPlan(q), x = reqPerson(q);
        if (!plan.ok) throw new Error(plan.why);
        if (!plan.noop) {
          if (plan.profile) {
            var ok = await saveProfile(x.email ? x : { email: lower(q.email), name: q.name, row: S.roles[lower(q.email)] || null }, plan.profile, plan.lead, 'access request #' + q.id);
            if (!ok) throw new Error('could not change the profile');
          } else if (plan.patch) {
            var r = await db.from('hub_roles').upsert(Object.assign({ email: lower(q.email), updated_by: actor(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }, plan.patch), { onConflict: 'email' });
            if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
            logChange('hub_role', lower(q.email), plan.patch.tabs ? 'tabs' : 'role', null, q.wanted + ' (access request #' + q.id + ')');
          }
        }
      }
      var up = await db.from('wa_access_requests').update({ status: grant ? 'granted' : 'declined', decided_by: myEmail(), decided_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', q.id);
      if (up && up.error) throw up.error;
      toastS(grant ? (manual ? 'Marked done' : 'Granted: ' + first(q.name || q.email) + ' sees it the next time they open the Hub') : 'Declined');
      S.busy = false;
      await afterChange(lower(q.email));
    } catch (e) { S.busy = false; toastS('Could not do it: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 100)); }
  }

  /* ---------- Invite: one person, a list, or everyone not invited yet ---------- */
  function openInvite(team) { S.sheet = { kind: 'invite', f: { name: '', email: '', dept: team || '', title: '' }, pick: null, lead: false, res: null }; renderSheet(); }
  function invPick() {
    var sh = S.sheet, tp = sh.f.dept ? teamProfile(sh.f.dept) : null;
    var key = sh.pick || (sh.f.dept === 'board' && S.byKey.board ? 'board' : (tp ? tp.key : null));
    var lead = key && isTeamP(S.byKey[key]) ? !!sh.lead : false;
    return { key: key, lead: lead };
  }
  function readInviteForm() {
    var f = S.sheet && S.sheet.f; if (!f) return;
    ['name', 'email', 'title'].forEach(function (k) { var el = document.getElementById('w131f_' + k); if (el) f[k] = el.value; });
    var d = document.getElementById('w131f_dept'); if (d) f.dept = d.value;
  }
  function inviteSheet() {
    var sh = S.sheet, f = sh.f, pk = invPick();
    if (sh.res) return { kicker: 'Invited', title: f.name, sub: f.email, body: '<div class="sec res">' + sh.res + '</div>', foot: '<a class="w131btn ghost" data-w131invnext="1">' + I('plus') + 'Invite someone else</a><span class="sp"></span><a class="w131btn" data-w131open="' + E(lower(f.email)) + '">Open their sheet</a>' };
    var body = '<div class="sec"><div class="two"><label class="f">Full name<input type="text" id="w131f_name" placeholder="First Last" value="' + E(f.name) + '" autocomplete="off"></label>'
      + '<label class="f">Email<input type="email" id="w131f_email" placeholder="name@welfareavenue.com" value="' + E(f.email) + '" autocomplete="off"></label></div>'
      + '<div class="two"><label class="f">Team<select id="w131f_dept"><option value="">Pick a team</option>' + TEAMS.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '"' + (f.dept === d ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>'
      + '<label class="f">Title<input type="text" id="w131f_title" placeholder="e.g. Content manager" value="' + E(f.title) + '"></label></div></div>'
      + '<div class="sec"><h4>Access profile</h4><p style="font-size:13px">' + (f.dept ? 'Their team\u2019s profile is picked for you. Change it if they need something else.' : 'Pick their team first; their team\u2019s profile is picked for you.') + '</p>' + pickerHtml(pk.key, pk.lead, f.dept, false)
      + (pk.key ? '<div style="margin-top:12px"><h4>What they get \u00b7 ' + pagesOfProfile(pk.key, pk.lead, null, f.email || 'x@welfareavenue.com').length + ' pages</h4>' + pagesHtml(pagesOfProfile(pk.key, pk.lead, null, f.email || 'x@welfareavenue.com'), null) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="sec"><label class="ck"><input type="checkbox" id="w131send" checked> Email them a sign-in invitation now (from hub@welfareavenue.com). They can also sign in with Google using this email straight away.</label></div><div class="err" id="w131err"></div>';
    return { kicker: 'People and access', title: 'Invite someone', sub: 'They join the roster, get the profile and a welcome.', body: body, foot: '<a class="w131btn ghost" data-w131list="1">' + I('list') + 'Invite a list instead</a><span class="sp"></span><a class="w131btn' + (pk.key ? '' : ' off') + '" data-w131invgo="1">' + I('mail') + 'Invite' + (pk.key ? ' as ' + E(profileName(pk.key, pk.lead)) : '') + '</a>' };
  }
  async function inviteGo() {
    if (S.busy) return; readInviteForm();
    var f = S.sheet.f, pk = invPick();
    var name = String(f.name || '').trim(), email = lower(f.email);
    if (!name) { sheetErr('Type their name.'); return; }
    if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) { sheetErr('Type a valid email.'); return; }
    if (!f.dept) { sheetErr('Pick their team.'); return; }
    if (!pk.key) { sheetErr('Pick a profile.'); return; }
    var send = !!(document.getElementById('w131send') || {}).checked;
    S.busy = true; var btn = document.querySelector('#w131sheet [data-w131invgo]'); if (btn) { btn.classList.add('off'); btn.textContent = 'Inviting\u2026'; }
    try {
      var res = await doInvite({ name: name, email: email, title: String(f.title || '').trim(), dept: f.dept }, pk.key, pk.lead, send);
      f.name = name; f.email = email;
      S.sheet.res = '<b>' + E(name) + '</b> is on the Hub as <b>' + E(profileName(pk.key, pk.lead)) + '</b>' + (res.existed ? ' (they were already on the roster)' : '') + '.<br>' + resultHtml(res, name);
      toastS(res.email_sent ? 'Invitation emailed to ' + first(name) : first(name) + ' can sign in now; share the message or a link');
      S.busy = false; await afterChange(email);
    } catch (e) { S.busy = false; if (btn) { btn.classList.remove('off'); btn.textContent = 'Invite'; } sheetErr('Could not invite: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 140)); }
  }

  /* a list: paste it, check it, invite them all */
  function parseList(text) {
    var out = [], seen = {};
    String(text || '').split(/\n|;/).forEach(function (line) {
      line = line.trim(); if (!line) return;
      var em = '', name = '', m = /<([^>]+@[^>]+)>/.exec(line);
      if (m) { em = lower(m[1]); name = line.replace(m[0], '').replace(/[",]/g, ' ').trim(); }
      else {
        var parts = line.split(/[,\t]/).map(function (s) { return s.trim(); });
        var hit = parts.filter(function (s) { return s.indexOf('@') > 0; })[0];
        if (hit) { em = lower(hit.split(/\s+/).filter(function (s) { return s.indexOf('@') > 0; })[0]); name = parts.filter(function (s) { return s !== hit; }).join(' ').trim(); }
      }
      if (!em || !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(em) || seen[em]) return;
      seen[em] = 1;
      out.push({ email: em, name: name || nameFromEmail(em) });
    });
    return out;
  }
  function openList() { S.sheet = { kind: 'list', step: 'paste', text: '', dept: '', rows: [], results: null }; renderSheet(); }
  function openInviteAll() {
    var ni = people().filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'notinvited' && x.roster; });
    if (!ni.length) { toastS('Everyone on the roster is invited'); return; }
    S.sheet = { kind: 'list', step: 'review', text: '', dept: '', results: null, fromRoster: true, rows: ni.map(function (x) { return { email: x.email, name: x.name, dept: x.dept, title: x.title, key: x.sug ? x.sug.key : (teamProfile(x.dept) || {}).key || '', lead: false }; }) };
    renderSheet();
  }
  function listSheet() {
    var sh = S.sheet;
    if (sh.results) {
      var okN = sh.results.filter(function (r) { return r.ok; }).length;
      return { kicker: 'Invited', title: okN + ' of ' + sh.results.length + ' invited', sub: '', body: '<div class="sec">' + sh.results.map(function (r) { return '<p><b>' + E(r.name || r.email) + '</b> ' + (r.ok ? '<span style="color:' + TEAL + ';font-weight:700">invited</span>' + (r.email_sent ? ', email sent' : ', no email') + (r.profile ? ', ' + E(r.profile) : '') : '<span style="color:' + FLAG + ';font-weight:700">not invited</span>: ' + E(r.error || 'failed')) + '</p>'; }).join('') + '</div>', foot: '<span class="sp"></span><a class="w131btn" data-w131close="1">Done</a>' };
    }
    if (sh.step === 'paste') {
      return { kicker: 'People and access', title: 'Invite a list', sub: 'One person per line: "Name, email", "Name <email>" or just the email.', body: '<div class="sec"><label class="f">The list<textarea id="w131paste" placeholder="Amina Diallo, amina.diallo@welfareavenue.com&#10;Jordan Lee <jordan.lee@welfareavenue.com>&#10;sam.ortiz@welfareavenue.com">' + E(sh.text) + '</textarea></label>'
        + '<label class="f">Their team<select id="w131ldept"><option value="">Pick a team</option>' + TEAMS.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '"' + (sh.dept === d ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label><p class="w131mut" style="font-size:12.5px">You can change the team and profile for each person on the next step.</p></div><div class="err" id="w131err"></div>',
        foot: '<a class="w131btn ghost" data-w131new="1">Just one person</a><span class="sp"></span><a class="w131btn" data-w131lreview="1">' + I('arrow') + 'Check the list</a>' };
    }
    var body = '<div class="sec"><p style="font-size:13px">' + (sh.fromRoster ? 'Everyone on the roster who is not invited yet, with the profile of their team.' : 'Check each person\u2019s team and profile.') + '</p>' + sh.rows.map(function (r, i) {
      var opts = '<option value="">No profile</option>' + S.profiles.map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + E(p.key) + '"' + (r.key === p.key && !r.lead ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(p.label) + '</option>' + (isTeamP(p) ? '<option value="' + E(p.key) + ':lead"' + (r.key === p.key && r.lead ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(p.label) + ' (lead)</option>' : ''); }).join('');
      return '<div class="w131er" style="flex-wrap:wrap"><span class="lb">' + E(r.name) + '<small>' + E(r.email) + '</small></span>'
        + '<select data-w131lrow="' + i + '|dept" style="font-size:12.5px;padding:6px 8px;border-radius:9px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.18);max-width:100%">' + '<option value="">Team</option>' + TEAMS.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '"' + (r.dept === d ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
        + '<select data-w131lrow="' + i + '|key" style="font-size:12.5px;padding:6px 8px;border-radius:9px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.18);max-width:100%">' + opts + '</select></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div><div class="sec"><label class="ck"><input type="checkbox" id="w131send" checked> Email each of them a sign-in invitation now</label></div><div class="err" id="w131err"></div>';
    var ready = sh.rows.filter(function (r) { return r.dept && r.key; }).length;
    return { kicker: 'People and access', title: plural(sh.rows.length, 'person to invite', 'people to invite'), sub: '', body: body, foot: (sh.fromRoster ? '' : '<a class="w131btn ghost" data-w131lback="1">Back</a>') + '<span class="sp"></span><a class="w131btn' + (ready ? '' : ' off') + '" data-w131lgo="1">' + I('mail') + 'Invite ' + (ready === sh.rows.length ? plural(ready, 'person', 'people') : ready + ' of ' + sh.rows.length) + '</a>' };
  }
  function listReview() {
    var ta = document.getElementById('w131paste'), dsel = document.getElementById('w131ldept');
    var sh = S.sheet; sh.text = ta ? ta.value : sh.text; sh.dept = dsel ? dsel.value : sh.dept;
    var ppl = parseList(sh.text);
    if (!ppl.length) { sheetErr('No emails found in the list.'); return; }
    var tp = sh.dept ? teamProfile(sh.dept) : null;
    sh.rows = ppl.map(function (p) {
      var x = personBy(p.email), d = (x && x.dept) || sh.dept, t = d ? teamProfile(d) : tp;
      return { email: p.email, name: (x && x.name) || p.name, dept: d, title: x ? x.title : '', key: d === 'board' && S.byKey.board ? 'board' : (t ? t.key : ''), lead: false };
    });
    sh.step = 'review'; renderSheet();
  }
  async function listGo() {
    var sh = S.sheet; if (S.busy) return;
    var rows = sh.rows.filter(function (r) { return r.dept && r.key; }); if (!rows.length) return;
    var send = !!(document.getElementById('w131send') || {}).checked, btn = document.querySelector('#w131sheet [data-w131lgo]');
    S.busy = true; if (btn) { btn.classList.add('off'); btn.textContent = 'Inviting ' + rows.length + '\u2026'; }
    var results = [];
    try {
      var res = await invoke({ action: 'bulk_invite', people: rows.map(function (r) { return { name: r.name, email: r.email, department: r.dept, role_title: r.title || '', hub_role: roleFor(r.key, r.lead), send_email: send }; }) });
      var list = Array.isArray(res.results) ? res.results : [];
      for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
        var r = rows[i], out = list.filter(function (o) { return lower(o.email) === r.email; })[0] || { ok: false, email: r.email, error: 'no result' };
        if (out.ok) {
          try {
            var u = await db.from('hub_roles').upsert({ email: r.email, role: roleFor(r.key, r.lead), profile: r.key, tabs: null, updated_by: actor(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }, { onConflict: 'email' });
            if (u && u.error) throw u.error;
            logChange('hub_role', r.email, 'profile', null, profileName(r.key, r.lead) + ' (invited in a list)');
            out.profile = profileName(r.key, r.lead);
          } catch (e) { out.profile = 'profile not saved'; }
        }
        out.name = r.name; results.push(out);
      }
    } catch (e) { S.busy = false; if (btn) { btn.classList.remove('off'); btn.textContent = 'Invite'; } sheetErr('Could not invite them: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 140)); return; }
    S.busy = false; sh.results = results;
    toastS(results.filter(function (r) { return r.ok; }).length + ' invited');
    await afterChange(myEmail());
  }

  /* ---------- the sheet ---------- */
  var lastFocus = null;
  function sheetEl() {
    var el = document.getElementById('w131sheet');
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w131sheet'; document.body.appendChild(el); }
    return el;
  }
  function sheetView() {
    var sh = S.sheet; if (!sh) return null;
    if (sh.kind === 'person') { var x = personBy(sh.key); return x ? personSheet(x) : null; }
    if (sh.kind === 'profile') return profileSheet();
    if (sh.kind === 'invite') return inviteSheet();
    if (sh.kind === 'list') return listSheet();
    if (sh.kind === 'apply') return applySheet();
    if (sh.kind === 'decide') return decideSheet();
    return null;
  }
  function renderSheet() {
    var el = sheetEl(), wasOn = el.classList.contains('on');
    if (wasOn && S.sheet && S.sheet.kind === 'invite' && !S.sheet.res) readInviteForm();
    var v = sheetView(); if (!v) { closeSheet(); return; }
