    var head = '<div style="min-width:0"><div class="kk">' + E(v.kicker) + '</div><h2>' + E(v.title) + '</h2>' + (v.sub ? '<div class="sub">' + E(v.sub) + '</div>' : '') + '</div><span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="xb" data-w131close="1" aria-label="Close">' + I('x') + '</button>';
    if (!wasOn || !el.querySelector('.pane')) {
      lastFocus = document.activeElement;
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w131close="1"></div><div class="pane" role="dialog" aria-modal="true"><div class="ph"></div><div class="bd"></div><div class="ft"></div></div>';
    }
    var pane = el.querySelector('.pane'), bd = el.querySelector('.bd'), st = bd.scrollTop;
    pane.setAttribute('aria-label', v.title);
    el.querySelector('.ph').innerHTML = head; bd.innerHTML = v.body; el.querySelector('.ft').innerHTML = v.foot;
    if (wasOn) bd.scrollTop = st;
    el.classList.add('on');
    if (!wasOn) setTimeout(function () { var f = el.querySelector('.bd input:not([type=checkbox]), .xb'); if (f) { try { f.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }, 40);
  }
  function closeSheet() {
    var el = document.getElementById('w131sheet'); if (el) { el.classList.remove('on'); el.innerHTML = ''; }
    S.sheet = null;
    if (lastFocus && lastFocus.focus && document.body.contains(lastFocus)) { try { lastFocus.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    lastFocus = null;
  }

  /* ---------- the card and the section ---------- */
  var HREF = { people: '#adminpanel/people', profiles: '#adminpanel/access', requests: '#adminpanel/people/requests' };
  function tabFromHash() {
    var p = String(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '').split('/');
    if (p[0] !== 'adminpanel') return null;
    if (p[1] === 'access') return 'profiles';
    if (p[1] === 'people') return p[2] === 'requests' ? 'requests' : p[2] === 'profiles' ? 'profiles' : 'people';
    return null;
  }
  /* the sections stay as wave118 and wave127 made them (the classic view needs them); the new card stands in for both */
  var MAST_D = 'Everyone who can sign in: their access profile, their invitations and the access requests, in one place.';
  function sectionFix() {
    try {
      var W = window.__wa118;
      if (W && Array.isArray(W.ADMIN)) W.ADMIN.forEach(function (a) { if (a[0] === 'people') a[3] = MAST_D; });
      var sub = document.querySelector('nav.wa-nav .w118sub'); if (sub) sub.classList.toggle('w131acc', /^#adminpanel\/access/.test(location.hash || ''));
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function mastFix() {
    if (document.body.classList.contains('w131classic')) return;
    var m = document.querySelector('#adminpanel > .w118mast'); if (!m || !tabFromHash()) return;
    var h = m.querySelector('h1'), p = m.querySelector('p');
    if (h && h.textContent !== 'People and access') h.textContent = 'People and access';
    if (p && p.textContent !== MAST_D) p.textContent = MAST_D;
  }
  function cardEl() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('adminpanel'); if (!panel || !amAdmin()) return null;
    var c = document.getElementById('w131card');
    if (!c) { c = document.createElement('div'); c.id = 'w131card'; c.className = 'w131wrap'; c.innerHTML = '<div id="w131"></div>'; }
    var sw = panel.querySelector(':scope > .w118adm');
    if (sw) { if (sw.nextElementSibling !== c) sw.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', c); }
    else if (c.parentNode !== panel) panel.insertBefore(c, panel.firstChild);
    return c;
  }
  function paint() {
    var c = cardEl(); if (!c) return;
    var host = document.getElementById('w131'); if (!host) return;
    var t = tabFromHash(); if (t) S.tab = t;
    var html;
    if (!S.loaded) html = '<div class="w131box"><p class="w131empty">Loading people and access\u2026</p></div>';
    else {
      var rq = openRequests().length, n = people().filter(function (x) { return x.status !== 'removed'; }).length;
      html = '<div class="w131top"><nav class="w131tabs" aria-label="People and access">'
        + [['people', 'People', n], ['profiles', 'Profiles', S.profiles.length], ['requests', 'Requests', rq]].map(function (x) { return '<a href="' + HREF[x[0]] + '" data-w131tab="' + x[0] + '"' + (S.tab === x[0] ? ' class="on" aria-current="page"' : '') + '>' + x[1] + ' <b' + (x[0] === 'requests' && rq ? ' class="hot"' : '') + '>' + x[2] + '</b></a>'; }).join('')
        + '</nav><span class="sp"></span><a class="w131btn ghost sm" data-w131list="1">' + I('list') + 'Invite a list</a><a class="w131btn" data-w131new="1">' + I('plus') + 'Invite someone</a></div>'
        + (S.tab === 'profiles' ? profilesHtml() : S.tab === 'requests' ? requestsHtml() : peopleHtml());
    }
    mastFix();
    if (host.__h === html) return;
    var q = document.activeElement && document.activeElement.id === 'w131q' ? document.activeElement : null, pos = q ? q.selectionStart : 0;
    host.innerHTML = html; host.__h = html;
    if (q) { var q2 = document.getElementById('w131q'); if (q2) { try { q2.focus({ preventScroll: true }); q2.setSelectionRange(pos, pos); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  }
  var showing = null;
  async function show() {
    if (!amAdmin() || !tabFromHash()) return;
    sectionFix(); cardEl(); paint();
    if (showing) return showing;
    showing = (async function () { await load(true); paint(); if (S.sheet) renderSheet(); })();
    try { await showing; } finally { showing = null; }
    openFromHash();
  }
  function openFromHash() {
    var p = String(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '').split('/');

/* =====================================================================
   WA v132 the bank (26/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "store tasks that can't be done in the next three to six months, but kept in a bank to
   resurface later on, like projects as well", and "I also want to give you the option" (the AI).

   - The bank (#bank, under Strategy): everything waiting, by the month it comes back, with why it
     waits and who put it there; what came back in the last two weeks; bring something back now,
     give it a new date, or put something new straight in.
   - Move to the bank: in every task drawer (a task goes with its open subtasks), and on a project
     page (the project, its sub-projects and every open task in them) for the project's lead or a
     leader. An Undo follows every move.
   - A team page shows what the team has in the bank, and a task that came back carries
     "back from the bank" on its board for two weeks.
   - The AI: process-transcript v13 banks what a meeting puts off by three months or more, and Ask
     the Hub can propose moving a task to the bank (bank_task), confirmed on its card.
   Data: wa132_bank (banked_at and friends on dept_tasks and projects; a restrictive read policy so
   no board, list, count or alert ever sees a banked row; bank_list(), bank_task(), bank_add_task(),
   bank_project(), bank_unbank(), bank_redate(), bank_undo(); the 6:05 daily job bank_resurface()).
   ===================================================================== */

  function mk(y, m, d) { var x = new Date(0); x.setFullYear(Number(y), Number(m), Number(d)); x.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0); return x; }
  function ymd(d) { return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate()); }

  /* today in Toronto, as the database counts it */
  function today() {
    try { var s = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto' }); var d = parseDay(s); if (d) return d; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  }

  function addMonths(d, n) {
    var y = d.getFullYear(), m = d.getMonth() + n, day = d.getDate();
    y += Math.floor(m / 12); m = ((m % 12) + 12) % 12;
    var last = mk(y, m + 1, 0).getDate();
    return mk(y, m, Math.min(day, last));
  }
  function daysBetween(a, b) { return Math.round((b.getTime() - a.getTime()) / 86400000); }

  function inDays(d) {
    var n = daysBetween(today(), d);
    if (n <= 0) return 'today';
    if (n === 1) return 'tomorrow';
    if (n < 60) return 'in ' + n + ' days';
    var m = Math.round(n / 30.4);
    return 'in about ' + m + ' months';
  }
  function ago(iso) {
    var d = dayOfIso(iso); if (!d) return '';

    if (n <= 0) return 'today'; if (n === 1) return 'yesterday';
    if (n < 45) return n + ' days ago';

  /* ---------- the data: bank_list() ---------- */
  function arr(x) { return Array.isArray(x) ? x : []; }
  function rpc(fn, args) {
    return Promise.resolve(db.rpc(fn, args || {})).then(function (r) { if (r && r.error) throw r.error; return r ? r.data : null; });
  }
  function norm(d) {
    d = d || {};
    return { tasks: arr(d.tasks), projects: arr(d.projects), ideas: arr(d.ideas), back: arr(d.back), today: d.today || ymd(today()), can: d.can_bank !== false, leader: !!d.leader };
  }
  function load(force) {
    if (!staffOk()) { B.data = norm(null); return Promise.resolve(B.data); }
    if (B.loading) return B.loading;
    if (!force && B.data && Date.now() - B.at < 30000) return Promise.resolve(B.data);
    B.loading = rpc('bank_list').then(function (d) {
      B.data = norm(d); B.at = Date.now(); B.err = ''; B.loading = null; B.ver++; return B.data;
    }, function (e) {
      B.loading = null; B.err = errText(e); if (!B.data) B.data = norm(null); B.ver++; return B.data;
    });
    return B.loading;
  }
  /* every task and project in the bank, as one list */
  function items() {
    var d = B.data || norm(null), out = [];
    d.projects.forEach(function (p) {
      out.push({ kind: 'project', id: p.id, title: p.name || 'A project', dept: p.department, until: p.bank_until, note: p.bank_note, by: p.banked_by, at: p.banked_at,
        parent: p.parent, desc: p.description, tasks: Number(p.tasks || 0), subs: Number(p.subprojects || 0), titles: arr(p.task_titles), lead: p.lead !== false, leads: arr(p.leads) });
    });
    d.tasks.forEach(function (t) {
      out.push({ kind: 'task', id: t.id, title: t.title || 'A task', dept: t.department, until: t.bank_until, note: t.bank_note, by: t.banked_by, at: t.banked_at,
        owner: t.owner, project: t.project, parent: t.parent, desc: t.description, subs: Number(t.subtasks || 0), due: t.bank_due, priority: !!t.priority, lead: true });
    });
    /* wave136: content ideas the Content calendar put here (Marketing) */
    d.ideas.forEach(function (c) {
      out.push({ kind: 'idea', id: c.id, title: c.title || 'A content idea', dept: c.department || 'marketing', until: c.bank_until, note: c.bank_note, by: c.banked_by, at: c.banked_at,
        desc: c.idea, source: c.source, sourceId: c.source_transcript_id, sourceDate: c.source_date, forDate: c.idea_for, owner: c.suggested_by, campaign: c.campaign, lead: true });
    });
    out.sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.until || '').localeCompare(String(b.until || '')) || String(a.title).localeCompare(String(b.title)); });
    return out;
  }
  function itemBy(kind, id) { return items().filter(function (x) { return x.kind === kind && String(x.id) === String(id); })[0] || null; }
  function backItems() { return arr((B.data || {}).back); }
  function teamCounts(d) {
    var list = items().filter(function (x) { return x.dept === d; });
    var back = backItems().filter(function (x) { return x.department === d; });
    return { list: list, back: back, tasks: list.filter(function (x) { return x.kind === 'task'; }).length, projects: list.filter(function (x) { return x.kind === 'project'; }).length };
  }
  function errText(e) {
    var m = String((e && (e.message || e.details || e.hint)) || e || '').replace(/^.*?ERROR:\s*/, '');
    if (/staff only/i.test(m)) return 'Your access can look at the bank but not move work in or out of it.';
    if (/not allowed/i.test(m)) return 'That one is not yours to move.';
    if (/after today/i.test(m)) return 'Pick a day after today.';
    if (/three years/i.test(m)) return 'Pick a day in the next three years.';
    if (/not open|not active/i.test(m)) return 'It is already closed or already in the bank. Refresh the page to see where it is.';
    if (/general list/i.test(m)) return 'A team\u2019s general list stays on the board. Move its tasks one by one instead.';
    if (/lead or a leader/i.test(m)) return 'Only the project\u2019s lead or a team leader can do that for a whole project.';
    if (/too late to undo/i.test(m)) return 'Too late to undo. Bring it back from the bank instead.';
    if (/not in the bank/i.test(m)) return 'It is not in the bank any more. Refresh to see where it is.';
    if (/fetch|network|Failed to/i.test(m)) return 'Could not reach the Hub. Nothing changed. Try again.';
    return m || 'Something went wrong. Nothing changed.';
  }

  /* ---------- keeping the rest of the page in step ---------- */
  function treeIds(id) {
    var ids = [String(id)], all = tasks(), grew = true;
    while (grew) {
      grew = false;
      all.forEach(function (t) { if (t.parent_task_id != null && ids.indexOf(String(t.parent_task_id)) >= 0 && ids.indexOf(String(t.id)) < 0 && !t.done) { ids.push(String(t.id)); grew = true; } });
    }
    return ids;
  }
  function dropTasks(ids) {
    var removed = [];
    try {
      removed = DB_TASKS.filter(function (t) { return ids.indexOf(String(t.id)) >= 0; });
      DB_TASKS = DB_TASKS.filter(function (t) { return ids.indexOf(String(t.id)) < 0; });
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return removed;
  }
  function repaintBoards(dept) {
    try { if (window.w116Board && window.w116Board.refresh) window.w116Board.refresh(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    setTimeout(function () {
      try { if (dept && typeof renderSection === 'function') renderSection(dept); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { if (typeof renderNeedsMe === 'function') renderNeedsMe(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { if (window.w116Route && document.getElementById('team') && document.getElementById('team').classList.contains('active')) window.w116Route(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    }, 40);
  }
  async function refreshProjects() {
    try { if (window.w116LoadProjects) { var rows = await window.w116LoadProjects(true); if (window.w116ProjectsChanged) window.w116ProjectsChanged(rows); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (typeof wa27Load === 'function') await wa27Load(true); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  async function reloadTasks() {
    try { if (typeof loadAll === 'function') await loadAll(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (typeof renderAllSections === 'function') renderAllSections(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    repaintBoards(null);
  }
  function afterChange() { B.at = 0; return load(true).then(function () { paintAll(); }); }

  /* ---------- the moves ---------- */
  async function bankTask(t, until, note) {
    var ids = treeIds(t.id);
    var res = await rpc('bank_task', { p_id: Number(t.id), p_until: until, p_note: note || null });
    try { if (window.w116Board && window.w116Board.closeDrawer) window.w116Board.closeDrawer(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var removed = dropTasks(ids);
    repaintBoards(t.department);
    var n = Number((res && res.banked) || 1);
    snack('In the bank until ' + fmtDay(parseDay(until), true) + (n > 1 ? ', with ' + plural(n - 1, 'subtask', 'subtasks') : '') + '. It comes back on its own.',
      [['Undo', function () { return undoMove('task', t.id, removed, t.department); }], ['Open the bank', function () { go('#bank'); }]]);
    afterChange();
    return res;
  }
  async function bankProject(p, until, note) {
    var res = await rpc('bank_project', { p_id: Number(p.id), p_until: until, p_note: note || null });
    await refreshProjects(); await reloadTasks();
    go('#bank');
    var n = Number((res && res.tasks) || 0);
    snack(p.name + ' is in the bank until ' + fmtDay(parseDay(until), true) + ' with ' + plural(n, 'open task', 'open tasks') + '.', [['Undo', function () { return undoMove('project', p.id, null, p.department); }]]);
    afterChange();
    return res;
  }
  async function addToBank(f) {
    var res = await rpc('bank_add_task', { p_department: f.dept, p_title: f.title, p_until: f.until, p_note: f.note || null, p_project_id: f.project ? Number(f.project) : null, p_owner: f.owner || null });
    snack('\u201c' + clip(f.title, 60) + '\u201d is in the bank until ' + fmtDay(parseDay(f.until), true) + '.', []);
    afterChange();
    return res;
  }
  async function undoMove(kind, id, removed, dept) {
    try {
      await rpc('bank_undo', { p_kind: kind, p_id: Number(id) });
      if (kind === 'task' && removed && removed.length) {
        var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').select('*').in('id', removed.map(function (x) { return Number(x.id); }));
        var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
        try { var have = {}; DB_TASKS.forEach(function (t) { have[String(t.id)] = 1; }); rows.forEach(function (row) { if (!have[String(row.id)]) DB_TASKS.push(row); }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        repaintBoards(dept);
      } else { await refreshProjects(); await reloadTasks(); }
      toastS('Back where it was');
      hideSnack();
      afterChange();
    } catch (e) { toastS(errText(e)); }
  }
  async function bringBack(kind, id) {
    var it = itemBy(kind, id);
    var res = await rpc('bank_unbank', { p_kind: kind, p_id: Number(id), p_why: 'brought back early' });
    if (kind === 'project') await refreshProjects();
    if (kind === 'idea') { try { if (window.w121Stale) window.w121Stale(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } toastS((it ? '\u201c' + clip(it.title, 50) + '\u201d' : 'It') + ' is back in the Content calendar\u2019s Ideas'); afterChange(); return res; }
    await reloadTasks();
    toastS((it ? '\u201c' + clip(it.title, 50) + '\u201d' : 'It') + ' is back on the ' + teamShort(it ? it.dept : '') + ' board');
    afterChange();
    return res;
  }
  async function redate(kind, id, until) {
    var res = await rpc('bank_redate', { p_kind: kind, p_id: Number(id), p_until: until });
    toastS('Comes back on ' + fmtDay(parseDay(until), true));
    afterChange();
    return res;
  }

  /* ---------- the undo bar ---------- */
  var SNACK = { acts: [] };
  function snackEl() { var el = document.getElementById('w132snack'); if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w132snack'; el.setAttribute('role', 'status'); el.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite'); document.body.appendChild(el); } return el; }
  function snack(text, acts) {
    var el = snackEl(); SNACK.acts = acts || [];
    el.innerHTML = '<span class="t">' + E(text) + '</span>' + SNACK.acts.map(function (a, i) { return '<button type="button" data-w132sn="' + i + '">' + E(a[0]) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '<button type="button" class="x" data-w132sn="x" aria-label="Dismiss">' + I('x') + '</button>';
    el.classList.add('on');
    clearTimeout(B.snackT); B.snackT = setTimeout(hideSnack, 12000);
  }
  function hideSnack() { var el = document.getElementById('w132snack'); if (el) el.classList.remove('on'); clearTimeout(B.snackT); }
  function go(h) { closeSheet(); if (location.hash === h) { try { applyHash(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } else location.hash = h; }

  /* ---------- the page: #bank ---------- */
  var BLURB = 'Work that can wait three months or more. It leaves every board, list and count, and comes back on its own on its date.';
  function mast() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('bank'); if (!sec) return;
    var m = sec.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
    if (!m) { m = document.createElement('div'); m.className = 'w118mast'; m.setAttribute('data-w132', 'bank'); sec.insertBefore(m, sec.firstChild); }
    var html = '<div class="crumb">Other Tools</div><h1>The Task Bank</h1><p>' + E(BLURB) + '</p>';
    if (m.getAttribute('data-h') !== html) { m.innerHTML = html; m.setAttribute('data-h', html); }
  }
  function host() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('bank'); if (!sec) return null;
    var h = document.getElementById('w132');
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = 'w132'; sec.appendChild(h); }
    return h;
  }
  function statsHtml(all) {
    var t = today(), soon = all.filter(function (x) { var d = parseDay(x.until); return d && daysBetween(t, d) <= 30; }).length;
    var nt = all.filter(function (x) { return x.kind === 'task'; }).length, ni = all.filter(function (x) { return x.kind === 'idea'; }).length, np = all.length - nt - ni, back = backItems().length;
    var add = canWork() && (B.data ? B.data.can : true);
    return '<div class="w132top">'
      + '<div class="w132st gold"><b>' + all.length + '</b><span>in the bank' + (all.length ? ' (' + [nt ? plural(nt, 'task', 'tasks') : '', np ? plural(np, 'project', 'projects') : '', ni ? plural(ni, 'content idea', 'content ideas') : ''].filter(Boolean).join(', ') + ')' : '') + '</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w132st"><b>' + soon + '</b><span>coming back in the next 30 days</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w132st"><b>' + back + '</b><span>back in the last two weeks</span></div>'
      + (add ? '<button type="button" class="w132add" data-w132new="1">' + I('plus') + 'Put something in the bank</button>' : '')
      + '</div>';
  }
  function howHtml() {
    return '<details class="w132how"' + (B.howOpen ? ' open' : '') + '><summary>' + I('vault') + 'How the bank works</summary>'
      + '<p>When a piece of work cannot start for three months or more, move it here instead of leaving it on a board: open the task and press <b>Move to the bank</b> (its open subtasks go with it), or bank a whole project from its page (the project, its sub-projects and every open task in them). Pick when it should come back and say why it waits.</p>'
      + '<p>Nothing in the bank shows on boards, lists, counts, alerts, digests or agendas. On the morning of its date it goes back to its team\u2019s board with its owner, subtasks and project, keeps its due date if that is still ahead, and carries <b>back from the bank</b> for two weeks.</p>'
      + '<p>The Hub does the same on its own when a meeting puts something off by three months or more, and Ask the Hub can move a task here when you ask it to. You can bring anything back early, or give it a new date.</p>'
      + '<p>Content ideas wait here too: an idea from a marketing meeting for three months or more away, or one someone moved from the Content calendar. It comes back to the calendar\u2019s Ideas on its date (six weeks before the date it is for, when it came from a meeting).</p></details>';
  }
  function backHtml(team) {
    var rows = backItems().filter(function (x) { return !team || x.department === team; });
    if (!rows.length) return '';
    return '<div class="w132card w132back" id="w132back"><h3>Back from the bank <span>the last two weeks, now on their boards</span></h3>'
      + rows.slice(0, 12).map(function (x) {
        var d = dayOfIso(x.at);
        return '<div class="w132brow"><span class="ic">' + I(x.kind === 'project' ? 'box' : 'back') + '</span><div style="min-width:0"><div class="t">' + E(x.title) + '</div><div class="m">' + E(teamShort(x.department)) + (x.kind === 'project' ? ' \u00b7 project' : x.kind === 'idea' ? ' \u00b7 content idea, in the calendar\u2019s Ideas' : '') + (x.owner ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(x.owner) : '') + ' \u00b7 back ' + E(d ? fmtShort(d) : '') + '</div></div>'
          + '<button type="button" class="w132lnk" data-w132open="' + E(x.kind) + ':' + E(x.id) + '" data-d="' + E(x.department) + '">Open</button></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function barHtml(all) {
    var counts = {}; all.forEach(function (x) { counts[x.dept] = (counts[x.dept] || 0) + 1; });
    var chips = '<button type="button" class="w132chip' + (!B.team ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w132team="">All teams <em>' + all.length + '</em></button>'
      + teams().filter(function (d) { return counts[d] || d === B.team; }).map(function (d) {
        return '<button type="button" class="w132chip' + (B.team === d ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w132team="' + E(d) + '"><i style="background:' + E(teamColor(d)) + '"></i>' + E(teamShort(d)) + ' <em>' + (counts[d] || 0) + '</em></button>';
      }).join('');
    return '<div class="w132bar"><div class="w132chips" role="group" aria-label="Teams">' + chips + '</div>'
      + '<label class="w132q">' + I('search') + '<input type="search" id="w132q" placeholder="Search the Task Bank" value="' + E(B.q) + '" aria-label="Search the Task Bank"></label></div>';
  }
  function whyHtml(x) {
    return x.note ? '<div class="why">' + I('note') + '<span>' + E(x.note) + '</span></div>' : '<div class="why none">No reason given.</div>';
  }
  function itemHtml(x) {
    var d = parseDay(x.until), key = x.kind + ':' + x.id, open = !!B.open[key];
    var meta = [];
    meta.push('<span><i class="dot" style="background:' + E(teamColor(x.dept)) + '"></i>' + E(teamShort(x.dept)) + '</span>');
    if (x.kind === 'task') {
      if (x.project) meta.push('<span>' + E(x.project) + '</span>');
      if (x.parent) meta.push('<span>subtask of ' + E(clip(x.parent, 48)) + '</span>');
      if (x.owner) meta.push('<span>' + E(x.owner) + '</span>');
      if (x.subs) meta.push('<span>with ' + plural(x.subs, 'subtask', 'subtasks') + '</span>');
    } else if (x.kind === 'idea') {
      meta.push('<span>content idea</span>');
      if (x.forDate) meta.push('<span>for ' + E(fmtShort(parseDay(x.forDate))) + '</span>');
      if (x.campaign) meta.push('<span>' + E(x.campaign) + '</span>');
      if (x.owner) meta.push('<span>raised by ' + E(x.owner) + '</span>');
      if (x.source) meta.push('<span>from ' + E(clip(x.source, 48)) + '</span>');
    } else {
      if (x.parent) meta.push('<span>part of ' + E(x.parent) + '</span>');
      meta.push('<span>' + plural(x.tasks, 'open task', 'open tasks') + (x.subs ? ' in it and ' + plural(x.subs, 'sub-project', 'sub-projects') : '') + '</span>');
      if (x.leads && x.leads.length) meta.push('<span>led by ' + E(x.leads.join(', ')) + '</span>');
    }
    var by = [];
    if (x.by) by.push('Put here by ' + E(x.by));
    if (x.at) by.push(E(ago(x.at)));
    if (x.kind === 'task' && x.due) by.push('its due date (' + E(fmtShort(parseDay(x.due))) + ') comes back with it if still ahead');
    var more = '';
    if (open) {
      var bits = '';
      if (x.desc) bits += (window.w116tMarkdown ? window.w116tMarkdown(x.desc) : '<p>' + E(x.desc) + '</p>');
      if (x.kind === 'project' && x.titles && x.titles.length) bits += '<p><b>Tasks waiting with it</b></p><ul>' + x.titles.map(function (tt) { return '<li>' + E(tt) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' + (x.tasks > x.titles.length ? '<p>and ' + (x.tasks - x.titles.length) + ' more</p>' : '');
      more = '<div class="more">' + (bits || '<p>No description.</p>') + '</div>';
    }
    var acts = '';
    if (canWork() && (B.data ? B.data.can : true) && (x.kind === 'task' || x.lead)) {
      acts = '<div class="acts"><button type="button" class="w132btn" data-w132date="' + E(key) + '">' + I('cal') + 'New date</button><button type="button" class="w132btn pri" data-w132back="' + E(key) + '">' + I('back') + 'Bring back now</button></div>';
    }
    return '<div class="w132it' + (x.kind === 'project' ? ' proj' : '') + '" data-w132it="' + E(key) + '">'
      + '<span class="ic">' + I(x.kind === 'project' ? 'box' : 'task') + '</span>'
      + '<div style="min-width:0"><button type="button" class="tt" data-w132more="' + E(key) + '" aria-expanded="' + (open ? 'true' : 'false') + '">' + E(x.title) + '</button>' + (x.kind === 'project' ? '<span class="kd">Project</span>' : x.kind === 'idea' ? '<span class="kd">Content idea</span>' : '')
      + '<div class="m">' + meta.join('') + '</div>' + whyHtml(x) + (by.length ? '<div class="by">' + by.join(' \u00b7 ') + '</div>' : '') + more + '</div>'
      + '<div class="r"><div class="when"><b>' + E(d ? fmtShort(d) : 'Later') + '</b><span>' + E(d ? inDays(d) : '') + '</span></div>' + acts + '</div>'
      + '</div>';
  }
  function monthTitle(k) {
    var p = k.split('-'); var y = Number(p[0]), m = Number(p[1]) - 1, t = today();
    if (y === t.getFullYear() && m === t.getMonth()) return 'Coming back this month';
    var nx = addMonths(mk(t.getFullYear(), t.getMonth(), 1), 1);
    if (y === nx.getFullYear() && m === nx.getMonth()) return 'Coming back next month (' + MONTHS[m] + ')';
    return 'Coming back in ' + MONTHS[m] + ' ' + y;
  }
  function listHtml(all) {
    var q = lower(B.q);
    var shown = all.filter(function (x) {
      if (B.team && x.dept !== B.team) return false;
      if (!q) return true;
      return lower([x.title, x.note, x.project, x.parent, x.owner, x.by, teamLabel(x.dept), (x.titles || []).join(' ')].join(' ')).indexOf(q) >= 0;
    });
    if (!all.length) return '<div class="w132card"><div class="w132empty">' + I('vault') + 'Nothing in the bank. When a piece of work cannot start for three months or more, move it here from its task or project page, and it comes back on its own.</div></div>';
    if (!shown.length) return '<div class="w132card"><div class="w132empty">Nothing in the bank matches' + (B.team ? ' for ' + E(teamShort(B.team)) : '') + (q ? ' \u201c' + E(B.q) + '\u201d' : '') + '.</div></div>';
    var groups = [], by = {};
    shown.forEach(function (x) { var k = String(x.until || '9999-12').slice(0, 7); if (!by[k]) { by[k] = []; groups.push(k); } by[k].push(x); });
    return groups.map(function (k) { return '<div class="w132card w132grp" data-m="' + E(k) + '"><h3>' + E(monthTitle(k)) + ' <span>' + plural(by[k].length, 'item', 'items') + '</span></h3>' + by[k].map(itemHtml).join('') + '</div>'; }).join('');
  }
  function paint() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('bank'); if (!sec) return;
    mast();
    var h = host(); if (!h) return;
    if (!B.data) { h.innerHTML = '<div class="w132load"></div><div class="w132load"></div>'; load().then(paint); return; }
    var all = items();
    var hadFocus = document.activeElement && document.activeElement.id === 'w132q', caret = hadFocus ? document.activeElement.selectionStart : null;
    h.innerHTML = statsHtml(all) + howHtml() + (B.err ? '<div class="w132err">' + E(B.err) + '</div>' : '') + backHtml(B.team) + barHtml(all) + '<div id="w132list">' + listHtml(all) + '</div>';
    if (hadFocus) { var q = document.getElementById('w132q'); if (q) { q.focus(); try { q.setSelectionRange(caret, caret); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  }
  function paintList() { var el = document.getElementById('w132list'); if (el) el.innerHTML = listHtml(items()); }
  function onPage() { var s = document.getElementById('bank'); return !!(s && s.classList.contains('active')); }
  function paintAll() {
    if (onPage()) paint();
    try { injectCard(true); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function route(h) {
    var p = String(h || '').replace(/^#/, '').split('/');
    var t = p[1] && teams().indexOf(p[1]) >= 0 ? p[1] : '';
    B.team = t;
    try { document.title = 'The Task Bank \u00b7 Welfare Avenue Hub'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    paint();
    load(!B.data || Date.now() - B.at > 30000).then(function () { if (onPage()) paint(); });
  }

  /* ---------- the sheet: move to the bank, put something new in, a new date ---------- */
  var lastFocus = null;
  function sheetEl() { var el = document.getElementById('w132sheet'); if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w132sheet'; document.body.appendChild(el); } return el; }
  function dateChoices() {
    var t = today(), out = [];
    var m3 = addMonths(t, 3), m6 = addMonths(t, 6);
    out.push({ k: '3m', t: 'In 3 months', d: m3 });
    out.push({ k: '6m', t: 'In 6 months', d: m6 });
    /* the new year, when it falls between the two and is not too close */
    var ny = mk(t.getFullYear() + 1, 0, 1), dn = daysBetween(t, ny);
    if (dn >= 60 && Math.abs(daysBetween(ny, m3)) > 10 && Math.abs(daysBetween(ny, m6)) > 10) out.push({ k: 'ny', t: 'In the new year', d: ny });
    out.sort(function (a, b) { return a.d.getTime() - b.d.getTime(); });
    return out;
  }
  function openSheet(cfg) {
    lastFocus = document.activeElement;
    var ch = dateChoices();
    cfg.pick = cfg.pick || (cfg.kind === 'date' ? 'custom' : '3m');
    cfg.custom = cfg.custom || (cfg.kind === 'date' && cfg.until ? cfg.until : ymd(addMonths(today(), 4)));
    cfg.choices = ch;
    B.sheet = cfg; renderSheet(true);
  }
  function chosenDate() {
    var s = B.sheet; if (!s) return '';
    if (s.pick === 'custom') return s.custom || '';
    var c = (s.choices || []).filter(function (x) { return x.k === s.pick; })[0];
    return c ? ymd(c.d) : '';
  }
  function projectOptions(dept, cur) {
    var ps = projects().filter(function (p) { return (p.status || 'active') === 'active' && p.department === dept && !isGeneralList(p); });
    return '<option value="">No project (the team\u2019s general list)</option>' + ps.map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + E(p.id) + '"' + (String(cur) === String(p.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(p.name) + '</option>'; }).join('');
  }
  function sheetBody() {
    var s = B.sheet, t = today(), html = '';
    if (s.kind === 'new') {
      html += '<div><div class="lb">What can wait</div><input type="text" id="w132ntitle" maxlength="250" placeholder="For example: plan the spring volunteer drive" value="' + E(s.title || '') + '"></div>'
        + '<div class="two"><div><div class="lb">Team</div><select id="w132nteam">' + teams().map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + E(d) + '"' + (s.dept === d ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div>'
        + '<div><div class="lb">Project</div><select id="w132nproj">' + projectOptions(s.dept, s.project || '') + '</select></div></div>';
    }
    if (s.kind === 'task') {
      var subs = treeIds(s.task.id).length - 1;
      html += '<p class="ex">It leaves every board, list, count and alert' + (subs ? ', with its ' + plural(subs, 'open subtask', 'open subtasks') : '') + ', and goes back to the ' + E(teamShort(s.task.department)) + ' board on the day you pick, with its owner' + (s.task.due_date ? ' and its due date if that is still ahead' : '') + '.</p>';
    }
    if (s.kind === 'project') {
      html += '<p class="ex">The project' + (s.subs ? ', its ' + plural(s.subs, 'sub-project', 'sub-projects') : '') + ' and ' + (s.count ? 'its ' + plural(s.count, 'open task', 'open tasks') : 'everything in it') + ' leave every board and list, and come back together on the day you pick.</p>';
    }
    if (s.kind === 'new') html += '<p class="ex">It goes straight to the bank and lands on the team\u2019s board on the day you pick.</p>';
    if (s.kind === 'date') html += '<p class="ex">It stays in the bank until then' + (s.until ? ' (it was coming back on ' + E(fmtDay(parseDay(s.until), true)) + ')' : '') + '.</p>';
    html += '<div><div class="lb">Comes back</div><div class="opts" role="radiogroup" aria-label="When it comes back">'
      + (s.kind === 'date' ? '' : s.choices.map(function (c) { return '<button type="button" class="opt' + (s.pick === c.k ? ' on' : '') + '" role="radio" aria-checked="' + (s.pick === c.k ? 'true' : 'false') + '" data-w132pick="' + c.k + '"><b>' + E(c.t) + '</b><span>' + E(fmtDay(c.d, c.d.getFullYear() !== t.getFullYear())) + '</span></button>'; }).join(''))
      + '<button type="button" class="opt' + (s.pick === 'custom' ? ' on' : '') + '" role="radio" aria-checked="' + (s.pick === 'custom' ? 'true' : 'false') + '" data-w132pick="custom"><b>' + (s.kind === 'date' ? 'On this day' : 'Pick a day') + '</b><span>' + E(s.pick === 'custom' && s.custom ? fmtDay(parseDay(s.custom), true) : 'any day') + '</span></button></div>'
      + (s.pick === 'custom' ? '<input type="date" class="pick" id="w132day" min="' + E(ymd(addDays(t, 1))) + '" max="' + E(ymd(addDays(t, 1095))) + '" value="' + E(s.custom || '') + '" aria-label="The day it comes back">' : '') + '</div>';
    if (s.kind !== 'date') html += '<div><div class="lb">Why it waits</div><input type="text" id="w132why" maxlength="280" placeholder="For example: after the gala in March, or once the grant decision is in" value="' + E(s.note || '') + '"></div>';
    var d = parseDay(chosenDate()), soft = '';
    if (d && daysBetween(t, d) < 60 && daysBetween(t, d) > 0) soft = 'The Task Bank is for work that waits three months or more. For anything sooner, a due date keeps it on the board.';
    html += '<div class="soft" id="w132soft">' + E(soft) + '</div><div class="err" id="w132err">' + E(s.err || '') + '</div>';
    return html;
  }
  function renderSheet(first) {
    var s = B.sheet; if (!s) { closeSheet(); return; }
    var el = sheetEl();
    var title = s.kind === 'project' ? 'Move the project to the bank' : s.kind === 'new' ? 'Put something in the bank' : s.kind === 'date' ? 'A new date' : 'Move to the bank';
    var sub = s.kind === 'task' ? s.task.title : s.kind === 'project' ? s.project.name : s.kind === 'date' ? s.title : '';
    var go = s.kind === 'date' ? 'Save the date' : s.kind === 'new' ? 'Put it in the bank' : 'Move to the bank';
    if (first || !el.querySelector('.pane')) {
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w132close="1"></div><div class="pane" role="dialog" aria-modal="true"><div class="ph"></div><div class="bd"></div><div class="ft"></div></div>';
    }
    el.querySelector('.pane').setAttribute('aria-label', title);
    el.querySelector('.ph').innerHTML = '<div style="min-width:0"><div class="kk">' + I('vault') + 'The Task Bank</div><h2>' + E(title) + '</h2>' + (sub ? '<div class="sub">' + E(sub) + '</div>' : '') + '</div><span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="xb" data-w132close="1" aria-label="Close">' + I('x') + '</button>';
    el.querySelector('.bd').innerHTML = sheetBody();
    el.querySelector('.ft').innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="w132btn" data-w132close="1">Cancel</button><button type="button" class="w132btn pri" data-w132go="1"' + (s.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I(s.kind === 'date' ? 'cal' : 'vault') + E(s.busy ? 'Saving\u2026' : go) + '</button>';
    el.classList.add('on');
    if (first) setTimeout(function () { var f = el.querySelector(s.kind === 'new' ? '#w132ntitle' : s.kind === 'date' ? '#w132day' : '#w132why') || el.querySelector('.xb'); if (f) { try { f.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }, 40);
  }
  /* keep what was typed before a repaint */
  function readSheet() {
    var s = B.sheet; if (!s) return;
    var g = function (id) { var x = document.getElementById(id); return x ? x.value : null; };
    var v;
    if ((v = g('w132ntitle')) !== null) s.title = v;
    if ((v = g('w132nteam')) !== null) s.dept = v;
    if ((v = g('w132nproj')) !== null) s.project = v;
    if ((v = g('w132why')) !== null) s.note = v;
    if ((v = g('w132day')) !== null && v) s.custom = v;
  }
  function closeSheet() {
    var el = document.getElementById('w132sheet'); if (el) { el.classList.remove('on'); el.innerHTML = ''; }
    B.sheet = null;
    if (lastFocus && lastFocus.focus && document.body.contains(lastFocus)) { try { lastFocus.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    lastFocus = null;
  }
  function sheetErr(msg) { var s = B.sheet; if (!s) return; s.err = msg; s.busy = false; renderSheet(false); }
  async function submitSheet() {
    var s = B.sheet; if (!s || s.busy) return;
    readSheet();
    var until = chosenDate(), d = parseDay(until), t = today();
    if (!d) return sheetErr('Pick the day it comes back.');
    if (daysBetween(t, d) <= 0) return sheetErr('Pick a day after today.');
    if (daysBetween(t, d) > 1095) return sheetErr('Pick a day in the next three years.');
    var note = String(s.note || '').trim();
    if (s.kind === 'new' && !String(s.title || '').trim()) return sheetErr('Say what can wait.');
    s.busy = true; s.err = ''; renderSheet(false);
    try {
      if (s.kind === 'task') await bankTask(s.task, until, note);
      else if (s.kind === 'project') await bankProject(s.project, until, note);
      else if (s.kind === 'new') await addToBank({ title: String(s.title).trim(), dept: s.dept, project: s.project, until: until, note: note, owner: myName() });
      else if (s.kind === 'date') await redate(s.itemKind, s.id, until);
      closeSheet();
    } catch (e) { sheetErr(errText(e)); }
  }

  /* ---------- the task drawer: Move to the bank ---------- */
  function drawerTaskId() {
    var hs = document.querySelectorAll('#w116dr .w116drh span');
    for (var i = 0; i < hs.length; i++) { var m = String(hs[i].textContent || '').match(/#(\d+)/); if (m) return Number(m[1]); }
    return null;
  }
  function injectDrawer() {
    var dr = document.getElementById('w116dr'); if (!dr || !dr.classList.contains('open')) return;
    var ft = dr.querySelector('.w116drf'); if (!ft) return;
    var id = drawerTaskId(), t = id ? taskById(id) : null;
    var cur = ft.querySelector('[data-w132bank]');
    var want = !!(t && !t.done && Number(t.id) > 0 && canWork());
    if (cur && (!want || cur.getAttribute('data-w132bank') !== String(t.id))) { cur.remove(); cur = null; }
    if (!want || cur) return;
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'w132drb'; b.setAttribute('data-w132bank', String(t.id));
    b.title = 'For work that cannot start for three months or more: it leaves every board and comes back on its date';
    b.innerHTML = I('vault') + 'Move to the bank';
    var del = ft.querySelector('.warn'); if (del) ft.insertBefore(b, del); else ft.appendChild(b);
  }
  var drObs = null;
  function watchDrawer() {
    var dr = document.getElementById('w116dr');
    if (dr && !dr.__w132) {
      dr.__w132 = true;
      try { new MutationObserver(function () { injectDrawer(); }).observe(dr, { childList: true, attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      injectDrawer();
    }
    if (!dr && !drObs) {
      /* the drawer is made the first time a task opens */
      try { drObs = new MutationObserver(function () { if (document.getElementById('w116dr')) { drObs.disconnect(); drObs = null; watchDrawer(); } }); drObs.observe(document.body, { childList: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    }
  }

  /* ---------- the project page: Move the project to the bank ---------- */
  function projectRef() { var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, ''); if (h.indexOf('project/') !== 0) return null; return h.split('/')[1] || null; }
  function injectProject() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('projectpage'); if (!sec) return;
    var ref = projectRef();
    var cur = sec.querySelector('.w132pbar');
    var p = ref && ref !== 'general' ? projById(ref) : null;
    var want = !!(p && (p.status || 'active') === 'active' && !isGeneralList(p) && canWork() && leadsProject(p));
    if (cur && (!want || cur.getAttribute('data-p') !== String(p.id))) { cur.remove(); cur = null; }
    if (!want || cur) return;
    var card = sec.querySelector('.card'); if (!card) return;
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.w27-desc') || card.querySelector('h2'); if (!anchor) return;
    var bar = document.createElement('div'); bar.className = 'w132pbar'; bar.setAttribute('data-p', String(p.id));
    bar.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="w132btn" data-w132bankproj="' + E(p.id) + '">' + I('vault') + 'Move the project to the bank</button><span>For a project that cannot move for three months or more. It comes back with everything in it.</span>';

  }
  var pjT = null;

    var sec = document.getElementById('projectpage'); if (!sec || sec.__w132) return;

  }
  function projectCounts(p) {
    var ids = [String(p.id)]; projects().forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.parent_id) === String(p.id)) ids.push(String(x.id)); });
    var open = tasks().filter(function (t) { return !t.done && ids.indexOf(String(t.project_id)) >= 0; });
    return { subs: ids.length - 1, count: open.length };
  }

  /* ---------- a team's overview page: In the bank ---------- */
  function teamRoute() {
    var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, ''); var p = h.split('/');
    if (p[0] !== 'team' || !p[1] || teams().indexOf(p[1]) < 0) return null;
    if (p[2] && p[2] !== 'overview') return null;
    return p[1];
  }
  var cardBusy = false;
  function cardHtml(d) {
    var c = teamCounts(d);
    var rows = c.back.slice(0, 3).map(function (x) { var dd = dayOfIso(x.at); return '<div class="w132trow back"><span>' + E(x.title) + '</span><em>back ' + E(dd ? fmtShort(dd) : '') + '</em></div>'; })
      .concat(c.list.slice(0, Math.max(0, 4 - Math.min(3, c.back.length))).map(function (x) { var dd = parseDay(x.until); return '<div class="w132trow"><span>' + (x.kind === 'project' ? 'Project: ' : x.kind === 'idea' ? 'Content idea: ' : '') + E(x.title) + '</span><em>' + E(dd ? fmtShort(dd) : '') + '</em></div>'; }));
    var sum = [];
    if (c.list.length) sum.push('<b>' + plural(c.list.length, 'item', 'items') + '</b> waiting' + (c.projects ? ' (' + plural(c.projects, 'project', 'projects') + ')' : ''));
    if (c.back.length) sum.push('<b>' + c.back.length + '</b> back in the last two weeks');
    return '<div class="w116card w132tcard" id="w132tcard" data-d="' + E(d) + '" data-v="' + B.ver + '"><h3>In the bank <span class="lnk" data-w132goto="#bank/' + E(d) + '">open the bank</span></h3>'
      + '<p class="w132tsum">' + sum.join(' \u00b7 ') + '. Work that waits three months or more, back on its own date.</p>' + rows.join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function injectCard(force) {
    if (cardBusy) return;
    var d = teamRoute();
    var cur = document.getElementById('w132tcard');
    if (!d || !staffOk() || !tabOk('bank')) { if (cur) cur.remove(); return; }
    var hostEl = document.getElementById('w116host'); if (!hostEl) return;
    var cols = hostEl.querySelectorAll('.w116grid > .w116col'); if (!cols.length) return;
    var col = cols[cols.length - 1];
    if (!B.data) { if (!B.loading) load().then(function () { injectCard(true); }); return; }
    var c = teamCounts(d);
    if (!c.list.length && !c.back.length) { if (cur) cur.remove(); return; }
    if (cur && !force && cur.getAttribute('data-d') === d && cur.parentNode === col && cur.getAttribute('data-v') === String(B.ver)) return;
    cardBusy = true;
    try { if (cur) cur.remove(); col.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', cardHtml(d)); } finally { cardBusy = false; }
    if (Date.now() - B.at > 60000 && !B.loading) load(true).then(function () { injectCard(true); });
  }
  var cardT = null;

  }

  }

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var el;
    /* the undo bar */
    if ((el = t.closest('#w132snack [data-w132sn]'))) {
      var k = el.getAttribute('data-w132sn');
      if (k === 'x') { hideSnack(); return; }
      var a = SNACK.acts[Number(k)]; hideSnack(); if (a && a[1]) a[1]();
      return;
    }
    /* the drawer and the project page */
    if ((el = t.closest('#w116dr [data-w132bank]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
      var tk = taskById(el.getAttribute('data-w132bank')); if (!tk) return;
      openSheet({ kind: 'task', task: tk });
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w132bankproj]'))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      var p = projById(el.getAttribute('data-w132bankproj')); if (!p) return;
      var pc = projectCounts(p);
      openSheet({ kind: 'project', project: p, subs: pc.subs, count: pc.count });
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w132goto]'))) { e.preventDefault(); go(el.getAttribute('data-w132goto')); return; }
    /* the sheet */
    var inSheet = !!t.closest('#w132sheet');
    if (inSheet) {
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w132close]'))) { closeSheet(); return; }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w132pick]'))) { readSheet(); B.sheet.pick = el.getAttribute('data-w132pick'); B.sheet.err = ''; renderSheet(false); if (B.sheet.pick === 'custom') { var dd = document.getElementById('w132day'); if (dd) { try { dd.focus(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } } return; }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w132go]'))) { submitSheet(); return; }
      return;
    }
    /* the page */
    if (!t.closest('#w132')) return;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w132new]'))) { var mt = myTeam(); openSheet({ kind: 'new', dept: B.team || (teams().indexOf(mt) >= 0 ? mt : teams()[0]) }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w132team]'))) { var tm = el.getAttribute('data-w132team'); var h = tm ? '#bank/' + tm : '#bank'; if (location.hash !== h) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', h); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } B.team = tm; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w132more]'))) { var key = el.getAttribute('data-w132more'); B.open[key] = !B.open[key]; paintList(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w132back]'))) {
      var kb = el.getAttribute('data-w132back').split(':'); var it = itemBy(kb[0], kb[1]); if (!it) return;
      el.disabled = true; el.textContent = 'Bringing it back\u2026';
      bringBack(kb[0], kb[1]).catch(function (x) { toastS(errText(x)); el.disabled = false; paintList(); });
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w132date]'))) {
      var kd = el.getAttribute('data-w132date').split(':'); var itd = itemBy(kd[0], kd[1]); if (!itd) return;
      openSheet({ kind: 'date', itemKind: kd[0], id: kd[1], title: itd.title, until: itd.until });
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w132open]'))) {
      var ko = el.getAttribute('data-w132open').split(':');
      if (ko[0] === 'project') { go('#project/' + ko[1]); return; }
      if (ko[0] === 'idea') { try { if (window.w121Stale) window.w121Stale(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } go('#contentcal/' + ko[1]); return; }
      var tt = taskById(ko[1]);
      if (tt && window.w116Board && window.w116Board.open) { window.w116Board.open(Number(ko[1])); return; }
      go('#team/' + (el.getAttribute('data-d') || '') + '/tasks');
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('details.w132how summary'))) { setTimeout(function () { var d = document.querySelector('#w132 details.w132how'); B.howOpen = !!(d && d.open); }, 0); }
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t) return;
    if (t.id === 'w132q') { B.q = t.value; paintList(); return; }
    if (t.id === 'w132day' && B.sheet) { B.sheet.custom = t.value; var d = parseDay(t.value), s = document.getElementById('w132soft'); if (s) s.textContent = d && daysBetween(today(), d) < 60 && daysBetween(today(), d) > 0 ? 'The Task Bank is for work that waits three months or more. For anything sooner, a due date keeps it on the board.' : ''; var sp = document.querySelector('#w132sheet [data-w132pick="custom"] span'); if (sp && d) sp.textContent = fmtDay(d, true); }
  });

    if (t.id === 'w132nteam') { readSheet(); B.sheet.project = ''; renderSheet(false); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {

    if (e.key === 'Enter' && B.sheet && e.target && (e.target.id === 'w132why' || e.target.id === 'w132ntitle')) { e.preventDefault(); submitSheet(); }
  }, true);
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { if (B.sheet) closeSheet(); setTimeout(function () { injectCard(false); injectProject(); }, 120); });

  /* ---------- boot ---------- */

      try {
        register(); watchDrawer(); watchTeam(); watchProject();
        try { var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar'); if (bar && !bar.__w132) { bar.__w132 = true; var nt = null; new MutationObserver(function () { if (nt) return; nt = setTimeout(function () { nt = null; placeTab(); }, 160); }).observe(bar, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        [400, 1500].forEach(function (ms) { setTimeout(placeTab, ms); });

  /* ---------- the page tour (wave122's engine; trainings row tour:bank) and a step on the project tour ---------- */
  function addTours() {
    try {
      var W = window.w122; if (!W || !W.tours) return;
      var OPT = { opt: 1 };
      var S = function (sel, t, p, o) { var x = { s: sel, t: t, p: p }; if (o) Object.keys(o).forEach(function (k) { x[k] = o[k]; }); return x; };
      if (!W.tours.bank) W.tours.bank = { tab: 'bank', icon: 'box', hash: '#bank', steps: [
        S('#bank > .w118mast', 'The Task Bank', 'Work that cannot start for three months or more waits here, out of every board, list and count, and comes back on its own date.'),
        S('#w132 .w132top', 'At a glance', 'How much is waiting, what comes back in the next 30 days, and what came back lately. Put something new straight in with the button.', OPT),
        S('#w132 details.w132how', 'How it works', 'Move a task from its drawer, or a whole project from its page. The Hub also banks what a meeting puts off, and content ideas for later in the year wait here until their time.', OPT),
        S('#w132back', 'Back from the bank', 'What came back in the last two weeks. It is on its team\u2019s board again, marked back from the bank.', OPT),
        S('#w132 .w132bar', 'Each team', 'Show one team\u2019s bank, or search for something.', OPT),
        S('#w132 .w132grp', 'By the month it comes back', 'Why each piece waits and who put it here. Bring it back now or give it a new date.', OPT)
      ] };
      var pt = W.tours.project;
      if (pt && Array.isArray(pt.steps) && !pt.steps.some(function (s) { return s && s.s === '#projectpage .w132pbar'; })) {
        pt.steps.splice(Math.min(2, pt.steps.length), 0, S('#projectpage .w132pbar', 'Park it in the bank', 'When the whole project cannot move for three months or more, its lead or a leader can move it to the bank. It comes back with everything in it.', OPT));
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  addTours();

  /* ---------- test hook ---------- */
  window.w132 = { state: function () { return B; }, load: load, items: items, paint: paint, open: openSheet, close: closeSheet, card: injectCard, drawer: injectDrawer, project: injectProject, back: isBackRecent, dates: dateChoices };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v133 the org chart, interactive (26/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "I want the org chart to be more interactive where we can choose direct reports and move
   things around as executives. Executives should also be able to visualize empty positions."

   - The chart (#orgchart) reads the reporting lines: the board, the executive directors, then each
     team with its lead and everyone under them, nested by who reports to whom. Empty positions show
     as dashed cards (open, hiring or on hold, with a note). Search, a team filter and a switch for the
     open positions. Anyone on staff reads it; click anyone to see who they report to and who reports
     to them, and open their profile.
   - Executives and admins: Rearrange (drag someone onto the person they report to, or onto a team to
     make them its lead, with an Undo), choose a person's direct reports, retitle a seat, add an empty
     position under anyone, fill it, take someone off a seat, remove an empty position, and place
     anyone who is not on the chart yet. The database checks each change and keeps it in the audit
     trail; the roster keeps the chart in step (a new person gets a seat, someone who leaves leaves an
     open position).
   - A person's page says who they report to and who reports to them.
   Data: wa133_org (org_positions, wa_org_editor(), org_move(), org_set_reports(), org_add_opening(),
   org_update(), org_fill(), org_place(), org_vacate(), org_remove(), the roster trigger).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa133) return; window.__wa133 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', GOLD = '#DCA527', FLAG = '#B3402F', TEAL = '#2A8C7A', INK = '#1F201E', PURPLE = '#6F64AB';
  var HEAD = "'Barlow Condensed','Arial Narrow','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var SH1 = '0 1px 2px rgba(16,63,57,.05),0 8px 24px rgba(16,63,57,.06)';
  var STATUS = { open: ['Open', 'rgba(16,63,57,.08)', GREEN], hiring: ['Hiring', 'rgba(220,165,39,.22)', '#6b4d05'], on_hold: ['On hold', 'rgba(121,119,115,.16)', '#5c5a56'] };

  function I(n, cls) { return '<svg class="w133i' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : '') + '" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">' + (P[n] || P.spark) + '</svg>'; }
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;'); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).toLowerCase().trim(); }
  function first(n) { return String(n || '').trim().split(/\s+/)[0] || ''; }
  function plural(n, one, many) { return n + ' ' + (n === 1 ? one : many); }
  function toastS(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function clip(s, n) { s = String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); return s.length > n ? s.slice(0, n - 1).trim() + '\u2026' : s; }
  function staffOk() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function tabOk(id) { try { return typeof wa40Allowed !== 'function' || (wa40Allowed() || []).indexOf(id) >= 0; } catch (e) { return true; } }
  function teams() { try { return ((window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.order) || (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' ? DEPT_ORDER : [])).filter(function (d) { return d !== 'board'; }); } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function teamLabel(d) { try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.labels && WA_TEAMS.labels[d]) || (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; } catch (e) { return d || ''; } }
  function teamShort(d) { try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.short && WA_TEAMS.short[d]) || teamLabel(d); } catch (e) { return d || ''; } }
  function teamColor(d) { try { return (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[d]) || GREEN; } catch (e) { return GREEN; } }
  function members() { try { return ((typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' && DB_MEMBERS) || []).filter(function (m) { return m.active !== false; }); } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function memberById(id) { if (id == null) return null; return members().filter(function (m) { return String(m.id) === String(id); })[0] || null; }
  function avatar(m, size) { try { if (window.w116Avatar) return window.w116Avatar(m, size); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return '<span class="w133ini" style="width:' + size + 'px;height:' + size + 'px">' + E(String((m && m.name) || '?').slice(0, 1)) + '</span>'; }
