  /* ---------- the data: org_positions and who may change them ---------- */
  function rpc(fn, args) {
    return Promise.resolve(db.rpc(fn, args || {})).then(function (r) { if (r && r.error) throw r.error; return r ? r.data : null; });
  }
  function load(force) {
    if (O.loading) return O.loading;
    if (!force && O.pos && Date.now() - O.at < 30000) return Promise.resolve(O.pos);
    O.loading = Promise.all([
      Promise.resolve(db.from('org_positions').select('*').order('sort').order('id')),
      rpc('wa_org_editor').catch(function () { return false; })
    ]).then(function (res) {
      var r = res[0];
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      O.pos = (r && r.data) || [];
      O.canEdit = res[1] === true;
      O.at = Date.now(); O.err = ''; O.loading = null; O.ver++;
      return O.pos;
    }).catch(function (e) { O.loading = null; O.err = errText(e); if (!O.pos) O.pos = null; throw e; });
    return O.loading;
  }
  function errText(e) {
    var m = String((e && (e.message || e.details || e.hint)) || e || '').replace(/^.*?ERROR:\s*/, '');
    if (/only executives and admins/i.test(m)) return 'Only executives and admins can change the chart.';
    if (/reports to them/i.test(m)) return 'Someone cannot report to a person who reports to them.';
    if (/stay at the top/i.test(m)) return 'The executive directors stay at the top of the chart.';
    if (/already filled/i.test(m)) return 'That position is already filled.';
    if (/already on the chart/i.test(m)) return 'They are already on the chart.';
    if (/off the seat first/i.test(m)) return 'Take the person off the seat first.';
    if (/give the position a title/i.test(m)) return 'Give the position a title.';
    if (/fetch|network|Failed to/i.test(m)) return 'Could not reach the Hub. Nothing changed. Try again.';
    return m || 'Something went wrong. Nothing changed.';
  }

  /* ---------- reading the chart ---------- */
  function ix() {
    var byId = {}, kids = {}, seatOf = {};
    (O.pos || []).forEach(function (p) { byId[p.id] = p; if (p.member_id != null) seatOf[p.member_id] = p; });
    (O.pos || []).forEach(function (p) { var k = p.reports_to == null ? 'top' : String(p.reports_to); (kids[k] = kids[k] || []).push(p); });
    Object.keys(kids).forEach(function (k) { kids[k].sort(seatOrder); });
    return { byId: byId, kids: kids, seatOf: seatOf };
  }
  function holder(p) { return p && p.member_id != null ? memberById(p.member_id) : null; }
  function isOpen(p) { return !holder(p); }
  function seatName(p) { var m = holder(p); return m ? m.name : 'Open position'; }
  function seatTeam(p) { var m = holder(p); return (m && m.department) || p.department || ''; }
  function seatOrder(a, b) {
    var oa = isOpen(a) ? 1 : 0, ob = isOpen(b) ? 1 : 0;
    if (oa !== ob) return oa - ob;
    if ((a.sort || 100) !== (b.sort || 100)) return (a.sort || 100) - (b.sort || 100);
    return lower(seatName(a) + a.title).localeCompare(lower(seatName(b) + b.title));
  }
  function execs(X) { return (O.pos || []).filter(function (p) { return p.kind === 'exec'; }).sort(seatOrder); }
  function isExec(p) { return !!p && p.kind === 'exec'; }
  /* the seats at the head of the teams: reporting to both executive directors, or to one of them */
  function heads(X) { return (O.pos || []).filter(function (p) { return p.kind !== 'exec' && (p.reports_to == null || isExec(X.byId[p.reports_to])); }).sort(seatOrder); }
  function kidsOf(X, p) { return (X.kids[String(p.id)] || []).filter(function (k) { return O.showOpen || !isOpen(k); }); }
  /* is node inside root's part of the chart (root included) */
  function isUnder(X, node, root) { var seen = {}, cur = X.byId[node]; while (cur && !seen[cur.id]) { if (String(cur.id) === String(root)) return true; seen[cur.id] = 1; cur = cur.reports_to == null ? null : X.byId[cur.reports_to]; } return false; }
  function managerLabel(X, p) {
    if (!p) return '';
    if (p.kind === 'exec') return 'the board';
    if (p.reports_to == null) return 'the executive directors';
    var g = X.byId[p.reports_to]; return g ? (holder(g) ? holder(g).name : 'an open position (' + g.title + ')') : 'the executive directors';
  }
  function mgrShort(X, p) {
    if (!p) return '';
    if (p.kind === 'exec') return 'the board';
    if (p.reports_to == null) return 'the co-EDs';
    var g = X.byId[p.reports_to]; return g ? (holder(g) ? first(holder(g).name) : 'an open position') : 'the co-EDs';
  }
  function countUnder(X, p) { var n = 0, st = (X.kids[String(p.id)] || []).slice(), seen = {}; while (st.length) { var k = st.pop(); if (seen[k.id]) continue; seen[k.id] = 1; n++; (X.kids[String(k.id)] || []).forEach(function (c) { st.push(c); }); } return n; }
  function unplaced(X) { return members().filter(function (m) { return m.department !== 'board' && !X.seatOf[m.id]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return lower(a.name).localeCompare(lower(b.name)); }); }
  function boardPeople(X) { return members().filter(function (m) { return m.department === 'board' && !X.seatOf[m.id]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return lower(a.name).localeCompare(lower(b.name)); }); }

  /* ---------- the changes (the database checks each one and logs it) ---------- */
  async function after(msg, undo) {
    await load(true);
    paint();
    try { if (O.sheet) renderSheet(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (msg) snack(msg, undo ? [['Undo', undo]] : []);
  }
  async function move(p, toId, dept, quiet) {
    var X = ix(), was = p.reports_to;
    await rpc('org_move', { p_id: Number(p.id), p_reports_to: toId == null ? null : Number(toId), p_department: dept || null });
    var nm = seatName(p), to = toId == null ? 'the executive directors' + (dept ? ' (' + teamShort(dept) + ')' : '') : seatName(X.byId[toId]);
    if (!quiet) await after(nm + ' now reports to ' + to + '.', function () { return move(p, was, null, true).then(function () { toastS('Moved back'); hideSnack(); }).catch(function (e) { toastS(errText(e)); }); });
    else await after(null);
  }
  async function setReports(p, ids) {
    var res = await rpc('org_set_reports', { p_id: Number(p.id), p_reports: ids.map(Number) });
    await after(null);
    toastS('Direct reports saved' + (res && res.moved_up ? ' (' + plural(res.moved_up, 'person moves', 'people move') + ' up a level)' : ''));
  }
  async function addOpening(toId, f) {
    var res = await rpc('org_add_opening', { p_reports_to: toId == null ? null : Number(toId), p_title: f.title, p_department: f.dept || null, p_status: f.status || 'open', p_note: f.note || null });
    await after(null);
    toastS('Open position added');
    return res;
  }
  async function updateSeat(p, f) {
    await rpc('org_update', { p_id: Number(p.id), p_title: f.title, p_status: f.status || null, p_note: f.note == null ? null : f.note, p_department: f.dept || null });
    var m = holder(p); if (m && f.title) m.role = f.title;
    await after(null);
    toastS('Saved');
  }
  async function fill(p, memberId) {
    var m = memberById(memberId);
    var res = await rpc('org_fill', { p_id: Number(p.id), p_member_id: Number(memberId) });
    if (m && p.title) m.role = p.title;
    await after((m ? m.name : 'They') + ' now holds ' + p.title + '.' + (res && res.left_open ? ' Their old seat is an open position now.' : ''), null);
  }
  async function place(memberId, toId, dept) {
    var m = memberById(memberId);
    var res = await rpc('org_place', { p_member_id: Number(memberId), p_reports_to: toId == null ? null : Number(toId) });
    var X = ix();
    await after((m ? m.name : 'They') + ' is on the chart, reporting to ' + (toId == null ? 'the executive directors' : seatName(X.byId[toId])) + '.', null);
    return res;
  }
  async function vacate(p) {
    var nm = seatName(p);
    await rpc('org_vacate', { p_id: Number(p.id) });
    await after(nm + ' is off the seat. ' + p.title + ' is an open position now.', null);
  }
  async function removeSeat(p) {
    var res = await rpc('org_remove', { p_id: Number(p.id) });
    O.sheet = null; closeSheet();
    await after('The open position is removed' + (res && res.moved_up ? '; ' + plural(res.moved_up, 'person moves', 'people move') + ' up a level' : '') + '.', null);
  }

  /* ---------- the undo bar ---------- */
  var SN = { acts: [] };
  function snackEl() { var el = document.getElementById('w133snack'); if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w133snack'; el.setAttribute('role', 'status'); el.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite'); document.body.appendChild(el); } return el; }
  function snack(text, acts) {
    var el = snackEl(); SN.acts = acts || [];
    el.innerHTML = '<span class="t">' + E(text) + '</span>' + SN.acts.map(function (a, i) { return '<button type="button" data-w133sn="' + i + '">' + E(a[0]) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '<button type="button" class="x" data-w133sn="x" aria-label="Dismiss">' + I('x') + '</button>';
    el.classList.add('on');
    clearTimeout(O.snackT); O.snackT = setTimeout(hideSnack, 10000);
  }
  function hideSnack() { var el = document.getElementById('w133snack'); if (el) el.classList.remove('on'); clearTimeout(O.snackT); }

  /* ---------- the chart ---------- */
  var MAX_KIDS = 12;
  function hostEl() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('orgchart'); if (!sec) return null;
    var h = document.getElementById('w133');
    if (!h) {
      h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = 'w133';
      var m = sec.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
      if (m && m.nextSibling) sec.insertBefore(h, m.nextSibling); else sec.appendChild(h);
    }
    return h;
  }
  function oldCard(on) {
    try { var b = document.getElementById('ocBody'); var c = b && b.closest('.card'); if (c) c.classList.toggle('w133old', !!on); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function statusChip(p) { var s = STATUS[p.status] || STATUS.open; return '<span class="w133st" style="background:' + s[1] + ';color:' + s[2] + '">' + E(s[0]) + '</span>'; }
  function online(m) { return !!(m && m.email && O.online[lower(m.email)]); }
  function cardHtml(X, p, colTeam) {
    var m = holder(p), n = (X.kids[String(p.id)] || []).length, drag = O.arrange && O.canEdit && p.kind !== 'exec';
    var tags = [];
    if (p.kind !== 'exec' && p.reports_to != null && isExec(X.byId[p.reports_to])) tags.push('<span class="w133tag">reports to ' + E(first(seatName(X.byId[p.reports_to]))) + '</span>');
    var tm = seatTeam(p);
    if (colTeam && tm && tm !== colTeam && tm !== 'board') tags.push('<span class="w133tag tm" style="background:' + E(teamColor(tm)) + '">' + E(teamShort(tm)) + '</span>');
    var attrs = ' data-w133p="' + E(p.id) + '" data-w133drop="pos:' + E(p.id) + '"' + (drag ? ' draggable="true" data-w133drag="pos:' + E(p.id) + '"' : '') + ' role="button" tabindex="0"';
    var add = O.canEdit ? '<button type="button" class="addk" data-w133addunder="' + E(p.id) + '" title="Add an empty position under ' + E(m ? first(m.name) : p.title) + '" aria-label="Add an empty position">' + I('plus') + '</button>' : '';
    if (!m) {
      return '<div class="w133c open"' + attrs + ' aria-label="Open position: ' + E(p.title) + '"><span class="oic">' + I('open') + '</span><div class="tx"><span class="kk">Open position</span><b>' + E(p.title || 'Untitled') + '</b>'
        + '<div class="tags">' + statusChip(p) + tags.join('') + '</div>' + (p.note ? '<span>' + E(clip(p.note, 90)) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' + (n ? '<span class="cnt">' + n + '</span>' : '') + add + '</div>';
    }
    return '<div class="w133c' + (p.kind === 'exec' ? ' ex' : '') + '"' + attrs + ' data-w133m="' + E(m.id) + '" aria-label="' + E(m.name + ', ' + (p.title || m.role || '')) + '"><span class="avw">' + avatar(m, p.kind === 'exec' ? 44 : 34) + (online(m) ? '<span class="w133on" title="On the Hub now"></span>' : '') + '</span>'
      + '<div class="tx"><b>' + E(m.name) + '</b><span>' + E(p.title || m.role || 'Team member') + '</span>' + (tags.length ? '<div class="tags">' + tags.join('') + '</div>' : '') + '</div>'
      + (n ? '<span class="cnt" title="' + plural(n, 'direct report', 'direct reports') + '">' + n + '</span>' : '') + add + '</div>';
  }
  function nodeHtml(X, p, colTeam, seen, depth) {
    if (seen[p.id] || depth > 14) return ''; seen[p.id] = 1;
    var ks = kidsOf(X, p), key = String(p.id), open = !!O.expand[key] || !!O.q || O.arrange;
    /* open positions always show; a long list of people folds after twelve */
    var shown = open ? ks : ks.filter(function (k, i) { return isOpen(k) || ks.filter(function (x, j) { return j <= i && !isOpen(x); }).length <= MAX_KIDS; });
    var html = '<div class="w133node" data-n="' + E(p.id) + '">' + cardHtml(X, p, colTeam);
    if (ks.length) {
      html += '<div class="w133kids">' + shown.map(function (k) { return nodeHtml(X, k, colTeam, seen, depth + 1); }).join('')
        + (ks.length > shown.length ? '<button type="button" class="w133more" data-w133exp="' + E(key) + '">Show ' + (ks.length - shown.length) + ' more</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    }
    return html + '</div>';
  }
  function teamCol(X, d, hs) {
    var seen = {}, list = hs.filter(function (p) { return seatTeam(p) === d && (O.showOpen || !isOpen(p)); });
    var ppl = 0, open = 0;
    list.forEach(function (p) { [p].concat(allUnder(X, p)).forEach(function (s) { if (isOpen(s)) open++; else ppl++; }); });
    var head = '<h3 data-w133drop="team:' + E(d) + '">' + E(teamLabel(d)) + ' <span>' + plural(ppl, 'person', 'people') + (open ? ', ' + plural(open, 'open position', 'open positions') : '') + '</span></h3>';
    var body = list.length ? list.map(function (p) { return nodeHtml(X, p, d, seen, 0); }).join('') : '<p class="empty">No one on this team yet.</p>';
    var add = O.canEdit && O.arrange ? '<div class="addrow"><button type="button" class="w133btn" data-w133addteam="' + E(d) + '">' + I('plus') + 'Add a position to lead ' + E(teamShort(d)) + '</button></div>' : '';
    return '<div class="w133team" data-team="' + E(d) + '" style="--tc:' + E(teamColor(d)) + '">' + head + body + add + '</div>';
  }
  /* wave297b: the board row in governance order. The President of the Board leads, then the officers, then members and advisers. */
  function w297rank(m) { var r = String(m.role || '').toLowerCase(); if (r.indexOf('president') >= 0) return 0; if (/chair|treasurer|secretary|vice/.test(r)) return 1; if (r.indexOf('advis') >= 0) return 3; return 2; }
  /* wave297 (09/11/26): the tree. The board above; one column per co-executive director, their card on top and
     the teams that report to them underneath, joined by lines; HR and any lead who reports to no one sits in a
     last column, "Shared by the co-executive directors". A team chip still opens the team's own column. */
  function treeHtml(X, ex, hs) {
    var shown = function (p) { return O.showOpen || !isOpen(p); }, seen = {};
    var order = teams();
    function groups(list) {
      var by = {}, keys = [];
      list.forEach(function (p) { var t = seatTeam(p) || ''; if (!by[t]) { by[t] = []; keys.push(t); } by[t].push(p); });
      keys.sort(function (a, b) { var ia = order.indexOf(a), ib = order.indexOf(b); return (ia < 0 ? 99 : ia) - (ib < 0 ? 99 : ib); });
      return keys.map(function (t) {
        var ppl = 0, open = 0;
        by[t].forEach(function (p) { [p].concat(allUnder(X, p)).forEach(function (s) { if (isOpen(s)) open++; else ppl++; }); });
        return '<div class="w297tm" data-team="' + E(t) + '" style="--tc:' + E(teamColor(t)) + '"><h4 data-w133drop="team:' + E(t) + '">' + E(teamLabel(t)) + ' <span>' + plural(ppl, 'person', 'people') + (open ? ', ' + plural(open, 'open position', 'open positions') : '') + '</span></h4>'
          + by[t].map(function (p) { return nodeHtml(X, p, t, seen, 0); }).join('') + '</div>';
      }).join('');
    }
    var cols = ex.map(function (e) {
      var under = hs.filter(function (p) { return String(p.reports_to) === String(e.id) && shown(p); });
      return '<div class="w297br"><div class="w297top">' + cardHtml(X, e, '') + '</div>'
        + (under.length ? '<div class="w297teams">' + groups(under) + '</div>' : '<p class="w297none">No team leads report here yet.</p>') + '</div>';
    });
    var shared = hs.filter(function (p) { return (p.reports_to == null || !isExec(X.byId[p.reports_to])) && shown(p); });
    if (shared.length) cols.push('<div class="w297br sh"><div class="w297top"><div class="w297shh"><b>Shared</b><span>by the co-executive directors</span></div></div><div class="w297teams">' + groups(shared) + '</div></div>');
    return '<div class="w133lv w133exec w297tree"><h3 data-w133drop="team:">Executive directors <span>each co-executive director, with the teams that report to them</span></h3><div class="w297row">' + cols.join('') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function allUnder(X, p) { var out = [], st = (X.kids[String(p.id)] || []).slice(), seen = {}; while (st.length) { var k = st.shift(); if (seen[k.id]) continue; seen[k.id] = 1; out.push(k); (X.kids[String(k.id)] || []).forEach(function (c) { st.push(c); }); } return out; }
  function barHtml(X) {
    var filled = (O.pos || []).filter(function (p) { return !isOpen(p); }).length, open = (O.pos || []).filter(isOpen).length, up = unplaced(X).length;
    var chips = '<button type="button" class="w133chip' + (!O.team ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w133team="">All teams</button>' + teams().map(function (d) { return '<button type="button" class="w133chip' + (O.team === d ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w133team="' + E(d) + '"><i style="background:' + E(teamColor(d)) + '"></i>' + E(teamShort(d)) + '</button>'; }).join('');
    return '<div class="w133bar"><div class="w133stats"><b>' + filled + '</b> people on the chart' + (open ? ' \u00b7 <b>' + open + '</b> open ' + (open === 1 ? 'position' : 'positions') : '') + (up ? ' \u00b7 <b>' + up + '</b> not placed yet' : '') + ' \u00b7 ' + teams().length + ' teams</div>'
      + '<label class="w133q">' + I('search') + '<input type="search" id="w133q" placeholder="Find someone" value="' + E(O.q) + '" aria-label="Find someone on the chart"></label>'
      + '<label class="w133tog"><input type="checkbox" id="w133open"' + (O.showOpen ? ' checked' : '') + '> Open positions</label>'
      + (O.canEdit ? '<button type="button" class="w133btn' + (O.arrange ? ' pri' : '') + '" data-w133arr="1">' + I(O.arrange ? 'check' : 'move') + (O.arrange ? 'Done' : 'Rearrange') + '</button><button type="button" class="w133btn" data-w133addteam="">' + I('plus') + 'Add an open position</button>' : '')
      + '</div><div class="w133chips" role="group" aria-label="Teams">' + chips + '</div>'
      + (O.arrange ? '<div class="w133arr"><span class="t"><b>Rearranging.</b> Drag someone onto the person they report to, or onto a team\u2019s name to make them one of its leads. Use + on any card to add an empty position under it. Every move can be undone.</span><button type="button" class="w133btn pri" data-w133arr="1">' + I('check') + 'Done</button></div>' : '');
  }
  function paint() {
    var h = hostEl(); if (!h) return;
    if (!O.pos) { h.innerHTML = '<div class="w133load"></div>'; return; }
    oldCard(true);
    var X = ix(), hs = heads(X), ex = execs(X), board = boardPeople(X), up = unplaced(X);
    var focus = document.activeElement && document.activeElement.id === 'w133q', caret = focus ? document.activeElement.selectionStart : null;
    var html = barHtml(X) + (O.err ? '<div class="w133err">' + E(O.err) + '</div>' : '');
    if (!O.team) {
      html += '<div class="w133lv w133board"><h3>Board of directors <span>governance, with the co-executive directors reporting to it</span>' + (tabOk('governance') ? '<a data-w133go="#governance">Board overview</a>' : '') + '</h3><div class="w133row">'
        + (board.length ? board.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return w297rank(a) - w297rank(b); }).map(function (m) { return '<button type="button" class="w133bchip' + (w297rank(m) === 0 ? ' lead' : '') + '" data-w133m="' + E(m.id) + '">' + avatar(m, 26) + '<b>' + E(m.name) + '</b><span class="r">' + E(clip(m.role || 'Board member', 40)) + '</span></button>'; }).join('') : '<p class="note">No directors on the roster.</p>') + '</div></div>'
        + '<div class="w133link"></div>'
        + treeHtml(X, ex, hs);   /* wave297: each co-executive director with the teams that report to them */
    }
    var cols = O.team ? [O.team] : [];
    if (O.team) html += '<div class="w133grid">' + cols.map(function (d) { return teamCol(X, d, hs); }).join('') + '</div>';
    var other = [];
    if (other.length) html += '<div class="w133team" style="--tc:' + GREEN + '"><h3>Other seats</h3>' + other.map(function (p) { return nodeHtml(X, p, '', {}, 0); }).join('') + '</div>';
    if (up.length && (!O.team || up.some(function (m) { return m.department === O.team; }))) {
      var list = O.team ? up.filter(function (m) { return m.department === O.team; }) : up;
      html += '<div class="w133tray"><h3>Not on the chart yet</h3><p>' + (O.canEdit ? 'On the roster but not placed. Open someone to choose who they report to' + (O.arrange ? ', or drag them onto a card.' : '.') : 'On the roster but not placed on the chart yet.') + '</p><div class="w133row">'
        + list.map(function (m) { return '<button type="button" class="w133bchip" data-w133m="' + E(m.id) + '"' + (O.arrange && O.canEdit ? ' draggable="true" data-w133drag="member:' + E(m.id) + '"' : '') + '>' + avatar(m, 26) + '<b>' + E(m.name) + '</b><span class="r">' + E(teamShort(m.department)) + '</span></button>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>';
    }
    h.innerHTML = html;
    h.classList.toggle('arr', !!O.arrange);
    applySearch();
    if (focus) { var q = document.getElementById('w133q'); if (q) { q.focus(); try { q.setSelectionRange(caret, caret); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  }
  function applySearch() {
    var h = document.getElementById('w133'); if (!h) return;
    var q = lower(O.q); h.classList.toggle('searching', !!q);
    if (!q) return;
    var first0 = null;
    h.querySelectorAll('.w133c, .w133bchip').forEach(function (el) {
      var hit = lower(el.textContent).indexOf(q) >= 0;
      el.classList.toggle('hit', hit);
      if (hit && !first0) first0 = el;
    });
    if (first0 && O.scrollHit) { O.scrollHit = false; try { first0.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center', behavior: 'smooth' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  O.expand = {};

  /* ---------- the sheet: a seat, a person, a new open position ---------- */
  var lastFocus = null;
  function sheetEl() { var el = document.getElementById('w133sheet'); if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w133sheet'; document.body.appendChild(el); } return el; }
  function openSheet(s) { if (!O.sheet) lastFocus = document.activeElement; O.sheet = s; renderSheet(true); }
  function closeSheet() {
    var el = document.getElementById('w133sheet'); if (el) { el.classList.remove('on'); el.innerHTML = ''; }
    O.sheet = null;
    if (lastFocus && lastFocus.focus && document.body.contains(lastFocus)) { try { lastFocus.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    lastFocus = null;
  }
  function whoRow(X, p) {
    var m = holder(p);
    return '<div class="who" data-w133p="' + E(p.id) + '" role="button" tabindex="0">' + (m ? avatar(m, 30) : '<span style="width:30px;height:30px;border-radius:50%;border:1.5px dashed rgba(140,106,46,.7);display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:#8C6A2E;flex:none">' + I('open') + '</span>') + '<div style="min-width:0"><b>' + E(m ? m.name : 'Open position') + '</b><br><span>' + E(p.title || (m && m.role) || '') + (m && seatTeam(p) ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(teamShort(seatTeam(p))) : '') + '</span></div></div>';
  }
  function seatLabel(X, s) { var m = holder(s); return (m ? m.name : 'Open position') + (s.title ? ' (' + clip(s.title, 40) + ')' : ''); }
  function mgrOptions(X, p, cur) {
    var out = '<option value=""' + (cur == null ? ' selected' : '') + '>The executive directors (both)</option>';
    execs(X).forEach(function (e) { out += '<option value="' + E(e.id) + '"' + (String(cur) === String(e.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(seatName(e)) + ' (executive director)</option>'; });
    teams().concat(['']).forEach(function (d) {
      var seats = (O.pos || []).filter(function (s) { return s.kind !== 'exec' && (d ? seatTeam(s) === d : teams().indexOf(seatTeam(s)) < 0) && (!p || !isUnder(X, s.id, p.id)); }).sort(seatOrder);
      if (!seats.length) return;
      out += '<optgroup label="' + E(d ? teamLabel(d) : 'Other') + '">' + seats.map(function (s) { return '<option value="' + E(s.id) + '"' + (String(cur) === String(s.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(seatLabel(X, s)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</optgroup>';
    });
    return out;
  }
  function teamOptions(cur) { return teams().map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + E(d) + '"' + (cur === d ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '</option>'; }).join(''); }
  function pickList(X, p) {
    var q = lower(O.sheet.pq || ''), mine = [], by = {};
    (O.pos || []).forEach(function (s) {
      if (String(s.id) === String(p.id) || s.kind === 'exec' || isUnder(X, p.id, s.id)) return;
      var hay = lower(seatName(s) + ' ' + s.title + ' ' + teamShort(seatTeam(s)));
      if (q && hay.indexOf(q) < 0) return;
      if (String(s.reports_to) === String(p.id)) mine.push(s); else (by[seatTeam(s)] = by[seatTeam(s)] || []).push(s);
    });
    var sel = O.sheet.sel || {};
    var ticked = function (s, dflt) { return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(sel, String(s.id)) ? sel[String(s.id)] : dflt; };
    var row = function (s, on0) { var on = ticked(s, on0); return '<label><input type="checkbox" data-w133pick="' + E(s.id) + '"' + (on ? ' checked' : '') + '><span>' + E(seatName(s)) + '</span><em>' + E(clip(s.title, 34)) + (on0 ? '' : ' \u00b7 now under ' + E(mgrShort(X, s))) + '</em></label>'; };
    var html = mine.length ? '<div class="gh">Report to ' + E(first(seatName(p))) + ' now</div>' + mine.sort(seatOrder).map(function (s) { return row(s, true); }).join('') : '';
    teams().concat(Object.keys(by).filter(function (k) { return teams().indexOf(k) < 0; })).forEach(function (d) { var l = by[d]; if (!l || !l.length) return; html += '<div class="gh">' + E(d ? teamLabel(d) : 'Other') + '</div>' + l.sort(seatOrder).map(function (s) { return row(s, false); }).join(''); });
    return html || '<p class="note">No one matches.</p>';
  }
  function view() {
    var s = O.sheet, X = ix();
    if (s.kind === 'new') {
      var under = s.under == null ? null : X.byId[s.under];
      return { kicker: 'The org chart', title: 'Add an open position', sub: under ? 'Reporting to ' + seatName(under) : (s.dept ? 'Leading ' + teamLabel(s.dept) : ''), body:
        '<div class="sec"><label class="f">Title<input type="text" id="w133ntitle" maxlength="120" placeholder="For example: Volunteer coordinator, Toronto" value="' + E(s.title || '') + '"></label>'
        + '<label class="f">Team<select id="w133nteam">' + teamOptions(s.dept || (under ? seatTeam(under) : teams()[0])) + '</select></label>'
        + '<label class="f">Reports to<select id="w133nmgr">' + mgrOptions(X, null, s.under) + '</select></label>'
        + '<h4>Where it stands</h4><div class="sts">' + ['open', 'hiring', 'on_hold'].map(function (k) { return '<button type="button" data-w133sts="' + k + '" class="' + ((s.status || 'open') === k ? 'on' : '') + '">' + STATUS[k][0] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
        + '<label class="f" style="margin-top:12px">Note<textarea id="w133nnote" maxlength="300" placeholder="For example: recruiting now, or once the fall budget is set">' + E(s.note || '') + '</textarea></label><div class="err" id="w133err">' + E(s.err || '') + '</div></div>',
        foot: '<span style="flex:1"></span><button type="button" class="w133btn" data-w133close="1">Cancel</button><button type="button" class="w133btn pri" data-w133do="create">' + I('plus') + 'Add the position</button>' };
    }
    if (s.kind === 'member') {
      var m = memberById(s.mid); if (!m) return null;
      var isBoard = m.department === 'board';
      var body = '<div class="sec"><p>' + (isBoard ? 'On the board of directors.' : (X.seatOf[m.id] ? '' : 'On the roster, not placed on the chart yet.')) + '</p>' + (isBoard && tabOk('governance') ? '<p><a class="w133lnk" data-w133go="#governance" style="color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:700;cursor:pointer">Open the Board overview</a></p>' : '') + '</div>';
      if (!isBoard && !X.seatOf[m.id] && O.canEdit) body += '<div class="sec"><h4>Place them on the chart</h4><label class="f">Reports to<select id="w133pmgr">' + mgrOptions(X, null, headOf(X, m.department)) + '</select></label><div class="err" id="w133err">' + E(s.err || '') + '</div><button type="button" class="w133btn pri" data-w133do="place">' + I('check') + 'Place ' + E(first(m.name)) + '</button></div>';
      return { kicker: isBoard ? 'Board of directors' : teamLabel(m.department), title: m.name, sub: m.role || '', body: body, foot: '<button type="button" class="w133btn" data-w133go="#person/' + E(m.id) + '">' + I('seat') + 'Open their profile</button><span style="flex:1"></span><button type="button" class="w133btn" data-w133close="1">Close</button>' };
    }
    var p = X.byId[s.id]; if (!p) return null;
    var mm = holder(p), ks = (X.kids[String(p.id)] || []).slice().sort(seatOrder), ed = O.canEdit;
    var html = '';
    /* reports to */
    if (p.kind === 'exec') html += '<div class="sec"><h4>Reports to</h4><p>The board of directors.</p></div>';
    else if (ed) html += '<div class="sec"><h4>Reports to</h4><select id="w133mgr">' + mgrOptions(X, p, p.reports_to) + '</select><div class="row" style="margin-top:10px"><button type="button" class="w133btn pri" data-w133do="mgr">' + I('check') + 'Save</button></div></div>';
    else { var g = p.reports_to == null ? null : X.byId[p.reports_to]; html += '<div class="sec"><h4>Reports to</h4>' + (g ? whoRow(X, g) : '<p>The executive directors.</p>') + '</div>'; }
    /* direct reports */
    if (ed) html += '<div class="sec"><h4>Direct reports' + (ks.length ? ' (' + ks.length + ')' : '') + '</h4><p class="note">Tick who reports to ' + E(mm ? first(mm.name) : 'this position') + '. Anyone you untick moves up to ' + E(mgrShort(X, p)) + '.</p>'
      + '<input type="search" id="w133pq" placeholder="Find someone" value="' + E(s.pq || '') + '" aria-label="Find someone"><div class="picks" id="w133picks">' + pickList(X, p) + '</div><div class="row" style="margin-top:10px"><button type="button" class="w133btn pri" data-w133do="reports">' + I('check') + 'Save direct reports</button></div></div>';
    else html += '<div class="sec"><h4>Direct reports' + (ks.length ? ' (' + ks.length + ')' : '') + '</h4>' + (ks.length ? ks.map(function (k) { return whoRow(X, k); }).join('') : '<p>No one reports to ' + E(mm ? first(mm.name) : 'this position') + '.</p>') + '</div>';
    /* the seat */
    if (!mm) {
      if (ed) html += '<div class="sec"><h4>The position</h4><label class="f">Title<input type="text" id="w133title" maxlength="120" value="' + E(p.title || '') + '"></label><label class="f">Team<select id="w133team">' + teamOptions(p.department || '') + '</select></label>'
        + '<h4>Where it stands</h4><div class="sts">' + ['open', 'hiring', 'on_hold'].map(function (k) { return '<button type="button" data-w133sts="' + k + '" class="' + ((s.status || p.status) === k ? 'on' : '') + '">' + STATUS[k][0] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
        + '<label class="f" style="margin-top:12px">Note<textarea id="w133note" maxlength="300">' + E(p.note || '') + '</textarea></label><div class="row"><button type="button" class="w133btn pri" data-w133do="save">' + I('check') + 'Save</button></div></div>'
        + '<div class="sec"><h4>Fill it</h4><select id="w133fill">' + fillOptions(X) + '</select><p class="note" style="margin-top:6px">Someone who already has a seat moves here, and their old seat stays on the chart as an open position.</p><div class="row"><button type="button" class="w133btn pri" data-w133do="fill">' + I('seat') + 'Put them in this position</button></div></div>';
      else html += '<div class="sec"><h4>The position</h4><p>' + statusChip(p) + ' ' + E(teamLabel(p.department)) + '</p>' + (p.note ? '<p>' + E(p.note) + '</p>' : '') + '</div>';
    } else if (ed) {
      html += '<div class="sec"><h4>The seat</h4><label class="f">Title (their role everywhere in the Hub)<input type="text" id="w133title" maxlength="120" value="' + E(p.title || mm.role || '') + '"></label><div class="row"><button type="button" class="w133btn pri" data-w133do="save">' + I('check') + 'Save the title</button></div></div>';
    }
    if (ed) {
      html += '<div class="sec"><h4>More</h4><div class="row"><button type="button" class="w133btn" data-w133addunder="' + E(p.id) + '">' + I('plus') + 'Add an open position under ' + E(mm ? first(mm.name) : 'it') + '</button>'
        + (mm && p.kind !== 'exec' ? '<button type="button" class="w133btn warn" data-w133do="vacate">' + I('out') + 'Take ' + E(first(mm.name)) + ' off this seat</button>' : '')
        + (!mm ? '<button type="button" class="w133btn warn" data-w133do="remove">' + I('trash') + 'Remove this position</button>' : '') + '</div><div class="err" id="w133err">' + E(s.err || '') + '</div></div>';
    }
    var foot = (mm ? '<button type="button" class="w133btn" data-w133go="#person/' + E(mm.id) + '">' + I('seat') + 'Open their profile</button>' : '<button type="button" class="w133btn" data-w133go="#requests/new/hr/hiring-request">' + I('mail') + 'Ask HR to recruit</button>') + '<span style="flex:1"></span><button type="button" class="w133btn" data-w133close="1">Close</button>';
    return { kicker: p.kind === 'exec' ? 'Executive director' : (mm ? teamLabel(seatTeam(p)) : 'Open position \u00b7 ' + teamLabel(p.department)), title: mm ? mm.name : (p.title || 'Open position'), sub: mm ? (p.title || mm.role || '') : (STATUS[p.status] || STATUS.open)[0] + (p.note ? ' \u00b7 ' + clip(p.note, 60) : ''), body: html, foot: foot };
  }
  function headOf(X, d) { var h = heads(X).filter(function (p) { return seatTeam(p) === d && !isOpen(p); })[0]; return h ? h.id : null; }
  function fillOptions(X) {
    var out = '<option value="">Pick someone</option>', up = unplaced(X);
    if (up.length) out += '<optgroup label="Not on the chart yet">' + up.map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(m.id) + '">' + E(m.name) + ' (' + E(teamShort(m.department)) + ')</option>'; }).join('') + '</optgroup>';
    teams().forEach(function (d) {
      var ms = members().filter(function (m) { return m.department === d && X.seatOf[m.id]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return lower(a.name).localeCompare(lower(b.name)); });
      if (ms.length) out += '<optgroup label="' + E(teamLabel(d)) + '">' + ms.map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(m.id) + '">' + E(m.name) + ' (now ' + E(clip(X.seatOf[m.id].title, 30)) + ')</option>'; }).join('') + '</optgroup>';
    });
    return out;
  }
  function renderSheet(first0) {
    var s = O.sheet; if (!s) { closeSheet(); return; }
    var v = view(); if (!v) { closeSheet(); return; }
    var el = sheetEl(), wasOn = el.classList.contains('on');
    if (!wasOn || !el.querySelector('.pane')) el.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w133close="1"></div><div class="pane" role="dialog" aria-modal="true"><div class="ph"></div><div class="bd"></div><div class="ft" style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:12px 20px calc(14px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.08);background:var(--wa-card,#FFFAF2)"></div></div>';
    var bd = el.querySelector('.bd'), st = bd.scrollTop;
    el.querySelector('.pane').setAttribute('aria-label', v.title);
    el.querySelector('.ph').innerHTML = '<div style="min-width:0"><div class="kk">' + E(v.kicker) + '</div><h2>' + E(v.title) + '</h2>' + (v.sub ? '<div class="sub">' + E(v.sub) + '</div>' : '') + '</div><span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="xb" data-w133close="1" aria-label="Close">' + I('x') + '</button>';
    bd.innerHTML = v.body; el.querySelector('.ft').innerHTML = v.foot;
    if (wasOn && !first0) bd.scrollTop = st;
    el.classList.add('on');
    if (first0) setTimeout(function () { var f = el.querySelector(s.kind === 'new' ? '#w133ntitle' : '.xb'); if (f) { try { f.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }, 40);
  }
  function sheetErr(msg) { if (!O.sheet) return; O.sheet.err = msg; O.sheet.busy = false; var e = document.getElementById('w133err'); if (e) e.textContent = msg; else renderSheet(false); }
  function val(id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); return el ? el.value : null; }
  async function sheetDo(what) {
    var s = O.sheet; if (!s || s.busy) return;
    var X = ix(), p = s.id != null ? X.byId[s.id] : null;
    s.busy = true; s.err = '';
    try {
      if (what === 'create') {
        var t = String(val('w133ntitle') || '').trim(); if (!t) { sheetErr('Give the position a title.'); return; }
        var mg = val('w133nmgr'); var res = await addOpening(mg ? Number(mg) : null, { title: t, dept: val('w133nteam'), status: s.status || 'open', note: val('w133nnote') });
        O.sheet = { kind: 'seat', id: res && res.id }; if (res && res.id) renderSheet(true); else closeSheet();
      } else if (what === 'place') {
        var pm = val('w133pmgr'); await place(s.mid, pm ? Number(pm) : null); var X2 = ix(); var seat = X2.seatOf[s.mid]; if (seat) { O.sheet = { kind: 'seat', id: seat.id }; renderSheet(true); } else closeSheet();
      } else if (what === 'mgr' && p) {
        var to = val('w133mgr'); var toId = to ? Number(to) : null;
        if (String(toId) === String(p.reports_to)) { s.busy = false; toastS('No change'); return; }
        await move(p, toId, null, false);
      } else if (what === 'reports' && p) {
        /* every tick, including the ones hidden by the search */
        var sel = s.sel || {}, ids = [];
        (O.pos || []).forEach(function (x) { var k = String(x.id); var on = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(sel, k) ? sel[k] : String(x.reports_to) === String(p.id); if (on && String(x.id) !== String(p.id)) ids.push(Number(x.id)); });
        await setReports(p, ids);
      } else if (what === 'save' && p) {
        var tt = String(val('w133title') || '').trim(); if (!tt) { sheetErr('Give the position a title.'); return; }
        await updateSeat(p, { title: tt, status: s.status || p.status, note: val('w133note'), dept: val('w133team') });
      } else if (what === 'fill' && p) {
        var who = val('w133fill'); if (!who) { sheetErr('Pick someone first.'); return; }
        await fill(p, Number(who));
      } else if (what === 'vacate' && p) {
        if (!confirm('Take ' + seatName(p) + ' off this seat? They stay on the roster, and ' + p.title + ' becomes an open position.')) { s.busy = false; return; }
        await vacate(p);
      } else if (what === 'remove' && p) {
        if (!confirm('Remove this open position?' + ((X.kids[String(p.id)] || []).length ? ' Whoever reports to it moves up a level.' : ''))) { s.busy = false; return; }
        await removeSeat(p);
      }
      if (O.sheet) { O.sheet.busy = false; }
    } catch (e) { sheetErr(errText(e)); }
  }

  /* ---------- rearranging: drag a card onto the person it reports to, or onto a team ---------- */
  function dragInfo(el) { var v = el && el.getAttribute('data-w133drag'); if (!v) return null; var i = v.indexOf(':'); return { kind: v.slice(0, i), id: v.slice(i + 1) }; }
  function dropInfo(el) { var v = el && el.getAttribute('data-w133drop'); if (v == null) return null; var i = v.indexOf(':'); return { kind: v.slice(0, i), id: v.slice(i + 1) }; }
  /* what a drop would do, or why it cannot */
  function plan(d, t) {
    var X = ix();
    if (!d || !t) return null;
    if (d.kind === 'pos') {
      var p = X.byId[d.id]; if (!p) return null;
      if (t.kind === 'pos') {
        if (String(t.id) === String(d.id)) return null;
        if (isUnder(X, t.id, d.id)) return { no: 'Someone cannot report to a person who reports to them.' };
        if (String(p.reports_to) === String(t.id)) return null;
        return { act: 'move', p: p, to: Number(t.id) };
      }
      if (t.kind === 'team') {
        if (!t.id) return p.reports_to == null ? null : { act: 'move', p: p, to: null };
        if (!isOpen(p) && seatTeam(p) !== t.id) return { no: seatName(p) + ' is on ' + teamShort(seatTeam(p)) + '. To change their team, use People and access; here you can have them report to anyone on ' + teamShort(t.id) + '.' };
        if (p.reports_to == null && seatTeam(p) === t.id) return null;
        return { act: 'move', p: p, to: null, dept: t.id };
      }
    }
    if (d.kind === 'member') {
      if (t.kind === 'pos') { var s = X.byId[t.id]; if (!s) return null; return isOpen(s) ? { act: 'fill', p: s, mid: Number(d.id) } : { act: 'place', mid: Number(d.id), to: Number(t.id) }; }
      if (t.kind === 'team') return { act: 'place', mid: Number(d.id), to: null };
    }
    return null;
  }
  function clearOver() { document.querySelectorAll('#w133 .over').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('over'); }); }
  document.addEventListener('dragstart', function (e) {
    var el = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w133 [data-w133drag]');
    if (!el || !O.arrange || !O.canEdit) return;
    O.drag = dragInfo(el); el.classList.add('dragging');
    try { e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', el.getAttribute('data-w133drag')); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
  });
  document.addEventListener('dragend', function () { O.drag = null; clearOver(); document.querySelectorAll('#w133 .dragging').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('dragging'); }); });
  document.addEventListener('dragover', function (e) {
    if (!O.drag) return;
    var el = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w133 [data-w133drop]'); if (!el) { clearOver(); return; }
    var pl = plan(O.drag, dropInfo(el));
    if (!pl) { clearOver(); return; }
    e.preventDefault();
    if (pl.no) { clearOver(); return; }
    if (!el.classList.contains('over')) { clearOver(); el.classList.add('over'); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('drop', function (e) {
    if (!O.drag) return;
    var el = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w133 [data-w133drop]');
    var d = O.drag; O.drag = null; clearOver();
    if (!el) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    doPlan(plan(d, dropInfo(el)));
  });
  async function doPlan(pl) {
    if (!pl) return;
    if (pl.no) { toastS(pl.no); return; }
    try {
      if (pl.act === 'move') await move(pl.p, pl.to, pl.dept || null, false);
      else if (pl.act === 'fill') await fill(pl.p, pl.mid);
      else if (pl.act === 'place') await place(pl.mid, pl.to);
    } catch (x) { toastS(errText(x)); }
  }

  /* ---------- who is on the Hub now (wave12's presence) ---------- */
  function loadOnline() {
    try {
      if (typeof waLoadPresence !== 'function' || typeof waOnlineEmails !== 'function') return Promise.resolve();
      return Promise.resolve(waLoadPresence()).then(function () { var on = {}; (waOnlineEmails() || []).forEach(function (r) { if (r && r.email) on[lower(r.email)] = 1; }); O.online = on; }).catch(function () { /* fine */ });
    } catch (e) { return Promise.resolve(); }
  }

  /* ---------- showing the chart ---------- */
  function onPage() { var s = document.getElementById('orgchart'); return !!(s && s.classList.contains('active')); }
  var pendingOpen = null;
  function show() {
    if (!staffOk()) return;
    hostEl();
    if (!O.pos) paint();
    load(!O.pos || Date.now() - O.at > 30000).then(function () {
      paint();
      loadOnline().then(function () { if (onPage()) paint(); });
      if (pendingOpen != null) { var mid = pendingOpen; pendingOpen = null; openFor(mid); }
    }).catch(function () {
      /* the chart could not load: the roster view stays */
      var h = document.getElementById('w133'); if (h) h.innerHTML = '<div class="w133err">' + E(O.err || 'The chart could not load.') + ' The team list below is the roster.</div>';
      oldCard(false);
    });
  }
  function openFor(mid) {
    var X = ix(), seat = X.seatOf[mid];
    if (seat) openSheet({ kind: 'seat', id: seat.id }); else if (memberById(mid)) openSheet({ kind: 'member', mid: Number(mid) });
    try { var el = document.querySelector('#w133 [data-w133m="' + mid + '"]'); if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  if (typeof renderOrgChart === 'function') {
    var __roc133 = renderOrgChart;
    renderOrgChart = async function () {
      var out;
      try { out = await __roc133.apply(this, arguments); } catch (e) { /* the old chart is only the fallback */ }

  /* ---------- a person's page: who they report to ---------- */
  function personLine() {
    try {
      var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, ''), mm = /^person\/(\d+)/.exec(h); if (!mm) return;
      var head = document.querySelector('#personpage .w116khead'); if (!head) return;

      if (cur && cur.getAttribute('data-m') === String(mid) && cur.getAttribute('data-v') === String(O.ver)) return;
      if (!O.pos) { if (!O.loading && staffOk()) load(false).then(personLine).catch(function () { /* fine */ }); return; }
      var X = ix(), seat = X.seatOf[mid], m = memberById(mid);
      if (!m || m.department === 'board' && !seat) { if (cur) cur.remove(); return; }
      var bits = [];
      if (seat) {
        if (seat.kind === 'exec') bits.push('Reports to the board');
        else if (seat.reports_to == null) bits.push('Reports to the executive directors');
        else { var g = X.byId[seat.reports_to], gm = holder(g); bits.push('Reports to ' + (gm ? '<a data-w133go="#person/' + E(gm.id) + '">' + E(gm.name) + '</a>' : 'an open position (' + E(g ? g.title : '') + ')')); }
        var ks = (X.kids[String(seat.id)] || []).slice().sort(seatOrder);
        var ppl = ks.filter(function (k) { return !isOpen(k); }), op = ks.length - ppl.length;
        if (ppl.length) bits.push(plural(ppl.length, 'direct report', 'direct reports') + ': ' + ppl.slice(0, 5).map(function (k) { var km = holder(k); return '<a data-w133go="#person/' + E(km.id) + '">' + E(km.name) + '</a>'; }).join(', ') + (ppl.length > 5 ? ' and ' + (ppl.length - 5) + ' more' : ''));
        if (op) bits.push(plural(op, 'open position', 'open positions') + ' under them');
      } else bits.push('Not placed on the org chart yet');
      bits.push('<a data-w133go="#orgchart/' + E(mid) + '">See on the org chart</a>');
      var html = bits.join(' \u00b7 ');
      if (!cur) { cur = document.createElement('div'); cur.className = 'w133pline'; var onb = head.querySelector('#w116konb'); if (onb) onb.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', cur); else { var tx = head.children[1] || head; tx.appendChild(cur); } }
      cur.setAttribute('data-m', String(mid)); cur.setAttribute('data-v', String(O.ver)); cur.innerHTML = html;
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  /* ---------- the page tour (wave122's engine; trainings row tour:orgchart) ---------- */

/* =====================================================================
   WA v134 the audit: one menu layout, and Ask the Hub's E (26/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "the department section is well formatted because it just has the departments but the
   other sections like strategy and other and admin like maybe regroup those into different
   categories on whatever makes sense" and "ASK THE HUB should have an animation that the E in THE
   should expand instead of the E alone on the left".

   - One place decides the menu: WA_NAV.layout below, applied by WA_NAV.applyLayout(). Home, the
     Executive dashboard and the Calendar sit at the top; Departments stay as they are; then Goals
     and projects, Meetings, Requests and inbox, People, Resources and Admin, whose sections (People
     and access, Security and audit trail, AI models ...) are menu items of their own. Directors
     (the board view) get a Board group. Buttons are moved, never rebuilt, so their wiring holds.
     The older placement code (waves 95, 116d, 116h, 124, 126, 132) defers to the layout when it is
     on, and page crumbs, the access profile editor and the menu tour use the same groups.
   - Ask the Hub: at rest the brand E; on hover or focus "ASK TH" and "HUB" open around it and its
     bars stretch, so the E is the E of THE.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa134) return; window.__wa134 = true;

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }

  /* ---------- the layout: the one list of where every page sits in the menu ---------- */

         places in the Human Resources and Fundraising rows (wave116), hidden the same way. */
      { key: 'apps', label: 'Apps', items: ['orggoals', 'goals', 'projects', 'goalmap', 'crm', 'partners', 'fund', 'studio', 'toronto', 'programs']   /* wave298: Program Builder */ },   /* wave193: the Fundraising app's page; wave225: the Content Studio's */   /* wave274: Toronto Volunteers' */
      /* Admin's own sections (wave118's links) follow the Admin page button, which the menu keeps out of sight */
      { key: 'admin', label: 'Admin', items: ['adminpanel', 'activity'] }
    ],
    /* the Events calendar sits in the Fundraising row for people who can open that team page, else right after the Calendar */
    afterCalendar: ['fundevents'],

    hidden: ['dashboard', 'create']

  /* wave163: directors (the board view) open no apps, so Partners (in the CRM for the staff) stays in their menu, in Organization */
  function directors() { try { return typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function itemsOf(g) {
    var ids = g.items || [];
    if (directors()) {
      if (g.key === 'org') ids = ids.concat(['partners']);
      /* wave205: directors read the goals (read only), in their Board group */
      if (g.key === 'board') ids = ids.concat(['orggoals']);
      if (g.key === 'apps') ids = ids.filter(function (x) { return x !== 'partners' && x !== 'orggoals'; });
    }
    return ids;
  }
  /* the label each button shows (the older code named a few differently) */
  var LABEL = { transcripts: 'Transcript archive' };

  /* ---------- the menu: move the buttons where the layout says ---------- */
  var OBS = null, busy = false, T = null, applied = 0, moves = [];
  function bar() { return document.getElementById('tabBar'); }
  function tabOf(root, id) { return root.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + id + '"]'); }
  function shown(el) { return !!el && el.style.display !== 'none'; }
  function kids(el) { return Array.prototype.slice.call(el.children); }
  /* a page button inside a team row whose team page this person can open (it stays in that row) */
  function inOpenRow(el) {
    var row = el && el.closest ? el.closest('.w42d') : null; if (!row) return false;
    var main = row.querySelector(':scope > .tab');
    return !!main && main !== el && shown(main) && row.getAttribute('data-w127hid') !== '1' && !row.classList.contains('w127flat');
  }
  function relabel(btn, text) {
    if (!btn || (btn.textContent || '').trim() === text) return;
    var dot = btn.querySelector('.tabdot');
    btn.innerHTML = (dot ? dot.outerHTML : '') + E(text);
  }
  function groupDef(key) { for (var i = 0; i < LAYOUT.groups.length; i++) if (LAYOUT.groups[i].key === key) return LAYOUT.groups[i]; return null; }
  function groupEl(b, g) {
    var el = b.querySelector('.tab-group[data-w42g="' + g.key + '"]');
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement('span'); el.className = 'tab-group'; el.setAttribute('data-w42g', g.key);
      var h = document.createElement('button'); h.className = 'tab-group-h'; h.type = 'button';
      var p = document.createElement('div'); p.className = 'tab-group-p';
      el.appendChild(h); el.appendChild(p);
      h.addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
        ev.stopPropagation();
        var was = el.classList.contains('open');
        document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .tab-group.open').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('open'); });
        if (!was) el.classList.add('open');
      });
    }
    if (!el.hasAttribute('data-w134')) el.setAttribute('data-w134', '1');
    var hd = el.querySelector(':scope > .tab-group-h');
    if (hd && g.label && g.items && hd.getAttribute('data-w134l') !== g.label) { hd.innerHTML = E(g.label) + ' <span class="caret">&#9660;</span>'; hd.setAttribute('data-w134l', g.label); }
    return el;
  }
  function hostOf(el) { return el.querySelector(':scope > .tab-group-p') || el; }
  function putAt(host, el, i) {
    if (host.children[i] === el) return;
    host.insertBefore(el, host.children[i] || null);
    moves.push(Date.now());
  }
  /* the buttons each group should hold, in order, for this person */
  function wanted(b, g) {
    var out = [];
    itemsOf(g).forEach(function (id) {
      var t = tabOf(b, id); if (!t) return;
      if (g.loose && inOpenRow(t)) return;
      out.push(t);
      if (id === 'adminpanel') { var sub = adminSub(b); if (sub) out.push(sub); }
    });
    return out;
  }
  /* Admin's section links (wave118 draws them after the Admin button; an older menu rebuild can drop them) */
  function adminSub(b) {
    var sub = b.querySelector('.w118sub');
    if (sub) return sub;
    var A = window.__wa118 && window.__wa118.ADMIN; if (!Array.isArray(A) || !A.length) return null;
    sub = document.createElement('div'); sub.className = 'w118sub';
    sub.innerHTML = A.map(function (a) { return '<a href="#adminpanel/' + E(a[0]) + '" data-w118sec="' + E(a[0]) + '">' + E(a[1]) + '</a>'; }).join('');
    sub.addEventListener('click', function () { try { document.body.classList.remove('wa-mob-open'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
    var cur = (String(location.hash || '').match(/^#adminpanel\/([a-z]+)/) || [])[1] || A[0][0];
