  }
  function isHidden(t) { var id = t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-tab'); return LAYOUT.hidden.indexOf(id) >= 0 || !!t.getAttribute('data-w73hide'); }
  function apply() {
    var b = bar(); if (!b || !WA_NAV.layout) return;
    /* a safety valve: if something keeps moving buttons back, stop arguing for a while */
    var now = Date.now(); moves = moves.filter(function (t) { return now - t < 10000; });
    if (moves.length > 120) { console.warn('wave134: the menu keeps moving; layout paused for 10 s'); return; }
    busy = true; if (OBS) OBS.disconnect();
    try {
      var seq = [];
      LAYOUT.top.forEach(function (id) { var t = tabOf(b, id); if (t) seq.push(t); });
      LAYOUT.afterCalendar.forEach(function (id) { var t = tabOf(b, id); if (t && !inOpenRow(t)) { t.classList.remove('w116tool'); seq.push(t); } });
      (LAYOUT.afterEvents || []).forEach(function (id) { var t = tabOf(b, id); if (t) { t.classList.remove('w116tool'); seq.push(t); } });
      var res = null;
      LAYOUT.groups.forEach(function (g) {
        if (g.fold) { seq.push(foldEl(b, g)); return; }   /* wave262: the More fold's header */
        if (!g.items) { var d = b.querySelector('.tab-group[data-w42g="' + g.key + '"]'); if (d) seq.push(d); return; }
        var el = groupEl(b, g), host = hostOf(el);
        wanted(b, g).forEach(function (t, i) {
          if (g.loose) t.classList.remove('w116tool');
          var id = t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-tab');
          if (id && LABEL[id]) relabel(t, LABEL[id]);
          putAt(host, t, i);
        });
        if (g.key === 'tools') res = host;   /* wave163: Other Tools takes what the layout does not name */
        seq.push(el);
      });
      /* what the layout does not name: an older group (Strategy, More) gives up its buttons and goes;
         a page registered later lands in Resources until the layout names it */
      kids(b).forEach(function (el) {
        if (seq.indexOf(el) >= 0) return;
        if (el.classList.contains('tab')) {
          if (isHidden(el)) return;
          if (res) { res.appendChild(el); moves.push(Date.now()); }
          return;
        }
        if (el.classList.contains('tab-group')) {
          kids(hostOf(el)).forEach(function (t) {
            if (!t.classList || !t.classList.contains('tab')) return;
            if (isHidden(t)) b.appendChild(t); else if (res) res.appendChild(t);
            moves.push(Date.now());
          });
          if (!el.querySelector('.tab, .w42d')) el.parentNode.removeChild(el);
        }
      });
      seq.forEach(function (el, i) { putAt(b, el, i); });
      heads(b);
      folds(b);
    } catch (e) { console.warn('wave134 layout', e); }
    busy = false; applied++;
    watch();
  }
  /* group headers show only when the group has something this person can open; Admin shows its sections */
  function heads(b) {
    kids(b).forEach(function (g) {
      if (!g.classList || !g.classList.contains('tab-group')) return;
      var key = g.getAttribute('data-w42g');
      if (key === 'departments') return;   /* wave116 looks after the Departments header */
      if (key === 'w262more') return;   /* wave262: folds() decides the More header (not 'more': that was an older group's key) */
      var any = Array.prototype.some.call(g.querySelectorAll('.tab'), function (t) { return shown(t) && t.getAttribute('data-tab') !== 'adminpanel'; });
      if (key === 'admin') {
        var adm = tabOf(g, 'adminpanel'), on = !!adm && shown(adm);
        if (g.classList.contains('w134on') !== on) g.classList.toggle('w134on', on);
        any = any || on;
      }
      var want = any ? '' : 'none';
      var h = g.querySelector(':scope > .tab-group-h');
      if (h && h.style.display !== want) h.style.display = want;
      if (g.style.display !== want) g.style.display = want;
    });
  }
  /* ---------- wave262: the folds (More, and the other teams) ----------
     Steel (20/10/26): keep each person's menu simple, "just really keeping the main important things on the main page and
     then a drop down of other tabs". Nothing is taken away: the buttons stay where the layout puts them and a fold only
     hides them until it is opened (a click, remembered on this device), and it opens by itself when you are on one of its
     pages. Computers only: the phone's Menu sheet (wave163b) already lists every page with its own Find a page. */
  var FOLD_KEY = { more: 'wa262.more', teams: 'wa262.teams' };
  function remembered(k) { try { return localStorage.getItem(FOLD_KEY[k]); } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function remember(k, v) { try { localStorage.setItem(FOLD_KEY[k], v ? '1' : '0'); } catch (e) { /* private mode */ } }
  function amAdmin() { try { return typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && !!WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'admin'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  /* the person's own team: their roster department, when it has a team row they can open */
  function myTeam(b) {
    var d = '';
    try { d = (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined' && WA_ME && WA_ME.department) || ''; } catch (e) { d = ''; }
    if (!d) return '';
    var t = tabOf(b, d), row = t && t.closest ? t.closest('.w42d') : null;
    return row && shown(t) ? d : '';
  }
  function foldEl(b, g) {
    var el = b.querySelector('.tab-group[data-w42g="' + g.key + '"]');
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement('span'); el.className = 'tab-group w262fold'; el.setAttribute('data-w42g', g.key);
      el.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="w262fh" aria-expanded="false" data-w262fold="more"><span class="w262lb">' + E(g.label) + '</span><span class="w262ch" aria-hidden="true">&#9662;</span></button>';
      el.querySelector('button').addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
        ev.stopPropagation();
        var open = !(bar() && bar().classList.contains('w262more'));
        remember('more', open); folds(bar(), true);
      });
    }
    return el;
  }
  function teamsToggle(host) {
    var t = host.querySelector(':scope > .w262tt');
    if (!t) {
      t = document.createElement('button'); t.type = 'button'; t.className = 'w262tt'; t.setAttribute('data-w262fold', 'teams');
      t.addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
        ev.stopPropagation();
        var open = !(bar() && bar().classList.contains('w262teams'));
        remember('teams', open); folds(bar(), true);
      });
      host.appendChild(t);
    }
    return t;
  }
  function activeTab(b) { return b.querySelector('.tab.active'); }
  function folds(b, byHand) {
    if (!b) return;
    try {
      /* More: Other Tools and Organization */
      var more = b.querySelector(':scope > .tab-group[data-w42g="w262more"]');
      var inside = (groupDef('w262more') || {}).fold || [];
      var any = inside.some(function (k) { var g = b.querySelector(':scope > .tab-group[data-w42g="' + k + '"]'); return !!g && g.style.display !== 'none' && Array.prototype.some.call(g.querySelectorAll('.tab'), shown); });
      var act = activeTab(b), here = !!act && inside.some(function (k) { var g = b.querySelector(':scope > .tab-group[data-w42g="' + k + '"]'); return !!g && g.contains(act); });
      var r = remembered('more'), open = here || r === '1';
      if (more) {
        var want = any ? '' : 'none'; if (more.style.display !== want) more.style.display = want;
        var hb = more.querySelector('.w262fh'); if (hb && hb.getAttribute('aria-expanded') !== String(open)) hb.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
      }
      if (b.classList.contains('w262more') !== open) b.classList.toggle('w262more', open);
      /* Teams: your team first; the others behind "Other teams" */
      var dep = b.querySelector(':scope > .tab-group[data-w42g="departments"]'), host = dep ? hostOf(dep) : null;
      var mine = myTeam(b);
      if (!host) return;
      var rows = Array.prototype.filter.call(host.children, function (c) { return c.classList && c.classList.contains('w42d'); });
      var others = rows.filter(function (row) { var m = row.querySelector(':scope > .tab'); return !(m && m.getAttribute('data-tab') === mine); });
      var visibleOthers = others.filter(function (row) { return row.style.display !== 'none' && Array.prototype.some.call(row.querySelectorAll('.tab'), shown); });
      var useFold = !!mine && visibleOthers.length > 0;
      if (dep.classList.contains('w262tf') !== useFold) dep.classList.toggle('w262tf', useFold);
      rows.forEach(function (row) {
        var isOther = others.indexOf(row) >= 0, isHere = isOther && ((!!act && row.contains(act)) || row.classList.contains('w116act'));
        if (row.classList.contains('w262other') !== (useFold && isOther)) row.classList.toggle('w262other', useFold && isOther);
        if (row.classList.contains('w262here') !== isHere) row.classList.toggle('w262here', isHere);
        if (!isOther && useFold && !row.hasAttribute('data-w262mine')) row.setAttribute('data-w262mine', '1');
      });
      if (!useFold) { var old = host.querySelector(':scope > .w262tt'); if (old && old.style.display !== 'none') old.style.display = 'none'; if (b.classList.contains('w262teams')) b.classList.remove('w262teams'); return; }
      var tr = remembered('teams'), topen = tr === null ? amAdmin() : tr === '1';
      if (b.classList.contains('w262teams') !== topen) b.classList.toggle('w262teams', topen);
      var tt = teamsToggle(host); if (tt.style.display) tt.style.display = '';
      var label = (topen ? 'Hide other teams' : 'Other teams') + ' (' + visibleOthers.length + ')';
      if (tt.getAttribute('data-l') !== label) { tt.setAttribute('data-l', label); tt.innerHTML = '<span>' + E(label) + '</span><span class="w262ch" aria-hidden="true">&#9662;</span>'; }
      if (tt.getAttribute('aria-expanded') !== String(topen)) tt.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(topen));
    } catch (e) { console.warn('wave262 folds', e); }
  }

  /* true when every page is where the layout wants it (the tests ask this) */
  function ok() {
    var b = bar(); if (!b) return true;
    var loose = LAYOUT.top.concat(LAYOUT.afterEvents || []);
    for (var i = 0; i < loose.length; i++) { var t = tabOf(b, loose[i]); if (t && t.parentNode !== b) return false; }
    for (var j = 0; j < LAYOUT.groups.length; j++) {
      var g = LAYOUT.groups[j]; if (!g.items) continue;
      var el = b.querySelector('.tab-group[data-w42g="' + g.key + '"]'), w = wanted(b, g);
      if (w.length && !el) return false;
      var host = el ? hostOf(el) : null;
      for (var k = 0; k < w.length; k++) if (w[k].parentNode !== host || host.children[k] !== w[k]) return false;
    }
    return !b.querySelector(':scope > .tab-group[data-w42g="strategy"], :scope > .tab-group[data-w42g="more"], :scope > .tab-group[data-w42g="goals"], :scope > .tab-group[data-w42g="resources"], :scope > .tab-group[data-w42g="people"], :scope > .tab-group[data-w42g="meetings"], :scope > .tab-group[data-w42g="inbox"]');
  }
  function watch() {
    var b = bar(); if (!b) return;
    if (!OBS) OBS = new MutationObserver(function () {
      if (busy) return;
      clearTimeout(T);
      T = setTimeout(function () { try { apply(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 90);
    });
    OBS.observe(b, { childList: true, subtree: true, attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['style'] });
  }

  /* ---------- Ask the Hub: the brand E is the E of THE ---------- */
  function fab() {
    var b = document.getElementById('waChatBtn'); if (!b) return false;
    var ei = b.querySelector('svg.w117ei'); if (!ei) return false;   /* wave117 draws the E; until then, wait */
    var w = b.querySelector(':scope > .w134w');
    if (!w) {
      w = document.createElement('span'); w.className = 'w134w'; w.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      w.innerHTML = '<span class="w134a">Ask th</span><span class="w134e"></span><span class="w134b">Hub</span>';
      b.insertBefore(w, b.firstChild);
    }
    var slot = w.querySelector('.w134e');
    if (ei.parentNode !== slot) slot.appendChild(ei);
    if (!b.classList.contains('w134fab')) b.classList.add('w134fab');
    if (b.getAttribute('aria-label') !== 'Ask the Hub') b.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Ask the Hub');
    if (!b.__w134) {
      b.__w134 = true;
      /* if a later wave redraws the button, put the words back around the E */
      try { new MutationObserver(function () { if (!b.querySelector(':scope > .w134w .w117ei')) setTimeout(fab, 0); }).observe(b, { childList: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    }
    return true;
  }

  /* ---------- Important info: everyone sees the list; a password is shown only to the people an admin approved ----------
     Steel (26/09/26): "By default the important info section with passwords should be there for everyone and if they
     want to see a password they must request it". The database no longer hands the password column to the page:
     info_secret() returns it to admins, to whoever added the login and to approved people (and logs the view),
     info_request() asks, info_decide() and info_grant() are the admin's answers (wa134_passwords). */
  var PW = { acc: [], shown: {}, asking: null, open: {} };
  function amAdmin() { try { return typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && !!WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'admin'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function myEmail() { try { return String((USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase(); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function nameOf(email) {
    var e = String(email || '').toLowerCase();
    try { var m = (DB_MEMBERS || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.email || '').toLowerCase() === e; })[0]; if (m) return m.name; } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
    return e.split('@')[0] || e;
  }
  function ago(iso) {
    var d = Math.round((Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime()) / 86400000);
    return d <= 0 ? 'today' : d === 1 ? 'yesterday' : d + ' days ago';
  }
  function rpcInfo(fn, args) { return Promise.resolve(db.rpc(fn, args || {})).then(function (r) { if (r && r.error) throw r.error; return r ? r.data : null; }); }
  function errMsg(e) { var m = String((e && (e.message || e.details)) || e || ''); return m.replace(/^.*?ERROR:\s*/, '') || 'Something went wrong.'; }
  function statusOf(linkId) {
    var me = myEmail();
    var a = PW.acc.filter(function (x) { return String(x.link_id) === String(linkId) && String(x.email).toLowerCase() === me; })[0];
    return a ? a.status : '';
  }
  function canSee(i) { return amAdmin() || statusOf(i.id) === 'granted'; }

  async function renderInfo134() {
    var body = document.getElementById('infoBody');
    if (!body) return;
    if (!isStaff()) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">This page is available to Welfare Avenue staff accounts only.</p>'; return; }
    var r = await db.from('info_links').select('id,kind,label,url,username,department,notes,sort,added_by,updated_at').order('kind').order('sort').order('label');
    if (r.error) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Could not load. Refresh to retry.</p>'; return; }
    INFO_CACHE = r.data || [];
    try { var a = await db.from('info_link_access').select('*').order('requested_at', { ascending: false }); PW.acc = (a && !a.error && a.data) || []; } catch (e) { PW.acc = []; }
    paintInfo();
    badge();
  }
  function pwRow(i) {
    var st = statusOf(i.id), see = canSee(i), id = String(i.id);
    var out = (i.username ? ' <span class="w134user">' + E(i.username) + '</span>' : '');
    if (see) {
      out += ' <span class="sec" data-sec="' + E(id) + '">' + (PW.shown[id] ? E(PW.shown[id]) : '&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;') + '</span>'
        + ' <button class="btn ghost mini" type="button" data-w134show="' + E(id) + '">' + (PW.shown[id] ? 'Hide' : 'Show') + '</button>'
        + ' <button class="btn ghost mini" type="button" data-w134copy="' + E(id) + '">Copy</button>';
    } else if (st === 'requested') {
      out += ' <span class="sec">&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;</span> <span class="w134chip">Requested, waiting for an admin</span>';
    } else if (PW.asking === id) {
      out += ' <span class="w134ask"><input type="text" maxlength="300" id="w134why" placeholder="What do you need it for? (optional)" aria-label="Why you need it">'
        + '<button class="btn mini w134go" type="button" data-w134send="' + E(id) + '">Send the request</button><button class="btn ghost mini" type="button" data-w134cancel="1">Cancel</button></span>';
    } else {
      out += ' <span class="sec">&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;</span>'
        + (st === 'declined' ? ' <span class="w134chip no">Declined</span>' : '')
        + ' <button class="btn ghost mini" type="button" data-w134ask="' + E(id) + '">' + (st === 'declined' ? 'Ask again' : 'Request access') + '</button>';
    }
    if (i.url) out += ' <a href="' + E(i.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="font-size:12px;">Open &#8599;</a>';
    if (amAdmin()) {
      var who = PW.acc.filter(function (x) { return String(x.link_id) === id && x.status === 'granted'; });
      out += ' <button class="w134who" type="button" data-w134who="' + E(id) + '">' + (who.length === 1 ? '1 person can see it' : who.length + ' people can see it') + '</button>';
      if (PW.open[id]) {
        var have = {}; who.forEach(function (x) { have[String(x.email).toLowerCase()] = 1; });
        var opts = []; try { opts = (DB_MEMBERS || []).filter(function (m) { return m.email && !have[String(m.email).toLowerCase()]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); }); } catch (e) { opts = []; }
        out += '<div class="w134acc">' + (who.length ? who.map(function (x) {
          return '<span class="w134p">' + E(nameOf(x.email)) + (x.decided_by === 'added it' ? ' <small>(added it)</small>' : '') + ' <button type="button" class="w134rm" data-w134revoke="' + E(x.id) + '" title="Remove their access" aria-label="Remove ' + E(nameOf(x.email)) + '">&#10005;</button></span>';
        }).join('') : '<span class="w134none">Only admins so far.</span>')
          + '<span class="w134give"><select id="w134give-' + E(id) + '" aria-label="Give access to"><option value="">Give access to...</option>' + opts.map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(String(m.email).toLowerCase()) + '">' + E(m.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
          + '<button class="btn ghost mini" type="button" data-w134give="' + E(id) + '">Give</button></span></div>';
      }
    }
    return out;
  }
  function paintInfo() {
    var body = document.getElementById('infoBody'); if (!body) return;
    var admin = amAdmin(), me = (typeof who === 'function' ? who() : '');
    var kinds = [
      { k: 'password', title: 'Logins and passwords', note: '<div class="pw-note">Everyone on staff sees this list. Passwords stay hidden: press <b>Request access</b> and an admin approves it, then you can show and copy it. Whoever adds a login can always see it, and every time a password is shown it goes in the audit trail. Keep it to shared team logins (Canva, social accounts, tools); never banking, government or personal credentials. Board viewers never see this page.</div>' },
      /* wave163: Steel: "remove trainings from there"; training videos live in the Avenue Academy (the old rows stay in info_links, unshown) */
      { k: 'folder', title: 'Shared Google Drive folders', note: '' },
      { k: 'link', title: 'Other key links', note: '' }
    ];
    var pend = PW.acc.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'requested'; });
    var reqHtml = '';
    if (admin && pend.length) {
      reqHtml = '<div class="w134req"><b>' + (pend.length === 1 ? '1 request' : pend.length + ' requests') + ' to see a password</b>' + pend.map(function (x) {
        var l = INFO_CACHE.filter(function (i) { return String(i.id) === String(x.link_id); })[0];
        return '<div class="w134rq"><span class="t"><b>' + E(nameOf(x.email)) + '</b> asked for <b>' + E(l ? l.label : 'a login') + '</b>' + (x.note ? ': ' + E(x.note) : '') + ' <small>' + E(ago(x.requested_at)) + '</small></span>'
          + '<button class="btn mini w134go" type="button" data-w134decide="' + E(x.id) + '" data-s="granted">Grant</button><button class="btn ghost mini" type="button" data-w134decide="' + E(x.id) + '" data-s="declined">Decline</button></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
    }
    body.innerHTML = kinds.map(function (K) {
      var rows = INFO_CACHE.filter(function (i) { return i.kind === K.k; });
      return '<h3>' + K.title + '</h3>' + K.note + (K.k === 'password' ? reqHtml : '') + (rows.length ? rows.map(function (i) {
        var dep = i.department && DEPT_LABEL[i.department] ? '<span class="dep" style="background:' + (DEPT_COLOR[i.department] || '#797773') + '">' + DEPT_LABEL[i.department] + '</span>' : '';
        var main2 = '<span class="lbl">' + E(i.label) + '</span>' + dep;
        if (K.k === 'password') main2 += pwRow(i);
        else main2 += (i.url ? ' <a href="' + E(i.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="font-size:12px;font-weight:600;">Open &#8599;</a>' : '');
        var mayDel = admin || (i.added_by && me && i.added_by === me);
        return '<div class="info-row" data-w134row="' + E(i.id) + '">' + main2
          + (mayDel ? '<button class="mini-x" type="button" data-w134del="' + E(i.id) + '" title="Remove" aria-label="Remove ' + E(i.label) + '">&#10005;</button>' : '')
          + (i.notes ? '<div class="nts">' + E(i.notes) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
      }).join('') : '<div class="info-row" style="color:var(--wa-muted);">Nothing here yet. Add the first one below.</div>');
    }).join('')
      + '<div class="info-add"><select id="ii-kind"><option value="password">Password / login</option><option value="folder">Drive folder</option><option value="link">Other link</option></select>'
      + '<input id="ii-label" placeholder="Label, e.g. Canva team login" />'
      + '<input id="ii-url" placeholder="URL (optional for passwords)" />'
      + '<input id="ii-user" placeholder="Username / email (passwords)" />'
      + '<input id="ii-secret" placeholder="Password (passwords only)" type="password" autocomplete="new-password" />'
      + '<select id="ii-dept"><option value="">No department</option>' + DEPT_ORDER.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '">' + DEPT_LABEL[d] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
      + '<input id="ii-notes" placeholder="Notes (optional)" />'
      + '<button class="btn mini" id="ii-add" type="button" style="background:var(--wa-green);color:#FFFAF2;border:none;border-radius:7px;cursor:pointer;">Add</button></div>';
  }
  /* the admin's menu item shows how many requests are waiting */
  function badge() {
    try {
      var t = document.querySelector('#tabBar .tab[data-tab="importantinfo"]'); if (!t) return;
      var n = amAdmin() ? PW.acc.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'requested'; }).length : 0;
      var b = t.querySelector('.w134n');
      if (!n) { if (b) b.parentNode.removeChild(b); return; }
      if (!b) { b = document.createElement('span'); b.className = 'w134n'; t.appendChild(b); }
      if (b.textContent !== String(n)) b.textContent = String(n);
      b.setAttribute('title', n === 1 ? '1 request to see a password' : n + ' requests to see a password');
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  async function badgeAtBoot() {
    if (!amAdmin()) return;
    try { var a = await db.from('info_link_access').select('id,link_id,email,status,requested_at').eq('status', 'requested'); if (a && !a.error) { PW.acc = a.data || []; badge(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('#infoBody')) return;
    var el;
    try {
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w134show]'))) {
        var id = el.getAttribute('data-w134show');
        if (PW.shown[id]) { delete PW.shown[id]; paintInfo(); return; }
        var r = await rpcInfo('info_secret', { p_link_id: Number(id) });
        PW.shown[id] = (r && r.secret) || '(empty)'; paintInfo(); return;
      }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w134copy]'))) {
        var cid = el.getAttribute('data-w134copy');
        var s = PW.shown[cid];
        if (!s) { var r2 = await rpcInfo('info_secret', { p_link_id: Number(cid) }); s = r2 && r2.secret; }
        if (s) { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(s); toast('Copied'); }
        return;
      }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w134ask]'))) { PW.asking = el.getAttribute('data-w134ask'); paintInfo(); var f = document.getElementById('w134why'); if (f) f.focus(); return; }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w134cancel]'))) { PW.asking = null; paintInfo(); return; }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w134send]'))) {
        var sid = el.getAttribute('data-w134send'), why = (document.getElementById('w134why') || {}).value || '';
        el.disabled = true;
        var st = await rpcInfo('info_request', { p_link_id: Number(sid), p_note: why });
        PW.asking = null;
        toast(st === 'granted' ? 'You can see it now' : 'Request sent. An admin will approve it.');
        await renderInfo134(); return;
      }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w134decide]'))) {
        el.disabled = true;
        var out = await rpcInfo('info_decide', { p_access_id: Number(el.getAttribute('data-w134decide')), p_status: el.getAttribute('data-s') });
        toast(out === 'granted' ? 'Granted' : 'Declined');
        await renderInfo134(); return;
      }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w134who]'))) { var wid = el.getAttribute('data-w134who'); PW.open[wid] = !PW.open[wid]; paintInfo(); return; }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w134revoke]'))) {
        if (!confirm('Remove their access to this password?')) return;
        await rpcInfo('info_decide', { p_access_id: Number(el.getAttribute('data-w134revoke')), p_status: 'revoked' });
        toast('Access removed'); await renderInfo134(); return;
      }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w134give]'))) {
        var gid = el.getAttribute('data-w134give'), sel = document.getElementById('w134give-' + gid);
        if (!sel || !sel.value) { toast('Pick a person'); return; }
        await rpcInfo('info_grant', { p_link_id: Number(gid), p_email: sel.value });
        toast('Access given'); await renderInfo134(); return;
      }
      if ((el = t.closest('[data-w134del]'))) {
        if (!confirm('Remove this entry for everyone?')) return;
        var d = await db.from('info_links').delete().eq('id', Number(el.getAttribute('data-w134del')));
        if (d && d.error) throw d.error;
        await renderInfo134(); return;
      }
      if ((el = t.closest('#ii-add'))) {
        var v = function (x) { return ((document.getElementById(x) || {}).value || '').trim(); };
        if (!v('ii-label')) { toast('Add a label'); return; }
        var ins = await db.from('info_links').insert({ kind: v('ii-kind') || 'link', label: v('ii-label'), url: v('ii-url') || null, username: v('ii-user') || null, secret: v('ii-secret') || null, department: v('ii-dept') || null, notes: v('ii-notes') || null, added_by: (typeof who === 'function' ? who() : null) });
        if (ins && ins.error) { toast('Could not save'); return; }
        toast('Added'); await renderInfo134(); return;
      }
    } catch (err) { toast(errMsg(err)); try { await renderInfo134(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } }
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && e.target && e.target.id === 'w134why') { var b = document.querySelector('[data-w134send]'); if (b) b.click(); }
  });
  /* the page's own renderer (wave4) becomes this one; every caller (the route, the tours) uses the global name */
  try { if (typeof renderInfoPage === 'function') renderInfoPage = renderInfo134; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  /* ---------- styles ---------- */
  var css = ''
    /* Admin: the section links are the menu items; the Admin page button stays out of sight (it still carries the access rule) */
    + 'nav.wa-nav #tabBar .tab-group[data-w42g="admin"] .tab[data-tab="adminpanel"]{display:none !important;}'
    + 'nav.wa-nav #tabBar .tab-group[data-w42g="admin"] .w118sub{display:none !important;padding:0 !important;}'

/* =====================================================================
   WA v138 @mentions: bring someone in, and they hear about it on Slack (26/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "I want the ability to @ someone anywhere in the hub and it will send them a direct message
   from me in Slack - no asking this is done automatically. I'd like the same capabilities for
   everyone at the charity. So if Ian comments @joy on a content task on slack Ian will have sent
   her a message of the comment and link to the task on the hub"

   - Type @ in a task comment or description, a goal comment, a request note, a team update, a
     decision, a committee note, a project description or a Content calendar post's notes: a list of
     people opens (the roster, with photos and teams); pick one with the keys or the mouse.
   - Under the field, a line says who will get a Slack message and how (as you, or from the Hub with
     your name until you connect Slack). Nothing to confirm: saving is sending.
   - The database does the sending (wa138_mentions): a trigger on each of those places files the new
     mentions in hub_mentions, and notify (comment_mention) sends the DM with the text and a link.
   - Settings, Connections: "Send @mentions as you" connects your own Slack account (slack-connect,
     Slack's sign-in page), and lists your recent mentions with what happened to each. Settings,
     Notifications: "Someone @mentions me in the Hub" can be switched off.
   - Mentions show as a highlighted name in task comments, goal comments and request notes.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa138) return; window.__wa138 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', GOLD = '#DCA527', FLAG = '#B3402F', INK = '#1F201E', MUTED = '#797773', SLACK = '#4A154B';
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var HEAD = "'Barlow Condensed','Arial Narrow','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var PAL = [['#103F39', '#FFFAF2'], ['#DCA527', '#1F201E'], ['#2A8C7A', '#FFFAF2'], ['#E9DCC4', '#103F39'], ['#8C6A2E', '#FFFAF2'], ['#B3402F', '#FFFAF2']];

  /* the places people write that send @mentions (each has a trigger in wa138_mentions) */
  var FIELDS = [
    { sel: '#w116cmtx', kind: 'comment' },                               /* task drawer comment -> task_comments */
    { sel: '#w51new', kind: 'comment' },                                 /* task comments window -> task_comments */
    { sel: '#w116ted', kind: 'desc' },                                   /* task description (rich) -> dept_tasks */
    { sel: 'input[data-glci]', kind: 'comment' },                        /* goal comment -> goal_comments */
    { sel: '#w205drawer textarea[data-w205cmt]', kind: 'comment' },        /* wave205: a comment on a goal or a milestone, and replies -> goal_comments */
    { sel: 'textarea[data-w123note]', kind: 'comment' },                 /* request note -> request_notes */
    { sel: '#w116ut', kind: 'save' },                                    /* team update -> dept_updates */
    { sel: '#dmMove', kind: 'save' }, { sel: '#ddNote', kind: 'save' },  /* decision -> decisions */
    { sel: '#w116pd', kind: 'save' }, { sel: '#w116ed', kind: 'save' },  /* project description -> projects */
    { sel: '#w121notes', kind: 'save' },                                 /* Content calendar notes for the team -> content_posts */
    { sel: '#w126sheet textarea[name="summary"]', kind: 'save', priv: true },          /* committee note -> board_touchpoints */
    { sel: '#w126sheet textarea[name="recommendation"]', kind: 'save', priv: true }
  ];
  var FIELD_SEL = FIELDS.map(function (f) { return f.sel; }).join(',');
  function fieldOf(el) {
    if (!el || !el.matches) return null;
    for (var i = 0; i < FIELDS.length; i++) { try { if (el.matches(FIELDS[i].sel)) return FIELDS[i]; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    return null;
  }

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).toLowerCase().trim(); }
  function toastS(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function staffOk() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  /* anyone signed in can mention (directors write committee notes too); connecting Slack is for staff (slack-connect checks) */
  function signedIn() { return !!myEmail(); }
  function teamShort(d) {
    try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.short && WA_TEAMS.short[d]) || (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; }
  }

  /* ---------- the roster the page already holds (team_members, active) ---------- */
  var R = { src: null, list: [], byLen: [] };
  function roster() {
    var src = null;
    try { src = (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' && DB_MEMBERS) || null; } catch (e) { src = null; }
    if (src !== R.src) {
      R.src = src;
      R.list = (src || []).filter(function (m) { return m && m.active !== false && String(m.name || '').trim(); });
      /* the database picks the longest name after the @ (then the lowest id): the page does the same */
      R.byLen = R.list.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.name).trim().length - String(a.name).trim().length || Number(a.id) - Number(b.id); });
    }
    return R.list;
  }
  function meRow() { var em = myEmail(); return roster().filter(function (m) { return lower(m.email) === em; })[0] || null; }
  function initials(name) { var p = String(name || '').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); if (!p.length) return '?'; return (p[0][0] + (p.length > 1 ? p[p.length - 1][0] : '')).toUpperCase(); }
  function palette(name) { var h = 0, s = String(name || ''); for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0; return PAL[h % PAL.length]; }
  function avatar(m, size) {
    var st = 'width:' + size + 'px;height:' + size + 'px;font-size:' + Math.round(size * 0.4) + 'px;';
    if (m && m.avatar_url) return '<span class="w138av" style="' + st + '"><img src="' + E(m.avatar_url) + '" alt="" loading="lazy" /></span>';
    var p = palette(m && m.name);
    return '<span class="w138av" style="' + st + 'background:' + p[0] + ';color:' + p[1] + '">' + E(initials(m && m.name)) + '</span>';
  }

  /* who a text @mentions, exactly as the database reads it (wa_mentioned_members): after each @ that is not part of an
     email address, the longest roster name that follows and ends on a word boundary (@joy.dumansi is a handle) */
  var WORD = /[0-9A-Za-z_\u00c0-\u024f]/;
  function mentioned(text) {
    var t = String(text || ''), low = t.toLowerCase(), out = [], seen = {};
    roster();
    if (t.indexOf('@') < 0) return out;
    for (var i = 0; i < t.length && i < 8000; i++) {
      if (t.charAt(i) !== '@') continue;
      if (i > 0 && (WORD.test(t.charAt(i - 1)) || t.charAt(i - 1) === '.')) continue;
      var rest = low.slice(i + 1, i + 61);
      for (var j = 0; j < R.byLen.length; j++) {
        var m = R.byLen[j], n = String(m.name).trim().toLowerCase();
        if (rest.indexOf(n) !== 0) continue;
        var nx = rest.charAt(n.length), nx2 = rest.charAt(n.length + 1);
        if (nx && WORD.test(nx)) continue;
        if (nx === '.' && nx2 && WORD.test(nx2)) continue;
        if (!seen[m.id]) { seen[m.id] = 1; out.push(m); }
        break;
      }
    }
    return out;
  }
  function names(list) {
    var n = list.map(function (m) { return String(m.name).trim(); });
    if (n.length <= 1) return n.join('');
    return n.slice(0, -1).join(', ') + ' and ' + n[n.length - 1];
  }

  var P = { open: false, el: null, ce: false, node: null, at: 0, caret: 0, q: '', list: [], idx: 0, box: null };
  function pickBox() {
    if (P.box && document.body.contains(P.box)) return P.box;
    var b = document.createElement('div');
    b.id = 'w138pick'; b.setAttribute('role', 'listbox'); b.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Mention someone');
    document.body.appendChild(b);
    /* mousedown, not click: the field keeps its focus (the task description saves when it loses it) */
    b.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) {
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
      var it = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w138i]');
      if (it) choose(Number(it.getAttribute('data-w138i')));
    });
    b.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) {
      var it = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w138i]'); if (!it) return;
      var i = Number(it.getAttribute('data-w138i')); if (i !== P.idx) { P.idx = i; paintPick(); }
    });
    P.box = b; return b;
  }

  /* what is typed after the @ right before the caret, if it can be a mention */
  function tokenIn(text, caret) {
    var before = String(text || '').slice(0, caret);
    var at = before.lastIndexOf('@');
    if (at < 0 || caret - at > 32) return null;
    var prev = at > 0 ? before.charAt(at - 1) : '';
    if (prev && (WORD.test(prev) || prev === '.')) return null;          /* an email address */
    var q = before.slice(at + 1);
    if (/[\n\r@]/.test(q) || /^\s/.test(q)) return null;
    return { at: at, q: q };
  }
  /* the team the field is about: the task in the drawer, else the team page, else mine */
  function contextTeam(el) {
    try {
      if (el && el.closest && el.closest('#w116dr')) {
        var m = /#(\d+)/.exec(((document.querySelector('#w116dr .w116drh') || {}).textContent) || '');
        var t = m && (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === m[1]; })[0];
        if (t && t.department) return t.department;
      }
      var h = /^#team\/([a-z]+)/.exec(location.hash || ''); if (h) return h[1];
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return (meRow() || {}).department || '';
  }
  function candidates(q, el) {
    q = String(q || '').toLowerCase().replace(/\u00a0/g, ' ');
    var me = myEmail(), mine = contextTeam(el), out = [];
    roster().forEach(function (m) {
      if (me && lower(m.email) === me) return;
      var n = String(m.name).trim().toLowerCase(), local = lower(m.email).split('@')[0], s = -1;
      if (!q) s = 3;
      else if (n.indexOf(q) === 0) s = 0;
      else if (n.split(/\s+/).some(function (w) { return w.indexOf(q) === 0; })) s = 1;
      else if (q.length >= 2 && local && (local.indexOf(q) === 0 || local.split(/[._-]/).some(function (w) { return w.indexOf(q) === 0; }))) s = 2;
      if (s >= 0) out.push({ m: m, s: s });
    });
    out.sort(function (a, b) {
      /* people Slack can reach (an email on the roster) before those it cannot, then the team the field is about */
      return a.s - b.s || ((a.m.email ? 0 : 1) - (b.m.email ? 0 : 1)) || ((a.m.department === mine ? 0 : 1) - (b.m.department === mine ? 0 : 1)) || String(a.m.name).localeCompare(String(b.m.name));
    });
    return out.slice(0, 6).map(function (x) { return x.m; });
  }

  /* where the caret is on screen */
  function caretRect(el, pos) {
    var r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    try {
      var cs = getComputedStyle(el), div = document.createElement('div'), st = div.style, isInput = el.tagName === 'INPUT';
      ['boxSizing', 'width', 'borderTopWidth', 'borderRightWidth', 'borderBottomWidth', 'borderLeftWidth', 'paddingTop', 'paddingRight', 'paddingBottom', 'paddingLeft',
        'fontStyle', 'fontVariant', 'fontWeight', 'fontStretch', 'fontSize', 'lineHeight', 'fontFamily', 'textAlign', 'textTransform', 'textIndent', 'letterSpacing', 'wordSpacing', 'tabSize'
      ].forEach(function (p) { st[p] = cs[p]; });
      st.position = 'absolute'; st.visibility = 'hidden'; st.top = '0'; st.left = '-9999px'; st.overflow = 'hidden';
      st.whiteSpace = isInput ? 'pre' : 'pre-wrap'; st.wordWrap = 'break-word'; st.height = 'auto';
      div.textContent = el.value.slice(0, pos);
      var sp = document.createElement('span'); sp.textContent = el.value.slice(pos, pos + 1) || '.'; div.appendChild(sp);
      document.body.appendChild(div);
      var lh = parseFloat(cs.lineHeight); if (!(lh > 0)) lh = (parseFloat(cs.fontSize) || 14) * 1.35;
      var x = r.left + (parseFloat(cs.borderLeftWidth) || 0) + sp.offsetLeft - el.scrollLeft, y = r.top + (parseFloat(cs.borderTopWidth) || 0) + sp.offsetTop - el.scrollTop;
      document.body.removeChild(div);
      x = Math.max(r.left, Math.min(x, r.right - 12)); y = Math.max(r.top, Math.min(y, r.bottom - lh));
      return { left: x, top: y, bottom: y + lh };
    } catch (e) { return { left: r.left, top: r.top, bottom: r.bottom }; }
  }
  function rangeRect(range, el) {
    try {
      var rs = range.getClientRects(); if (rs && rs.length) return rs[rs.length - 1];
      var b = range.getBoundingClientRect(); if (b && (b.top || b.left)) return b;
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return el.getBoundingClientRect();
  }

  function refresh(el) {
    if (!el || !signedIn()) { closePick(); return; }
    var tok = null;
    if (el.isContentEditable) {
      var sel = window.getSelection();
      if (!sel || !sel.rangeCount || !sel.isCollapsed) { closePick(); return; }
      var rg = sel.getRangeAt(0), node = rg.startContainer;
      if (!node || node.nodeType !== 3 || !el.contains(node)) { closePick(); return; }
      tok = tokenIn(node.data, rg.startOffset);
      if (!tok) { closePick(); return; }
      P.ce = true; P.node = node; P.caret = rg.startOffset;
    } else {
      if (typeof el.selectionStart !== 'number' || el.selectionStart !== el.selectionEnd) { closePick(); return; }
      tok = tokenIn(el.value, el.selectionStart);
      if (!tok) { closePick(); return; }
      P.ce = false; P.node = null; P.caret = el.selectionStart;
    }
    var list = candidates(tok.q, el);
    if (!list.length) { closePick(); return; }
    var same = P.open && P.el === el && P.q === tok.q;
    P.el = el; P.at = tok.at; P.q = tok.q; P.list = list;
    if (!same) P.idx = 0;
    P.open = true;
    paintPick();
    placePick();
  }
  function paintPick() {
    var b = pickBox();
    b.innerHTML = '<div class="hd">Mention someone</div>' + P.list.map(function (m, i) {
      var team = teamShort(m.department) || m.role || '';
      return '<div class="it' + (i === P.idx ? ' on' : '') + '" role="option" aria-selected="' + (i === P.idx ? 'true' : 'false') + '" data-w138i="' + i + '">' + avatar(m, 28) +
        '<div><b>' + E(String(m.name).trim()) + '</b><small>' + E(team) + (m.email ? '' : (team ? ' \u00b7 ' : '') + '<span class="nomail">no email, so no Slack message</span>') + '</small></div></div>';
    }).join('') + '<div class="ft">\u2191 \u2193 to choose, Enter to mention, Esc to close</div>';
    b.classList.add('on');
  }
  function placePick() {
    var b = pickBox(), el = P.el; if (!el) return;
    var c;
    if (P.ce) { var r = document.createRange(); try { r.setStart(P.node, P.caret); r.collapse(true); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } c = rangeRect(r, el); }
    else c = caretRect(el, P.at);
    var w = b.offsetWidth || 280, h = b.offsetHeight || 240, vw = window.innerWidth, vh = window.innerHeight;
    var left = Math.max(8, Math.min(c.left - 10, vw - w - 8));
    var top = c.bottom + 6;
    if (top + h > vh - 8 && c.top - h - 6 > 8) top = c.top - h - 6;
    b.style.left = Math.round(left) + 'px'; b.style.top = Math.round(Math.max(8, top)) + 'px';
  }
  function closePick() {
    P.open = false; P.list = []; P.node = null;
    if (P.box) { P.box.classList.remove('on'); P.box.innerHTML = ''; }
  }
  function choose(i) {
    var m = P.list[i]; if (!m || !P.el) { closePick(); return; }
    var el = P.el, name = String(m.name).trim(), ok = false;
    if (P.ce) {
      var node = P.node; if (!node || !el.contains(node)) { closePick(); return; }
      var text = '@' + name + ' ';
      if (document.activeElement !== el) el.focus();
      var r = document.createRange(); r.setStart(node, P.at); r.setEnd(node, Math.min(P.caret, node.data.length));
      var sel = window.getSelection(); sel.removeAllRanges(); sel.addRange(r);
      try { ok = document.execCommand('insertText', false, text); } catch (e) { ok = false; }
      if (!ok) {
        r.deleteContents(); var tn = document.createTextNode('@' + name + '\u00a0'); r.insertNode(tn);
        var r2 = document.createRange(); r2.setStartAfter(tn); r2.collapse(true); sel.removeAllRanges(); sel.addRange(r2);
        el.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
      }
    } else {
      var v = el.value, after = v.slice(P.caret);
      var ins = '@' + name + (/^\s/.test(after) ? '' : ' ');
      el.focus();
      try { el.setSelectionRange(P.at, P.caret); ok = document.execCommand('insertText', false, ins); } catch (e2) { ok = false; }
      if (!ok || el.value.slice(P.at, P.at + ins.length) !== ins) {
        el.value = v.slice(0, P.at) + ins + after;
        var p = P.at + ins.length; try { el.setSelectionRange(p, p); } catch (e3) { /* fine */ }
        el.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
      }
    }
    closePick();
    hintSoon(el, 0);
  }

  /* ---------- the line under a field: who gets a Slack message, and from whom ---------- */
  var C = { at: 0, connected: null, loading: null };   /* my own slack_links row: have I connected Slack? */
  function loadLink(force) {
    if (!signedIn()) return Promise.resolve(null);
    if (C.loading) return C.loading;
    if (!force && C.at && Date.now() - C.at < 120000) return Promise.resolve(C.connected);
    C.loading = Promise.resolve(db.from('slack_links').select('slack_user_id, connected_at').eq('email', myEmail()).maybeSingle()).then(function (r) {
      C.loading = null; C.at = Date.now(); C.connected = !!(r && r.data && r.data.slack_user_id); return C.connected;
    }, function () { C.loading = null; C.at = Date.now(); C.connected = false; return false; });
    return C.loading;
  }
  function textOf(el) { return el.isContentEditable ? (el.innerText || el.textContent || '') : String(el.value == null ? '' : el.value); }
  /* the hint sits under the field, or under its row when the field shares a row with its Post button */
  function hintHost(el) {
    var p = el.parentElement; if (!p) return el;
    for (var i = 0; i < p.children.length; i++) { var c = p.children[i]; if (c !== el && c.tagName === 'BUTTON') return p; }
    return el;
  }
  function hintText(list, f) {
    var withMail = list.filter(function (m) { return !!m.email; }), noMail = list.filter(function (m) { return !m.email; });
    var when = f.kind === 'comment' ? 'when you post it' : (f.kind === 'desc' ? 'once it saves' : 'when you save');
    var s = '';
    if (withMail.length) {
      var who = E(names(withMail)), verb = withMail.length === 1 ? ' gets' : ' get';
      if (C.connected === true) s = who + verb + ' a Slack message from you ' + when + (f.priv ? ', saying where to look (the note itself stays in the Hub).' : ', with what you wrote and a link back here.');
      else if (C.connected === false) s = who + verb + ' a Slack message ' + when + ', from the Hub with your name' + (f.priv ? ' (the note itself stays in the Hub)' : '') + '.' + (staffOk() ? ' To send it as you, connect Slack in Settings.' : '');
      else s = who + verb + ' a Slack message ' + when + '.';
    }
    if (noMail.length) s += (s ? ' ' : '') + '<span class="warn">' + E(names(noMail)) + (noMail.length === 1 ? ' has' : ' have') + ' no email on the Hub, so no Slack message.</span>';
    return SLACK_SVG + '<div>' + s + '</div>';
  }
  function dropHint(el) {
    var h = el.__w138hint; if (h && h.parentNode) h.parentNode.removeChild(h);
    el.__w138hint = null;
    if (el.__w138w) { clearInterval(el.__w138w); el.__w138w = null; }
  }
  function hint(el) {
    if (!el) return;
    if (!document.body.contains(el)) { dropHint(el); return; }
    var f = fieldOf(el);
    var list = f && signedIn() ? mentioned(textOf(el)) : [];
    if (list.length) {
      var me = myEmail(), before = el.__w138base ? mentioned(el.__w138base).map(function (m) { return m.id; }) : [];
      list = list.filter(function (m) { return lower(m.email) !== me && before.indexOf(m.id) < 0; });
    }
    if (!list.length) { dropHint(el); return; }
    if (C.connected === null && !C.loading) loadLink().then(function () { hint(el); });
    var html = hintText(list, f), h = el.__w138hint;
    if (!h || !document.body.contains(h)) {
      h = document.createElement('div'); h.className = 'w138hint'; h.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
      hintHost(el).insertAdjacentElement('afterend', h); el.__w138hint = h;
    }
    if (h.__html !== html) { h.innerHTML = html; h.__html = html; }
    /* a posted comment empties its field without an input event: look again now and then while the line shows */
    if (!el.__w138w) el.__w138w = setInterval(function () { hint(el); }, 900);
  }
  function hintSoon(el, ms) { clearTimeout(el.__w138t); el.__w138t = setTimeout(function () { hint(el); }, ms == null ? 250 : ms); }

  /* ---------- Settings, Connections: Send @mentions as you ---------- */
  var S = { status: null, at: 0, busy: '', recent: null, banner: null };
  function slackFn(body) {
    return Promise.resolve(db.functions.invoke('slack-connect', { body: body })).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) throw new Error(String(r.error.message || r.error));
      return (r && r.data) || {};
    });
  }
  function loadStatus(force) {
    if (!force && S.status && !S.status.error && Date.now() - S.at < 60000) return Promise.resolve(S.status);
    return slackFn({ action: 'status' }).then(function (d) {
      S.status = d && typeof d === 'object' ? d : {}; S.at = Date.now();
      if ('connected' in S.status) { C.connected = !!S.status.connected; C.at = Date.now(); }
      return S.status;
    }, function (e) { S.status = { error: String((e && e.message) || e || 'no answer') }; S.at = Date.now(); return S.status; });
  }
  function loadRecent() {
    return Promise.resolve(db.from('hub_mentions').select('id, created_at, to_name, kind, ref_title, private, dm_status, dm_via, dm_error')
      .eq('from_email', myEmail()).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(5)).then(function (r) { S.recent = (r && r.data) || []; return S.recent; }, function () { S.recent = []; return S.recent; });
  }
  var KIND = { task: 'a task', goal: 'a goal', request: 'a request', update: 'a team update', decision: 'a decision', committee: 'a committee note', project: 'a project', content: 'a post' };
  function day(iso) { try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', timeZone: 'America/Toronto' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso || '').slice(0, 10); } }
  function outcome(m) {
    if (m.dm_status === 'sent') return '<em>' + (m.dm_via === 'user' ? 'Sent as you' : 'Sent from the Hub') + '</em>';
    if (m.dm_status === 'pending') return '<em class="wait">Sending</em>';
    var why = String(m.dm_error || '');
    if (/opted out/.test(why)) return '<em class="wait">They turned these off</em>';
    if (/removed/.test(why)) return '<em class="wait">You took it out</em>';
    if (/no Slack account/.test(why)) return '<em class="no">Not sent: no Slack account found</em>';
    if (/no email/.test(why)) return '<em class="no">Not sent: no email on the Hub</em>';
    return '<em class="no">Not sent' + (m.dm_status === 'failed' ? ': Slack refused it' : '') + '</em>';
  }
  function connInner() {
    var st = S.status, on = !!(st && st.connected), sub, cls = '', right = '';
    if (!st) sub = 'Checking your Slack connection...';
    else if (st.error) { sub = 'Could not check the connection. ' + E(String(st.error).slice(0, 120)); cls = ' err'; right = '<button class="w138btn ghost" data-w138c="retry">Try again</button>'; }
    else if (on) { sub = 'On. People you @mention in the Hub get a Slack message from you, like any message you send.' + (st.since ? ' Connected ' + E(day(st.since)) + '.' : ''); cls = ' ok'; right = '<button class="w138btn ghost" data-w138c="disconnect"' + (S.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy === 'disconnect' ? 'Disconnecting...' : 'Disconnect') + '</button>'; }
    else if (st.ready === false) sub = 'People you @mention in the Hub get a Slack message from the Hub with your name. Sending it as you is not set up yet.';
    else { sub = 'People you @mention in the Hub get a Slack message. Connect your Slack account and it comes from you; until then, it comes from the Hub with your name.'; right = '<button class="w138btn" data-w138c="start"' + (S.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy === 'start' ? 'Opening Slack...' : 'Connect Slack') + '</button>'; }
    var out = '';
    if (S.banner) out += '<div class="w138banner' + (S.banner.ok ? '' : ' err') + '">' + E(S.banner.text) + '</div>';
    out += '<div class="row w138row"><span class="ic" style="background:' + SLACK + '">@</span><div><b>Send @mentions as you</b><div class="s' + cls + '">' + sub + '</div></div>' + right + '</div>';
    var rec = S.recent || [];
    if (rec.length) {
      out += '<div class="w138rec"><div class="t">Your latest @mentions</div>' + rec.map(function (m) {
        var what = (KIND[m.kind] || 'something') + (m.ref_title && !m.private ? ': ' + m.ref_title : '');
        return '<div class="r"><span>' + E(String(m.to_name || 'Someone')) + ', on ' + E(what) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(day(m.created_at)) + '</span>' + outcome(m) + '</div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
    }
    return out;
  }
  function connRow() { return staffOk() ? '<div id="w138conn">' + connInner() + '</div>' : ''; }
  function repaintConn() { var box = document.getElementById('w138conn'); if (box) box.innerHTML = connInner(); }
  function afterPaint(tab) {
    if (tab !== 'connections' || !document.getElementById('w138conn')) return;
    var stale = !S.status || S.status.error || Date.now() - S.at > 60000;
    if (stale) loadStatus(true).then(repaintConn);
    loadRecent().then(repaintConn);
  }
  function act(el) {
    var a = el.getAttribute('data-w138c');
    if (a === 'retry') { S.status = null; repaintConn(); loadStatus(true).then(repaintConn); return; }
    if (a === 'start') {
      if (S.busy) return; S.busy = 'start'; S.banner = null; repaintConn();
      slackFn({ action: 'start' }).then(function (d) {
        if (d && d.url && /^https:\/\/slack\.com\//.test(d.url)) { location.href = d.url; return; }
        S.busy = ''; repaintConn(); toastS(String((d && d.error) || 'Slack did not answer. Try again.').slice(0, 160));
      }, function () { S.busy = ''; repaintConn(); toastS('Could not reach the Hub. Try again.'); });
      return;
    }
    if (a === 'disconnect') {
      if (S.busy) return; S.busy = 'disconnect'; S.banner = null; repaintConn();
      slackFn({ action: 'disconnect' }).then(function (d) {
        S.busy = '';
        if (d && d.ok) { toastS('Disconnected. Your @mentions come from the Hub with your name again.'); C.connected = false; C.at = Date.now(); }
        else toastS(String((d && d.error) || 'Could not disconnect. Try again.').slice(0, 160));
        return loadStatus(true);
      }, function () { S.busy = ''; toastS('Could not reach the Hub. Try again.'); }).then(repaintConn);
    }
  }

  /* back from Slack's page: slack-connect sends the browser to /?slack=connected (or error, cancelled) */
  var BACK = (function () {
    try {
      var q = new URLSearchParams(location.search || ''), v = q.get('slack'); if (!v) return null;
      var why = q.get('why') || ''; q.delete('slack'); q.delete('why');
      var rest = q.toString();
      history.replaceState(history.state, '', location.pathname + (rest ? '?' + rest : '') + location.hash);
      return { result: v, why: why };
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  })();
  if (BACK) {
    var tries = 0;
    (function wait() {
      var ready = document.documentElement.classList.contains('wa-ready') && typeof window.w116Settings === 'function' && myEmail() && staffOk();
      if (!ready) { if (++tries < 120) setTimeout(wait, 500); return; }
      if (BACK.result === 'connected') S.banner = { ok: true, text: 'Slack is connected. From now on, the people you @mention get a Slack message from you.' };
      else if (BACK.result === 'cancelled') S.banner = { ok: false, text: 'Slack was not connected: you cancelled on Slack\u2019s page. Your @mentions still reach people, from the Hub with your name.' };
      else S.banner = { ok: false, text: 'Slack was not connected. ' + (BACK.why || 'Something went wrong on the way back from Slack.') };
      S.status = null; C.connected = null; C.at = 0;
      setTimeout(function () { S.banner = null; }, 45000);
      toastS(BACK.result === 'connected' ? 'Slack connected: your @mentions now come from you' : 'Slack was not connected');
      try { window.w116Settings('connections'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    })();
  }

  /* ---------- names shown as mentions in comments and notes (the renderers call this on escaped text) ---------- */
  function markText(t) {
    var low = t.toLowerCase(), out = '', last = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < t.length; i++) {
      if (t.charAt(i) !== '@') continue;
      if (i > 0 && (WORD.test(t.charAt(i - 1)) || t.charAt(i - 1) === '.')) continue;
      var rest = low.slice(i + 1, i + 91);
      for (var j = 0; j < R.byLen.length; j++) {
        var n = E(String(R.byLen[j].name).trim()).toLowerCase();
        if (!n || rest.indexOf(n) !== 0) continue;
        var nx = rest.charAt(n.length), nx2 = rest.charAt(n.length + 1);
        if (nx && WORD.test(nx)) continue;
        if (nx === '.' && nx2 && WORD.test(nx2)) continue;
        out += t.slice(last, i) + '<span class="w138at">' + t.slice(i, i + 1 + n.length) + '</span>';
        last = i + 1 + n.length; i = last - 1;
        break;
      }
    }
    return out + t.slice(last);
  }
  function mark(html) {
    var s = String(html == null ? '' : html);
    if (s.indexOf('@') < 0) return s;
    roster(); if (!R.byLen.length) return s;
    return s.split(/(<[^>]*>)/).map(function (part) { return !part || part.charAt(0) === '<' ? part : markText(part); }).join('');
  }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  function stop(e) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); if (e.stopImmediatePropagation) e.stopImmediatePropagation(); }
  function isField(el) { return !!(el && el.matches && fieldOf(el)); }
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var el = e.target;
    if (!isField(el)) { if (P.open && P.el !== el) closePick(); return; }
    refresh(el); hintSoon(el);
  });
  document.addEventListener('focusin', function (e) {
    var el = e.target; if (!isField(el)) return;

  });

  });

    if (P.box && P.box.contains(t)) return;

  }, true);

    if (k === 'Enter' || k === 'Tab') { if (e.shiftKey || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) { closePick(); return; } stop(e); choose(P.idx); return; }
    if (k === 'Escape') { stop(e); closePick(); }
  }, true);
  window.addEventListener('scroll', function () { if (P.open) placePick(); }, true);
  window.addEventListener('resize', function () { if (P.open) closePick(); });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { closePick(); });

  window.w138Mentioned = function (text) { return mentioned(text).map(function (m) { return String(m.name).trim(); }); };

  function I(n, cls) { return '<svg class="w140i' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : '') + '" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">' + (P[n] || P.spark) + '</svg>'; }
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;'); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).toLowerCase().trim(); }
  /* for matching: lower case, accents off (\u00c9milie finds emilie) */
  function fold(s) { try { return lower(s).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, ''); } catch (e) { return lower(s); } }
  function clip(s, n) { s = String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); return s.length > n ? s.slice(0, n - 1).trim() + '\u2026' : s; }
  function toastS(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }

  function nowIso() { return new Date().toISOString(); }
  function yearNow() { try { return Number(new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto' }).slice(0, 4)) || new Date().getFullYear(); } catch (e) { return new Date().getFullYear(); } }

  function myName() {

  /* ---------- dates: a moment's date has a precision (day, month, season, year) ---------- */
  function parts(s) { var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/.exec(String(s || '')); return m ? { y: Number(m[1]), m: Number(m[2]), d: Number(m[3]) } : null; }
  function fmtWhen(s, prec) {
    var p = parts(s); if (!p) return '';
    if (prec === 'year') return String(p.y);
    if (prec === 'month' || prec === 'season') return MONTHS[p.m - 1] + ' ' + p.y;
    return p.d + ' ' + MONTHS[p.m - 1] + ' ' + p.y;
  }
  function fmtSide(s, prec) {
    var p = parts(s); if (!p) return '';
    if (prec === 'year') return '';
    if (prec === 'month' || prec === 'season') return MON[p.m - 1];
    return p.d + ' ' + MON[p.m - 1];
  }
  function fmtRange(h) {
    var a = fmtWhen(h.date_start, h.date_precision);
    if (!h.date_end) return a;
    var b = fmtWhen(h.date_end, h.date_end_precision || h.date_precision || 'day');
    return b && b !== a ? a + ' to ' + b : a;
  }
  /* what someone types (2021-03-20, 2021-03 or 2021) as a start, and as an end */
  function daysIn(y, mo) { return new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo, 0)).getUTCDate(); }
  function readDate(s, isEnd) {
    s = String(s || '').trim();
    var m, y, mo;
    if ((m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})$/.exec(s))) {
      y = Number(m[1]); mo = Number(m[2]);
      if (mo < 1 || mo > 12 || Number(m[3]) < 1 || Number(m[3]) > daysIn(y, mo)) return null;
      return { d: m[1] + '-' + pad(m[2]) + '-' + pad(m[3]), p: 'day' };
    }
    if ((m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})$/.exec(s))) {
      y = Number(m[1]); mo = Number(m[2]);
      if (mo < 1 || mo > 12) return null;
      return { d: m[1] + '-' + pad(m[2]) + '-' + (isEnd ? pad(daysIn(y, mo)) : '01'), p: 'month' };
    }
    if ((m = /^(\d{4})$/.exec(s))) return { d: m[1] + (isEnd ? '-12-31' : '-01-01'), p: 'year' };
    return null;
  }
  function asTyped(s, prec) { var p = parts(s); if (!p) return ''; if (prec === 'year') return String(p.y); if (prec === 'month' || prec === 'season') return p.y + '-' + pad(p.m); return p.y + '-' + pad(p.m) + '-' + pad(p.d); }
  function numbersText(n) {
    if (!n || typeof n !== 'object') return '';
    var out = [], f = function (v) { return Number(v).toLocaleString('en-CA'); };
    if (n.meals) out.push(f(n.meals) + ' meals');
    if (n.people_helped) out.push(f(n.people_helped) + ' people helped');

    if (n.attendees) out.push(f(n.attendees) + ' attendees');
    if (n.raised_cad) out.push('$' + f(n.raised_cad) + ' raised');
    if (n.other) out.push(String(n.other));
    return out.join(' \u00b7 ');
  }

  /* the database decides what each person reads (RLS); the page keeps to the same rule, so nothing
     from a leadership document or a hidden moment can show to someone the database would not show it to */
  function allowed(what, rows) {
    if (isLeaderUI()) return rows;
    if (what === 'docs') return rows.filter(function (d) { return d.visibility !== 'leadership'; });
    if (what === 'hist' || what === 'lessons') return rows.filter(function (r) { return r.status !== 'hidden' && (r.visibility !== 'leadership' || seesLead()); });
    if (what === 'partners') return rows.filter(function (r) { return r.visibility !== 'leadership' || seesLead(); });
    return rows;
  }
  function load(what, force) {
    if (S.loading[what]) return S.loading[what];
    if (S[what] && !force && Date.now() - (S.at[what] || 0) < 120000) return Promise.resolve(S[what]);
    if (typeof db === 'undefined' || !db || !db.from) return Promise.resolve(S[what] || []);
    S.loading[what] = Promise.resolve(LOADERS[what]()).then(function (res) {
      S.loading[what] = null;
      if (res && res.error) { S.err[what] = String(res.error.message || res.error); S[what] = S[what] || []; return S[what]; }
      S.err[what] = ''; S[what] = allowed(what, (res && res.data) || []); S.at[what] = Date.now();
      if (what === 'partners') indexPartners();
      return S[what];
    }).catch(function (e) { S.loading[what] = null; S.err[what] = String((e && e.message) || e); S[what] = S[what] || []; return S[what]; });
    return S.loading[what];
  }

  function indexPartners() {

  /* the type of a partner: the category, else read from how they help (older rows, the tests' fixture) */
  function catOf(p) {

    if (r === 'collaborator') return 'collaborator';
    if (r === 'sponsor' || r === 'funder' || r === 'donor organisation') return 'sponsor';

  }

  /* a source: the document's name, where it sits, and the words */
  function srcLabel(s) {
    var u = String(s.url || '');
    if (s.where === 'Social posts') return /instagram\.com/.test(u) ? 'Instagram post' : /facebook\.com/.test(u) ? 'Facebook post' : /linkedin\.com/.test(u) ? 'LinkedIn post' : 'Our social posts';
    return String(s.name || 'A document').replace(/\.(pdf|docx?|pptx?|xlsx?|txt)$/i, '');
  }
  function srcHtml(list, label) {
    list = Array.isArray(list) ? list : [];
    if (!list.length) return '';
    var lead = seesLead();
    return '<details class="w140src"><summary>' + I('quote') + E(label || plural(list.length, 'source', 'sources')) + '</summary><ul>' + list.map(function (s) {
      var hide = !!s.lead && !lead;
      if (hide) return '<li class="lk">' + I('lock') + '<span>A board or leadership document</span></li>';
      var nm = E(srcLabel(s));
      return '<li>' + (s.url ? '<a href="' + E(s.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + nm + ' ' + I('ext') + '</a>' : '<b>' + nm + '</b>') +
        (s.where && s.where !== 'Social posts' ? '<span class="w">' + E(s.where) + '</span>' : '') + (s.quote ? '<q>' + E(clip(s.quote, 280)) + '</q>' : '') + '</li>';
    }).join('') + '</ul></details>';
  }

  /* the masthead and the host of a page */
  function mastFor(id, crumb, title, blurb) {
    var sec = document.getElementById(id); if (!sec) return null;
    var m = sec.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
    if (!m) { m = document.createElement('div'); m.className = 'w118mast'; m.setAttribute('data-w140', id); sec.insertBefore(m, sec.firstChild); }
    var html = '<div class="crumb">' + E(crumb) + '</div><h1>' + E(title) + '</h1><p>' + E(blurb) + '</p>';
    if (m.getAttribute('data-h') !== html) { m.innerHTML = html; m.setAttribute('data-h', html); }
    var h = sec.querySelector(':scope > .w140root');
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.className = 'w140root'; sec.appendChild(h); }
    return h;
  }
  function chip(on, attr, val, label, count, color) {
    return '<button class="w140chip' + (on ? ' on' : '') + '" ' + attr + '="' + E(val) + '">' + (color ? '<i style="background:' + E(color) + '"></i>' : '') + E(label) + (count != null ? ' <em>' + count + '</em>' : '') + '</button>';
  }
  function busy(msg) { return '<div class="w140empty">' + E(msg || 'Loading\u2026') + '</div>'; }
