  /* ======================= Our story (#ourstory) ======================= */
  var BLURB_S = 'What Welfare Avenue has done since April 2020, from our own documents, posts and designs. Every moment shows where it came from.';
  function visibleHist() {
    var lead = isLeaderUI();
    return (S.hist || []).filter(function (h) { return h.status !== 'hidden' || (lead && S.ui.hidden); });
  }
  function histMatch(h) {
    var u = S.ui;
    if (u.kind && h.kind !== u.kind) return false;
    if (u.city && fold(h.city) !== fold(u.city)) return false;
    if (u.q) {
      var hay = fold([h.title, h.summary, h.city, (h.partners || []).join(' '), numbersText(h.numbers), (h.date_start || '').slice(0, 4)].join(' '));
      var ok = fold(u.q).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).every(function (t) { return hay.indexOf(t) >= 0; });
      if (!ok) return false;
    }
    return true;
  }
  function yearOf(h) { return String(h.date_start || '').slice(0, 4); }
  function statsHtml() {
    var hs = visibleHist(), first = 2020, last = yearNow();
    var nYears = Math.max(1, last - first + 1);
    return '<div class="w140stats">' +
      '<div class="w140st"><b>' + nYears + '</b><span>years in our story, ' + first + ' to ' + last + '</span></div>' +
      '<button class="w140st" data-w140go="#ourstory"><b>' + hs.length + '</b><span>moments in our story</span></button>' +
      '<button class="w140st" data-w140go="#partners"><b>' + ((S.partners || []).filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'active' || p.status === 'past'; }).length || '\u2013') + '</b><span>organizations we have worked with</span></button>' +
      '<button class="w140st" data-w140go="#ourstory/lessons"><b>' + ((S.lessons || []).filter(function (l) { return l.status !== 'hidden'; }).length || '\u2013') + '</b><span>lessons learned</span></button></div>';
  }
  function tabsHtml() {
    var t = S.ui.tab;
    return '<nav class="w140tabs" role="tablist"><button role="tab" class="' + (t === 'timeline' ? 'on' : '') + '" data-w140go="#ourstory">' + I('book') + ' Timeline</button>' +
      '<button role="tab" class="' + (t === 'lessons' ? 'on' : '') + '" data-w140go="#ourstory/lessons">' + I('bulb') + ' Lessons learned</button></nav>';
  }
  function hlHtml(hs) {
    var hl = hs.filter(function (h) { return h.highlight && h.status !== 'hidden'; });
    if (!hl.length) return '';
    return '<div class="w140card"><h3>Milestones <span>The moments that shaped Welfare Avenue. Click one to find it in the timeline.</span></h3><div class="w140hl">' +
      hl.map(function (h) { return '<button class="w140hc" data-w140ev="' + E(h.id) + '"><span class="y">' + E(yearOf(h)) + '</span><b>' + E(h.title) + '</b><span>' + E(fmtRange(h)) + '</span></button>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function barHtml(hs) {
    var u = S.ui, counts = {}, cities = {};
    hs.forEach(function (h) { counts[h.kind] = (counts[h.kind] || 0) + 1; if (h.city) cities[h.city] = (cities[h.city] || 0) + 1; });
    var lead = isLeaderUI(), nHidden = (S.hist || []).filter(function (h) { return h.status === 'hidden'; }).length;
    return '<div class="w140bar"><div class="w140chips">' + chip(!u.kind, 'data-w140kind', '', 'Everything', hs.length) +
      KIND_ORDER.filter(function (k) { return counts[k]; }).map(function (k) { return chip(u.kind === k, 'data-w140kind', k, KINDS[k][0], counts[k], KINDS[k][1]); }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<select class="w140sel" data-w140city aria-label="City"><option value="">Every city</option>' + Object.keys(cities).sort().map(function (c) { return '<option' + (lower(u.city) === lower(c) ? ' selected' : '') + ' value="' + E(c) + '">' + E(c) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<div class="w140q">' + I('search') + '<input type="search" data-w140q="story" placeholder="Search the story" value="' + E(u.q) + '" aria-label="Search the story"></div>' +
      '<button class="w140btn soft" data-w140order="1" title="Change the order">' + I(u.order === 'asc' ? 'down' : 'up') + (u.order === 'asc' ? ' Oldest first' : ' Newest first') + '</button>' +
      (lead ? '<button class="w140btn" data-w140add="hist">' + I('plus') + ' Add a moment</button>' + (nHidden ? '<button class="w140btn ghost" data-w140hidden="1">' + I(S.ui.hidden ? 'eyeoff' : 'eye') + (S.ui.hidden ? ' Hide the hidden' : ' Show hidden (' + nHidden + ')') + '</button>' : '') : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function partnerChips(names) {
    return (names || []).map(function (n) {
      var p = partnerFor(n);
      return p ? '<button class="w140pl" data-w140partner="' + E(p.id) + '">' + E(n) + '</button>' : '<span class="w140pl">' + E(n) + '</span>';
    }).join(' ');
  }
  function evHtml(h) {
    var k = KINDS[h.kind] || ['Moment', GREEN], lead = isLeaderUI();
    var meta = [];
    if (h.date_precision !== 'day' || h.date_end) meta.push('<span>' + E(fmtRange(h)) + '</span>');
    if (h.city) meta.push('<span>' + I('pin') + ' ' + E(h.city) + '</span>');
    var nums = numbersText(h.numbers); if (nums) meta.push('<span><b>' + E(nums) + '</b></span>');
    if (h.partners && h.partners.length) meta.push('<span>' + partnerChips(h.partners) + '</span>');
    return '<li class="w140ev' + (h.highlight ? ' hl' : '') + (h.status === 'hidden' ? ' hid' : '') + '" id="w140e' + E(h.id) + '" data-w140id="' + E(h.id) + '">' +
      '<div class="d">' + E(fmtSide(h.date_start, h.date_precision)) + '</div><div class="ln"><i style="background:' + k[1] + '"></i></div><div class="c">' +
      '<div class="t">' + (h.highlight ? I('star', 'st') + ' ' : '') + E(h.title) + '<span class="w140kd" style="color:' + k[1] + ';background:' + k[1] + '1f">' + E(k[0]) + '</span>' +
      (h.visibility === 'leadership' ? '<span class="w140tag lead">' + I('lock') + 'Leaders and directors</span>' : '') + (h.status === 'hidden' ? '<span class="w140tag hid">Hidden</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
      (h.summary ? '<p>' + E(h.summary) + '</p>' : '') + (meta.length ? '<div class="w140m">' + meta.join('') + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="w140m">' + srcHtml(h.sources) + (lead ? '<button class="w140mini" data-w140edit="hist" data-id="' + E(h.id) + '">' + I('edit') + ' Edit</button>' : '') + '</div></div></li>';
  }
  function yearsHtml(hs) {
    var u = S.ui, by = {}, order = [];
    hs.filter(histMatch).forEach(function (h) { var y = yearOf(h); if (!by[y]) { by[y] = []; order.push(y); } by[y].push(h); });
    order.sort(); if (u.order === 'desc') order.reverse();
    if (!order.length) return '<div class="w140empty">Nothing matches. Try another word, or show everything.</div>';
    var ysum = {}; (S.years || []).forEach(function (y) { ysum[String(y.year)] = y; });
    var lead = isLeaderUI();
    var nav = order.length > 1 ? '<div class="w140yn" aria-label="Jump to a year">' + order.map(function (y) { return '<button data-w140year="' + E(y) + '">' + E(y) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '';
    return nav + order.map(function (y) {
      var list = by[y].slice().sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.date_start).localeCompare(String(b.date_start)) || (a.id - b.id); });
      if (u.order === 'desc') list.reverse();
      var ys = ysum[y];
      return '<section class="w140year" id="w140y' + E(y) + '"><div class="yh"><h2>' + E(y) + '</h2><div>' + (ys && ys.summary ? '<p>' + E(ys.summary) + '</p>' : '') +
        (lead ? '<button class="w140mini" data-w140edit="year" data-id="' + E(y) + '">' + I('edit') + (ys ? ' Edit the year' : ' Add a line about the year') + '</button>' : '') + '</div></div>' +
        '<ol class="w140tl">' + list.map(evHtml).join('') + '</ol></section>';
    }).join('');
  }

  /* ---- Lessons learned ---- */
  function lessonsHtml() {
    var u = S.ui, lead = isLeaderUI();
    var all = (S.lessons || []).filter(function (l) { return l.status !== 'hidden' || lead; });
    var counts = {}; all.forEach(function (l) { counts[l.area] = (counts[l.area] || 0) + 1; });
    var list = all.filter(function (l) {
      if (u.lArea && l.area !== u.lArea) return false;
      if (u.lq) { var hay = fold([l.lesson, l.context, l.topic, l.area, l.years].join(' ')); if (!fold(u.lq).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).every(function (t) { return hay.indexOf(t) >= 0; })) return false; }
      return true;
    }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.area).localeCompare(String(b.area)) || String(a.topic || '').localeCompare(String(b.topic || '')); });
    var bar = '<div class="w140bar"><div class="w140chips">' + chip(!u.lArea, 'data-w140area', '', 'Every area', all.length) +
      AREAS.filter(function (a) { return counts[a[0]]; }).map(function (a) { return chip(u.lArea === a[0], 'data-w140area', a[0], a[1], counts[a[0]]); }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w140q">' + I('search') + '<input type="search" data-w140q="lessons" placeholder="Search the lessons" value="' + E(u.lq) + '" aria-label="Search the lessons"></div>' +
      (lead ? '<button class="w140btn" data-w140add="lesson">' + I('plus') + ' Add a lesson</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    var intro = '<div class="w140card"><h3>What we learned <span>Advice from Welfare Avenue\u2019s own events, programs and campaigns: what happened, and what to do next time.</span></h3></div>';
    if (!list.length) return intro + bar + '<div class="w140empty">No lesson matches. Try another word or area.</div>';
    var areaLabel = {}; AREAS.forEach(function (a) { areaLabel[a[0]] = a[1]; });
    return intro + bar + '<div class="w140lg">' + list.map(function (l) {
      return '<article class="w140lc' + (l.status === 'hidden' ? ' hid' : '') + '" data-w140lesson="' + E(l.id) + '"><div class="ls">' + E(l.lesson) + '</div>' + (l.context ? '<div class="cx">' + E(l.context) + '</div>' : '') +
        '<div class="ft"><span class="w140tag">' + E(areaLabel[l.area] || l.area) + '</span>' + (l.topic ? '<span class="w140tag" style="background:rgba(220,165,39,.16);color:#7a5a12">' + E(l.topic) + '</span>' : '') +
        (l.years ? '<span class="w140count">' + E(l.years) + '</span>' : '') + (l.visibility === 'leadership' ? '<span class="w140tag lead">' + I('lock') + 'Leaders</span>' : '') + (l.status === 'hidden' ? '<span class="w140tag hid">Hidden</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="w140m">' + srcHtml(l.sources) + (lead ? '<button class="w140mini" data-w140edit="lesson" data-id="' + E(l.id) + '">' + I('edit') + ' Edit</button>' : '') + '</div></article>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }

  function paintStory() {
    var root = mastFor('ourstory', 'Organization', 'Our story', BLURB_S); if (!root) return;   /* wave163: in Organization */
    if (!S.hist || !S.lessons) { root.innerHTML = busy('Loading our story\u2026'); return; }
    if (S.err.hist && !S.hist.length) { root.innerHTML = '<div class="w140empty">Our story could not load (' + E(S.err.hist) + ').</div>'; return; }
    var hs = visibleHist();
    var focus = document.activeElement && document.activeElement.getAttribute ? document.activeElement.getAttribute('data-w140q') : null;
    var caret = focus && document.activeElement.selectionStart;
    var html = statsHtml() + tabsHtml();
    if (S.ui.tab === 'lessons') html += lessonsHtml();
    else html += hlHtml(hs) + barHtml(hs) + yearsHtml(hs);
    root.innerHTML = html;
    if (focus) { var inp = root.querySelector('[data-w140q="' + focus + '"]'); if (inp) { inp.focus(); try { inp.setSelectionRange(caret, caret); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  }
  function openStory(h) {
    var rest = String(h || '').split('/').slice(1);
    S.ui.tab = rest[0] === 'lessons' ? 'lessons' : 'timeline';
    Promise.all([load('hist'), load('years'), load('lessons'), load('partners')]).then(function () {
      paintStory();
      if (rest[0] === 'e' && rest[1]) flashEv(rest[1]);
      else if (/^\d{4}$/.test(rest[0] || '')) { var y = document.getElementById('w140y' + rest[0]); if (y) y.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); }
    });
    paintStory();
  }
  function flashEv(id) {
    var h = (S.hist || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0];
    if (h && (S.ui.kind || S.ui.q || S.ui.city)) { S.ui.kind = ''; S.ui.q = ''; S.ui.city = ''; paintStory(); }
    var el = document.getElementById('w140e' + id); if (!el) return;
    el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
    el.classList.remove('flash'); void el.offsetWidth; el.classList.add('flash');
  }
  /* ======================= Partners (#partners) ======================= */
  var BLURB_P = 'Every organization Welfare Avenue has worked with since 2020, and the ones we approached: our partners, collaborators and sponsors, what they did, when, and where it is written down.';
  var PSETS = [['worked', 'Worked with us'], ['active', 'Active'], ['past', 'Past'], ['prospect', 'Prospects'], ['declined', 'Declined'], ['all', 'All']];
  function inSet(p, set) {
    if (set === 'all') return true;
    if (set === 'worked') return p.status === 'active' || p.status === 'past';
    return p.status === set;
  }
  function yearsText(p) {
    if (p.first_year && p.last_year && p.first_year !== p.last_year) return p.first_year + ' to ' + p.last_year;
    return String(p.first_year || p.last_year || '');
  }
  function statusChip(st) { var s = STATUS[st] || [st, MUTED]; return '<span class="w140st2" style="background:' + s[1] + '1c;color:' + s[1] + '"><i style="background:' + s[1] + '"></i>' + E(s[0]) + '</span>'; }
  function partnerMatch(p) {
    var u = S.ui;
    if (!inSet(p, u.pSet)) return false;
    if (u.pCat && u.pCat !== 'all' && catOf(p) !== u.pCat) return false;
    if (u.pRel && p.relationship !== u.pRel) return false;
    if (u.pKind && p.kind !== u.pKind) return false;
    if (u.pCity && fold(p.city) !== fold(u.pCity)) return false;
    if (u.pq) { var hay = fold([p.name, (p.aka || []).join(' '), p.summary, p.kind, p.city, p.relationship, PCATS[catOf(p)][0], p.contact_role].join(' ')); if (!fold(u.pq).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).every(function (t) { return hay.indexOf(t) >= 0; })) return false; }
    return true;
  }
  function paintPartners() {
    var root = mastFor('partners', boardMode() ? 'Organization' : 'CRM', 'Partners', BLURB_P); if (!root) return;   /* wave163: the page lives in the CRM (directors open no apps: it sits in their Organization) */
    if (!S.partners) { root.innerHTML = busy('Loading our partners\u2026'); return; }
    if (S.err.partners && !S.partners.length) { root.innerHTML = '<div class="w140empty">Partners could not load (' + E(S.err.partners) + ').</div>'; return; }
    var u = S.ui, all = S.partners, lead = isLeaderUI();
    /* the counts answer the other filter: each type within the status shown, each status within the type shown */
    var ofCat = all.filter(function (p) { return u.pCat === 'all' || !u.pCat || catOf(p) === u.pCat; });
    var cnt = {}; PSETS.forEach(function (s) { cnt[s[0]] = ofCat.filter(function (p) { return inSet(p, s[0]); }).length; });
    var inStatus = all.filter(function (p) { return inSet(p, u.pSet); });
    var ccnt = { all: inStatus.length }; PCAT_ORDER.forEach(function (c) { ccnt[c] = inStatus.filter(function (p) { return catOf(p) === c; }).length; });
    var curCat = PCATS[u.pCat] ? u.pCat : 'all';
    var rels = {}, kinds = {}, cities = {};
    all.forEach(function (p) { if (p.relationship) rels[p.relationship] = 1; if (p.kind) kinds[p.kind] = 1; if (p.city) cities[p.city] = (cities[p.city] || 0) + 1; });
    var focus = document.activeElement && document.activeElement.getAttribute ? document.activeElement.getAttribute('data-w140q') : null;
    var caret = focus && document.activeElement.selectionStart;
    var list = all.filter(partnerMatch).sort(function (a, b) {
      var o = { active: 0, past: 1, prospect: 2, declined: 3 };
      return (o[a.status] - o[b.status]) || ((b.last_year || 0) - (a.last_year || 0)) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name));
    });
    var html = '<div class="w140types" role="group" aria-label="Type">' + [['all', 'Everyone']].concat(PCAT_ORDER.map(function (c) { return [c, PCATS[c][1]]; })).map(function (x) {
        var on = curCat === x[0], col = PCATS[x[0]] ? PCATS[x[0]][2] : GREEN;
        return '<button type="button" class="w140ty' + (on ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w140pcat="' + x[0] + '" aria-pressed="' + (on ? 'true' : 'false') + '">' + (PCATS[x[0]] ? '<i style="background:' + col + '"></i>' : '') + E(x[1]) + ' <em>' + ccnt[x[0]] + '</em></button>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<p class="w140def">' + (curCat === 'all' ? '<b>Partners</b> work with us over time toward a shared goal; <b>collaborators</b> join us for an event or an initiative; <b>sponsors</b> give money or in-kind support and get recognition.' : '<b>A ' + E(PCATS[curCat][0].toLowerCase()) + '</b> ' + E(PCATS[curCat][3])) + '</p>' +
      '<div class="w140stats">' + ['active', 'past', 'prospect', 'declined'].map(function (s) {
      return '<button class="w140st" data-w140pset="' + s + '"><b style="color:' + STATUS[s][1] + '">' + cnt[s] + '</b><span>' + E({ active: 'working with us now', past: 'worked with us before', prospect: 'approached, nothing yet', declined: 'said no' }[s]) + '</span></button>';
    }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w140bar"><div class="w140chips">' + PSETS.map(function (s) { return chip(u.pSet === s[0], 'data-w140pset', s[0], s[1], cnt[s[0]]); }).join('') + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="w140bar"><select class="w140sel" data-w140prel aria-label="How they helped"><option value="">Every kind of help</option>' + Object.keys(REL).filter(function (r) { return rels[r]; }).map(function (r) { return '<option value="' + E(r) + '"' + (u.pRel === r ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(REL[r]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<select class="w140sel" data-w140pkind aria-label="Kind of organization"><option value="">Every kind of organization</option>' + PKINDS.filter(function (k) { return kinds[k]; }).map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + E(k) + '"' + (u.pKind === k ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(k.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + k.slice(1)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<select class="w140sel" data-w140pcity aria-label="City"><option value="">Every city</option>' + Object.keys(cities).sort().map(function (c) { return '<option value="' + E(c) + '"' + (lower(u.pCity) === lower(c) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(c) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<div class="w140q">' + I('search') + '<input type="search" data-w140q="partners" placeholder="Search partners" value="' + E(u.pq) + '" aria-label="Search partners"></div>' +
      (lead ? '<button class="w140btn" data-w140add="partner">' + I('plus') + ' Add a partner</button>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w140count">' + plural(list.length, 'organization', 'organizations') + '</div>' +
      (list.length ? '<div class="w140pg">' + list.map(function (p) {
        return '<button class="w140pc" data-w140partner="' + E(p.id) + '"><div class="nm">' + E(p.name) + '</div><div class="sub">' + E([p.kind, p.city].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</div>' +
          (p.summary ? '<div class="sm">' + E(p.summary) + '</div>' : '') + '<div class="ft">' + catTag(p) + statusChip(p.status) + (relDetail(p) ? '<span class="w140tag">' + E(relDetail(p)) + '</span>' : '') +
          (yearsText(p) ? '<span class="w140count">' + E(yearsText(p)) + '</span>' : '') + (p.visibility === 'leadership' ? '<span class="w140tag lead">' + I('lock') + 'Leaders</span>' : '') + '</div></button>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="w140empty">No organization matches. Try another word or show all of them.</div>');
    root.innerHTML = html;
    if (focus) { var inp = root.querySelector('[data-w140q="' + focus + '"]'); if (inp) { inp.focus(); try { inp.setSelectionRange(caret, caret); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  }
  /* where a partner appears: the moments that name them, and the documents that mention them */
  function partnerMoments(p) {
    var ns = namesOf(p);
    return visibleHist().filter(function (h) {
      if ((h.partners || []).some(function (n) { return ns.indexOf(fold(n)) >= 0; })) return true;
      var hay = fold(h.title + ' ' + (h.summary || ''));
      return ns.some(function (n) { return n.length > 4 && hay.indexOf(n) >= 0; });
    });
  }
  function partnerDocs(p) {
    var ns = namesOf(p);
    return (S.docs || []).filter(function (d) { return (d.organisations || []).some(function (n) { return ns.indexOf(fold(n)) >= 0; }); }).slice(0, 12);
  }
  function partnerSheet(id) {
    var p = (S.partners || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0];
    if (!p) { toastS('That partner is not in the list'); return; }
    var lead = isLeaderUI();
    var ms = partnerMoments(p), ds = partnerDocs(p);
    var rows = [['Type', PCATS[catOf(p)][0] + (relDetail(p) ? ' (' + relDetail(p).toLowerCase() + ')' : '')], ['Kind', [p.kind, p.city].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')], ['Years', yearsText(p)], ['Contact', p.contact_role || '']]
      .filter(function (r) { return r[1]; }).map(function (r) { return '<div class="row"><b>' + E(r[0]) + '</b><span>' + E(r[1]) + '</span></div>'; }).join('');
    var html = '<h2>' + E(p.name) + '</h2><div class="sub">' + catTag(p) + statusChip(p.status) + (p.aka && p.aka.length ? ' Also written ' + E(p.aka.join(', ')) : '') + '</div>' +
      (p.summary ? '<p style="font-size:14px;line-height:1.55;margin:8px 0 4px">' + E(p.summary) + '</p>' : '') + rows +
      (p.notes ? '<h4>Good to know</h4><p style="font-size:13px;margin:0">' + E(p.notes) + '</p>' : '') +
      '<h4>In our story</h4>' + (ms.length ? ms.slice(0, 14).map(function (h) { return '<div class="row"><b>' + E(yearOf(h)) + '</b><button class="w140mini" style="padding:0;text-align:left" data-w140go="#ourstory/e/' + E(h.id) + '">' + E(h.title) + '</button></div>'; }).join('') : '<p class="w140count">No moment in the timeline names them yet.</p>') +
      (S.docs ? '<h4>In our documents</h4>' + (ds.length ? ds.map(function (d) { return '<div class="row"><b>' + E(d.year || '') + '</b>' + (d.url ? '<a href="' + E(d.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + E(d.title) + '</a>' : '<span>' + E(d.title) + '</span>') + '</div>'; }).join('') : '<p class="w140count">No document in the finder mentions them.</p>') : '') +
      '<h4>Where this comes from</h4>' + (srcHtml(p.sources, 'Show the sources') || '<p class="w140count">Added by hand.</p>') +
      '<div class="acts">' + (lead ? '<button class="w140btn soft" data-w140edit="partner" data-id="' + E(p.id) + '">' + I('edit') + ' Edit</button>' : '') + '<button class="w140btn" data-w140close="1">Done</button></div>';
    openSheet(html, 'partner:' + p.id);
    if (!S.docs && staffOk() && !boardMode()) load('docs').then(function () { if (S.sheet === 'partner:' + p.id) partnerSheet(id); });
  }
  function openPartners(h) {
    var rest = String(h || '').split('/').slice(1);
    Promise.all([load('partners'), load('hist')]).then(function () {
      paintPartners();
      if (rest[0]) partnerSheet(rest[0]);
    });
    paintPartners();
  }
  /* ======================= Document finder (#documents) ======================= */
  var BLURB_D = 'Find a document by what it is about: every useful file in our shared drives and Canva, with a summary, the date it covers and the organizations in it. Private files are never here.';
  var TEAM_LABEL = {}; DTEAMS.forEach(function (t) { TEAM_LABEL[t[0]] = t[1]; });
  var KIND_ONE = { guide: 'Guide', plan: 'Plan', report: 'Report', 'meeting notes': 'Meeting notes', presentation: 'Presentation', proposal: 'Proposal', letter: 'Letter',
    newsletter: 'Newsletter', application: 'Application', list: 'List', posts: 'Post or poster' };
  var DOC_HAY = null;
  function docHay() {
    if (DOC_HAY && DOC_HAY.src === S.docs) return DOC_HAY.map;
    var map = {};
    (S.docs || []).forEach(function (d) {
      map[d.file_id] = {
        t: fold(d.title), o: fold((d.organisations || []).join(' | ')), r: fold((d.resource_title || '') + ' ' + (d.resource_what || '')),
        s: fold(d.summary), p: fold((d.topics || []).join(' | ')), x: fold([d.location, d.kind, d.doc_date, d.year, TEAM_LABEL[d.team]].join(' '))
      };
    });
    DOC_HAY = { src: S.docs, map: map };
    return map;
  }
  function docResults() {
    var u = S.ui, toks = fold(u.dq).split(/[^a-z0-9$&'\-]+/).filter(function (t) { return t.length > 1 || /\d/.test(t); });
    var hay = docHay(), phrase = fold(u.dq).trim();
    var out = [];
    (S.docs || []).forEach(function (d) {
      if (u.dTeam && d.team !== u.dTeam) return;
      if (u.dYear && String(d.year) !== String(u.dYear)) return;
      if (u.dKind && (d.kind || 'other') !== u.dKind) return;
      if (u.dReuse && !d.reusable) return;
      var h = hay[d.file_id], score = 0;
      if (toks.length) {
        for (var i = 0; i < toks.length; i++) {
          var t = toks[i], sc = 0;
          if (h.t.indexOf(t) >= 0) sc += 5;
          if (h.o.indexOf(t) >= 0) sc += 3;
          if (h.r.indexOf(t) >= 0) sc += 3;
          if (h.s.indexOf(t) >= 0) sc += 2;
          if (h.p.indexOf(t) >= 0) sc += 1;
          if (h.x.indexOf(t) >= 0) sc += 1;
          if (!sc) return;
          score += sc;
        }
        if (phrase.length > 3 && h.t.indexOf(phrase) >= 0) score += 8;
      }
      out.push({ d: d, score: score });
    });
    out.sort(function (a, b) { return (b.score - a.score) || ((b.d.year || 0) - (a.d.year || 0)) || String(a.d.title).localeCompare(String(b.d.title)); });
    return out;
  }
  function paintDocs() {
    var root = mastFor('documents', 'Other Tools', 'Document finder', BLURB_D); if (!root) return;   /* wave163: in Other Tools */
    if (!S.docs) { root.innerHTML = busy('Loading the document finder\u2026'); return; }
    if (S.err.docs && !S.docs.length) { root.innerHTML = '<div class="w140empty">The document finder could not load (' + E(S.err.docs) + ').</div>'; return; }
    var u = S.ui, lead = isLeaderUI();
    var years = {}, teams = {}, kinds = {};
    S.docs.forEach(function (d) { if (d.year) years[d.year] = 1; if (d.team) teams[d.team] = 1; kinds[d.kind || 'other'] = 1; });
    var focus = document.activeElement && document.activeElement.getAttribute ? document.activeElement.getAttribute('data-w140q') : null;
    var caret = focus && document.activeElement.selectionStart;
    var res = docResults(), shown = res.slice(0, u.dShow);
    var html = '<div class="w140bar"><div class="w140q big">' + I('search') + '<input type="search" data-w140q="docs" placeholder="What are you looking for? For example: flag football sponsorship, volunteer drivers, Moisson" value="' + E(u.dq) + '" aria-label="Search the documents"></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w140bar"><select class="w140sel" data-w140dteam aria-label="Team"><option value="">Every team</option>' + DTEAMS.filter(function (t) { return teams[t[0]]; }).map(function (t) { return '<option value="' + t[0] + '"' + (u.dTeam === t[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(t[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<select class="w140sel" data-w140dyear aria-label="Year"><option value="">Every year</option>' + Object.keys(years).sort().reverse().map(function (y) { return '<option value="' + y + '"' + (String(u.dYear) === y ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + y + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<select class="w140sel" data-w140dkind aria-label="Kind of document"><option value="">Every kind</option>' + DKINDS.filter(function (k) { return kinds[k[0]]; }).map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + E(k[0]) + '"' + (u.dKind === k[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(k[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      chip(u.dReuse, 'data-w140dreuse', '1', 'Guides and templates to reuse', S.docs.filter(function (d) { return d.reusable; }).length) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w140count">' + (u.dq ? plural(res.length, 'document matches', 'documents match') : plural(res.length, 'document', 'documents')) + (lead ? ' \u00b7 documents from the board, management, HR and finance drives show to leaders only' : '') + '</div>' +
      (shown.length ? '<div class="w140dl">' + shown.map(function (r) {
        var d = r.d, cv = d.source === 'canva';
        var meta = [d.location, d.doc_date ? String(d.doc_date).slice(0, 7) : (d.year || ''), KIND_ONE[d.kind] || '', TEAM_LABEL[d.team] || ''].filter(Boolean);
        var orgs = (d.organisations || []).slice(0, 6);
        return '<article class="w140doc"><div class="ic' + (cv ? ' cv' : '') + '">' + I(cv ? 'spark' : 'file') + '</div><div>' +
          (d.url ? '<a class="tt" href="' + E(d.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + E(d.title) + ' ' + I('ext') + '</a>' : '<span class="tt">' + E(d.title) + '</span>') +
          (d.visibility === 'leadership' ? '<span class="w140tag lead">' + I('lock') + 'Leaders</span>' : '') +
          '<div class="w140m">' + E(meta.join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</div>' + (d.summary ? '<p>' + E(d.summary) + '</p>' : '') +
          (d.reusable && d.resource_title ? '<div class="re">' + I('check') + '<span><b>Reuse it:</b> ' + E(d.resource_title) + (d.resource_what ? ': ' + E(d.resource_what) : '') + '</span></div>' : '') +
          (orgs.length ? '<div class="w140m">' + partnerChips(orgs) + '</div>' : '') + '</div></article>';
      }).join('') + (res.length > shown.length ? '<button class="w140btn soft w140more" data-w140dmore="1">Show more (' + (res.length - shown.length) + ' left)</button>' : '') + '</div>'
        : '<div class="w140empty">Nothing matches' + (u.dq ? ' \u201c' + E(u.dq) + '\u201d' : '') + '. Try fewer or different words, or clear the filters. The finder knows what each document is about, the organizations in it and the date it covers, so a partner\u2019s name, an event or a topic works well.</div>');
    root.innerHTML = html;
    if (focus) { var inp = root.querySelector('[data-w140q="' + focus + '"]'); if (inp) { inp.focus(); try { inp.setSelectionRange(caret, caret); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  }
  function openDocs(h) {
    var rest = String(h || '').split('/').slice(1);
    if (rest[0] === 'q' && rest[1]) { S.ui.dq = decodeURIComponent(rest.slice(1).join('/')); S.ui.dShow = 40; }
    Promise.all([load('docs'), load('partners')]).then(function () {
      paintDocs();
      var inp = document.querySelector('#documents [data-w140q="docs"]'); if (inp && !rest[0] && window.innerWidth > 700) { try { inp.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    });
    paintDocs();
  }
  /* ======================= the sheet: details and leaders' edits ======================= */
  var lastFocus = null;
  function openSheet(html, tag) {
    var o = document.getElementById('w140sheet');
    if (!o) {
      o = document.createElement('div'); o.id = 'w140sheet'; o.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); o.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true');
      document.body.appendChild(o);
      o.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (e.target === o) closeSheet(); });
    }
    if (!S.sheet) lastFocus = document.activeElement;
    S.sheet = tag || 'sheet';
    o.innerHTML = '<div class="sh"><button class="xb" data-w140close="1" aria-label="Close">' + I('x') + '</button>' + html + '</div>';
    o.style.display = 'flex';
    var f = o.querySelector('input,select,textarea') || o.querySelector('.xb'); if (f) { try { f.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  function closeSheet() {
    var o = document.getElementById('w140sheet'); if (o) { o.style.display = 'none'; o.innerHTML = ''; }
    S.sheet = null;
    if (lastFocus && lastFocus.focus) { try { lastFocus.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    lastFocus = null;
  }
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && S.sheet) { closeSheet(); } });

  function opt(list, cur) { return list.map(function (x) { var v = Array.isArray(x) ? x[0] : x, l = Array.isArray(x) ? x[1] : x; return '<option value="' + E(v) + '"' + (String(cur) === String(v) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(l) + '</option>'; }).join(''); }
  function field(label, html, full, hint) { return '<label' + (full ? ' class="full"' : '') + '>' + E(label) + (hint ? ' <span class="hint">' + E(hint) + '</span>' : '') + html + '</label>'; }
  function inp(name, val, ph) { return '<input data-f="' + name + '" value="' + E(val == null ? '' : val) + '"' + (ph ? ' placeholder="' + E(ph) + '"' : '') + '>'; }
  function area(name, val) { return '<textarea data-f="' + name + '">' + E(val == null ? '' : val) + '</textarea>'; }
  function sel(name, list, cur) { return '<select data-f="' + name + '">' + opt(list, cur) + '</select>'; }
  var VIS = [['everyone', 'Everyone (staff and directors)'], ['leadership', 'Leaders and directors only']];
  var NUMS = [['meals', 'Meals', '350'], ['volunteers', 'Volunteers', '25'], ['people_helped', 'People helped', ''], ['attendees', 'Attendees', ''], ['raised_cad', 'Raised ($)', '2500']];
  /* 1,200 or $2 500 or 350 \u2192 a number; empty \u2192 null; anything else \u2192 NaN */
  function readNum(s) { s = String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[\s,$]/g, ''); if (!s) return null; var n = Number(s); return isFinite(n) && n >= 0 ? n : NaN; }

  function editForm(kind, id) {
    var html = '', row = null;
    if (kind === 'hist') {
      row = id ? (S.hist || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0] : null;
      var h = row || { date_precision: 'day', kind: 'event', visibility: 'everyone', status: 'published', partners: [] };
      html = '<h2>' + (row ? 'Edit this moment' : 'Add a moment') + '</h2><div class="sub">Our story keeps what happened, with the date it happened. Say where it is written down in the summary if you can.</div><div class="w140form">' +
        field('When', inp('date', asTyped(h.date_start, h.date_precision), '2021-03-20, 2021-03 or 2021'), false, 'a day, a month or a year') +
        field('Until', inp('date_end', h.date_end ? asTyped(h.date_end, h.date_end_precision || 'day') : '', 'optional'), false, 'for something that ran a while') +
        field('Title', inp('title', h.title, 'First serving at Place \u00c9milie-Gamelin'), true) +
        field('What happened', area('summary', h.summary), true) +
        field('Kind', sel('kind', KIND_ORDER.map(function (k) { return [k, KINDS[k][0]]; }), h.kind)) +
        field('City', inp('city', h.city, 'Montreal')) +
        field('Organizations', inp('partners', (h.partners || []).join(', '), 'Sportira, Vanier College Student Association'), true, 'separated by commas') +
        '<div class="full w140fh">The numbers <span>only what a document or post gives; leave the rest empty</span></div>' +
        NUMS.map(function (n) { return field(n[1], inp('n_' + n[0], (h.numbers && h.numbers[n[0]] != null) ? h.numbers[n[0]] : '', n[2])); }).join('') +
        field('Who sees it', sel('visibility', VIS, h.visibility)) +
        field('Shown', sel('status', [['published', 'In the story'], ['hidden', 'Hidden']], h.status)) +
        '<label class="chk full"><input type="checkbox" data-f="highlight"' + (h.highlight ? ' checked' : '') + '> One of the milestones at the top</label>' +
        '<div class="err" data-w140err></div></div>';
    } else if (kind === 'year') {
      row = (S.years || []).filter(function (y) { return String(y.year) === String(id); })[0] || { year: Number(id), summary: '' };
      html = '<h2>' + E(id) + '</h2><div class="sub">Two or three sentences on what the year was for Welfare Avenue, shown at the head of the year.</div><div class="w140form">' +
        field('The year in a few lines', area('summary', row.summary), true) + '<div class="err" data-w140err></div></div>';
    } else if (kind === 'partner') {
      row = id ? (S.partners || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0] : null;
      var p = row || { category: 'partner', relationship: 'partner', status: 'active', visibility: 'everyone', aka: [] };
      html = '<h2>' + (row ? 'Edit ' + E(p.name) : 'Add a partner') + '</h2><div class="sub">An organization, never a private person or an individual donor.</div><div class="w140form">' +
        field('Name', inp('name', p.name), true) +
        field('Also written', inp('aka', (p.aka || []).join(', '), 'other names, separated by commas'), true) +
        field('Kind', sel('kind', [['', 'Choose']].concat(PKINDS.map(function (k) { return [k, k.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + k.slice(1)]; })), p.kind || '')) +
        field('City', inp('city', p.city)) +
        field('Type', sel('category', PCAT_ORDER.map(function (c) { return [c, PCATS[c][0]]; }), catOf(p)), false, 'partner: over time, toward a shared goal; collaborator: an event or an initiative; sponsor: money or in-kind support, with recognition') +
        field('How they help', sel('relationship', Object.keys(REL).map(function (r) { return [r, REL[r]]; }), p.relationship), false, 'the detail: a funder, a venue, a supplier ...') +
        field('Status', sel('status', Object.keys(STATUS).map(function (s) { return [s, STATUS[s][0]]; }), p.status)) +
        field('First year', inp('first_year', p.first_year, '2021')) + field('Last year', inp('last_year', p.last_year, String(yearNow()))) +
        field('What they did with us', area('summary', p.summary), true) +
        field('Contact', inp('contact_role', p.contact_role, 'a role, e.g. VP Events (never a phone or an email)'), true) +
        field('Who sees it', sel('visibility', VIS, p.visibility)) +
        field('Notes', inp('notes', p.notes, 'where the sources disagree, what to check'), false, 'shown with the partner') +
        '<div class="err" data-w140err></div></div>';
    } else if (kind === 'lesson') {
      row = id ? (S.lessons || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0] : null;
      var l = row || { area: 'events', visibility: 'everyone', status: 'published' };
      html = '<h2>' + (row ? 'Edit this lesson' : 'Add a lesson') + '</h2><div class="sub">Advice the team can act on, and what happened that taught it.</div><div class="w140form">' +
        field('The lesson', area('lesson', l.lesson), true) +
        field('What happened', area('context', l.context), true) +
        field('Area', sel('area', AREAS, l.area)) + field('Topic', inp('topic', l.topic, 'Driver no-shows')) +
        field('Years', inp('years', l.years, '2021, 2023')) + field('Who sees it', sel('visibility', VIS, l.visibility)) +
        field('Shown', sel('status', [['published', 'In the list'], ['hidden', 'Hidden']], l.status)) +
        '<div class="err" data-w140err></div></div>';
    }
    html += '<div class="acts"><button class="w140btn ghost" data-w140close="1">Cancel</button><button class="w140btn" data-w140save="' + kind + '" data-id="' + E(id || '') + '">' + I('check') + ' Save</button></div>';
    openSheet(html, 'edit:' + kind + ':' + (id || 'new'));
  }
  function formVals() {
    var o = document.getElementById('w140sheet'), v = {};
    if (!o) return v;
    o.querySelectorAll('[data-f]').forEach(function (el) { v[el.getAttribute('data-f')] = el.type === 'checkbox' ? el.checked : String(el.value || '').trim(); });
    return v;
  }
  function showErr(m) { var el = document.querySelector('#w140sheet [data-w140err]'); if (el) el.textContent = m; }
  function list(s) { return String(s || '').split(',').map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(Boolean); }
  function yearNum(s) { var n = Number(String(s || '').trim()); return n >= 2000 && n <= 2100 ? n : null; }
  function stamp(row) { row.updated_by = myName() || 'a leader'; row.updated_at = nowIso(); return row; }

  async function saveEdit(kind, id) {
    var v = formVals(), row, table, cache, res;
    try {
      if (kind === 'hist') {
        var a = readDate(v.date, false); if (!a) { showErr('Give the date as 2021-03-20, 2021-03 or 2021.'); return; }
        var b = v.date_end ? readDate(v.date_end, true) : null; if (v.date_end && !b) { showErr('Give the end date as 2021-06-30, 2021-06 or 2021, or leave it empty.'); return; }
        if (!v.title) { showErr('Give it a title.'); return; }
        if (b && b.d < a.d) { showErr('The end date comes before the start.'); return; }
        var was = id ? (S.hist || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0] : null;
        var nums = {}; Object.keys((was && was.numbers) || {}).forEach(function (k) { if (!NUMS.some(function (n) { return n[0] === k; })) nums[k] = was.numbers[k]; });
        for (var ni = 0; ni < NUMS.length; ni++) {
          var nv = readNum(v['n_' + NUMS[ni][0]]);
          if (nv !== nv) { showErr(NUMS[ni][1] + ' takes a number only, like 1200.'); return; }
          if (nv != null) nums[NUMS[ni][0]] = nv;
        }
        row = stamp({ date_start: a.d, date_precision: a.p, date_end: b ? b.d : null, date_end_precision: b ? b.p : null, title: v.title, summary: v.summary || null, kind: v.kind,
          city: v.city || null, partners: list(v.partners), numbers: Object.keys(nums).length ? nums : null, visibility: v.visibility, status: v.status, highlight: !!v.highlight });
        table = 'wa_history'; cache = 'hist';
      } else if (kind === 'year') {
        if (!v.summary) { showErr('Write a line or two, or cancel.'); return; }
        res = await db.from('wa_history_years').upsert({ year: Number(id), summary: v.summary, updated_by: myName() || 'a leader', updated_at: nowIso() }, { onConflict: 'year' }).select();
        if (res && res.error) throw res.error;
        var ys = (S.years || []).filter(function (y) { return String(y.year) !== String(id); }); ys.push((res.data && res.data[0]) || { year: Number(id), summary: v.summary }); S.years = ys;
        closeSheet(); toastS('Saved'); paintStory(); return;
      } else if (kind === 'partner') {
        if (!v.name) { showErr('Give the organization\u2019s name.'); return; }
        if (/@|\+?\d[\d\s().-]{7,}\d/.test(v.contact_role || '')) { showErr('Contact takes a role only, never an email or a phone number.'); return; }
        if (PCATS[v.relationship] && v.relationship !== v.category) v.relationship = v.category;   /* how they help never contradicts the type */
        if ((v.relationship === 'funder' || v.relationship === 'donor organisation') && v.category !== 'sponsor') { showErr('A funder or a donor organization is a sponsor: pick Sponsor as the type, or another way they help.'); return; }
        row = stamp({ name: v.name, aka: list(v.aka), kind: v.kind || null, city: v.city || null, category: PCATS[v.category] ? v.category : 'partner', relationship: v.relationship, status: v.status, first_year: yearNum(v.first_year), last_year: yearNum(v.last_year),
          summary: v.summary || null, contact_role: v.contact_role || null, visibility: v.visibility, notes: v.notes || null });
        table = 'wa_partners'; cache = 'partners';
      } else if (kind === 'lesson') {
        if (!v.lesson) { showErr('Write the lesson.'); return; }
        row = stamp({ lesson: v.lesson, context: v.context || null, area: v.area, topic: v.topic || null, years: v.years || null, visibility: v.visibility, status: v.status });
        table = 'wa_lessons'; cache = 'lessons';
      } else return;
      if (id) res = await db.from(table).update(row).eq('id', Number(id)).select();
      else { row.key = 'hub-' + Date.now().toString(36); row.created_by = row.updated_by; row.sources = []; res = await db.from(table).insert(row).select(); }
      if (res && res.error) throw res.error;
      /* an update the database refused for this person comes back empty, not as an error */
      if (id && res && Array.isArray(res.data) && !res.data.length) { showErr('Could not save: only leaders can change this.'); return; }
      var old = id ? (S[cache] || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0] : null;
      var saved = Object.assign({}, old || {}, row, (res && res.data && res.data[0]) || {});
      if (saved.id == null) saved.id = id ? Number(id) : ('tmp' + Date.now());
      var arr = (S[cache] || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) !== String(saved.id); }); arr.push(saved);
      if (cache === 'hist') arr.sort(function (x, y) { return String(x.date_start).localeCompare(String(y.date_start)); });
      S[cache] = arr; if (cache === 'partners') indexPartners();
      closeSheet(); toastS(id ? 'Saved' : 'Added');
      if (cache === 'partners') paintPartners(); else paintStory();
      if (cache === 'hist' && saved.id) setTimeout(function () { flashEv(saved.id); }, 80);
    } catch (e) { showErr('Could not save: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 140)); }
  }
  /* ======================= wiring ======================= */
  function go(h) { if (location.hash === h) { try { applyHash(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } else location.hash = h; }
  function inOurs(t) { return t.closest && (t.closest('.w140root') || t.closest('#w140sheet')); }
  var qT = null;
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !inOurs(t)) return;
    var el;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140close]'))) { e.preventDefault(); closeSheet(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140save]'))) { e.preventDefault(); el.disabled = true; saveEdit(el.getAttribute('data-w140save'), el.getAttribute('data-id')).then(function () { el.disabled = false; }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140go]'))) { e.preventDefault(); closeSheet(); go(el.getAttribute('data-w140go')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140partner]'))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      var pid = el.getAttribute('data-w140partner');
      if (location.hash.indexOf('#partners') === 0) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#partners/' + pid); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } partnerSheet(pid); }
      else { closeSheet(); go('#partners/' + pid); }
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140edit]'))) { e.preventDefault(); editForm(el.getAttribute('data-w140edit'), el.getAttribute('data-id')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140add]'))) { e.preventDefault(); editForm(el.getAttribute('data-w140add'), null); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140ev]'))) { e.preventDefault(); flashEv(el.getAttribute('data-w140ev')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140year]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var y = document.getElementById('w140y' + el.getAttribute('data-w140year')); if (y) y.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140kind]'))) { S.ui.kind = el.getAttribute('data-w140kind'); paintStory(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140order]'))) { S.ui.order = S.ui.order === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc'; paintStory(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140hidden]'))) { S.ui.hidden = !S.ui.hidden; paintStory(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140area]'))) { S.ui.lArea = el.getAttribute('data-w140area'); paintStory(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140pset]'))) { S.ui.pSet = el.getAttribute('data-w140pset'); paintPartners(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140pcat]'))) { S.ui.pCat = el.getAttribute('data-w140pcat'); paintPartners(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140dreuse]'))) { S.ui.dReuse = !S.ui.dReuse; S.ui.dShow = 40; paintDocs(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w140dmore]'))) { S.ui.dShow += 40; paintDocs(); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.getAttribute || !t.hasAttribute('data-w140q')) return;
    var which = t.getAttribute('data-w140q'), v = t.value;
    clearTimeout(qT);
    qT = setTimeout(function () {
      if (which === 'story') { S.ui.q = v; paintStory(); }
      else if (which === 'lessons') { S.ui.lq = v; paintStory(); }
      else if (which === 'partners') { S.ui.pq = v; paintPartners(); }
      else if (which === 'docs') { S.ui.dq = v; S.ui.dShow = 40; paintDocs(); }
    }, 160);
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('.w140root')) return;
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w140city')) { S.ui.city = t.value; paintStory(); }
    else if (t.hasAttribute('data-w140prel')) { S.ui.pRel = t.value; paintPartners(); }
    else if (t.hasAttribute('data-w140pkind')) { S.ui.pKind = t.value; paintPartners(); }

  });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () {

    if (S.sheet && !(root === 'partners' && /^#partners\/\d+/.test(location.hash))) closeSheet();
  });
  /* a link to a Hub page inside an Avenue Academy lesson (#ourstory, #partners, #documents) opens here, not in a new tab;
     the lesson closes itself when the page changes (wave122's route) */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var a = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('#w122sheet a[href^="#"]') : null;
    if (!a || e.defaultPrevented || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey || e.button > 0) return;
    var h = a.getAttribute('href') || ''; if (h.length < 2) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    location.hash = h;
  }, true);

  /* ---------- the menu: Resources > Our story, Partners, Document finder ---------- */
  var PAGES = [
    { id: 'ourstory', label: 'Our story', group: 'org', dot: GOLD, gate: 'member', onShow: function (h) { openStory(h); } },

/* =====================================================================
   WA v143 the board pack (26/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   The first of the Google projects Steel approved ("go for all the above", 26/09/26): the board pack from Docs and Slides,
   the quarterly board deck and agenda filled from the teams' updates and numbers. Under the next board meeting on the Board
   overview (#governance), a Board pack card:
   - before it is built: what the pack holds, and (admins and the Executive profile) Build the board pack, which asks the
     board-pack edge function to create the agenda, the meeting notes and the deck in the Board of Directors drive;
   - once built: links to the agenda, the notes, the deck and the folder, who built it and the date of its numbers, and
     (admins and the Executive profile) Refresh the deck numbers, which rewrites only the Hub's own slides.
   Directors and leaders who can see the board page see the links. The Board overview tour gains a step.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa143) return; window.__wa143 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', GOLD = '#DCA527', INK = '#1F201E', MUTED = '#797773', FLAG = '#B3402F';
  var HEAD = "'Barlow Condensed','Arial Narrow','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;'); }
  /* a link is drawn only for a web address (the meeting's links can be edited by leaders) */
  function U(u) { u = String(u || '').trim(); return /^https:\/\//i.test(u) ? u : ''; }
  function toastS(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  var P = {
    doc: '<path d="M6 3h8l5 5v13H6z"/><path d="M14 3v5h5M9 13h7M9 16.5h5"/>',
    deck: '<rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="12" rx="1.5"/><path d="M12 16v4M8 20h8"/>',
    folder: '<path d="M3 7a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h4l2 2h8a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v8a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2z"/>',
    spark: '<path d="M12 3.5 13.8 9.2 19.5 11 13.8 12.8 12 18.5 10.2 12.8 4.5 11 10.2 9.2z"/>',
    refresh: '<path d="M20 11a8 8 0 0 0-14.3-4.9L4 8M4 4v4h4M4 13a8 8 0 0 0 14.3 4.9L20 16M20 20v-4h-4"/>'
  };
  function I(n) { return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">' + (P[n] || P.spark) + '</svg>'; }

  var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'w143css';
  st.textContent = ''
    + '.w143pack{margin:12px 0 4px;background:var(--wa-card,' + CREAM + ');border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.1);border-radius:16px;padding:14px 16px;display:flex;gap:14px;align-items:flex-start;flex-wrap:wrap;font-family:' + BODY + ';}'
    + '.w143pack .ic{width:40px;height:40px;border-radius:12px;background:rgba(220,165,39,.16);color:#7a5a12;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex:0 0 auto;}'
    + '.w143pack .ic svg{width:21px;height:21px;}'
    + '.w143pack .tx{flex:1 1 320px;min-width:0;}'
    + '.w143pack .tx b{display:block;font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-size:19px;font-weight:600;color:' + GREEN + ';text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.3px;}'
    + '.w143pack .tx p{margin:3px 0 0;font-size:13px;line-height:1.5;color:var(--wa-text,' + INK + ');}'
    + '.w143pack .tx .meta{font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted,' + MUTED + ');margin-top:6px;}'
    + '.w143pack .links{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:10px;}'
    + '.w143pack .links a{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:7px 11px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.07);color:' + GREEN + ';font-size:13px;font-weight:600;text-decoration:none;}'
    + '.w143pack .links a:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.13);}'
    + '.w143pack .links svg{width:15px;height:15px;}'
    + '.w143pack .acts{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;}'
    + '.w143btn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;border:none;border-radius:999px;padding:9px 15px;background:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ';font:600 13px ' + BODY + ';cursor:pointer;}'
    + '.w143btn.soft{background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);color:' + GREEN + ';}'

    + '@media (max-width:700px){.w143pack{padding:12px;}.w143pack .acts{width:100%;}.w143btn{flex:1 1 auto;justify-content:center;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  /* ---------- who and what ---------- */
  function state() { try { return window.w126 && typeof w126.state === 'function' ? w126.state() : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function meetings() { var B = state(); return (B && B.data && Array.isArray(B.data.meetings)) ? B.data.meetings : []; }
  function nextMeeting() {
    var now = Date.now();
    return meetings().filter(function (m) { return m.meets_at && m.kind !== 'strategy' && new Date(m.meets_at).getTime() >= now - 3 * 3600000 && m.status !== 'cancelled' && m.status !== 'held'; })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return new Date(a.meets_at) - new Date(b.meets_at); })[0] || null;
  }
  function mine() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } }
  function canBuild() { var r = mine(); return r.role === 'admin' || r.profile === 'executive'; }
  function dayOf(iso) { try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso || '').slice(0, 10); } }
  var BUSY = {}, ERR = {};

  function cardHtml(m) {
    var pk = m.pack || {}, built = !!(pk.deck_ok || m.deck_url), busy = BUSY[m.id], err = ERR[m.id];
    var who = canBuild();
    var html = '<div class="ic">' + I('deck') + '</div><div class="tx"><b>Board pack</b>';
    if (!built) {
      html += '<p>The agenda, the meeting notes to fill in, and a deck of each team’s update, the numbers, the finances as totals, the three jobs and the committees, made from the Hub and saved in the Board of Directors drive. Everything can be edited after.</p>';
      if (!who) html += '<div class="meta">The executive directors build it before the meeting.</div>';
    } else {
      html += '<p>For the ' + E(m.title || 'board meeting') + ', ready in the Board of Directors drive. People updates stay out of it: the HR slide shows counts only.</p>'
        + '<div class="links">'
        + (U(m.agenda_url) ? '<a href="' + E(U(m.agenda_url)) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('doc') + 'Agenda</a>' : '')
        + (U(m.notes_url) ? '<a href="' + E(U(m.notes_url)) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('doc') + 'Meeting notes</a>' : '')
        + (U(m.deck_url) ? '<a href="' + E(U(m.deck_url)) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('deck') + 'Deck</a>' : '')
        + (U(pk.folder_url) ? '<a href="' + E(U(pk.folder_url)) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('folder') + 'Folder</a>' : '')
        + '</div><div class="meta">' + (pk.built_at ? 'Built ' + E(dayOf(pk.built_at)) + (pk.built_by && pk.built_by !== 'automation' ? ' by ' + E(pk.built_by) : '') : '')
        + (pk.as_of ? ' · numbers as of ' + E(pk.as_of) : '') + '</div>';
    }
    if (err) html += '<div class="err">' + E(err) + '</div>';
    html += '</div>';
    if (who) {
      html += '<div class="acts">' + (!built
        ? '<button class="w143btn" data-w143build="' + m.id + '"' + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('spark') + (busy ? ' Building… about half a minute' : ' Build the board pack') + '</button>'
        : '<button class="w143btn soft" data-w143refresh="' + m.id + '"' + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + ' title="Rewrites the Hub’s own slides with today’s updates and numbers; slides you added and the two documents stay as they are">' + I('refresh') + (busy ? ' Refreshing…' : ' Refresh the deck numbers') + '</button>') + '</div>';
    }
    return html;
  }
  function paint() {
    var host = document.getElementById('w126gov'); if (!host) return;
    var meet = host.querySelector('.w126meet'), el = host.querySelector('.w143pack'), m = nextMeeting();
    if (!meet || !m) { if (el) el.remove(); return; }
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.className = 'w143pack'; meet.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', el); }
    var html = cardHtml(m);
    if (el.__h !== html) { el.innerHTML = html; el.__h = html; el.setAttribute('data-meeting', m.id); }
  }
  /* the board page repaints itself; the card follows it */
  var mo = null;
  function watch() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('governance'); if (!sec || mo) return;
    mo = new MutationObserver(function () { if (!document.querySelector('#w126gov .w143pack') && document.querySelector('#w126gov .w126meet')) paint(); });
    mo.observe(sec, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  }

  async function run(action, id) {
    BUSY[id] = true; ERR[id] = ''; paint();
    try {
      var r = await db.functions.invoke('board-pack', { body: { action: action, meeting_id: Number(id) } });
      var d = (r && r.data) || {};
      if (r && r.error) { var msg = ''; try { msg = (await r.error.context.json()).error; } catch (e) { msg = String(r.error.message || r.error); } throw new Error(msg || 'The Hub could not build the pack'); }
      if (d.error) throw new Error(d.error);
      var m = meetings().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0];
      if (m && d.pack) { m.pack = d.pack; if (d.pack.agenda_url) m.agenda_url = d.pack.agenda_url; if (d.pack.notes_url) m.notes_url = d.pack.notes_url; if (d.pack.deck_url) m.deck_url = d.pack.deck_url; }
      toastS(action === 'build' ? 'The board pack is ready in the Board of Directors drive' : 'The deck has today’s updates and numbers');
    } catch (e) { ERR[id] = String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 220); }
    BUSY[id] = false; paint();
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var b = t.closest('[data-w143build]'); if (b) { e.preventDefault(); if (!b.disabled) run('build', b.getAttribute('data-w143build')); return; }
    var r = t.closest('[data-w143refresh]'); if (r) { e.preventDefault(); if (!r.disabled) run('refresh', r.getAttribute('data-w143refresh')); return; }
  });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { setTimeout(function () { watch(); paint(); }, 60); });
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa143 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () { var out = await __sa143.apply(this, arguments); try { watch(); paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }

  /* the Board overview tour: a step for the pack, after the next meeting */
  function addStep() {
    try {
      var T = window.w122 && window.w122.tours && window.w122.tours.governance; if (!T || T.__w143) return;
      var i = T.steps.map(function (s) { return s.s; }).indexOf('#w126gov .w126meet');
      T.steps.splice(i >= 0 ? i + 1 : 2, 0, { s: '#w126gov .w143pack', t: 'The board pack', p: 'The agenda, the meeting notes and a deck of the teams’ updates and numbers, built from the Hub into the Board of Directors drive. The executive directors build it and refresh the numbers before the meeting.', opt: 1 });
      T.__w143 = true;
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  addStep(); setTimeout(addStep, 1500);

  window.w143 = { paint: paint, next: nextMeeting, card: cardHtml, run: run };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v144 Meet attendance (26/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel approved Meet attendance with the other Google projects ("go for all the above", 26/09/26). The meet-attendance
   edge function reads the Google Meet log every six hours and matches each call to the meetings on the hub@ calendar and
   to the board meetings (tables meet_meetings and meet_attendance; the database decides who reads what). Here:
   - each team page, under Upcoming meetings: Meeting attendance. Everyone on the team sees how many of the team came to
     each of the last eight meetings and their own attendance; whoever runs the meeting, the team's lead, the admins and
     the Executive profile also see who came, who missed their last three, and who is not on the invite;
   - the HR centre (the admins and the Executive profile): every team's attendance over eight weeks with the trend, the
     leadership meetings, and the people who missed their team's last three meetings, with Refresh now;
   - the Board overview: how many directors came to the last board meeting, and each director's board meetings this year.
   Someone counts as there after three minutes on the call; meeting notetakers are left out; a meeting with no call on Meet
   (in person, or cancelled) is not counted. The team page and HR centre tours gain a step.
   wave149 (27/09/26), Steel: "for attendance, I dont think it should be on the department overview page. I think it should
   only be seen on the HR center or in one of the admin pages". The team pages and the Board overview no longer show it (and
   the database only hands it to the admins and the Executive profile, wa149_attendance); the HR centre card gains each
   team's last eight meetings (click a team) and the board meetings.
   ===================================================================== */

    + '.w144ppl{border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.08);padding-top:8px;}'
