    + '.w144ppl .r{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) auto 54px 50px;gap:8px;align-items:center;padding:4px 0;font-size:13px;}'
    + '.w144ppl .r a{color:var(--wa-text,' + INK + ');text-decoration:none;cursor:pointer;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;}'
    + '.w144ppl .r a:hover{color:' + GREEN + ';text-decoration:underline;}'
    + '.w144ppl .r.flag a{color:' + FLAG + ';font-weight:600;}'
    + '.w144ppl .r .n{font-weight:700;color:' + GREEN + ';text-align:right;font-size:12.5px;}'
    + '.w144ppl .r .m{color:var(--wa-muted,' + MUTED + ');font-size:11.5px;text-align:right;}'
    + '.w144dots{display:inline-flex;gap:4px;}'
    + '.w144dots i{width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;display:inline-block;border:1.5px solid ' + TEAL + ';box-sizing:border-box;}'
    + '.w144dots i.came{background:' + TEAL + ';}'
    + '.w144dots i.missed{border-color:' + FLAG + ';}'
    + '.w144dots i.na{border:none;background:rgba(121,119,115,.25);transform:scale(.5);}'
    + '.w144ppl .more{font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted,' + MUTED + ');margin:6px 0 0;line-height:1.5;}'
    + '.w144ppl .more a{color:' + GREEN + ';cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;}'
    + '.w144note{font-size:12px;line-height:1.5;color:var(--wa-muted,' + MUTED + ');margin:8px 0 0;}'
    + '.w144nudge{font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.5;background:rgba(220,165,39,.13);border-radius:10px;padding:8px 10px;margin-top:8px;color:var(--wa-text,' + INK + ');}'
    + '.w144tbl{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:13px;margin:4px 0 10px;}'
    + '.w144tbl th{font:700 10.5px ' + BODY + ';letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--wa-muted,' + MUTED + ');text-align:left;padding:6px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.1);}'
    + '.w144tbl td{padding:7px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.06);vertical-align:middle;}'
    + '.w144tbl td.num{font-weight:700;color:' + GREEN + ';white-space:nowrap;}'
    + '.w144tbl a{color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:600;text-decoration:none;cursor:pointer;}'
    + '.w144tr{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:2px;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:600;}'
    + '.w144tr svg{width:12px;height:12px;}'
    + '.w144tr.up{color:' + TEAL + ';}.w144tr.down{color:' + FLAG + ';}'
    + '.w144top{display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:space-between;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:4px;}'
    + '.w144top .read{flex:1 1 320px;margin:0;}'
    + '.w144top .w144btn{flex:0 0 auto;}'
    + '.w144btn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;border:none;border-radius:999px;padding:7px 12px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);color:' + GREEN + ';font:600 12.5px ' + BODY + ';cursor:pointer;}'
    + '.w144btn[disabled]{opacity:.55;cursor:progress;}'
    + '.w144btn svg{width:14px;height:14px;}'
    + '.w144sub{font:700 11px ' + BODY + ';letter-spacing:1.2px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + GREEN + ';margin:14px 0 6px;}'
    + '.w144list{margin:0;padding:0;list-style:none;font-size:13px;}'
    + '.w144tbl tr.on td{background:rgba(16,63,57,.05);}'
    + '.w144teamdet{border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.12);border-radius:12px;padding:4px 12px 10px;margin:0 0 12px;}'
    + '.w144teamdet .w144sub{margin-top:10px;display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;}'
    + '.w144teamdet .w144sub .lnk{margin-left:auto;font:600 12px ' + BODY + ';letter-spacing:0;text-transform:none;color:' + GREEN + ';cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;}'
    + '.w144list li{padding:5px 0;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.06);display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:space-between;flex-wrap:wrap;}'
    + '.w144list li span{color:var(--wa-muted,' + MUTED + ');font-size:12px;}'
    + '.w144list li a{color:var(--wa-text,' + INK + ');font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:none;}'
    + '.w144b{color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:600;}'
    + '.w126meet .w144att{display:block;font-size:12px;margin-top:4px;color:var(--wa-muted,' + MUTED + ');}'
    + '@media (max-width:700px){.w144ppl .r{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) auto 46px;}.w144ppl .r .m{display:none;}.w144tbl .opt{display:none;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  /* ---------- the data (the database decides what each person reads) ---------- */
  var M = { meetings: [], rows: [], by: {}, at: 0, ok: false, busy: null, state: null, sel: {}, more: {} };
  async function all(q) {
    var out = [], from = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
      var r = await q().range(from, from + 999);
      if (r.error) throw r.error;
      out = out.concat(r.data || []);
      if (!r.data || r.data.length < 1000) break;
      from += 1000;
    }
    return out;
  }
  function load(force) {
    if (!force && M.ok && Date.now() - M.at < 5 * 60000) return Promise.resolve(M);
    if (M.busy) return M.busy;
    M.busy = (async function () {
      try {
        var since = new Date(Date.now() - 400 * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
        var ms = await all(function () { return db.from('meet_meetings').select('*').or('scheduled_start.gte.' + since + ',started_at.gte.' + since).order('id'); });
        var rs = await all(function () { return db.from('meet_attendance').select('meeting_id,person,member_id,director_id,name,expected,came,home_team,optional,rsvp,joined_at,minutes,late_min').order('meeting_id').order('person'); });
        M.meetings = ms; M.rows = rs; M.by = {};
        rs.forEach(function (r) { (M.by[r.meeting_id] = M.by[r.meeting_id] || []).push(r); });
        M.ok = true; M.at = Date.now();
        if (isEditor()) { try { var s = await db.from('meet_sync_state').select('*').eq('id', 1).maybeSingle(); M.state = (s && s.data) || null; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
      } catch (e) { M.ok = false; }
      M.busy = null;
      return M;
    })();
    return M.busy;
  }

  /* ---------- who ---------- */
  function mine() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } }
  function isEditor() { var r = mine(); return r.role === 'admin' || (r.profile === 'executive' && r.role !== 'blocked'); }
  function myEmail() { try { return String((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase(); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function members() { try { return typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' && Array.isArray(DB_MEMBERS) ? DB_MEMBERS : []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function me() { var em = myEmail(); return members().filter(function (m) { return String(m.email || '').toLowerCase() === em; })[0] || null; }
  function isMine(r) { var m = me(); return (m && r.member_id != null && String(r.member_id) === String(m.id)) || r.person === myEmail(); }
  function label(d) { try { if (typeof DB_META !== 'undefined' && DB_META[d] && DB_META[d].label) return DB_META[d].label; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return LABELS[d] || d; }

  /* ---------- dates and numbers ---------- */
  function t(iso) { return iso ? new Date(iso).getTime() : 0; }
  function when(m) { return m.scheduled_start || m.started_at; }
  function dshort(iso) { try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { timeZone: TZ, month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso || '').slice(5, 10); } }
  function dlong(iso) { try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleString('en-US', { timeZone: TZ, weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso || '').slice(0, 16); } }
  function pct(a, b) { return b ? Math.round(100 * a / b) : null; }
  function rowsOf(m) { return M.by[m.id] || []; }
  function firstName(r) { var n = String(r.name || r.person || ''); return n; }
  function names(list) { return list.map(function (r) { return E(firstName(r)) + (r.late_min >= 10 ? ' <span class="miss">(' + r.late_min + ' min late)</span>' : ''); }).join(', '); }

  /* a team's scheduled meetings so far, oldest first */
  function teamMeetings(d) {
    var now = Date.now();
    return M.meetings.filter(function (m) { return m.kind === 'team' && m.department === d && m.scheduled_start && t(m.scheduled_start) <= now; })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return t(a.scheduled_start) - t(b.scheduled_start); });
  }
  /* the team's share of a meeting: the team's own people among the invited, or everyone invited when none of them is */
  function share(m) { return m.team_n ? { c: m.team_came_n, n: m.team_n } : { c: m.expected_came_n, n: m.expected_n }; }
  function rate(ms) { var c = 0, n = 0; ms.forEach(function (m) { if (!m.held) return; var s = share(m); c += s.c; n += s.n; }); return { c: c, n: n, p: pct(c, n), held: ms.filter(function (m) { return m.held; }).length }; }
  function seesPeople(ms) { return ms.some(function (m) { return rowsOf(m).some(function (r) { return !isMine(r); }); }); }

  /* each person across the meetings shown: a dot per meeting, how many they came to of those they were invited to */
  function people(ms) {
    var P = {}, order = [];
    ms.forEach(function (m) {
      rowsOf(m).forEach(function (r) {
        if (!r.expected && !r.came) return;
        var p = P[r.person];
        if (!p) { p = P[r.person] = { key: r.person, name: r.name || r.person, member_id: r.member_id, home: false, expectedSome: false, cells: {}, came: 0, of: 0, mins: [], last: null, run: 0 }; order.push(p); }
        if (r.home_team) p.home = true;
        if (!r.expected) { p.cells[m.id] = r.came ? 'came' : 'na'; if (r.came) p.extra = (p.extra || 0) + 1; return; }
        p.expectedSome = true;
        if (!m.held) { p.cells[m.id] = 'na'; return; }
        p.of++;
        if (r.came) { p.came++; p.cells[m.id] = 'came'; p.mins.push(r.minutes || 0); p.last = m.scheduled_start; }
        else p.cells[m.id] = 'missed';
      });
    });
    /* missed in a row, from the most recent meeting back */
    order.forEach(function (p) {
      var run = 0;
      for (var i = ms.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { var c = p.cells[ms[i].id]; if (c === 'missed') run++; else if (c === 'came') break; }
      p.run = run;
    });
    order.sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    return order;
  }
  function dots(p, ms) { return '<span class="w144dots">' + ms.map(function (m) { var c = p.cells[m.id] || 'na'; return '<i class="' + c + '" title="' + E(dshort(m.scheduled_start || m.started_at)) + ': ' + (c === 'came' ? 'came' : c === 'missed' ? 'missed' : m.held ? 'not invited' : 'no call') + '"></i>'; }).join('') + '</span>'; }
  function avgMin(p) { if (!p.mins.length) return ''; return Math.round(p.mins.reduce(function (a, b) { return a + b; }, 0) / p.mins.length) + ' min'; }
  function personRow(p, ms) {
    var go = p.member_id != null ? ' data-w144person="' + E(p.member_id) + '"' : '';
    return '<div class="r' + (p.run >= 3 ? ' flag' : '') + '"><a' + go + ' title="' + E(p.name) + (p.run >= 3 ? ': missed the last ' + p.run : '') + '">' + E(p.name) + '</a>' + dots(p, ms)
      + '<span class="n">' + (p.of ? p.came + ' of ' + p.of : '–') + '</span><span class="m">' + E(avgMin(p)) + '</span></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the team page card ---------- */
  function detailHtml(m, withPeople, d) {
    if (!m) return '';
    var rs = rowsOf(m), s = share(m);
    var head = '<span class="h">' + E(dlong(m.scheduled_start || m.started_at)) + '</span>';
    if (!m.held) return '<div class="w144det">' + head + ': no call on Meet. It may have been held in person, moved or cancelled; it is left out of the numbers.</div>';
    var line = head + ': ' + s.c + ' of ' + s.n + ' of the team came' + (m.guests_n ? ', with ' + m.guests_n + ' other' + (m.guests_n === 1 ? '' : 's') : '') + (m.avg_minutes ? ', ' + m.avg_minutes + ' minutes on average' : '') + '.';
    if (!withPeople) {
      var mineRow = rs.filter(isMine)[0];
      return '<div class="w144det">' + line + (mineRow && mineRow.expected ? ' ' + (mineRow.came ? 'You came.' : 'You were not on the call.') : '') + '</div>';
    }
    var came = rs.filter(function (r) { return r.came; }), missed = rs.filter(function (r) { return r.expected && !r.came; });
    return '<div class="w144det">' + line + (came.length ? '<br>Came: ' + names(came) + '.' : '')
      + (missed.length ? '<br><span class="miss">Not on the call: ' + missed.map(function (r) { return E(firstName(r)) + (r.rsvp === 'declined' ? ' (declined)' : ''); }).join(', ') + '.</span>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function teamCardHtml(d, inHR) {
    var all = teamMeetings(d);
    var head = inHR ? '<div class="w144sub">' + E(label(d)) + ': the last eight meetings <a class="lnk" data-w144team="' + E(d) + '">Close</a></div>' : '<h3>Meeting attendance</h3>';
    if (!all.length) return head + '<p class="w116empty">No team meeting on the hub calendar yet. When the team meeting invites hub@welfareavenue.com, who came to each one shows here.</p>';
    var ms = all.slice(Math.max(0, all.length - WINDOW));
    var r = rate(ms), withPeople = seesPeople(ms);
    var selId = M.sel[d]; var sel = ms.filter(function (m) { return m.id === selId; })[0] || ms.slice().reverse().filter(function (m) { return m.held; })[0] || ms[ms.length - 1];
    var strip = '<div class="w144strip" role="list">' + ms.map(function (m) {
      var s = share(m), p = m.held ? pct(s.c, s.n) : null;
      return '<button type="button" role="listitem" class="col' + (m.held ? (p != null && p < 50 ? ' low' : '') : ' nocall') + (sel && m.id === sel.id ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w144m="' + E(m.id) + '" data-d="' + E(d) + '" title="' + E(dlong(m.scheduled_start)) + '">'
        + '<span class="bar"><i style="height:' + (m.held ? Math.max(4, p || 0) : 0) + '%"></i></span><b>' + (m.held ? s.c + '/' + s.n : 'no call') + '</b><small>' + E(dshort(m.scheduled_start)) + '</small></button>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
    var lead = r.held ? 'Over the last ' + (ms.length === 1 ? 'meeting' : ms.length + ' meetings') + (r.held < ms.length ? ' (' + r.held + ' with a call)' : '') + ', <b>' + r.p + '%</b> of the team came on average.'
      : 'None of the last ' + ms.length + ' meetings had a call on Meet.';
    var myP = people(ms).filter(function (p) { return p.key === myEmail() || (me() && String(p.member_id) === String(me().id)); })[0];
    if (myP && myP.of) lead += ' You came to ' + myP.came + ' of the ' + myP.of + ' you were invited to.';
    var html = (inHR ? head : '<h3>Meeting attendance <span class="lnk" data-w144help="1" title="How it is counted">from Google Meet</span></h3>') + '<p class="lead">' + lead + '</p>' + strip + detailHtml(sel, withPeople, d);
    if (withPeople) {
      var ps = people(ms), team = ps.filter(function (p) { return p.home && p.expectedSome; }), others = ps.filter(function (p) { return !p.home && p.expectedSome; }), visitors = ps.filter(function (p) { return !p.expectedSome && p.extra; });
      if (!team.length) { team = others; others = []; }
      html += '<div class="w144ppl"><h4>Who comes</h4>' + team.map(function (p) { return personRow(p, ms); }).join('');
      var open = M.more[d];
      if (others.length) html += open ? '<h4>Also invited</h4>' + others.map(function (p) { return personRow(p, ms); }).join('') + '<p class="more"><a data-w144more="' + E(d) + '">Hide the others invited</a></p>'
        : '<p class="more">Also invited: ' + others.map(function (p) { return E(p.name) + ' (' + p.came + ' of ' + p.of + ')'; }).join(', ') + '. <a data-w144more="' + E(d) + '">Show</a></p>';
      if (visitors.length) html += '<p class="more">Came without an invitation: ' + visitors.map(function (p) { return E(p.name); }).join(', ') + '.</p>';
      html += '</div>';
      /* on the team but not on the invite of the latest meeting */
      var latest = all[all.length - 1], invited = {};
      rowsOf(latest).forEach(function (x) { if (x.expected && x.member_id != null) invited[String(x.member_id)] = 1; });
      var missing = members().filter(function (m) { return m.department === d && m.active !== false && !invited[String(m.id)]; });
      if (missing.length && Object.keys(invited).length) html += '<div class="w144nudge"><b>Not on the meeting invite:</b> ' + missing.map(function (m) { return E(m.name) + (m.email ? '' : ' (no Welfare Avenue email on the roster)'); }).join(', ') + '. Add them to the recurring invite in Google Calendar so the meeting reaches them.</div>';
    }
    html += '<p class="w144note" data-w144note="1" hidden>Someone counts as there after three minutes on the call. Signed-out guests are recognised by their name when they can be, and meeting notetakers are left out. A meeting with no call on Meet is not counted. ' + (withPeople ? 'Who came is for whoever runs the meeting, the team lead and the executive directors; everyone on the team sees the counts and their own attendance.' : 'You see the counts and your own attendance; whoever runs the meeting and the team lead see who came.') + '</p>';
    return html;
  }
  function curTeam() { try { var c = window.W116 && W116.cur; return c && c.page === 'overview' ? c.d : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  /* the card shows when the team has meetings this person can read, and always for the admins and the Executive profile */
  function wantTeam(d) { return teamMeetings(d).length > 0 || (isEditor() && !!LABELS[d]); }
  /* wave149: attendance is no longer on the team pages; a card left from before is taken away */
  function paintTeam() { var el = document.getElementById('w144team'); if (el) el.remove(); }

  /* ---------- the HR centre card (admins and the Executive profile) ---------- */
  function trendHtml(now, before) {
    if (now == null || before == null) return '';
    var dlt = now - before; if (Math.abs(dlt) < 3) return '<span class="w144tr">steady</span>';
    return '<span class="w144tr ' + (dlt > 0 ? 'up' : 'down') + '">' + I(dlt > 0 ? 'up' : 'down') + Math.abs(dlt) + ' pts</span>';
  }
  function hrCardHtml() {
    var now = Date.now(), W8 = 56 * 86400000;
    var depts = {}; Object.keys(LABELS).forEach(function (k) { depts[k] = 1; }); M.meetings.forEach(function (m) { if (m.kind === 'team' && m.department) depts[m.department] = 1; });
    var keys = Object.keys(depts).sort(function (a, b) { return label(a).localeCompare(label(b)); });
    var rows = keys.map(function (d) {
      var ms = teamMeetings(d), cur = ms.filter(function (m) { return t(m.scheduled_start) > now - W8; }), prev = ms.filter(function (m) { var x = t(m.scheduled_start); return x <= now - W8 && x > now - 2 * W8; });
      var rc = rate(cur), rp = rate(prev), inv = 0, invC = 0;
      cur.forEach(function (m) { if (m.held) { inv += m.expected_n; invC += m.expected_came_n; } });
      if (!ms.length) return '<tr' + (M.sel.team === d ? ' class="on"' : '') + '><td><a data-w144team="' + E(d) + '" title="Show the team’s meetings">' + E(label(d)) + '</a></td><td colspan="4"><span class="w144note">No team meeting on the hub calendar: invite hub@welfareavenue.com to it.</span></td></tr>';
      return '<tr' + (M.sel.team === d ? ' class="on"' : '') + '><td><a data-w144team="' + E(d) + '" title="Show the last eight meetings and who came">' + E(label(d)) + '</a></td><td>' + (cur.length ? rc.held + ' of ' + cur.length : '<span class="w144note">none</span>') + '</td><td class="num">' + (rc.p != null ? rc.p + '%' : '–') + '</td><td>' + trendHtml(rc.p, rp.p) + '</td><td class="opt">' + (inv ? pct(invC, inv) + '%' : '–') + '</td></tr>';
    }).join('');
    var lead = M.meetings.filter(function (m) { return m.kind === 'leadership' && when(m) && t(when(m)) <= now; }).sort(function (a, b) { return t(when(b)) - t(when(a)); }).slice(0, 4);
    var leadHtml = lead.map(function (m) {
      var rs = rowsOf(m), came = rs.filter(function (r) { return r.came; });
      return '<li><a data-w144lm="' + E(m.id) + '">' + E(m.title || 'Leadership meeting') + '</a><span>' + E(dshort(when(m))) + ' · ' + (!m.held ? 'no call' : m.expected_n ? m.expected_came_n + ' of ' + m.expected_n + ' came' : came.length + ' came') + '</span></li>'
        + (M.sel.lm === m.id ? '<li><span>' + (came.length ? 'Came: ' + names(came) : 'No one recognised on the call') + (rs.filter(function (r) { return r.expected && !r.came; }).length ? '. Not on the call: ' + rs.filter(function (r) { return r.expected && !r.came; }).map(function (r) { return E(firstName(r)); }).join(', ') : '') + '.</span></li>' : '');
    }).join('');
    /* people who missed their own team's last three meetings with a call */
    var flagged = [];
    keys.forEach(function (d) {
      var ms = teamMeetings(d).filter(function (m) { return m.held; }); ms = ms.slice(Math.max(0, ms.length - WINDOW));
      people(ms).forEach(function (p) { if (p.home && p.run >= 3) flagged.push({ p: p, d: d }); });
    });
    var flagHtml = flagged.map(function (f) {
      return '<li><a' + (f.p.member_id != null ? ' data-w144person="' + E(f.p.member_id) + '"' : '') + '>' + E(f.p.name) + '</a><span>' + E(label(f.d)) + ' · missed the last ' + f.p.run + (f.p.last ? ' · last came ' + E(dshort(f.p.last)) : ' · not at any meeting on record') + '</span></li>';
    }).join('');
    /* wave149: the board meetings (they used to show on the Board overview) */
    var boards = M.meetings.filter(function (m) { return m.kind === 'board' && m.scheduled_start && t(m.scheduled_start) <= now; }).sort(function (a, b) { return t(b.scheduled_start) - t(a.scheduled_start); });
    var boardHtml = '';
    if (boards.length) {
      var lastB = boards[0], yr = boards.filter(function (m) { return m.held && t(m.scheduled_start) > now - 365 * 86400000; }), dirs = {}, dOrder = [];
      yr.forEach(function (m) { rowsOf(m).forEach(function (r) { if (!r.expected || r.director_id == null) return; var k = String(r.director_id); if (!dirs[k]) { dirs[k] = { name: r.name || r.person, c: 0, n: 0 }; dOrder.push(k); } dirs[k].n++; if (r.came) dirs[k].c++; }); });
      dOrder.sort(function (a, b) { return String(dirs[a].name).localeCompare(String(dirs[b].name)); });
      boardHtml = '<div class="w144sub">Board meetings</div><ul class="w144list"><li><b>' + E(lastB.title || 'Last board meeting') + '</b><span>' + E(dshort(lastB.scheduled_start)) + ' · ' + (lastB.held ? lastB.expected_came_n + ' of ' + lastB.expected_n + ' directors came' : 'no call found on Meet') + '</span></li>'
        + dOrder.map(function (k) { var x = dirs[k]; return '<li>' + E(x.name) + '<span>' + x.c + ' of ' + x.n + ' in the last twelve months</span></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>';
    }
    var teamDet = M.sel.team ? '<div class="w144teamdet">' + teamCardHtml(M.sel.team, true) + '</div>' : '';
    var stt = M.state;
    return '<h2>Meeting attendance</h2><div class="w144top"><p class="read">Who came to each team’s meetings on Google Meet over the last eight weeks, against the eight weeks before. Team came is the team’s own people among those invited; invited came counts everyone on the invite.</p>'
      + '<button type="button" class="w144btn" data-w144sync="1"' + (M.syncing ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('refresh') + (M.syncing ? ' Refreshing…' : ' Refresh now') + '</button></div>'
      + (rows ? '<table class="w144tbl"><thead><tr><th>Team</th><th>Meetings with a call</th><th>Team came</th><th>Trend</th><th class="opt">Invited came</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + rows + '</tbody></table>' + teamDet : '<p class="w116empty">No team meeting on the hub calendar yet.</p>')
      + (leadHtml ? '<div class="w144sub">Leadership meetings</div><ul class="w144list">' + leadHtml + '</ul>' : '')
      + boardHtml
      + '<div class="w144sub">Worth a check-in</div>' + (flagHtml ? '<ul class="w144list">' + flagHtml + '</ul><p class="w144note">Missed their own team’s last three meetings with a call. A kind word first: people step back for many reasons.</p>' : '<p class="w144note">No one has missed their team’s last three meetings.</p>')
      + '<p class="w144note">From the Google Meet log, every six hours. Someone counts as there after three minutes on the call; notetakers are left out; a meeting with no call on Meet is not counted.' + (stt && stt.last_run_at ? ' Last read ' + E(dlong(stt.last_run_at)) + (stt.last_ok === false ? ' (it failed: ' + E(String(stt.note || '').slice(0, 120)) + ')' : '') + '.' : '') + '</p>';
  }
  function paintHR() {
    var host = document.getElementById('hrcentre'); if (!host || !isEditor()) return;
    var el = document.getElementById('w144hr');
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement('div'); el.className = 'card w144 w144hr'; el.id = 'w144hr'; el.innerHTML = '<h2>Meeting attendance</h2><p class="read">Loading…</p>'; el.__h = '';
      var after = document.getElementById('w103scope') || document.getElementById('w116jhours');
      if (after && after.parentNode) after.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', el); else host.appendChild(el);
    }
    if (!M.ok) {
      (M.busy || load()).then(function () { if (M.ok) paintHR(); else { var x = document.getElementById('w144hr'); if (x) { x.innerHTML = '<h2>Meeting attendance</h2><p class="read">The attendance could not load just now. Open the page again in a moment.</p>'; x.__h = ''; } } });
      return;
    }
    var html = hrCardHtml();
    if (el.__h !== html) { el.innerHTML = html; el.__h = html; }
  }

  /* ---------- the Board overview: wave149 took attendance off it (it is in the HR centre card); leftovers go ---------- */
  function paintBoard() { document.querySelectorAll('#w126gov .w144att, #w126gov .w144b').forEach(function (x) { x.remove(); }); }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  function paintAll() { try { paintTeam(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } try { paintHR(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } try { paintBoard(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  var obs = {};
  function watch(id, fn, test) {
    var sec = document.getElementById(id); if (!sec || obs[id]) return;
    var pending = false;
    obs[id] = new MutationObserver(function () { if (pending || !test()) return; pending = true; setTimeout(function () { pending = false; if (test()) fn(); }, 40); });
    obs[id].observe(sec, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  }
  function watchAll() {
    watch('hrcentre', paintHR, function () { return isEditor() && !document.getElementById('w144hr'); });
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var x = e.target; if (!x || !x.closest) return;
    var el;
    if ((el = x.closest('[data-w144m]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var d = el.getAttribute('data-d'); M.sel[d] = el.getAttribute('data-w144m'); paintHR(); return; }
    if ((el = x.closest('[data-w144more]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var k = el.getAttribute('data-w144more'); M.more[k] = !M.more[k]; paintHR(); return; }
    if ((el = x.closest('[data-w144help]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var box = el.closest('.w144'), n = box && box.querySelector('[data-w144note]'); if (n) n.hidden = !n.hidden; return; }
    if ((el = x.closest('[data-w144team]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var tm = el.getAttribute('data-w144team'); M.sel.team = M.sel.team === tm ? null : tm; paintHR(); return; }
    if ((el = x.closest('[data-w144lm]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var id = el.getAttribute('data-w144lm'); M.sel.lm = M.sel.lm === id ? null : id; paintHR(); return; }
    if ((el = x.closest('[data-w144go]'))) { e.preventDefault(); location.hash = el.getAttribute('data-w144go'); return; }
    if ((el = x.closest('[data-w144person]'))) { e.preventDefault(); location.hash = '#person/' + el.getAttribute('data-w144person'); return; }
    if ((el = x.closest('[data-w144sync]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); if (el.disabled || M.syncing) return;
      M.syncing = true; paintHR();
      db.functions.invoke('meet-attendance', { body: { action: 'sync', days: 14 } }).then(async function (r) {
        var ok = r && !r.error && r.data && r.data.ok;
        M.syncing = false; await load(true); paintAll();
        toastS(ok ? 'Meeting attendance is up to date' : 'The Hub could not read the Meet log just now');
      }).catch(function () { M.syncing = false; paintHR(); toastS('The Hub could not read the Meet log just now'); });
    }
  });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { setTimeout(function () { watchAll(); paintAll(); }, 80); });
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa144 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () { var out = await __sa144.apply(this, arguments); try { watchAll(); paintAll(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }

  /* ---------- tours: the team page and the HR centre ---------- */
  function addSteps() {
    try {
      var T = window.w122 && window.w122.tours; if (!T) return;
      var put = function (tour, after, step, flag) {
        var x = T[tour]; if (!x || x[flag]) return;
        var i = x.steps.map(function (s) { return s.s; }).indexOf(after);
        x.steps.splice(i >= 0 ? i + 1 : x.steps.length, 0, step); x[flag] = true;
      };
      put('hrcentre', '#w103scope', { s: '#w144hr', t: 'Meeting attendance', p: 'Each team’s attendance over eight weeks with the trend (click a team for its last eight meetings and who came), the leadership meetings, the board meetings, and anyone who missed their team’s last three meetings. Only the executive directors and admins see it.', opt: 1 }, '__w144');
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  addSteps(); setTimeout(addSteps, 1500);

  window.w144 = { load: load, state: function () { return M; }, team: paintTeam, hr: paintHR, board: paintBoard, teamHtml: teamCardHtml, hrHtml: hrCardHtml, people: people, rate: rate };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v145 Forms flows (26/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel approved Forms flows with the other Google projects ("go for all the above", 26/09/26). The forms-flow edge function
   reads the Google Forms the Hub knows (form_sources) every six hours and files each answer (form_responses). Here:
   - the Volunteering manager: on each serving, What volunteers said (the survey volunteers fill in after the serving: how
     it went, what beneficiaries needed, what slowed service, suggestions, the router's summary) and the Event audit (the
     lead's audit and restock form), with the survey link to send volunteers; on the calendar, the latest servings'
     feedback and the new sign-ups from the recurrent volunteers form waiting for review;
   - the HR centre (HR, the executive directors and admins): the onboarding quizzes (the policy quiz and the 30 day quiz):
     who took them, their score, whether the Hub ticked the step in their onboarding, and what new members said about the
     onboarding. Leaders, HR and admins can read the forms again now (Sync now).
   ===================================================================== */

  /* ---------- the data (the database decides what each person reads) ---------- */
  var F = { sources: [], rows: [], sums: [], ok: false, at: 0, busy: null, syncing: false };
  async function all(q) {
    var out = [], from = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < 20; i++) { var r = await q().range(from, from + 999); if (r.error) throw r.error; out = out.concat(r.data || []); if (!r.data || r.data.length < 1000) break; from += 1000; }
    return out;
  }
  function load(force) {
    if (!force && F.ok && Date.now() - F.at < 5 * 60000) return Promise.resolve(F);
    if (F.busy) return F.busy;
    F.busy = (async function () {
      try {
        var s = await db.from('form_sources').select('form_id,kind,label,title,quiz_step,responder_url,edit_url,questions,responses_n,last_response_at,last_synced_at,last_note').order('sort');
        F.sources = (s && s.data) || [];
        F.rows = await all(function () { return db.from('form_responses').select('response_id,form_id,kind,submitted_at,name,answers,score,max_score,event_id,member_id,volunteer_id,done').order('submitted_at', { ascending: false }); });
        var m = await db.from('form_summaries').select('form_id,scope,n,summary,created_at');
        F.sums = (m && m.data) || [];
        F.ok = true; F.at = Date.now();
      } catch (e) { F.ok = false; }
      F.busy = null; return F;
    })();
    return F.busy;
  }

  /* ---------- who ---------- */
  function mine() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } }
  function canSync() { var r = mine(); return r.role === 'admin' || r.role === 'leader' || r.profile === 'executive'; }
  function isHRView() { var r = mine(); return r.role === 'admin' || r.profile === 'executive' || r.profile === 'hr'; }
  function members() { try { return typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' && Array.isArray(DB_MEMBERS) ? DB_MEMBERS : []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function memberName(id) { var m = members().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; return m ? m.name : ''; }

  /* ---------- helpers ---------- */
  function dshort(iso) { try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { timeZone: TZ, month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso || '').slice(5, 10); } }
  function dday(d) { try { return new Date(d + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return d; } }
  function source(kind) { return F.sources.filter(function (s) { return s.kind === kind; }); }
  function ofKind(kind) { return F.rows.filter(function (r) { return r.kind === kind; }); }
  function qType(src, title) { var q = ((src && src.questions) || []).filter(function (x) { return x.title === title; })[0]; return q ? q.type : ''; }
  function isChoice(t) { return /choice|scale|rating|grid/i.test(t || ''); }
  function quiet(a) {
    var t = String(a || '').trim();
    if (/^nothing in particular/i.test(t)) return true;
    return t.length < 60 && /^(no|non|none|n\/?a|nothing|nope|rien|-|\.|not really|all good|no major|everything (was|is|seemed) (great|good|perfect|fine))\b/i.test(t) && !/\bbut\b|however/i.test(t);
  }
  function shortQ(q) { return String(q || '').replace(/\s*\(.*?\)\s*/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); }
  function events() { try { return typeof WA_VOL_EVENTS !== 'undefined' && Array.isArray(WA_VOL_EVENTS) ? WA_VOL_EVENTS : []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function eventDate(id) { var e = events().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; return e ? e.event_date : ''; }
  function summaryOf(eid) { var s = F.sums.filter(function (x) { return x.scope === 'event:' + eid; })[0]; return s || null; }

  /* ---------- the event: what volunteers said, and the audit ---------- */
  function feedbackHtml(eid) {
    var src = source('volunteer_feedback')[0];
    var rows = ofKind('volunteer_feedback').filter(function (r) { return String(r.event_id) === String(eid); });
    var send = src && U(src.responder_url) ? '<div class="send">' + I('link') + '<span>Send volunteers the survey after the serving:</span><a class="lk" href="' + E(U(src.responder_url)) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">open it</a><a class="lk" data-w145copy="' + E(U(src.responder_url)) + '">copy the link</a></div>' : '';
    if (!rows.length) return '<h4>What volunteers said</h4><p class="sub">No survey answers for this serving yet.</p>' + send;
    var html = '<h4>What volunteers said <span class="sub" style="margin:0">' + rows.length + ' answer' + (rows.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span></h4>';
    var s = summaryOf(eid);
    if (s && s.summary) html += '<div class="sum"><b>' + I('spark') + 'In short</b>' + E(s.summary) + '</div>';
    /* each question in the form's order: choices as counts, words as a list */
    var order = [], by = {};
    rows.forEach(function (r) { (r.answers || []).forEach(function (x) { if (!by[x.q]) { by[x.q] = []; order.push(x.q); } by[x.q].push(x.a); }); });
    order.forEach(function (q) {
      if (/contact|e-?mail|phone|cell/i.test(q)) return;
      var vals = by[q], t = qType(src, q);
      if (isChoice(t) || (!t && vals.every(function (v) { return String(v).length < 40; }) && new Set(vals).size < vals.length)) {
        var c = {}; vals.forEach(function (v) { String(v).split('; ').forEach(function (p) { if (p) c[p] = (c[p] || 0) + 1; }); });
        var keys = Object.keys(c).sort(function (a, b) { return c[b] - c[a]; });
        html += '<div class="q">' + E(shortQ(q)) + '</div><div class="chips">' + keys.map(function (k) { return '<span>' + E(k) + '<i>' + c[k] + '</i></span>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
      } else {
        var words = vals.filter(function (v) { return !quiet(v); });
        if (words.length) html += '<div class="q">' + E(shortQ(q)) + '</div><ul class="tx">' + words.map(function (v) { return '<li>' + E(v) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>';
      }
    });
    var contacts = order.filter(function (q) { return /contact|e-?mail|phone|cell/i.test(q); }).reduce(function (a, q) { return a.concat(by[q].filter(function (v) { return !quiet(v); })); }, []);
    if (contacts.length) html += '<div class="q">Left a contact for future opportunities</div><ul class="tx">' + contacts.map(function (v) { return '<li>' + E(v) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>';
    return html + send + foot(src);
  }
  function auditHtml(eid) {
    var src = source('event_audit')[0];
    var rows = ofKind('event_audit').filter(function (r) { return String(r.event_id) === String(eid); });
    if (!rows.length) return '';
    var cap = function (q) { q = shortQ(q); if (q === q.toUpperCase()) q = q.charAt(0) + q.slice(1).toLowerCase(); return q.length > 70 ? q.slice(0, 68).replace(/\s+\S*$/, '') + '…' : q; };
    return '<h4>Event audit</h4>' + rows.map(function (r) {
      var grid = [], rest = [];
      (r.answers || []).forEach(function (x) { if (/full name|event date/i.test(x.q)) return; (qType(src, x.q) === 'grid' ? grid : rest).push(x); });
      var html = '<p class="sub">' + E(r.name || 'The event lead') + ', ' + E(dshort(r.submitted_at)) + '</p><dl class="kv">';
      if (grid.length) {
        var met = grid.filter(function (x) { return /^(yes|oui)\b/i.test(x.a); }), not = grid.filter(function (x) { return !/^(yes|oui)\b/i.test(x.a); });
        html += '<dt>Standards met</dt><dd>' + met.length + ' of ' + grid.length + '</dd>';
        if (not.length) html += '<dt>Not met</dt><dd class="no">' + not.map(function (x) { return E((String(x.q).split(': ').slice(1).join(': ') || x.q).replace(/[.\s]+$/, '')) + (/^no\b/i.test(x.a) ? '' : ' (' + E(x.a) + ')'); }).join('; ') + '</dd>';
      }
      html += rest.map(function (x) { return '<dt>' + E(cap(x.q)) + '</dt><dd' + (/^no\b/i.test(x.a) && /dropped|spacious/i.test(x.q) ? ' class="no"' : '') + '>' + E(x.a) + '</dd>'; }).join('');
      return html + '</dl>';
    }).join('') + foot(src);
  }
  function foot(src) {
    if (!src) return '';
    return '<div class="foot">From the Google Form ' + E(String(src.title || src.label).trim()) + (src.last_synced_at ? ', read ' + E(dshort(src.last_synced_at)) : '') + '.' + (canSync() ? ' <a data-w145sync="1">' + (F.syncing ? 'Reading the forms…' : 'Read the forms now') + '</a>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function curEventId() { try { return typeof WA_VOL_VIEW !== 'undefined' && WA_VOL_VIEW === 'event' && typeof WA_VOL_EVENT !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_EVENT : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function put(host, id, html, first) {
    var el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!html) { if (el) el.remove(); return; }
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = id; el.className = 'cardy w145'; el.__h = ''; if (first && host.firstChild) host.insertBefore(el, host.firstChild.nextSibling || null); else host.appendChild(el); }
    if (el.__h !== html) { el.innerHTML = html; el.__h = html; }
  }
  function paintEvent() {
    var eid = curEventId(); var rail = document.getElementById('w119rail');
    if (!eid || !rail) return;
    if (!F.ok) { (F.busy || load()).then(function () { if (F.ok) paintEvent(); }); return; }
    if (!F.sources.length) return;
    put(rail, 'w145fb', feedbackHtml(eid), true);
    put(rail, 'w145au', auditHtml(eid), false);
  }

  /* ---------- the calendar: the latest feedback and the new sign-ups ---------- */
  function homeHtml() {
    var fb = ofKind('volunteer_feedback').filter(function (r) { return r.event_id; });
    var by = {}; fb.forEach(function (r) { (by[r.event_id] = by[r.event_id] || []).push(r); });
    var ids = Object.keys(by).sort(function (a, b) { return String(by[b][0].submitted_at).localeCompare(String(by[a][0].submitted_at)); }).slice(0, 4);
    var src = source('volunteer_feedback')[0];
    var html = '<h4>What volunteers said</h4><p class="sub">The survey volunteers fill in after each serving, by event. Open one for everything they said.</p>';
    if (ids.length) html += '<div class="evs">' + ids.map(function (id) {
      var rs = by[id], s = summaryOf(id), d = eventDate(id) || String(rs[0].submitted_at).slice(0, 10);
      var first = s && s.summary ? String(s.summary).split('. ')[0].replace(/\.?$/, '.') : '';
      return '<div class="ev" data-w145ev="' + E(id) + '"><b>' + E(dday(d)) + '</b><small>' + rs.length + ' volunteer' + (rs.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' answered</small>' + (first ? '<p>' + E(first) + '</p>' : '') + '</div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
    else html += '<p class="sub">No answers yet.</p>';
    var fresh = ofKind('volunteer_signup').filter(function (r) { return r.done && r.done.volunteer === 'created'; });
    if (fresh.length) html += '<div class="new"><b>' + fresh.length + ' new ' + (fresh.length === 1 ? 'volunteer' : 'volunteers') + '</b> from the recurrent volunteers form, added to the volunteer list for review (look for the review flag under Volunteers): ' + fresh.slice(0, 6).map(function (r) { return E(r.name || 'someone'); }).join(', ') + '.</div>';
    if (src && U(src.responder_url)) html += '<div class="send">' + I('link') + '<span>The survey to send after a serving:</span><a class="lk" href="' + E(U(src.responder_url)) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">open it</a><a class="lk" data-w145copy="' + E(U(src.responder_url)) + '">copy the link</a></div>';
    return html + foot(src);
  }
  function paintHome() {
    var cal = document.getElementById('w119cal'); if (!cal) return;

  }

  /* ---------- the HR centre: the onboarding quizzes ---------- */
  function hrHtml() {
    var rows = ofKind('onboarding_quiz'), srcs = source('onboarding_quiz');
    var label = {}; srcs.forEach(function (s) { label[s.form_id] = s.label; });
    var html = '<h2>Onboarding quizzes</h2><div class="w145top"><p class="read">The policy quiz and the 30 day quiz from the onboarding, read from their Google Forms. When someone with an onboarding in progress takes one, the Hub ticks that step for them with their score.</p>'
      + (canSync() ? '<button type="button" class="w145btn" data-w145sync="1"' + (F.syncing ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('refresh') + (F.syncing ? ' Reading…' : ' Sync now') + '</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    if (!rows.length) {
      var any = srcs.some(function (x) { return Number(x.responses_n) > 0; });
      return html + '<p class="read">' + (any ? 'The results are kept for the HR access list, the executive directors and the person who took the quiz.' : 'No quiz answers yet.') + '</p>';
    }
    html += '<table class="w145tbl"><thead><tr><th>When</th><th>Who</th><th>Quiz</th><th>Graded score</th><th class="opt">Onboarding</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
      + rows.slice(0, 12).map(function (r) {
        var who = r.member_id != null ? (memberName(r.member_id) || r.name || '') : (r.name || '');
        var go = r.member_id != null ? ' data-w145person="' + E(r.member_id) + '"' : '';
        var on = r.done && r.done.onboarding === 'ticked' ? '<span class="ok">Step ticked</span>' : '<span class="muted">' + (r.member_id == null ? 'Not linked to anyone on the roster' : 'No onboarding in progress') + '</span>';
        return '<tr><td>' + E(dshort(r.submitted_at)) + '</td><td>' + (go ? '<a class="lk"' + go + '>' + E(who) + '</a>' : E(who || 'Unknown')) + '</td><td>' + E(label[r.form_id] || '') + '</td><td class="num">' + (r.score != null ? E(r.score) + (r.max_score ? ' of ' + E(r.max_score) : '') : '–') + '</td><td class="opt">' + on + '</td></tr>';
      }).join('') + '</tbody></table><p class="w145 foot" style="margin-top:0">The score counts the questions the form grades itself (five on the 30 day quiz); HR reads the written answers in the form.</p>';
    /* what new members said about the onboarding (the 30 day quiz asks) */
    var said = [];
    rows.forEach(function (r) { (r.answers || []).forEach(function (x) { if (/feedback|anything to add/i.test(x.q) && !quiet(x.a) && String(x.a).length > 3) said.push({ a: x.a, at: r.submitted_at, who: r.member_id != null ? memberName(r.member_id) : r.name }); }); });
    if (said.length) html += '<div class="w145 q" style="margin-top:10px">What new members said about the onboarding</div>' + said.slice(0, 5).map(function (x) { return '<div class="w145quote">' + E(x.a) + '<small>' + E(x.who || 'Someone') + ', ' + E(dshort(x.at)) + '</small></div>'; }).join('');
    var s = srcs[0];
    return html + '<p class="w145 foot">Read every six hours' + (s && s.last_synced_at ? ', last ' + E(dshort(s.last_synced_at)) : '') + '. Answers stay with HR, the executive directors and the person who took the quiz.</p>';
  }
  function paintHR() {
    var host = document.getElementById('hrcentre'); if (!host || !isHRView()) return;
    var el = document.getElementById('w145quiz');
    if (!F.ok) { (F.busy || load()).then(function () { if (F.ok) paintHR(); }); return; }
    if (!source('onboarding_quiz').length) { if (el) el.remove(); return; }
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement('div'); el.className = 'card w145'; el.id = 'w145quiz'; el.__h = '';
      var after = document.getElementById('w144hr') || document.getElementById('w103scope') || document.getElementById('w116jhours');
      if (after && after.parentNode) after.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', el); else host.appendChild(el);
    }
    var html = hrHtml();
    if (el.__h !== html) { el.innerHTML = html; el.__h = html; }
  }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  function paintAll() { try { paintEvent(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } try { paintHome(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } try { paintHR(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  var obs = {};
  function watch(id, fn, test) {
    var sec = document.getElementById(id); if (!sec || obs[id]) return;
    var pending = false;
    obs[id] = new MutationObserver(function () { if (pending || !test()) return; pending = true; setTimeout(function () { pending = false; if (test()) fn(); }, 60); });
    obs[id].observe(sec, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  }
  function watchAll() {
    watch('volunteering', function () { paintEvent(); paintHome(); }, function () {
      if (!F.ok || !F.sources.length) return false;
      if (curEventId() && document.getElementById('w119rail') && !document.getElementById('w145fb')) return true;
      return !!(document.getElementById('w119cal') && !document.getElementById('w145home'));
    });
    watch('hrcentre', paintHR, function () { return F.ok && isHRView() && source('onboarding_quiz').length > 0 && !document.getElementById('w145quiz'); });
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var x = e.target; if (!x || !x.closest) return;
    var el;
    if ((el = x.closest('[data-w145ev]'))) { e.preventDefault(); location.hash = '#volunteering/e/' + el.getAttribute('data-w145ev'); return; }
    if ((el = x.closest('[data-w145person]'))) { e.preventDefault(); location.hash = '#person/' + el.getAttribute('data-w145person'); return; }
    if ((el = x.closest('[data-w145copy]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); var u = el.getAttribute('data-w145copy');
      try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(u).then(function () { toastS('The survey link is copied'); }, function () { toastS(u); }); } catch (er) { toastS(u); }
      return;
    }
    if ((el = x.closest('[data-w145sync]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); if (F.syncing || el.disabled) return;
      F.syncing = true; paintAll();
      db.functions.invoke('forms-flow', { body: { action: 'sync' } }).then(async function (r) {
        var ok = r && !r.error && r.data && r.data.ok;
        F.syncing = false; await load(true); paintAll();
        toastS(ok ? 'The forms are read and filed' : 'The Hub could not read the forms just now');
      }).catch(function () { F.syncing = false; paintAll(); toastS('The Hub could not read the forms just now'); });
    }
  });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { setTimeout(function () { watchAll(); paintAll(); }, 120); });
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa145 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () { var out = await __sa145.apply(this, arguments); try { watchAll(); paintAll(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }

  /* ---------- tours: the volunteering page and the HR centre ---------- */
  function addSteps() {
    try {
      var T = window.w122 && window.w122.tours; if (!T) return;
      var putStep = function (tour, after, step, flag) {
        var x = T[tour]; if (!x || x[flag]) return;
        var i = x.steps.map(function (s) { return s.s; }).indexOf(after);
        x.steps.splice(i >= 0 ? i + 1 : x.steps.length, 0, step); x[flag] = true;
      };
      putStep('volunteering', '#w119cal', { s: '#w145home', t: 'What volunteers said', p: 'The survey volunteers fill in after each serving, event by event, with a short summary. New sign-ups from the recurrent volunteers form wait for review in the volunteer list.', opt: 1 }, '__w145a');
      putStep('volunteering', '#w119rail', { s: '#w145fb', t: 'Volunteer feedback', p: 'Everything the volunteers said about this serving, and the link to send them the survey. The lead’s event audit sits below it once it is filled in.', opt: 1 }, '__w145b');
      putStep('hrcentre', '#w144hr', { s: '#w145quiz', t: 'Onboarding quizzes', p: 'Who took the policy quiz and the 30 day quiz, their score, and whether the Hub ticked the step in their onboarding.', opt: 1 }, '__w145');
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  addSteps(); setTimeout(addSteps, 1500);

  window.w145 = { load: load, state: function () { return F; }, event: paintEvent, home: paintHome, hr: paintHR, feedbackHtml: feedbackHtml, hrHtml: hrHtml };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v146 the monthly security check (26/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel approved a monthly security check with the other Google projects ("go for all the above", 26/09/26). The
   security-check edge function reads the Workspace directory, the Drive and the Google audit logs on the first of each month
   (and when an admin asks) and keeps what it found in security_checks (admins only). In Admin, Security and audit trail, at
   the top of the card: the latest check (the headline numbers against the check before), each finding with what to do and
   a link to the right Admin console page, the earlier checks, and Run the check now.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {

  var TZ = 'America/Toronto';
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;'); }
  function toastS(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function U(u) { u = String(u || '').trim(); return /^https:\/\//i.test(u) ? u : ''; }
  var P = {

  };
  function I(n) { return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">' + (P[n] || P.shield) + '</svg>'; }
  var LV = { high: ['Fix now', FLAG, 'rgba(179,64,47,.12)'], medium: ['Plan it', '#8a6414', 'rgba(220,165,39,.18)'], low: ['Worth a look', GREEN, 'rgba(16,63,57,.08)'], info: ['For the record', MUTED, 'rgba(121,119,115,.12)'], ok: ['Good', TEAL, 'rgba(42,140,122,.12)'] };

    + '#w146sec .hd h3 svg{width:17px;height:17px;}'

    + '@media (max-width:700px){#w146sec{padding:12px;}#w146sec .f .body{padding-left:4px;}#w146sec .hist .opt{display:none;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  var S = { rows: [], ok: false, at: 0, busy: null, running: false, open: {} };
  function mine() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } }
  function isAdmin() { return mine().role === 'admin'; }
  function load(force) {
    if (!force && S.ok && Date.now() - S.at < 5 * 60000) return Promise.resolve(S);
    if (S.busy) return S.busy;
    S.busy = (async function () {
      try { var r = await db.from('security_checks').select('*').order('ran_at', { ascending: false }).limit(12); S.rows = (r && r.data) || []; S.ok = !(r && r.error); S.at = Date.now(); }
      catch (e) { S.ok = false; }
      S.busy = null; return S;
    })();
    return S.busy;
  }
  function dlong(iso) { try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { timeZone: TZ, month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso || '').slice(0, 10); } }
  function dshort(iso) { try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { timeZone: TZ, month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso || '').slice(5, 10); } }
  function nextFirst() { var d = new Date(); var n = new Date(Date.UTC(d.getUTCFullYear(), d.getUTCMonth() + 1, 1, 16)); return dlong(n.toISOString()); }
  function delta(cur, prev, key, goodDown) {
    if (!prev || prev.summary == null || prev.summary[key] == null || cur[key] == null) return '';
    var d = Number(cur[key]) - Number(prev.summary[key]); if (!d) return ' <i>same</i>';
    var worse = goodDown ? d > 0 : d < 0;
    return ' <i class="' + (worse ? 'up' : 'down') + '">' + (d > 0 ? '+' : '') + d + '</i>';
  }
  function tile(n, label, bad, dl) { return '<div class="tile"><b' + (bad ? ' class="bad"' : '') + '>' + E(n) + '</b><span>' + label + dl + '</span></div>'; }

  function html() {
    var c = S.rows[0], prev = S.rows[1];
    var h = '<div class="hd"><div><h3>' + I('shield') + 'Monthly security check</h3>';
    if (!c) return h + '<p class="when">No check yet. It runs on the first of each month; run it now to see where Welfare Avenue stands.</p></div>' + btn() + '</div>';
    var s = c.summary || {};
    h += '<p class="when">Last run <b>' + E(dlong(c.ran_at)) + '</b>' + (c.ran_by && c.ran_by !== 'automation' ? ' by ' + E(c.ran_by) : '') + ', covering the ' + E(s.days || 30) + ' days before. <b>' + (s.high || 0) + '</b> to fix now, <b>' + (s.medium || 0) + '</b> to plan. Next on ' + E(nextFirst()) + '.</p></div>' + btn() + '</div>';
    h += '<div class="tiles">'
      + tile((s.with_2sv != null ? s.with_2sv : '–') + ' of ' + (s.active != null ? s.active : '–'), 'accounts with 2-step verification', (s.without_2sv || 0) > 0, delta(s, prev, 'with_2sv', false))
      + tile(s.unused_60 != null ? s.unused_60 : '–', 'active accounts unused for 60 days', (s.unused_60 || 0) > 0, delta(s, prev, 'unused_60', true))
      + tile(s.public_files != null ? s.public_files : '–', 'files open to anyone with the link' + (s.public_sensitive ? ', ' + s.public_sensitive + ' sensitive-looking' : ''), (s.public_sensitive || 0) > 0, delta(s, prev, 'public_files', true))
      + tile(s.shared_outside != null ? s.shared_outside : '–', 'files shared outside Welfare Avenue in ' + (s.days || 30) + ' days', false, '')
      + tile((s.sign_in_alarms || 0) + (s.sign_in_unusual || 0), 'sign-in warnings and unusual sign-ins', (s.sign_in_alarms || 0) > 0, '')
      + tile(s.apps_wide != null ? s.apps_wide : '–', 'apps with full Drive or Gmail access' + (s.apps ? ' (of ' + s.apps + ')' : ''), false, '')
      + '</div>';
    h += (c.findings || []).map(function (f, i) {
      var lv = LV[f.level] || LV.info, open = S.open[c.id + ':' + f.key];
      var items = (f.items || []).length ? '<ul>' + f.items.map(function (x) { return '<li>' + (U(x.url) ? '<a href="' + E(U(x.url)) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + E(x.label) + '</a>' : E(x.label)) + (x.sub ? ' <small>' + E(x.sub) + '</small>' : '') + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '';
      return '<div class="f' + (open ? ' open' : '') + '"><button type="button" data-w146f="' + E(c.id + ':' + f.key) + '" aria-expanded="' + (open ? 'true' : 'false') + '"><svg class="ch" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">' + P.chev + '</svg><span class="lv" style="color:' + lv[1] + ';background:' + lv[2] + '">' + lv[0] + '</span><span>' + E(f.title) + '</span></button>'
        + '<div class="body">' + E(f.detail || '') + (f.fix && f.level !== 'ok' ? '<div class="fix"><b>What to do:</b> ' + E(f.fix) + (U(f.link) ? ' <a class="out" href="' + E(U(f.link)) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open ' + (/admin\.google/.test(f.link) ? 'the Admin console' : 'Drive') + I('out') + '</a>' : '') + '</div>' : '') + items + '</div></div>';
    }).join('');
    if (c.note) h += '<p class="note">Some sources could not be read this time: ' + E(c.note) + '</p>';
    if (S.rows.length > 1) h += '<div class="hist">Earlier checks<table><thead><tr><th>Run</th><th>Without 2-step</th><th>Unused</th><th class="opt">Public files</th><th>To fix</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + S.rows.slice(1, 7).map(function (r) { var x = r.summary || {}; return '<tr><td>' + E(dshort(r.ran_at)) + '</td><td>' + E(x.without_2sv != null ? x.without_2sv : '–') + '</td><td>' + E(x.unused_60 != null ? x.unused_60 : '–') + '</td><td class="opt">' + E(x.public_files != null ? x.public_files : '–') + '</td><td>' + E(x.high || 0) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</tbody></table></div>';
    return h;
  }
  function btn() { return '<button type="button" class="w146btn" data-w146run="1"' + (S.running ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('refresh') + (S.running ? ' Checking… about a minute' : ' Run the check now') + '</button>'; }

  function paint() {
    var card = document.getElementById('w86card'); if (!card || !isAdmin()) return;
    if (!S.ok) { (S.busy || load()).then(function () { if (S.ok) paint(); }); return; }
    var el = document.getElementById('w146sec');
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w146sec'; el.__h = ''; var h2 = card.querySelector('h2'); if (h2) h2.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', el); else card.insertBefore(el, card.firstChild); }
    var x = html();
    if (el.__h !== x) { el.innerHTML = x; el.__h = x; }
  }
  var mo = null;
  function watch() {
    var p = document.getElementById('adminpanel'); if (!p || mo) return;
    var pending = false;
    mo = new MutationObserver(function () { if (pending || !S.ok || !isAdmin() || !document.getElementById('w86card') || document.getElementById('w146sec')) return; pending = true; setTimeout(function () { pending = false; paint(); }, 60); });
    mo.observe(p, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var el;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w146f]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var k = el.getAttribute('data-w146f'); S.open[k] = !S.open[k]; paint(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w146run]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); if (S.running || el.disabled) return;
      S.running = true; paint();
      db.functions.invoke('security-check', { body: { action: 'run' } }).then(async function (r) {
        var ok = r && !r.error && r.data && r.data.ok;
        S.running = false; await load(true); paint();
        toastS(ok ? 'The security check is done' : 'The security check could not run just now');
      }).catch(function () { S.running = false; paint(); toastS('The security check could not run just now'); });
    }
  });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { setTimeout(function () { watch(); paint(); }, 100); });
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa146 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () { var out = await __sa146.apply(this, arguments); try { watch(); paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }
  if (typeof wa40Render === 'function') {
    var __ar146 = wa40Render;
    wa40Render = async function () { var out = await __ar146.apply(this, arguments); try { watch(); paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }

  /* the admin tour: a step for the check, before the audit trail */
  function addStep() {
    try {
      var T = window.w122 && window.w122.tours; if (!T) return;
      Object.keys(T).forEach(function (k) {
        var x = T[k]; if (!x || !x.steps || x.__w146) return;
        var i = x.steps.map(function (s) { return s.s; }).indexOf('#w86card');
        if (i < 0) return;
        x.steps.splice(i + 1, 0, { s: '#w146sec', t: 'Monthly security check', p: 'On the first of each month the Hub checks the Workspace: 2-step verification, unused accounts, files anyone with the link can open, sharing outside Welfare Avenue, unusual sign-ins and connected apps. Each finding says what to do.', opt: 1 });
        x.__w146 = true;
      });
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  addStep(); setTimeout(addStep, 1500);

  window.w146 = { load: load, state: function () { return S; }, paint: paint, html: html };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v147 Google Search in Analytics (26/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel approved Search Console in Analytics with the other Google projects ("go for all the above", 26/09/26). The
   search-console edge function reads Google Search Console once a day and connects on its own as soon as the Hub's service
   account (or Steel's account) can see the Welfare Avenue property. On the Analytics page, under the platform tiles, a
   Google Search card: clicks, impressions, click-through rate and average position over the last 28 days of data against
   the 28 days before, the clicks day by day, and the top searches and pages. Until Search Console is connected, staff see
   the one step that connects it; directors see nothing.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa147) return; window.__wa147 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', GOLD = '#DCA527', INK = '#1F201E', MUTED = '#797773', FLAG = '#B3402F', TEAL = '#2A8C7A', BLUE = '#4285F4';
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;'); }

  var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'w147css';
  st.textContent = ''
    + '#w147{background:var(--wa-card,' + CREAM + ');border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.1);border-radius:16px;padding:16px 18px;margin:0 0 16px;font-family:' + BODY + ';}'
    + '#w147 .hd{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;}'
    + '#w147 .hd .g{width:30px;height:30px;border-radius:9px;background:' + BLUE + ';color:' + CREAM + ';display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-weight:700;font-size:15px;flex:0 0 auto;}'
    + '#w147 .hd h4{margin:0;font:700 12px ' + BODY + ';letter-spacing:1.3px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '#w147 .hd p{margin:2px 0 0;font-size:12.5px;color:var(--wa-muted,' + MUTED + ');}'
    + '#w147 .tl{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(140px,1fr));gap:8px;margin:12px 0;}'
    + '#w147 .t{border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.08);border-radius:12px;padding:9px 11px;}'
    + '#w147 .t b{display:block;font-size:21px;color:' + GREEN + ';line-height:1.1;}'
    + '#w147 .t span{display:block;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--wa-muted,' + MUTED + ');margin-top:3px;}'
    + '#w147 .t i{font-style:normal;font-weight:700;font-size:11px;margin-left:4px;}'
    + '#w147 .t i.up{color:' + TEAL + ';}#w147 .t i.down{color:' + FLAG + ';}'
    + '#w147 svg.sp{width:100%;height:64px;display:block;margin:4px 0 10px;}'
    + '#w147 .cols{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(260px,1fr));gap:14px;}'
    + '#w147 h5{margin:0 0 6px;font:700 11px ' + BODY + ';letter-spacing:1.1px;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--wa-muted,' + MUTED + ');}'
    + '#w147 table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12.5px;}'
    + '#w147 td{padding:5px 4px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.06);vertical-align:top;}'
    + '#w147 td.k{overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:var(--wa-text,' + INK + ');}'
    + '#w147 td.n{text-align:right;white-space:nowrap;font-weight:700;color:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '#w147 td.m{text-align:right;white-space:nowrap;color:var(--wa-muted,' + MUTED + ');font-size:11.5px;}'
    + '#w147 .ft{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--wa-muted,' + MUTED + ');margin-top:10px;}'
    + '#w147 .ft b{color:var(--wa-text,' + INK + ');}'
    + '#w147 code{font-size:11.5px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.07);padding:1px 5px;border-radius:5px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;}'
    + '#w147 .err{color:' + FLAG + ';font-size:12px;margin-top:6px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  var G = { site: null, daily: [], top: [], ok: false, at: 0, busy: null, sa: null, asked: false };
  function mine() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } }
  function staff() { var r = mine(); return !!r.role && r.role !== 'viewer' && r.role !== 'blocked'; }
  function load(force) {
    if (!force && G.ok && Date.now() - G.at < 10 * 60000) return Promise.resolve(G);
    if (G.busy) return G.busy;
    G.busy = (async function () {
      try {
        var s = await db.from('search_sites').select('*').limit(1);
        G.site = (s && s.data && s.data[0]) || null;
        if (G.site) {
          var since = new Date(Date.now() - 130 * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
          var d = await db.from('search_daily').select('day,clicks,impressions,ctr,position').eq('site_url', G.site.site_url).gte('day', since).order('day', { ascending: true }).limit(400);
          G.daily = (d && d.data) || [];
          var t = await db.from('search_top').select('kind,key,clicks,impressions,ctr,position,period_start,period_end').eq('site_url', G.site.site_url).order('clicks', { ascending: false }).limit(60);
          G.top = (t && t.data) || [];
        }
        G.ok = true; G.at = Date.now();
      } catch (e) { G.ok = false; }
      G.busy = null; return G;
    })();
    return G.busy;
  }
  function num(n) { n = Number(n) || 0; return n >= 100000 ? Math.round(n / 1000) + 'k' : n >= 1000 ? (n / 1000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'k' : String(Math.round(n)); }
  function pct(n) { return (Math.round((Number(n) || 0) * 1000) / 10) + '%'; }
  function dshort(d) { try { return new Date(d + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return d; } }
  /* the last 28 days that have data, and the 28 before */
  function windows() {
    var days = G.daily; if (!days.length) return null;
    var last = days[days.length - 1].day, lastT = new Date(last + 'T12:00:00Z').getTime();
    var cur = days.filter(function (r) { var t = new Date(r.day + 'T12:00:00Z').getTime(); return t > lastT - 28 * 86400000; });
    var prev = days.filter(function (r) { var t = new Date(r.day + 'T12:00:00Z').getTime(); return t <= lastT - 28 * 86400000 && t > lastT - 56 * 86400000; });
    function agg(list) { var c = 0, i = 0, pw = 0; list.forEach(function (r) { c += Number(r.clicks) || 0; i += Number(r.impressions) || 0; pw += (Number(r.position) || 0) * (Number(r.impressions) || 0); }); return { clicks: c, imps: i, ctr: i ? c / i : 0, pos: i ? pw / i : null, n: list.length }; }
    return { cur: agg(cur), prev: prev.length >= 14 ? agg(prev) : null, from: cur.length ? cur[0].day : last, to: last, series: cur };
  }
  function chg(a, b, lowerBetter, asPct) {
    if (b == null || a == null) return '';
    var d = asPct ? (a - b) * 100 : b ? (a - b) / b * 100 : null;
    if (d == null || !isFinite(d)) return '';
    if (Math.abs(d) < (asPct ? 0.1 : 1)) return '<i>same</i>';
    var good = lowerBetter ? d < 0 : d > 0;
    return '<i class="' + (good ? 'up' : 'down') + '">' + (d > 0 ? '+' : '') + (asPct ? d.toFixed(1) + ' pts' : Math.round(d) + '%') + '</i>';
  }
  function spark(series) {
    if (series.length < 2) return '';
    var max = Math.max.apply(null, series.map(function (r) { return Number(r.clicks) || 0; })) || 1, W = 600, H = 60;
    var yOf = function (v) { return H - 4 - (Number(v) || 0) / max * (H - 10); };
    var pts = series.map(function (r, i) { return (i / (series.length - 1) * W).toFixed(1) + ',' + yOf(r.clicks).toFixed(1); }).join(' ');
    /* wave149: hovering across the line shows the day's clicks, appearances, click rate and position */
    try {
      var full = function (v) { v = Number(v); return isFinite(v) ? Math.round(v).toLocaleString('en-CA') : '\u2013'; };
      (window.__w149ch = window.__w149ch || {}).search = { w: W, h: H,
        keys: series.map(function (r, i) { return { k: String(r.day).slice(0, 10), x: i / (series.length - 1) * W, r: r }; }),
        head: function (k) { try { return new Date(String(k).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return k; } },
        rows: function (k, kk) { var r = kk.r || {}; return [
          { label: 'Clicks from Google', value: full(r.clicks), color: BLUE, y: yOf(r.clicks) },
          { label: 'Times we appeared', value: full(r.impressions), color: null, y: null },
          { label: 'Click rate', value: r.ctr != null ? (Number(r.ctr) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '\u2013', color: null, y: null },
          { label: 'Average position', value: r.position != null ? (Math.round(Number(r.position) * 10) / 10).toString() : '\u2013', color: null, y: null }]; } };
    } catch (e) { /* the hover is extra */ }
    return '<svg class="sp" viewBox="0 0 ' + W + ' ' + H + '" preserveAspectRatio="none" role="img" data-w149h="search" tabindex="0" aria-label="Clicks from Google, day by day: hover or use the arrow keys to read the numbers"><polyline points="' + pts + '" fill="none" stroke="' + BLUE + '" stroke-width="2.2" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/></svg>';
  }
  function rows(kind) {
    var list = G.top.filter(function (r) { return r.kind === kind; }).slice(0, 10);
    if (!list.length) return '<p class="ft">Nothing yet.</p>';
    return '<table>' + list.map(function (r) {
      var k = kind === 'page' ? String(r.key).replace(/^https?:\/\/(www\.)?welfareavenue\.com/i, '') || '/' : r.key;
      return '<tr><td class="k">' + (kind === 'page' && /^https?:\/\//i.test(String(r.key)) ? '<a href="' + E(r.key) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:inherit">' + E(k) + '</a>' : E(k)) + '</td><td class="n">' + num(r.clicks) + '</td><td class="m">' + num(r.impressions) + ' seen' + (r.position ? ' · #' + Math.round(r.position) : '') + '</td></tr>';
    }).join('') + '</table>';
  }
  function html() {
    var head = '<div class="hd"><span class="g">G</span><div><h4>Google Search</h4><p>How people find welfareavenue.com on Google.</p></div></div>';
    if (!G.site) {
      return head + '<p class="ft" style="margin-top:10px">Not connected yet. In Google Search Console, open the Welfare Avenue property, Settings, Users and permissions, Add user, and add <code>' + E(G.sa || 'the Hub’s service account (the same one Google Analytics uses)') + '</code> with Restricted permission. The Hub connects on its own within a day.</p>';
    }
    var w = windows();
    if (!w) return head + '<p class="ft" style="margin-top:10px">Connected to <b>' + E(G.site.site_url) + '</b>. The first numbers arrive with the next daily read.' + (G.site.last_error ? '</p><p class="err">' + E(G.site.last_error) + '</p>' : '</p>');
    var c = w.cur, p = w.prev;
    return head
      + '<div class="tl">'
      + '<div class="t"><b>' + num(c.clicks) + chg(c.clicks, p && p.clicks) + '</b><span>clicks from Google</span></div>'
      + '<div class="t"><b>' + num(c.imps) + chg(c.imps, p && p.imps) + '</b><span>times we appeared in results</span></div>'
      + '<div class="t"><b>' + pct(c.ctr) + chg(c.ctr, p && p.ctr, false, true) + '</b><span>of those who saw us clicked</span></div>'
      + '<div class="t"><b>' + (c.pos ? '#' + c.pos.toFixed(1) : '–') + (c.pos && p && p.pos ? chg(c.pos, p.pos, true) : '') + '</b><span>average position</span></div>'
      + '</div>' + spark(w.series)
      + '<div class="cols"><div><h5>What people searched</h5>' + rows('query') + '</div><div><h5>The pages they opened</h5>' + rows('page') + '</div></div>'
      + '<p class="ft">The last 28 days of data, ' + E(dshort(w.from)) + ' to ' + E(dshort(w.to)) + (p ? ', against the 28 days before' : '') + '. Google reports about three days late. Read ' + E(G.site.last_sync_at ? new Date(G.site.last_sync_at).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }) : '') + ' from ' + E(G.site.site_url) + '.</p>'
      + (G.site.last_error ? '<p class="err">The last read failed: ' + E(G.site.last_error) + '</p>' : '');
  }
  function paint() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('socialstats'); if (!sec) return;
    var tiles = sec.querySelector('.w120tiles'); if (!tiles) return;
    if (!G.ok) { (G.busy || load()).then(function () { if (G.ok) paint(); }); return; }
    var el = document.getElementById('w147');
    if (!G.site && !staff()) { if (el) el.remove(); return; }
    if (!G.site && staff() && !G.asked) {
      G.asked = true;
      var again = function () { if (G.retried) return; G.retried = true; setTimeout(function () { G.asked = false; paint(); }, 3000); };
      db.functions.invoke('search-console', { body: { action: 'status' } }).then(function (r) {
        if (r && r.data && r.data.sa_email) { G.sa = r.data.sa_email; var x = document.getElementById('w147'); if (x) { x.__h = ''; paint(); } }
        else again();
      }).catch(again);
    }
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w147'; el.__h = ''; tiles.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', el); }
    var h = html();
    if (el.__h !== h) { el.innerHTML = h; el.__h = h; }
  }
  var mo = null;
  function watch() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('socialstats'); if (!sec || mo) return;
    var pending = false;
    mo = new MutationObserver(function () { if (pending || !G.ok || document.getElementById('w147') || !sec.querySelector('.w120tiles') || (!G.site && !staff())) return; pending = true; setTimeout(function () { pending = false; paint(); }, 60); });
    mo.observe(sec, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { setTimeout(function () { watch(); paint(); }, 150); });
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa147 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () { var out = await __sa147.apply(this, arguments); try { watch(); paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }
  /* the Analytics tour: a step for Google Search, after the tiles */
  function addStep() {
    try {
      var T = window.w122 && window.w122.tours; if (!T) return;
      Object.keys(T).forEach(function (k) {
        var x = T[k]; if (!x || !x.steps || x.__w147) return;
        var s = x.steps.map(function (y) { return y.s; }), i = -1;
        s.forEach(function (sel, j) { if (/w120tiles/.test(sel)) i = j; });
        if (i < 0) return;
        x.steps.splice(i + 1, 0, { s: '#w147', t: 'Google Search', p: 'How people find the website on Google: clicks, how often we appear, the searches that bring people and the pages they open, from Search Console.', opt: 1 });
        x.__w147 = true;
      });
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  addStep(); setTimeout(addStep, 1500);
  window.w147 = { load: load, state: function () { return G; }, paint: paint, html: html };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v149 the loading screen, the news banner, numbers on the Analytics graphs and the post photos (27/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (27/09/26): "On the loading page: I want you to put learnings, lessons, and milestones along with shoutouts. I've
   been seeing more shoutouts. And refresh it every week. Also at the bottom, I'd like for you to add sort of like a news
   banner of events that happened recently, updates that the organization should know about." "For the analytics graphs in
   marketing, I'd like to be able to hover accross to and see numbers." "The photos from instagram posts don't show up."
   "For attendance ... it should only be seen on the HR center or in one of the admin pages" (wave144 edited).
   - The loading screen: 000_head_base's first-paint script rotates milestones, shout-outs, lessons learned, learnings and
     fun facts in turn (one of each kind before any repeats, a random order each time) and runs the news banner along the
     bottom. It can only use what this browser kept from the last visit, so after sign-in this segment reads
     hub_reel_current() (this week's lines, rebuilt every Monday, and today's news, rebuilt every morning, by hub-reel) and
     the live shout-outs into localStorage wa149reel. Signing out clears it (and wave116p's older wa116s).
   - Admin, Loading screen and news (#w149card, admins): this week's lines and today's news, each can be taken off and put

  var cacheBusy = false;
  async function refreshCache() {
    if (!signedIn() || cacheBusy) return;
    cacheBusy = true;
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('hub_reel_current');
      if (r && !r.error) {
        var d = r.data || {}, sh = [];
        try {
          /* the Hub's own shout-outs only (kind auto): ones staff post stay in the HR centre */
          var q = await db.from('hub_shoutouts').select('text').eq('active', true).eq('kind', 'auto').gt('expires_at', new Date().toISOString()).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(12);
          sh = ((q && q.data) || []).map(function (x) { return String(x.text || '').trim(); }).filter(function (t) { return t && !CONF.test(t); });
        } catch (e) { sh = []; }
        var c = { v: 3, at: Date.now(), week: d.week || null,
          lines: (d.lines || []).filter(function (x) { return x && x.t && /^(milestone|lesson|learning)$/.test(x.k); }).slice(0, 24).map(function (x) { return { k: x.k, t: String(x.t) }; }),
          shouts: sh.slice(0, 12) };
        try { localStorage.setItem('wa149reel', JSON.stringify(c)); } catch (e) { /* private window: the fun facts show */ }
      } else clearCache(); /* not allowed (a former member) or an error: nothing stays behind */
    } catch (e) { clearCache(); }
    cacheBusy = false;
  }
  function refreshNews() { try { if (window.w151 && typeof window.w151.refresh === 'function') window.w151.refresh(true); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
