  /* ---------- 2. the hover on the Analytics graphs ---------- */
  var tip = null, guide = null, dots = [], cur = null;
  function ensure() {
    if (tip && document.body.contains(tip)) return;
    tip = document.createElement('div'); tip.id = 'w149tip'; tip.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); document.body.appendChild(tip);
    guide = document.createElement('div'); guide.id = 'w149guide'; document.body.appendChild(guide);
    dots = [];
  }
  function hide() { if (!tip) return; tip.style.display = 'none'; guide.style.display = 'none'; dots.forEach(function (d) { d.style.display = 'none'; }); cur = null; }
  function entryOf(el) { var k = el.getAttribute('data-w149h'); var reg = window.__w149ch || {}; var x = reg[k]; return x && x.keys && x.keys.length ? x : null; }
  function nearest(entry, vx) {
    var ks = entry.keys, lo = 0, hi = ks.length - 1;
    if (vx <= ks[0].x) return 0; if (vx >= ks[hi].x) return hi;
    while (hi - lo > 1) { var mid = (lo + hi) >> 1; if (ks[mid].x < vx) lo = mid; else hi = mid; }
    return (vx - ks[lo].x) <= (ks[hi].x - vx) ? lo : hi;
  }
  function place(el, entry, idx, clientY) {
    ensure();
    var r = el.getBoundingClientRect(); if (!r.width || !r.height) { hide(); return; }
    var kk = entry.keys[idx]; var px = r.left + kk.x / entry.w * r.width;
    guide.style.display = 'block'; guide.style.left = Math.round(px) + 'px'; guide.style.top = Math.round(r.top) + 'px'; guide.style.height = Math.round(r.height) + 'px';
    var rows = []; try { rows = entry.rows(kk.k, kk) || []; } catch (e) { rows = []; }
    var head = kk.k; try { head = entry.head ? entry.head(kk.k, kk) : kk.k; } catch (e) { /* the date as it is */ }
    tip.innerHTML = '<b>' + E(head) + '</b>' + rows.map(function (x) { return '<div class="r' + (x.strong ? ' s' : '') + '">' + (x.color ? '<i style="background:' + E(x.color) + '"></i>' : '<i class="n"></i>') + '<span>' + E(x.label) + '</span><em>' + E(x.value) + '</em></div>'; }).join('');
    tip.style.display = 'block';
    var withY = rows.filter(function (x) { return x.y != null && isFinite(x.y); });
    while (dots.length < withY.length) { var dd = document.createElement('div'); dd.className = 'w149dot'; document.body.appendChild(dd); dots.push(dd); }
    dots.forEach(function (d, i) { var x = withY[i]; if (!x) { d.style.display = 'none'; return; } d.style.display = 'block'; d.style.left = Math.round(px) + 'px'; d.style.top = Math.round(r.top + x.y / entry.h * r.height) + 'px'; d.style.background = x.color || GREEN; });
    var tw = tip.offsetWidth, th = tip.offsetHeight, vw = window.innerWidth, vh = window.innerHeight;
    var left = px + 16; if (left + tw > vw - 8) left = px - 16 - tw; if (left < 8) left = 8;
    var top = (clientY != null ? clientY : r.top + r.height / 2) - th / 2; top = Math.max(8, Math.min(vh - th - 8, top));
    tip.style.left = Math.round(left) + 'px'; tip.style.top = Math.round(top) + 'px';
    cur = { el: el, idx: idx };
  }
  function showTip(el, x, y) {
    ensure(); guide.style.display = 'none'; dots.forEach(function (d) { d.style.display = 'none'; });
    tip.innerHTML = E(el.getAttribute('data-w149tip')); tip.style.display = 'block';
    var tw = tip.offsetWidth, th = tip.offsetHeight;
    var left = Math.max(8, Math.min(window.innerWidth - tw - 8, x + 12)), top = y - th - 12; if (top < 8) top = y + 18;
    tip.style.left = Math.round(left) + 'px'; tip.style.top = Math.round(top) + 'px'; cur = { el: el, idx: -1 };
  }
  function move(e) {
    var t = e.target, el = t && t.closest ? t.closest('[data-w149h]') : null;
    if (!el) {
      var bt = t && t.closest ? t.closest('[data-w149tip]') : null;
      if (bt) { showTip(bt, e.clientX, e.clientY); return; }
      if (cur) hide(); return;
    }
    var entry = entryOf(el); if (!entry) { if (cur) hide(); return; }
    var r = el.getBoundingClientRect(); if (!r.width) return;
    place(el, entry, nearest(entry, (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width * entry.w), e.clientY);
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) { if (e.pointerType === 'touch' && !cur) return; move(e); }, { passive: true });
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) { if (e.pointerType === 'touch') move(e); }, { passive: true });
  document.documentElement.addEventListener('mouseleave', hide);
  window.addEventListener('scroll', function () { if (cur) hide(); }, true);
  window.addEventListener('resize', function () { if (cur) hide(); });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', hide);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    var el = document.activeElement; if (!el || !el.hasAttribute || !el.hasAttribute('data-w149h')) return;
    var entry = entryOf(el); if (!entry) return;
    var n = entry.keys.length, idx = cur && cur.el === el && cur.idx >= 0 ? cur.idx : n - 1;
    if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') idx = Math.max(0, idx - 1); else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') idx = Math.min(n - 1, idx + 1);
    else if (e.key === 'Home') idx = 0; else if (e.key === 'End') idx = n - 1; else if (e.key === 'Escape') { hide(); return; } else return;
    e.preventDefault(); place(el, entry, idx, null);
  });
  document.addEventListener('focusin', function (e) { var el = e.target; if (el && el.hasAttribute && el.hasAttribute('data-w149h')) { var entry = entryOf(el); if (entry) place(el, entry, entry.keys.length - 1, null); } });
  document.addEventListener('focusout', function (e) { var el = e.target; if (el && el.hasAttribute && el.hasAttribute('data-w149h')) hide(); });
  /* a post photo that cannot load shows the platform mark (older posts whose copy failed) */
  document.addEventListener('error', function (e) {
    var img = e.target; if (!img || img.tagName !== 'IMG' || !img.closest) return;
    var th = img.closest('#socialstats .post .th');
    if (th) { var mk = th.querySelector('.mk'); var big = document.createElement('span'); big.className = 'big'; big.textContent = mk ? mk.textContent : ''; img.replaceWith(big); return; }
    if (img.closest('#socialstats')) img.remove();
  }, true);

  }

'<div class="sub">Earlier updates</div><div class="news"><ul>' + ups.filter(function (u) { return !u.live; }).slice(0, 8).map(function (u) { return '<li class="off"><span>' + E(u.text) + '<small>' + (u.hidden ? 'Taken down' : 'Ended ' + E(dayLong(u.until))) + '</small></span></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div>' : '')
      + '<p class="note">Shout-outs are written every morning from finished projects, big weeks and anniversaries; take one down in the HR centre. <a data-w149go="#hrcentre">Open the HR centre</a></p>';
    if (host.__h !== html) { host.innerHTML = html; host.__h = html; }
  }
  function showAdmin() { if (!isAdmin() || !onLoading()) return; if (!cardEl()) return; paintAdmin(); loadAdmin(); }
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.id) return;
    if (t.id === 'w149t') { A.draft = t.value; var c = document.getElementById('w149c'); if (c) c.textContent = (140 - t.value.length) + ' characters left'; }
    else if (t.id === 'w149l') A.link = t.value; else if (t.id === 'w149u') A.until = t.value;
  });
  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var x = e.target; if (!x || !x.closest) return;
    var el;
    if ((el = x.closest('#w149card [data-w149hide]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); var id = Number(el.getAttribute('data-w149hide')), on = el.getAttribute('data-on') === '1';
      el.disabled = true;
      var where = el.getAttribute('data-k') === 'news' ? 'the news banner' : 'the loading screen';
      try { var r = await db.rpc('hub_reel_hide', { p_id: id, p_hidden: on }); if (r.error) throw r.error; toastS(on ? 'Taken off ' + where : 'Back on ' + where); }
      catch (e2) { toastS('Could not change it: ' + ((e2 && e2.message) || e2)); }
      await loadAdmin(); refreshCache(); refreshNews(); return;
    }
    if ((el = x.closest('#w149card [data-w149add]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); var text = (A.draft || '').trim();
      if (text.length < 3) { toastS('Write the update first'); return; }

      catch (e3) { toastS('Could not add it: ' + ((e3 && e3.message) || e3)); }
      A.adding = false; await loadAdmin(); refreshCache(); refreshNews(); return;
    }
    if ((el = x.closest('#w149card [data-w149rebuild]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); if (A.rebuilding) return; A.rebuilding = true; paintAdmin();
      try { var r3 = await db.functions.invoke('hub-reel', { body: { action: 'build', force: true } }); if (r3.error) throw r3.error; var dd = r3.data || {}; if (dd.error) throw new Error(dd.error); toastS('Rebuilt: ' + ((dd.reel && dd.reel.lines) || 0) + ' lines and ' + ((dd.news && dd.news.news) || 0) + ' news'); }
      catch (e4) { toastS('Could not rebuild just now: ' + ((e4 && e4.message) || e4)); }
      A.rebuilding = false; await loadAdmin(); refreshCache(); refreshNews(); return;
    }
    if ((el = x.closest('#w149card [data-w149go]'))) { e.preventDefault(); location.hash = el.getAttribute('data-w149go'); }
  });

  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp149 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) { var out = __sp149.apply(this, arguments); try { if (id === 'adminpanel') setTimeout(showAdmin, 80); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }

  /* ---------- 4. the tours: the Admin tour gets a stop ---------- */
  function addTours() {

      }

    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {

/* =====================================================================
   WA v151 the news banner moves from the loading screen to Home (27/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (27/09/26): "I see the news banner, I don't want [it] to be on the loading page, instead put it on the dashboard
   itself", then chose Home (the page staff land on after the loading screen).
   - The banner sits at the top of Home, under the greeting: a gold News label and the day's news scrolling on the green,
     read live from hub_reel_current() (admins' updates first, then the news hub-reel rebuilds every morning). No news, no
     banner. Items with a link open it (a Hub page, or a web page in a new tab).
   - Moving content can be stopped: hovering or focusing it pauses it, and the pause button stops it until pressed again
     (remembered per browser). With reduced motion it shows one item at a time. Keyboard focus on an item brings it into
     view. Screen readers read the list once; the copies that make the loop seamless are hidden from them.
   - The loading screen (000_head_base) no longer shows or caches the news; Admin, Loading screen and news (wave149) edits
     it as before and refreshes this banner.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa151) return; window.__wa151 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', GOLD = '#DCA527', INK = '#1F201E';
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;'); }
  function lsGet(k) { try { return localStorage.getItem(k); } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function lsSet(k, v) { try { if (v == null) localStorage.removeItem(k); else localStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch (e) { /* private window */ } }
  function signedIn() { try { return typeof db !== 'undefined' && !!db && typeof USER !== 'undefined' && !!USER && !!USER.email; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function reduced() { try { return !!(window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches); } catch (e) { return false; } }

  var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'w151css';
  st.textContent = ''
    + '#w151news{position:relative;display:flex;align-items:stretch;height:42px;margin:12px 0 14px;border-radius:14px;overflow:hidden;background:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ';font:13.5px/1.2 ' + BODY + ';box-shadow:0 1px 2px rgba(16,63,57,.12);}'
    + '#w151news .lb{flex:none;display:flex;align-items:center;padding:0 14px 0 16px;background:' + GOLD + ';color:' + INK + ';font-weight:700;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:2.2px;text-transform:uppercase;}'
    + '#w151news .trk{flex:1;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;display:flex;align-items:center;-webkit-mask-image:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent 0,#000 22px,#000 calc(100% - 22px),transparent 100%);mask-image:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent 0,#000 22px,#000 calc(100% - 22px),transparent 100%);}'
    + '#w151news .run{display:inline-flex;white-space:nowrap;will-change:transform;}'
    + '#w151news .run.go{animation:w151tick 30s linear infinite;}'
    + '#w151news:hover .run.go,#w151news:focus-within .run.go,#w151news.paused .run.go{animation-play-state:paused;}'
    + '#w151news .it{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;padding:0 34px 0 0;color:inherit;text-decoration:none;}'

    + '#w151news a.it{cursor:pointer;}#w151news a.it:hover span,#w151news a.it:focus span{text-decoration:underline;text-underline-offset:3px;}'
    + '#w151news a.it:focus{outline:none;}#w151news a.it:focus-visible span{outline:2px solid ' + GOLD + ';outline-offset:3px;border-radius:3px;}'

    + '#w151news .pz{flex:none;width:40px;border:0;border-left:1px solid rgba(255,250,242,.14);background:transparent;color:' + CREAM + ';cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:0;}'

    + '#w151news .pz svg{width:14px;height:14px;fill:currentColor;}'
    + '@keyframes w151tick{from{transform:translateX(0);}to{transform:translateX(-50%);}}'
    + '@media (max-width:600px){#w151news{height:40px;font-size:12.5px;margin:10px 0 12px;}#w151news .lb{padding:0 10px 0 12px;letter-spacing:1.6px;}#w151news .pz{width:36px;}}'

  var N = { items: null, at: 0, busy: null, day: null };
  function cleanLink(l) { l = String(l || '').trim(); if (/^#[\w\/-]{1,80}$/.test(l)) return l; if (/^https:\/\/[^\s"'<>]{4,300}$/.test(l)) return l; return ''; }
  function load(force) {
    if (!signedIn()) return Promise.resolve();
    if (N.busy) return N.busy;
    if (!force && N.items && Date.now() - N.at < 10 * 60000) return Promise.resolve();
    N.busy = (async function () {
      try {
        var r = await db.rpc('hub_reel_current');
        if (r && !r.error) {
          var d = r.data || {};
          N.items = (d.news || []).map(function (n) { return n && n.t ? { t: String(n.t).trim(), link: cleanLink(n.link) } : null; }).filter(function (n) { return n && n.t; }).slice(0, 12);
          N.day = d.day || null; N.at = Date.now();
        } else if (r && r.error) { N.items = []; N.at = Date.now(); }
      } catch (e) { /* offline: keep what we had */ }
      N.busy = null;
    })();
    return N.busy;
  }

  /* ---------- the banner ---------- */
  var PAUSE = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="6" y="5" width="4.2" height="14" rx="1"/><rect x="13.8" y="5" width="4.2" height="14" rx="1"/></svg>';
  var PLAY = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 5.5v13l10.5-6.5z"/></svg>';
  var timer = null;
  function itemHtml(n, copy) {
    var inner = '<span>' + E(n.t) + '</span>';
    if (!n.link) return '<span class="it">' + inner + '</span>';
    var ext = /^https:/.test(n.link);
    return '<a class="it" href="' + E(n.link) + '"' + (ext ? ' target="_blank" rel="noopener"' : '') + (copy ? ' tabindex="-1"' : '') + '>' + inner + (ext ? '<span class="ex" aria-hidden="true">↗</span>' : '') + '</a>';
  }
  function paused() { return lsGet('w151paused') === '1'; }
  function paint() {
    var body = document.getElementById('mpBody');
    var old = document.getElementById('w151news');
    var items = N.items || [];
    var head = body && body.querySelector('.mp-head');
    /* only on the real Home (a board viewer's page has no greeting), and only when there is news */
    if (!body || !head || !items.length) { if (old) old.remove(); stopTimer(); return; }
    var bar = old;
    var sig = JSON.stringify(items);
    if (bar && bar.__sig === sig && bar.parentNode === body) { syncPause(bar); return; }
    if (!bar) { bar = document.createElement('div'); bar.id = 'w151news'; bar.setAttribute('role', 'region'); bar.setAttribute('aria-label', 'News'); }
    if (bar.previousElementSibling !== head) head.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', bar);
    stopTimer();
    var one = reduced();
    bar.className = one ? 'one' : '';
    bar.__sig = sig;
    bar.innerHTML = '<span class="lb" aria-hidden="true">News</span><div class="trk"><div class="run">' + items.map(function (n) { return itemHtml(n, false); }).join('') + '</div></div>'
      + '<button type="button" class="pz" data-w151pause="1"></button>';
    syncPause(bar);
    var run = bar.querySelector('.run');
    if (one) { startOne(bar); return; }
    /* copies of the list make the loop seamless; they are hidden from screen readers and from the keyboard */
    requestAnimationFrame(function () {
      if (!document.body.contains(run)) return;
      var trk = bar.querySelector('.trk'), w0 = (trk && trk.clientWidth) || 800, copies = 1, guard = 0;
      var copyHtml = '<span class="cp" aria-hidden="true" style="display:inline-flex">' + items.map(function (n) { return itemHtml(n, true); }).join('') + '</span>';
      while ((run.scrollWidth < w0 * 2 || copies % 2) && guard++ < 10) { run.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', copyHtml); copies++; }
      var d = Math.max(16, (run.scrollWidth / 2) / 60);
      run.style.animationDuration = d.toFixed(1) + 's';
      run.classList.add('go');
    });
  }
  function startOne(bar) {
    var its = bar.querySelectorAll('.run > .it'); if (!its.length) return;
    var j = 0; its[0].classList.add('on');
    if (its.length < 2) return;
    timer = setInterval(function () {
      if (!document.body.contains(bar)) { stopTimer(); return; }
      if (bar.classList.contains('paused') || bar.matches(':hover') || bar.contains(document.activeElement)) return;
      its[j].classList.remove('on'); j = (j + 1) % its.length; its[j].classList.add('on');
    }, 5000);
  }
  function stopTimer() { if (timer) { clearInterval(timer); timer = null; } }
  function syncPause(bar) {
    var p = paused(), b = bar.querySelector('[data-w151pause]');
    bar.classList.toggle('paused', p);
    if (b) { b.innerHTML = p ? PLAY : PAUSE; b.setAttribute('aria-label', p ? 'Play the news' : 'Pause the news'); b.title = p ? 'Play the news' : 'Pause the news'; }
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w151news [data-w151pause]');
    if (!b) return;
    e.preventDefault(); lsSet('w151paused', paused() ? null : '1');
    var bar = document.getElementById('w151news'); if (bar) syncPause(bar);
  });
  /* a focused item comes into view: the scroll stops while the keyboard is on it */
  document.addEventListener('focusin', function (e) {
    var a = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w151news .run > a.it');
    if (!a) return;
    var bar = document.getElementById('w151news'), run = bar && bar.querySelector('.run'), trk = bar && bar.querySelector('.trk');
    if (!run || !trk || bar.classList.contains('one')) return;
    run.style.animation = 'none';
    run.style.transform = 'translateX(' + (-Math.max(0, a.offsetLeft - 22)) + 'px)';
  });
  document.addEventListener('focusout', function (e) {
    var bar = document.getElementById('w151news');
    if (!bar || !bar.contains(e.target)) return;
    setTimeout(function () { if (bar.contains(document.activeElement)) return; var run = bar.querySelector('.run'); if (run) { run.style.animation = ''; run.style.transform = ''; } }, 0);
  });

  function refresh(force) { return load(force).then(paint).catch(function () { /* fine */ }); }
  if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') {
    var __rm151 = renderMyPage;
    renderMyPage = async function () {
      var r = await __rm151.apply(this, arguments);
      try { paint(); refresh(false); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return r;
    };
  }
  /* the news is rebuilt every morning: a page left open picks up the new day */
  setInterval(function () { if (document.visibilityState !== 'hidden' && document.getElementById('mpBody')) refresh(true); }, 30 * 60000);
  try { if (typeof db !== 'undefined' && db && db.auth) db.auth.onAuthStateChange(function (evt) { if (evt === 'SIGNED_OUT') { N.items = null; N.at = 0; var b = document.getElementById('w151news'); if (b) b.remove(); } }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  /* ---------- the Home tour gets a stop ---------- */
  function addTour() {
    try {
      var T = window.w122 && window.w122.tours; if (!T || !T.home) return;
      var s = T.home.steps || [];
      if (!s.some(function (x) { return x.s === '#w151news'; })) {

Hover over it or press pause to stop it; an item with a link opens it.', opt: 1 });
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  addTour(); setTimeout(addTour, 1500);

  window.w151 = { refresh: refresh, state: function () { return N; }, paint: paint };
})();

   Steel: "hub.welfareavenue.com: the Hub ... the CRM as an app inside the Hub, hub being the back end",

  var PERM = {

  function I(n, cls) { return '<svg class="w152i' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : '') + '" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">' + (P[n] || P.spark) + '</svg>'; }
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;'); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).toLowerCase().trim(); }
  /* for matching: lower case, accents off (\u00c9milie finds emilie) */
  function fold(s) { try { return lower(s).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, ''); } catch (e) { return lower(s); } }
  function digits(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/\D+/g, ''); }
  function clip(s, n) { s = String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); return s.length > n ? s.slice(0, n - 1).trim() + '\u2026' : s; }
  function toastS(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function plural(n, one, many) { return Number(n).toLocaleString('en-CA') + ' ' + (n === 1 ? one : many); }
  function arr(a) { return Array.isArray(a) ? a.filter(function (x) { return x != null && String(x).trim() !== ''; }) : []; }
  function uniq(a) { var seen = {}, out = []; a.forEach(function (x) { var k = lower(x); if (k && !seen[k]) { seen[k] = 1; out.push(x); } }); return out; }
  function initials(n) { var p = String(n || '').replace(/[()]/g, '').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); if (!p.length) return '?'; return (p[0][0] + (p.length > 1 ? p[p.length - 1][0] : '')).toUpperCase(); }
  function avColor(id) { var n = Math.abs(Number(id) || 0); return AV[n % AV.length]; }
  function money(n) { var v = Number(n) || 0, f = v % 1 ? 2 : 0; try { return v.toLocaleString('en-CA', { style: 'currency', currency: 'CAD', minimumFractionDigits: f, maximumFractionDigits: f }); } catch (e) { return '$' + v; } }
  function listJoin(a) { a = (a || []).filter(Boolean); if (a.length <= 1) return a.join(''); return a.slice(0, -1).join(', ') + ' and ' + a[a.length - 1]; }

  /* ---------- dates (the Hub's clock is Montreal time) ---------- */
  function torontoDay(t) { try { return new Date(t).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto' }); } catch (e) { return new Date(t).toISOString().slice(0, 10); } }
  function today() { return torontoDay(Date.now()); }
  /* a date column (2026-09-14) or a timestamp, as the day it was in Montreal */
  function dayOf(s) {
    s = String(s || ''); if (!s) return '';
    if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s)) return s;
    var t = new Date(s).getTime(); return isNaN(t) ? '' : torontoDay(t);
  }
  function fmtDay(s) { var d = dayOf(s); if (!d) return ''; var p = d.split('-'); return Number(p[2]) + ' ' + MON[Number(p[1]) - 1] + ' ' + p[0]; }
  function daysSince(s) { var d = dayOf(s); if (!d) return null; return Math.round((Date.parse(today() + 'T12:00:00Z') - Date.parse(d + 'T12:00:00Z')) / 86400000); }
  function ago(s) {
    var n = daysSince(s); if (n == null) return '';
    if (n < 0) return 'in ' + plural(-n, 'day', 'days');
    if (n === 0) return 'today';
    if (n === 1) return 'yesterday';
    if (n < 14) return n + ' days ago';
    if (n < 60) return Math.round(n / 7) + ' weeks ago';
    if (n < 700) return Math.round(n / 30.4) + ' months ago';
    return Math.round(n / 365.25) + ' years ago';
  }
  function addYears(day, n) { var p = String(day || '').split('-'); if (p.length < 3) return ''; return (Number(p[0]) + n) + '-' + p[1] + '-' + (p[1] === '02' && p[2] === '29' ? '28' : p[2]); }

  /* ---------- who is looking ---------- */
  function staffOk() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function boardMode() { try { return typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function wRole() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function amAdmin() { return wRole() === 'admin'; }   /* wave283: role admin only (no hard-coded addresses) */
  function actor() { return myEmail() || 'staff'; }
  /* wave283: what the signed-in person may do (wa_my_caps, wave281: gifts_edit, import_admin, hr_private), loaded once by openCrm */
  function can(cap) { return !!(S.caps || {})[cap]; }

  var RULE_TEXT = 'Newsletters, fundraising and volunteer emails go to everyone with a working address who has not unsubscribed. Receipts and confirmations go to any working address.';

  var COLS = 'id,full_name,first_name,last_name,emails,phones,primary_email,address,city,province,postal_code,country,language,kinds,tags,source,volunteer_id,member_id,' +
    'email_status,email_consent,consent_source,consent_at,implied_consent_until,sms_opt_out,notes,last_activity_at,created_at,updated_at,created_by,updated_by,legacy_refs,' +

    'donation_total,donation_count,last_donation_at,org_names,job_title,do_not_contact,dnc_reason';   /* wave283: the last three (wave281's crm_contacts_v) */
  var S = {
    contacts: null, dupes: null, counts: null, partners: null, gifts: null, loading: {}, at: {}, err: {},
    ui: { tab: 'contacts', kind: '', role: '', act: '', perm: '', q: '', sort: 'recent', show: 60, gq: '', gplat: '', gyear: '', gshow: 60 },
    sheet: null, detail: {}, confirm: null
  };
  /* the API answers at most 1,000 rows a call: read a table page by page until a short page (or nothing new) */
  var PAGE_ROWS = 1000;
  function pageAll(query, key) {
    var rows = [], seen = {};
    function step(from) {
      return Promise.resolve(query(from, from + PAGE_ROWS - 1)).then(function (res) {
        if (!res || res.error) return { data: rows, error: res && res.error };
        var got = res.data || [], fresh = 0;
        got.forEach(function (r) { var k = key(r); if (seen[k]) return; seen[k] = 1; rows.push(r); fresh++; });
        if (got.length < PAGE_ROWS || !fresh || rows.length > 50000) return { data: rows, error: null };
        return step(from + PAGE_ROWS);
      });
    }
    return step(0);
  }
  var LOADERS = {
    contacts: function () { return pageAll(function (a, b) { return db.from('crm_contacts_v').select(COLS).order('id', { ascending: true }).range(a, b); }, function (r) { return String(r.id); }); },
    dupes: function () { return pageAll(function (a, b) { return db.from('crm_duplicates_v').select('*').order('contact_id', { ascending: true }).order('duplicate_id', { ascending: true }).range(a, b); }, function (r) { return r.contact_id + ':' + r.duplicate_id + ':' + r.matched_on + ':' + r.value; }); },
    partners: function () { return db.from('wa_partners').select('id,name,aka,status,visibility').order('name').limit(3000); },
    /* wave153: the Gifts tab */
    gifts: function () { return pageAll(function (a, b) { return db.from('crm_donations').select('id,contact_id,amount,currency,donated_at,platform,campaign,designation,recurring,receipt_number,receipt_issued_at,receipt_url,receipt_source,receipt_eligible,external_id,notes,kind,method,advantage_amount,advantage_desc,in_kind_desc,appraiser,tribute_kind,tribute_name,created_by,updated_by,fee,net_amount,refunded_amount,refunded_at,plan_id').order('donated_at', { ascending: false }).order('id', { ascending: false }).range(a, b); }, function (r) { return String(r.id); }); }
  };
  /* the rows the page works with: a few derived fields for filters, sorting and search */
  function shape(c) {
    c.emails = arr(c.emails); c.phones = arr(c.phones); c.kinds = arr(c.kinds); c.tags = arr(c.tags);
    c.volunteer_roles = arr(c.volunteer_roles); c.volunteer_tags = arr(c.volunteer_tags); c.org_names = arr(c.org_names);
    c._roles = uniq(c.volunteer_roles.concat(c.volunteer_tags.filter(function (t) { return ROLES[t]; })));
    c._served = (Number(c.hub_servings) || 0) + (Number(c.legacy_servings) || 0);
    var acts = [dayOf(c.last_serving_on), dayOf(c.last_activity_at), dayOf(c.last_donation_at)].filter(Boolean).sort();
    c._last = acts.length ? acts[acts.length - 1] : '';
    c._donor = c.kinds.indexOf('donor') >= 0 || Number(c.donation_count) > 0;
    c._partner = c.kinds.indexOf('partner') >= 0 || c.org_names.length > 0;
    c._perm = permKey(c);
    c._digits = c.phones.map(digits).filter(function (d) { return d.length >= 7; });
    c._hay = fold([c.full_name, c.emails.join(' '), c.phones.join(' '), c.city, c.volunteer_organization, c.org_names.join(' '), c.tags.join(' '),
      c._roles.map(function (r) { return ROLES[r] || r; }).join(' '), c.kinds.map(function (k) { return (KINDS[k] || [k])[0]; }).join(' ')].join(' '));
    return c;
  }
  /* the permission that applies today: an opt-out wins, an implied consent runs out */
  function permKey(c) {
    var st = lower(c.email_status);
    if (st === 'unsubscribed' || st === 'complained') return 'unsub';
    if (st === 'bounced') return 'bounced';
    var k = lower(c.email_consent);
    if (k === 'express') return 'express';
    if (k === 'implied') return (!c.implied_consent_until || dayOf(c.implied_consent_until) >= today()) ? 'implied' : 'unknown';
    if (k === 'none') return 'none';
    return 'unknown';
  }
  function load(what, force) {
    if (S.loading[what]) return S.loading[what];
    if (S[what] && !force && Date.now() - (S.at[what] || 0) < 120000) return Promise.resolve(S[what]);
    if (typeof db === 'undefined' || !db || !db.from) return Promise.resolve(S[what] || []);
    S.loading[what] = Promise.resolve(LOADERS[what]()).then(function (res) {
      S.loading[what] = null;
      if (res && res.error) { S.err[what] = String(res.error.message || res.error); S[what] = S[what] || []; return S[what]; }
      S.err[what] = ''; S.at[what] = Date.now();
      var rows = (res && res.data) || [];
      if (what === 'contacts') { rows = rows.filter(function (c) { return c && c.id != null; }).map(shape); indexContacts(rows); }
      S[what] = rows;
      return rows;
    }).catch(function (e) { S.loading[what] = null; S.err[what] = String((e && e.message) || e); S[what] = S[what] || []; return S[what]; });
    return S.loading[what];
  }
  var BY_ID = {};
  function indexContacts(rows) { BY_ID = {}; rows.forEach(function (c) { BY_ID[String(c.id)] = c; }); }
  function contact(id) { return BY_ID[String(id)] || null; }
  /* the pairs to look at: both still in the CRM, one card per pair whatever matched */
  function dupePairs() {
    var by = {};
    (S.dupes || []).forEach(function (d) {
      var a = Math.min(Number(d.contact_id), Number(d.duplicate_id)), b = Math.max(Number(d.contact_id), Number(d.duplicate_id));
      if (!contact(a) || !contact(b)) return;
      var k = a + '-' + b;
      if (!by[k]) by[k] = { a: a, b: b, on: [], values: [] };
      if (by[k].on.indexOf(d.matched_on) < 0) by[k].on.push(d.matched_on);
      if (d.value && by[k].values.indexOf(d.value) < 0) by[k].values.push(d.value);
    });
    return Object.keys(by).map(function (k) { return by[k]; }).sort(function (x, y) { return (y.on.length - x.on.length) || (x.a - y.a); });
  }

  /* the masthead and the host of the page */
  function mastFor() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('crm'); if (!sec) return null;
    var m = sec.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
    if (!m) { m = document.createElement('div'); m.className = 'w118mast'; m.setAttribute('data-w152', 'crm'); sec.insertBefore(m, sec.firstChild); }
    var html = '<div class="crumb">Apps</div><h1>CRM</h1><p>Everyone Welfare Avenue knows (volunteers, donors, partners and the team) in one place, with what they have done with us and when we last heard from them.</p>';
    if (m.getAttribute('data-h') !== html) { m.innerHTML = html; m.setAttribute('data-h', html); }
    var h = sec.querySelector(':scope > .w152root');
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.className = 'w152root'; sec.appendChild(h); }
    return h;
  }
  function busy(msg) { return '<div class="w152empty">' + E(msg || 'Loading\u2026') + '</div>'; }
  function kindChip(k) { var d = KINDS[k] || [k.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + k.slice(1), MUTED]; return '<span class="w152kd" style="background:' + d[1] + '1a;color:' + d[1] + '">' + E(d[0]) + '</span>'; }
  function kindsHtml(c) {
    var ks = c.kinds.slice();
    if (c._donor && ks.indexOf('donor') < 0) ks.push('donor');
    if (c._partner && ks.indexOf('partner') < 0) ks.push('partner');
    ks.sort(function (a, b) { var x = KIND_ORDER.indexOf(a), y = KIND_ORDER.indexOf(b); return (x < 0 ? 99 : x) - (y < 0 ? 99 : y); });
    return ks.map(kindChip).join('');
  }
  function permChip(key) { var p = PERM[key] || PERM.unknown; return '<span class="w152pm" style="background:' + p[1] + '17;color:' + p[1] + '"><i style="background:' + p[1] + '"></i>' + E(p[0]) + '</span>'; }
  function avatar(c, big) { return '<span class="w152av' + (big ? ' big' : '') + '" style="background:' + avColor(c.id) + '" aria-hidden="true">' + E(initials(c.full_name)) + '</span>'; }
  function roleName(r) { return ROLES[r] || String(r || '').replace(/_/g, ' '); }
